### [1] [Separating perception from reasoning in vision-language models: a model-free render ceiling for crystal structures](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.00663)
*Can Polat,Mustafa Kurban,Erchin Serpedin,Hasan Kurban*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出"渲染天花板"（render ceiling）——一种无需第二模型介入的无模型参照基准，用于分离视觉-语言模型（vision-language model）的图像误读与推理错误。通过逆向相机和内化重建交叉视图对应关系，可精确恢复图像所支持的信息，并在2160个渲染晶体结构上验证该天花板仅失败于可枚举的投影巧合集合。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有多模态评估无法分辨视觉-语言模型是误读图像还是推理失误，因为所有分离这两种错误的方法都依赖第二个模型参与，形成循环依赖。作者旨在建立无模型的基准参照来打破这一局限。

Method: 提出"渲染天花板"方法：通过逆向冻结的相机参数并重新求解跨视图对应关系，从已知物体的渲染基准中恢复图像精确支持的信息。作者用数学证明该天花板仅在可枚举的投影巧合下失败，并在2160个渲染的晶体结构上验证该集合为空，从而确保模型的所有误差都可归因于模型本身。

Result: 在十四个视觉-语言模型上的实验显示：以文本形式提供精确几何信息能提升所有模型的表现，但其中十三个模型的差距仅被缩小不到一半；而一个不含语言组件的监督视觉模型在相同图像上达到0.8952的分数，超过所有视觉-语言模型。该工具还暴露了提取阶段的捏造问题，并为基准构建者提供了相机放置规则。

Conclusion: 渲染天花板提供了一种可迁移的错误归因工具，适用于任何具有可逆前向渲染的基准测试，能够将下游准确率可能误判为推理缺陷的提取阶段问题区分开来。

Abstract: Multimodal evaluations cannot say whether a vision-language model misread an image or misreasoned about it, because every existing method for separating the two places a second model in the loop. We introduce the render ceiling, a model-free reference for benchmarks built by rendering known objects: inverting the frozen cameras and re-solving cross-view correspondence recovers exactly the answer the images support. We prove the ceiling fails only through an enumerable set of projection coincidences and certify that set empty on 2,160 rendered crystal structures, so every point of a model's deficit belongs to the model. Across fourteen vision-language models, supplying exact geometry as text lifts every model yet closes under half the gap for thirteen, while a supervised vision model with no language component reads the same images at 0.8952, above every vision-language model. The instrument exposes extraction-stage fabrication that downstream accuracy would misattribute to reasoning, yields camera-placement rules for benchmark builders, and transfers to any benchmark with an invertible forward rendering.

</details>

### [2] [Why Multi-Layer Message Passing Works: Completeness Theory for Graph Neural Network Interatomic Potentials](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.00528)
*Pingbing Ming,Han Wang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文证明超图神经网络（Hypergraph Neural Network）——一种基于三体消息传递（3-body message passing）的不变架构——是势能面（potential energy surface）的通用近似器。核心贡献是提出多层完备性理论（multi-layer completeness theory），表明在满足一般性、重叠条件和连通性条件时，稀疏截断图上的 $L$ 层消息传递与访问完整 $L$ 跳邻域具有相同的表示能力，为实际机器学习原子间势（machine-learned interatomic potentials）中普遍采用逐层截断小于物理相互作用范围的做法提供了首个严格理论依据。作为直接推论，DPA3 和 CHGNet 架构也具有通用近似性质。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 实际基于图神经网络（graph neural network）的机器学习原子间势几乎都采用逐层截断半径小于物理相互作用范围的多层消息传递，但此前缺乏严格的理论证明来解释这种做法为何能够捕获长程相互作用。本文旨在填补这一理论空白，为这种广泛使用的实践提供严格的表示能力分析。

Method: 作者证明超图神经网络这一具有三体消息传递的不变架构是势能面的通用近似器。主要技术路线是建立多层完备性理论（multi-layer completeness theory），即证明在配置是一般性（generic）、满足重叠条件（overlap condition）和连通性条件（connectivity condition）的前提下，在稀疏截断图上进行 $L$ 层消息传递与访问完整 $L$ 跳邻域在表示能力上是等价的。

Result: 证明了 $L$ 层截断图上的消息传递具有与完整 $L$ 跳邻域相同的表示能力，从而确立超图神经网络的通用近似性。作为直接推论，DPA3 和 CHGNet 两种架构均继承了通用近似性质。

Conclusion: 该工作为多层消息传递配合小于物理相互作用范围的逐层截断这一普遍实践提供了首个严格的数学依据，有助于深化对现有机器学习原子间势架构表达能力的理论理解。

Abstract: We prove that the Hypergraph Neural Network, an invariant architecture with 3-body message passing, is a universal approximator for potential energy surfaces. Our main contribution is a multi-layer completeness theory. We show that $L$ layers of message passing on sparse, cutoff-based graphs achieve the same representational power as having access to the full $L$-hop neighborhood, provided the configurations are generic, satisfy an overlap condition and a connectivity condition. This provides the first rigorous justification for the common practice of using multi-layer message passing with a per-layer cutoff smaller than the physical interaction range, the setting used by virtually all practical graph neural network based machine-learned interatomic potentials. As immediate consequences, we show that both DPA3 and CHGNet architectures inherit universal approximation.

</details>


### [3] [Generative artificial intelligence for reliable mechanistic reasoning for corrosion](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.00099)
*Bharath M N,R K Singh Raman,Alankar Alankar*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种面向腐蚀知识综合的领域自适应检索增强生成（retrieval-augmented generation, RAG）框架，以镁合金腐蚀为案例，通过对三个开源语言模型进行微调并结合混合稠密-词汇检索流程实现腐蚀速率的高保真预测与可解释推理；同时引入Reason Map命题图框架，用于检测因果方向反转和缺乏支持的推理跳跃。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 腐蚀约占全球GDP的4%，及时缓解需要可靠的预测；然而机器学习虽能有效预测腐蚀速率，却无法解释其潜在机制，而现有事实性（factuality）度量指标也无法评估安全关键材料工程所需的机制可辩护推理能力。

Method: 在840篇同行评审论文的3,309个专家验证问答对（question-answer pairs）基础上微调三个开源权重语言模型（Llama-3.1-8B、Qwen-2.5-7B、Mistral-7B），并与混合稠密-词法检索流程（hybrid dense-lexical retrieval pipeline）集成；此外引入Reason Map命题图框架，从生成的回答和检索到的文献中独立构建有向证据图。

Result: 检索增强使Token F1得分提升143%-194%，系统忠实度（faithfulness）达0.964，上下文召回率（context recall）达0.988；基于新发表文献和内部电化学数据的盲外部验证（blind external validation）证实了趋势级别的泛化能力。

Conclusion: Reason Map能够系统性检测因果方向反转和缺乏支持的推理跳跃，而这些是平坦事实性指标无法暴露的缺陷；该模块化架构可跨领域应用，为实现可信的AI辅助知识综合提供了可推广的蓝图，不仅适用于缓蚀，也可推广至其他工程领域。

Abstract: Corrosion accounts for approximately 4% of global GDP, and reliable prediction is essential for timely mitigation. Machine learning effectively predicts corrosion rates from composition, microstructure, and environmental variables, but cannot explain the underlying mechanisms. A reliable approach in safety-critical materials engineering requires not only accurate retrieval but also mechanistically defensible reasoning, a capability that existing factuality metrics cannot assess. This work presents a domain-adapted retrieval-augmented generation framework for corrosion knowledge synthesis, demonstrated on magnesium alloy corrosion. Three open-weight language models (Llama-3.1-8B, Qwen-2.5-7B, Mistral-7B) are fine-tuned on 3,309 expert-verified question-answer pairs from 840 peer-reviewed papers and integrated with a hybrid dense-lexical retrieval pipeline. Retrieval augmentation produces Token F1 gains of 143-194%, with system faithfulness of 0.964 and context recall of 0.988. Blind external validation on newly published literature and in-house electrochemical data confirms trend-level generalisation. Reason Map, a proposition-graph framework, is further introduced; it independently constructs directed evidence graphs from generated answers and retrieved literature, enabling systematic detection of causal direction inversions and unsupported inferential leaps that flat factuality metrics cannot expose. The modular architecture can be applied across domains, offering a generalizable blueprint for trustworthy AI-assisted knowledge synthesis to circumvent corrosion, which can also be applied to other engineering domains.

</details>

### [4] [How Landau caterpillars turn into Hofstadter butterflies by tuning the periodic potential strength](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.00146)
*Ivo A. Gabrovski,Louk Rademaker*

Main category: cond-mat.mes-hall

TL;DR: 本文通过连续增大周期性势场强度，将二维电子在磁场中的朗道能级（Landau levels）与紧束缚模型中的Hofstadter蝶形谱（Hofstadter butterfly spectrum）联系起来，揭示了蝶形谱从展宽的朗道能级中涌现的机制，并识别出一系列拓扑相变及其在磁通φ>1时的拓扑差异，还对基于Wannier化的有效紧束缚模型参数随磁通变化的行为进行了分析。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 二维电子在垂直磁场中的谱为分立的平坦朗道能级，而二维紧束缚模型则给出分形的Hofstadter蝶形谱，这两个极端极限之间的关联尚不清晰。作者旨在连接这两个相反极限，理解蝶形谱如何从朗道能级中涌现，并为人工材料（如莫尔系统）及大磁场下的多体计算提供理论基础。

Method: 通过连续增加周期性势场强度，使朗道能级展宽并演化为蝶形谱；识别系列拓扑相变以隔离最低平庸带（trivial band）；在φ>1范围内对比蝶形谱与标准Hofstadter蝶形谱的拓扑差异；并通过大磁场下的Wannier化（Wannierization）方法提取有效紧束缚模型的跳迁参数，分析其对磁通的依赖关系。

Result: 发现蝶形谱从展宽的朗道"毛虫"能级中涌现，并识别出隔离最低平庸带所必需的系列拓扑相变。该蝶形谱在φ>1时因对角线跳跃（diagonal hopping）的异常行为而与标准Hofstadter蝶形谱在拓扑上不同。Wannier化得到的跳迁参数强烈依赖于磁通，表明Wannier轨道本身在磁场下会发生改变。

Conclusion: 该方法与结果对实验上可实现每晶胞一个完整磁通量子的莫尔系统等人工材料具有直接相关性；在理论层面，针对具体磁通的Wannier化方法能够支持大磁场下更精确的多体计算。

Abstract: It is well-known that the spectrum of two-dimensional electrons in a perpendicular magnetic field is given by discrete flat Landau levels. By contrast, electrons in a two-dimensional tight-binding model give rise to a fractal Hofstadter butterfly spectrum. In this paper, we connect these two opposite limits by showing how a butterfly spectrum emerges from broadened Landau `caterpillars', by continuously increasing the periodic potential strength. We identify a series of topological transitions that isolate a lowest trivial band, a necessary condition for the butterfly to emerge. The resulting butterfly is topologically distinct from the Hofstadter butterfly at fluxes $φ>1$, due to anomalous behavior of diagonal hopping. Moreover, the hopping parameters of an effective tight-binding model, obtained by a Wannierization procedure at large magnetic field, are highly dependent on the flux, revealing that Wannier orbitals themselves change under the applied magnetic field. Our methods and results are relevant for artificial materials, such as moiré systems, where a full quantum of flux per lattice unit cell is experimentally accessible. On a theoretical level, having Wannierization methods for each specific flux allows more accurate many-body calculations in large magnetic fields.

</details>


### [5] [Entanglement from particle number fluctuations in a second-order topological insulator](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.00239)
*Mahla Moridi Farimani,Kim Pöyhönen*

Main category: cond-mat.mes-hall

TL;DR: 本文将关联数量涨落的探测方法从一维系统推广到二维二阶拓扑绝缘体（higher-order topological insulator），提出四分法（quadripartite）子系统构造，从而在双分纠缠熵（bipartite entanglement entropy）与粒子数涨落中提取出清晰的拓扑相变信号，为高阶拓扑绝缘体中纠缠的实验探测提供了可行的可扩展路径。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 高阶拓扑绝缘体具有修改后的体-边对应关系（bulk-boundary correspondence），其引入关注的拓扑特性虽非平庸，但其检测方案尚不充分。已有研究表明，一维系统中守恒量的涨落可用于研究拓扑（其末端模式为零维），本文欲将此思路推广至二维二阶拓扑绝缘体，其角色对应的角态（corner states）与一维末端模式具有类似性质。

Method: 作者首先检验标准双分纠缠熵与粒子数涨落能否反映二维二阶拓扑绝缘体中的拓扑相变，发现其在静态及猝灭（quench）后的时间演化中均被子系统边缘的面积律（area-law）项所掩盖，缺乏明显信号。随后引入四分割（quadripartite）子系统构造，将子系统划分为四个区域以隔离拓扑贡献，从而提取出尖锐的相变峰。

Result: 结果表明，在四分构建下，双分纠缠熵与粒子数涨落两种量均能在远小于全系统的子系统尺度上清晰呈现尖锐的相变峰。由于粒子数涨落在现有实验中可直接测量，这一结果提供了探测高阶拓扑绝缘体中纠缠信号的实验上可行且可扩展的手段。

Conclusion: 该工作建立了高阶拓扑绝缘体中基于守恒量涨落探测纠缠与拓扑相变的通用框架，克服了面积律干扰，并提出可通过实验获取粒子数涨落来实现实际探测，具备可扩展的应用前景。

Abstract: Higher-order topological insulators are materials with a bulk energy gap featuring gapless modes at $(d-n)$-dimensional edges, where $n > 1$. Despite this modified bulk-boundary correspondence, their nontrivial topology can nevertheless be observed through the bipartite entanglement spectrum. Inspired by works suggesting fluctuations in conserved quantities could be used to study topology in one-dimensional systems, which likewise feature zero-dimensional edge modes, we extend this treatment to a two-dimensional second-order topological insulator where corner states play an analogous role. We show that the standard bipartite entanglement entropy and particle number fluctuation lack striking signals of topological phase transitions, both in the static case and in the time evolution after a quench, obscured by an area-law term from the subsystem edges. By introducing a quadripartite construction of the subsystem, we show that it is possible to isolate the topological contributions, producing sharp transition peaks in both quantities, observable by studying subsystems much smaller than the full system. As particle number fluctuations are accessible in existing experiments, these results establish a concrete and scalable path to experimental detection of the entanglement in higher-order topological insulators.

</details>


### [6] [Chiral phonons driven by chiral cavities](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.00368)
*V. A. S. V. Bittencourt,N. Shabala,R. M. Geilhufe,A. Metelmann*

Main category: cond-mat.mes-hall

TL;DR: 本文提出利用驱动电磁腔（driven electromagnetic cavity）产生稳态手性声子（chiral phonons）的新途径，推导了腔-手性声子相互作用的普适描述，并证明在优化条件下通过实际功率的外部驱动可产生显著的有效声子诱导磁场。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 携带角动量的晶格振动可引发声子霍尔效应（phonon Hall effect）和爱因斯坦-德哈斯效应（Einstein-de Haas effect）等基本现象，为凝聚态系统中操纵角动量提供了新机会。现有产生圆极化声子的方法依赖外部磁场或偏振光脉冲，后者虽不需要材料的磁响应，但脉冲持续时间有限导致声子诱导效应是瞬态的，需要稳态的手性声子产生方案。

Method: 采用驱动电磁腔作为产生稳态布居手性声子的途径，推导了腔-手性声子相互作用的一般理论描述，尤其关注手性腔（chiral cavities）模式的特征，并在优化条件下评估外部驱动产生声子诱导磁场的效果。

Result: 在优化条件下，通过具有实际可行功率的外部驱动可以产生显著的有效声子诱导磁场（effective phonon-induced magnetic field）。该方法特别适用于太赫兹（THz）频率的Gamma点声子。

Conclusion: 该方案为利用电磁腔研究稳态手性声子开辟了新途径，克服了光脉冲方法中效应瞬态性的局限。

Abstract: Lattice vibrations carrying angular momentum give rise to fundamental phenomena such as the phonon Hall effect and the Einstein-de Haas effect, offering new opportunities for manipulating angular momentum in condensed-matter systems. Generating such circularly polarized phonons requires either the use of an external magnetic field, or of polarized light pulses. The latter approach does not require the use of any magnetic response of the material, but it has limitations, in particular regarding the duration of the pulse. Consequently, any effect stemming from phonons generated by light pulses is transient. In this paper, we propose the use of a driven electromagnetic cavity as a route to generate a steady-state population of chiral phonons. We derive a general description of cavity-chiral phonon interaction, with a particular focus on modes of chiral cavities. We show that, under optimized conditions, a significant effective phonon-induced magnetic field can be generated by means of an external drive with realistic power. Our approach is specially tailored for Gamma point phonons with THz frequencies, and opens a new route for investigating chiral phonons with electromagnetic cavities.

</details>


### [7] [Floquet scattering and Fano resonances in nodal-ring and multi-Weyl semimetals: Role of propagating and evanescent modes](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.00631)
*Sandip Bera,Ipsita Mandal*

Main category: cond-mat.mes-hall

TL;DR: 本文将早期忽略倏逝模式（evanescent modes）的Floquet散射理论推广至包含传播与倏逝两种通道的完备形式主义，用于周期驱动的节点环半金属（nodal-ring semimetals）和多Weyl半金属（multi-Weyl semimetals）的量子输运研究，证明倏逝模式虽为满足边界条件所必需但不贡献可观测输运量，并揭示了一种含双传播通道相干散射的新输运机制。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Abstract: We develop a comprehensive Floquet scattering theory for quantum transport in periodically driven nodal-ring semimetals (NRSs) and multi-Weyl semimetals (mWSMs), extending our earlier study reported in Annalen der Physik 535, 2200460 (2023), in which evanescent modes were neglected, to a complete formalism that includes both propagating and evanescent channels. By solving the full boundary-value problem, we obtain the complete set of scattering states and show that, although evanescent modes are indispensable for satisfying the matching conditions at the potential interfaces, they carry zero net probability current and do not contribute to any observable transport quantity. We identify a previously unexplored transport regime in NRSs in which two propagating channels coexist and participate in coherent scattering, producing multi-channel quantum interference and Floquet-induced Fano resonances. The transmission, reflection, pumped shot noise, and the associated Fano resonance features are determined entirely by the propagating channels, and the resonance energies coincide with those of the corresponding quasi-bound states of the static potential well. Our results establish a unified framework for Floquet transport in anisotropic topological semimetals, and confirm that the approximation of neglecting evanescent modes in our earlier work is justified for all measurable transport properties.

Abstract: Critically tilted Dirac cones have attracted considerable attention because of their unconventional electronic properties in two-dimensional massless Dirac-fermion systems. This band structure produces a pronounced enhancement of the density of states at the Fermi energy and, consequently, a substantial enhancement of the electronic specific heat compared with that expected for an ideal narrow-sense type-III Dirac cone. Our results show that the honeycomb lattice with anisotropic next-nearest-neighbor hoppings provides a simple platform for exploring unconventional thermodynamic properties arising from the energy coincidence of narrow-sense type-III Dirac cones and additional flat lines at the Fermi energy.

To support these findings, the gate-voltage-dependent differential-conductance line shapes measured in the odd-particle sector of a QDSET are theoretically reproduced. Abstract: Duality is a fundamental concept in physics that connects complementary opposites like particles and holes. Similarly, while topological states in topological insulators localize at boundaries, the existence and nature of their dual counterparts remain unexplored. Here, we introduce anti-topological states as the dual of topological states, exemplified by anti-higher-order topological insulators. Unlike higher-order topological insulators, where states localize at corners, anti-higher-order topological insulators host states along edges but absent at corners, realizing an inverted distribution of states. We demonstrate this phenomenon in a bilayer Chern insulator with opposite Chern numbers, where the band inversion surfaces enclose distinct high-symmetry points. The topological invariant distinguishing these phases is given by the topological charges enclosed by band inversion surfaces. This work establishes anti-topology as a new paradigm, opening a chapter in topological research.

</details>

Abstract: Higher-order topology extends the bulk-boundary correspondence by enabling corner or hinge localized states. Using a minimal two-band model, we show how Weyl points separate the Brillouin zone into quantum anomalous Hall and anti-higher-order topological insulating regions, with the latter characterized by a band-inversion surface enclosing two distinct high-symmetry points carrying opposite topological charges. Analytical solutions for Weyl points, Berry curvature monopoles, and slice Chern numbers are derived, with numerical simulations confirming the resulting anti-hinge behavior. Our work establishes anti-higher-order topology as a dual counterpart to conventional higher-order phenomena, further extending the exploration of topological phases.

Abstract: We theoretically study a hybrid superconductor-semiconductor platform hosting Majorana bound states coupled to a single mode photonic cavity. Starting with a one-dimensional wire coupled to a bulk $s$-wave superconductor embedded in a photonic cavity, we derive an effective light-matter Hamiltonian for such a platform. Cavity embedding has the strongest effect on the phase diagram in the semiclassical regime, corresponding to a large number of photons. In all cases, we find that at large values of the light-matter coupling strength the effective superconducting pairing is suppressed, driving the system into the gapless phase. We demonstrate that even small light-matter coupling strength allows for entering the topological phase at values of the Zeeman energy compared to the uncoupled platform.

Main category: cond-mat.mes-hall

  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 强驱动两能级系统的准能量谱中会出现由隐藏的时间非局域对称性（hidden time-nonlocal symmetries）所实现的锥形交叉，但其是否携带量子化的拓扑荷尚待阐明；同时，驱动幅值趋于零时出现的另一类简并及其拓扑性质也是此前未被系统研究的问题。

Abstract: Topological valley kink states (VKSs), which are quantum valley Hall states emerging at the interfaces between adjacent domains with opposite valley Chern numbers, have attracted considerable interest in graphene-based systems. Two prominent transport characteristics are revealed. First, in the absence of a magnetic field, the kink states exhibit strong layer polarization, with their wave functions predominantly localized and equally distributed on the outermost top and bottom layers. As a result, their transport properties are highly sensitive to the layer-selective disorder distribution. Second, under a perpendicular magnetic field, the layer-symmetric spatial distribution of VKSs is broken, leading to a significant reduction in the wave-function overlap between counter-propagating VKSs from opposite valleys. Consequently, intervalley scattering is suppressed, and the transmission of VKSs through disordered regions is substantially enhanced. Abstract: The edge modes of spin-singlet fractional quantum Hall (FQH) phases are manifestations of multicomponent topological order and SU(2) spin symmetry. Here, we develop a spin-sensitive probe of graphene FQH edges by using a p-n junction to interface a target FQH state with a probe integer quantum Hall (QH) state of opposite polarity. An Aharonov-Bohm (AB) interferometer forms along the interface, when the target and probe edge channels carry the same spin. We identify the spin-unpolarized and polarized edges of the $ν$=2/3 states at lower and higher magnetic fields, respectively, through spin-dependent interference.   <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 实现具有多重马约拉纳通道（multiple Majorana channels）的稳健拓扑超导态，是可扩展拓扑量子计算（topological quantum computing）的关键步骤。现有平台通常支持的马约拉纳通道数量有限，限制了可扩展性，因此亟需探索能够承载多通道马约拉纳模的物理平台与调控机制。

Method: 采用完整三维扩展Bernevig-Hughes-Zhang（BHZ）框架对磁掺杂三维拓扑绝缘体–s波超导体异质结构进行建模，明确计入垂直方向（vertical confinement）限制效应；系统分析磁性交换耦合、轨道混合与体能带反转的协同作用，并结合垂直受限模式与正常态高阶陈数相（high-Chern-number phases）推导多重马约拉纳拓扑超导相的解析判据。

Result: 研究表明磁性交换、轨道混合与体能带反转共同产生有效等自旋p波配对（effective equal-spin p-wave pairing）通道，支持多重手性马约拉纳模；通道数量由垂直受限模式与正常态陈数决定，并可通过解析判据预测。这些马约拉纳模式对强无序和中等对称性破缺扰动保持稳健；此外，垂直约瑟夫森结（vertical Josephson junction）的超导相位差可作为可调参数，控制低能马约拉纳通道的杂化、微隙（minigap）及有效多重性。

Conclusion: 该工作确立了磁性三维拓扑绝缘体异质结构作为工程化多重手性马约拉纳模式的有前景平台，为可扩展拓扑量子计算提供了新的物理基础与设计原则。

Abstract: Achieving robust topological superconductivity with multiple Majorana channels is a key step for scalable topological quantum computing. By employing the global matrix method with non-trivial matching conditions, we find that these WSL antiresonances correspond to states that are exponentially localized at the potential's incident boundary. These findings provide a robust, unified framework for identifying WSLs across diverse scales, ranging from electronic transport in trans-polyacetylene to classical-wave analogs in phononic crystals and surface water waves.

</details>

Abstract: It was suggested by 't Hooft that to avoid the divergence of the free energy or entropy of the probe fields, one has to introduce an infrared (IR) cutoff. This introduction of the IR cutoff, namely, the brick wall model, has been studied for the Schwarzschild black hole and has been shown by 't Hooft that to have the entropy of the order of Bekenstein-Hawking entropy, the cutoff must be of the order of Planck length. In this study, we investigated the brick wall model in the context of a Reissner-Nordstróm, quantum corrected Schwarzschild and quantum corrected Reissner-Nordström black holes. Abstract: The Black Hole-String Correspondence identifies the microstates of a minimal black hole with those of a highly excited string. First, we expand the free thermodynamic analysis to include towers with string-oscillators and Kaluza--Klein modes, and clarify whether the ensemble connects to a $d$-dimensional black hole or a wrapped black string. Second, we include gravitational backreaction in the pure Kaluza--Klein tower transition. With no winding-tachyon analogue, the interpolating configuration is the one-loop Kaluza--Klein gas, which reorganizes into higher-dimensional radiation and yields self-gravitating solutions whose instability points to the black string and whose thermodynamics match it at the correspondence point, $S\sim N_{\rm sp}$, where the size approaches the species length. This shows that the matching is robust under self-interactions. The cobordism classes of the two saddles agree in every structure we check, but their brane-charge lattices, given by bordism groups, differ, which is the source of the type II obstruction. This obstruction is thus perturbative, showing that the transition can only proceed through the non-perturbative, charge-violating processes of the kind predicted by the Cobordism Conjecture.

  <summary>Details</summary>

Result: 严格证明了任何满足条件的 $c=1$ VOA 或 VOSA 要么是秩1整格对应的格代数 $V_L$，要么可表示为 $V_L^G$（其中 $G$ 为有限自同构群），且所有此类 $G$ 已知，从而给出了"好的" $c=1$ VO(S)A 的完整显式枚举。文中还讨论了这些手征代数的不可逆对称性（non-invertible symmetries）。

Conclusion: 本文完成了 $c=1$ 情形下手征代数（chiral algebra）的严格分类，验证了先前的物理直觉。在伴生论文（companion paper）中，作者将利用所得到的表示范畴的ribbon自等价（ribbon auto-equivalences）信息以及三维TQFT技术，进一步推进到玻色与费米型 $c=1$ 完整共形场论的完全分类。

Abstract: For mathematicians: In this first in a series of two papers, we give a mathematically rigorous classification of (sufficiently nice) $c=1$ vertex operator algebras (VOAs) and vertex operator superalgebras (VOSAs). We confirm the lore that any such VO(S)A is either a lattice VO(S)A $V_L$ associated to a rank-1 integral lattice $L$, or can be obtained as an orbifold thereof, i.e. a $G$-invariant subalgebra $V_L^G$ for some finite group $G$ of automorphisms. All such $G$ are known, allowing for an explicit enumeration of nice $c=1$ VO(S)As. A key ingredient in our approach is to establish a general criterion for nice VOAs, requiring knowledge only of the vacuum character, for testing when the simple modules with integer conformal dimension span a symmetric fusion subcategory which is braided tensor equivalent to $Rep(G)$. In our companion paper, we calculate the ribbon auto-equivalences of the representation categories of the nice $c=1$ VOAs, and leverage this to obtain the classification of nice bosonic and fermionic $c=1$ full conformal field theories.

Thinking of chiral algebras as gapless boundary conditions of 3D topological quantum field theories (TQFTs), our result says that any such chiral algebra is either a holomorphic boundary of $U(1)_k$ Chern-Simons theory, or can be obtained by passing to the $G$-invariant states thereof for some finite group $G$ of symmetries. We explicitly enumerate these chiral algebras and also discuss their non-invertible symmetries. In a companion paper, we build on these results using techniques from the study of 3D TQFTs to classify full bosonic and fermionic rational $c=1$ CFTs.

</details>

Abstract: Quantum complexity has emerged as a natural probe of chaos, thermalization, and the black hole interior on timescales long after local observables have equilibrated. However, its time evolution has been studied almost exclusively in subsystems confined to a single connected region. We show, using complementary tools from holography and random quantum circuits, that noncontiguous subsystems composed of multiple disjoint regions give rise to qualitatively new physics compared to the contiguous case. First, at finite temperature, a subsystem occupying less than half of the total system can carry high complexity at late times, even as the complexity of its larger complement remains low. This inversion of the usual hierarchy is intrinsically thermal: it vanishes in the infinite-temperature limit, which is the regime modeled by random quantum circuits. Second, if a subsystem's complexity equilibrates at an early time, then fragmenting it into $m$ disjoint components can further reduce this timescale by a factor of $m$, a phenomenon we exhibit in both holography and random quantum circuits. Extending to arbitrary values of $t$ is also immediate giving rise to counterparts of the Noumi-Shiraishi power series. Abstract: We construct helicity-conditioned statistical states for a Whitney-discretized electromagnetic field on a closed oriented three-manifold. The simplicial de Rham complex provides exact discrete gauge symmetry, while the Whitney inner product separates exact, harmonic, and coexact sectors. The spatial Abelian Chern--Simons functional is gauge invariant and depends only on the coexact potential. After fixing harmonic modes, we introduce a helicity-biased Gaussian ensemble on the reduced electromagnetic phase space and derive explicit formulas for its admissible parameters, partition function, mean helicity, relative entropy, and Fisher information. The distribution uniquely minimizes relative entropy under a prescribed mean-helicity constraint. Its helicity susceptibility equals the variance of the discrete Chern--Simons functional and controls the local distinguishability of neighboring statistical states. Because magnetic helicity is generally not conserved under unconstrained Maxwell dynamics, these states represent conditioned inference rather than dynamical equilibrium.

  $$

Abstract: We develop a representation-independent symmetric-Fock formulation of the nonlinear Boltzmann collision operator for Maxwell molecules. Starting from the standard velocity-space operator, we give a short derivation of Bobylev's Fourier identity and isolate its characteristic structure: two linearly related Fourier arguments followed by multiplication. We then prove, independently of collision geometry and of any coordinate realization, a canonical lift--fusion intertwining theorem: one-particle linear maps lift to the two incoming Fock legs and symmetric-algebra multiplication fuses them into a single outgoing state. Bargmann coordinates make this intrinsic construction especially transparent, but do not define it; Bobylev's collision maps subsequently select the Maxwell vertex from the already graded lift--fusion class. The resulting bilinear map obeys the exact grading \[ \widehat{\mathcal Q}(\mathscr H_p,\mathscr H_q)\subseteq\mathscr H_{p+q}, \] which gives an intrinsic origin for the triangular structures of Maxwell kinetics. For the inhomogeneous problem, we extend realization covariance to the nonlinear two-leg vertex and evaluate the moving-frame connection independently through Hermite-function and Fourier realizations. The two routes give the same abstract Fock propagation and the same first Chapman--Enskog source; its traceless level-$2$ and vector level-$3$ sectors are relaxed by the exact Maxwell rates, giving $\Pr=2/3$. Abstract: We compute the large-$N$ asymptotics of the determinant of the identity plus or minus a generalized Hilbert matrix. For $N \in \mathbb{N}$ and $δ\in \mathbb{R} \setminus \{-1, -2,
  \ldots\}$, let \[
  H_N^δ
  := \left(
  \frac{\sin((1+δ)π)}{π(j+k+1+δ)}
  \right)_{j,k=0}^{N-1} \] be the generalized Hilbert matrix. For $δ$ not a half-integer, we prove the large-$N$ power-law asymptotics of the determinant \[
  \log \text{det}(I_N \pm H_N^δ) = -\frac{θ_δ^2 \pm θ_δ}{2} \log N + O(1) \] where $θ_δ:= δ$ if $δ< -1/2$, and $θ_δ:= \frac 1 π\arcsin(\sin(δπ))$ if $δ\ge -1/2$ and $\arcsin \colon [-1,1] \to [-\fracπ{2}, \fracπ{2}]$ denotes the principal branch of $\arcsin$. For $δ\geq -\frac 12$ the asymptotics is known. The novelty of the present paper is the regime $δ< - \frac 12$ and understanding the dichotomy in the decay exponent. This stems from the $\pm 1$ edge eigenvalues of the limiting operator appearing for $δ< - \frac 12$. Most notably, we obtain for $δ< -\frac12$ \[
  \log \det\bigl(I_N - (H_N^δ)^2\bigr) = -δ^2 \log
  N + O(1). \] Such determinants arise in the study of Anderson's orthogonality catastrophe.

Abstract: We investigate the discrete spectrum of finitely many point interactions for Neumann and Robin Laplacians on special unbounded and exterior $C^{1,1}$ domains in dimensions two and three. If the background operator is non-negative, this also gives the number of negative eigenvalues without assuming a finite zero-energy limit of the Weyl matrix. In the one-centre case we identify the critical coupling and prove that the unique eigenvalue branch is real analytic, strictly increasing, and strictly concave. For scalar multicentre couplings $Θ=αI_N$, sufficiently strong attraction produces exactly $N$ eigenvalues below the background spectrum, and all of them have universal leading asymptotics coinciding with the whole-space laws. If the bottom of the background spectrum is an isolated eigenvalue, the branch exists for every finite coupling and we determine its leading decoupling asymptotics as the coupling tends to $+\infty$. Explicit exterior-sphere and exterior-disk models illustrate the critical couplings and their threshold behaviour.

Abstract: Recently in the physics literature there have been a small, but growing, number of papers that have applied the Eynard-Orantin topological recursion procedure to spectral curves with ramification loci of infinite cardinality. However, there is a corresponding gap in the mathematics literature; indeed, it has not been checked rigorously whether the resulting infinite sums converge, or whether the resulting correlators have all the desired properties of the topological recursion. The present work aims to bridge this gap by defining topological recursion on a suitably broad class of spectral curves to cover the aforementioned physics applications and to rigorously prove that this definition has the properties one would intuitively expect. As a by-product, some interesting coincidences involving quantum curves are discovered, where two different spectral curves yield the same quantum curve, a situation that has, hitherto, not appeared in the literature.

Abstract: The quantum Rabi model occupies a distinguished role in the study of quantum light and matter, describing the most fundamental interactions. Elucidation of its spectral properties, along with those of its generalizations, is necessary for quantum optics and applications in areas such as quantum information technologies. In this paper, we explore a precise description of energy degeneracy of the two-photon asymmetric quantum Rabi model and the nature of the system's symmetries; as a result, we demonstrate that these have significant relationships with the structure of certain algebraic curves (hyperelliptic curves) and number theory. Concretely, we first prove the existence of degenerate eigenvalues using the monodromy data of the Fuchsian ODE, which draws the eigenvalue problem of the system, and the existence of associated symmetry operators, previously discovered and studied heuristically in physics. Subsequently, we give a complete characterization of spectral degeneracies and present a series of conjectures showing that the degeneracy may determine the entire energy spectral structure. More precisely, for sufficiently strong interaction, we obtain an excellent approximation of the energy curves by the zero locus of a two-variable polynomial associated to degenerate eigenstates. In addition, we postulate an explicit description of the relation between the missing symmetry at the Hamiltonian level (hidden symmetry) and the degeneracy of the two-photon quantum Rabi model.

Conclusion: 该工作将碰撞奇异性的经典分析框架扩展到了能量不守恒、势能非吸引且存在多个同时奇异性的一般情形，为n体系统奇异性理论提供了新的工具，并以更简洁的方式重新证明了碰撞初始条件集的零测度性质。

Abstract: We study finite-time singularities in $n$-body systems with pair potentials that are homogeneous of degree $-α$, where $0<α<2$, that are not necessarily attractive and with bounded time-dependent external forces. We develop a systematic method to analyse a colliding subsystem in the presence of simultaneous collision or non-collision singularities elsewhere in the system. We extend classical results of von Zeipel and Painlevé to such subsystems, establish bounds on their internal energy near total collision, and implement a McGehee blow-up that remains effective without conservation of energy. The blow-up yields the asymptotics of the cluster size, and in the case of attractive potentials, the convergence towards the set of central configurations and asymptotics of relative position, relative velocities, and internal angular momentum. As an application of the asymptotic results, we construct a simplified proof that the initial conditions leading to collision form a set of measure zero.

</details>


### [52] [A general improbability result for non-collision singularities as subsystems with at most four particles](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.00876)
*Manuel Quaschner*

Main category: math-ph

TL;DR: 本文证明了n体问题（n-body problem）中一类非碰撞奇点（non-collision singularity）初始条件集的不可能性，该结果涵盖了所有已知情形，并为解决一般问题指明了方向。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: n体问题中导致非碰撞奇点的初始条件集合是否"不可能"（improbable）这一问题是1984年Barry Simon列出的十五个数学物理难题之首，目前仅在n=4的情形下得到肯定回答，一般情形仍属开放问题。

Method: 采用最终子系统聚簇分解（final cluster decomposition）的方法，将系统分解为子系统内粒子强相互作用、子系统间粒子仅受弱相互作用的多个子系统，并据此构造证明条件。

Result: 在满足以下条件时证明了所有奇异轨道初始条件集合的不可能性：任意多的子系统在质心处发生完全碰撞；可能存在恰好由四个粒子组成的子系统发生非碰撞奇点，且这些子系统的渐近方向（asymptotic directions）彼此不同（此为温和条件）。

Conclusion: 该结果涵盖并统一了此前所有已知结论，同时给出了一条可能的路径，用以解决非碰撞奇点集合在一般情形下是否不可能的原始问题。

Abstract: Non-collision singularities of the $n$-body problem are initial conditions for which no global solution exists and which, however, do not lead to a collision in the limit; i.e., the moment of inertia of the system diverges. The question of whether the set of initial conditions leading to non-collision singularities in the $n$-body problem is improbable is open and is the first on Barry Simon's list of fifteen problems in mathematical physics from the year 1984. So far, this question has only been answered positively in the case $n=4$. Using a final cluster decomposition into subsystems whose particles interact strongly with each other and exert only small forces on particles from different subsystems, we can prove the improbability of the set of all singular orbits with the following conditions: -There may be arbitrarily many subsystems undergoing total collision at their center of mass. - There may be subsystems exhibiting a non-collision singularity, i.e. the respective moment of inertia of the subsystem diverges. These subsystems consist of exactly four particles each and the asymptotic directions of these subsystems have to differ (which is only a mild condition). This result includes all known statements and indicates a way how the general problem could be solved.

</details>

Abstract: Motivated by mirror symmetry, we study exponential sums over the $\mathbb{F}_q$-points of the $A_1$ Coulomb branch. We show that their fiberwise structure is governed by polynomial Gauss sums, providing a geometric realization of Selberg character sums studied by Evans. Using Evans's evaluation, we obtain explicit formulas for the Coulomb-branch exponential sums as products of classical Gauss sums.

</details>


### [54] [Collective superintegrable systems from the Guillemin--Sternberg torus action](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.01878)
*L. Feher*

Main category: math-ph

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种关于集体哈密顿系统（collective Hamiltonians）超可积性（superintegrability）的新方法，该系统在连通半单紧致李群（connected semisimple compact Lie group）$G$ 对辛流形（symplectic manifold）$M$ 的哈密顿作用下保持不变。通过利用可追溯至 Guillemin 和 Sternberg [GS,1983] 的哈密顿环面作用，作者证明了 $\mathfrak{H}=\mathcal{J}^*(C^\infty(\mathfrak{g}^*)^G)$ 与其中心化子 $\mathfrak{F}$ 的函数维数之和等于 $M$ 的维数，结合非平凡性条件确保了 $\mathfrak{H}$ 构成超可积系统，并指出 GS 环面作用的动量映射给出了该系统的作用变量（action variables）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 旨在从新的视角理解集体哈密顿系统的超可积性结构，为超级可积性的研究提供不同于 Bolsinov 和 Jovanović 早期结果的途径。

Method: 利用 Guillemin 和 Sternberg [GS,1983] 引入的哈密顿环面作用，分析由 $G$ 作用的动量映射（momentum map）$\mathcal{J}:M\to\mathfrak{g}^*$ 拉回产生的阿贝尔泊松代数（Abelian Poisson algebra）$\mathfrak{H}$ 及其在 $C^\infty(M)$ 中的中心化子 $\mathfrak{F}$，通过验证函数维数等式与一个非平凡性条件来建立超可积性。

Result: 证明了函数维数等式 $\mathrm{ddim}(\mathfrak{H})+\mathrm{ddim}(\mathfrak{F})=\mathrm{dim}(M)$；在此等式与非平凡性条件共同成立的前提下，阿贝尔泊松代数 $\mathfrak{H}\subset C^\infty(M)$ 代表一个超可积系统；GS 环面作用的动量映射为该系统的作用变量。

Conclusion: 该工作为集体超可积性（collective superintegrability）提供了新洞见，补充并丰富了 Bolsinov 和 Jovanović 的既有结果。

Abstract: We present a novel approach to the superintegrability of collective Hamiltonians invariant under a Hamiltonian action of a connected semisimple compact Lie group, $G$, on a symplectic manifold, $M$. By exploiting a Hamiltonian torus action that goes back to Guillemin and Sternberg [GS,1983], we demonstrate that the functional dimensions of $\mathfrak{H} := \mathcal{J}^*(C^\infty(\mathfrak{g}^*)^G)$, where $\mathcal{J}: M \to \mathfrak{g}^*$ is the momentum map of the $G$ action, and its centralizer $\mathfrak{F}$ in $C^\infty(M)$ satisfy the equality $\mathrm{ddim}(\mathfrak{H}) + \mathrm{ddim}(\mathfrak{F}) = \mathrm{dim}(M)$. Together with a non-triviality condition, this ensures that the Abelian Poisson algebra $\mathfrak{H}\subset C^\infty(M)$ represents a superintegrable system, and it also follows that the momentum map of the GS torus action yields action variables for the system. Our work provides a new insight into collective superintegrability complementing earlier results of Bolsinov and Jovanović.

</details>

### [55] [Client-side transparent caching for remote ROOT data analysis](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.00400)
*Dmytro Kovalskyi,Jan Eysermans,Mariarosaria D'Alfonso,Christoph Paus*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: 本文提出uCache（xrd-ucache），一种基于XRootD客户端插件的透明客户端缓存系统，通过本地存储缓存实际读取的数据，并可重建为分支对齐、重新压缩的形式，以减少远程数据访问的延迟和后续遍历的输入输出及解压开销。在CMS开放数据上的基准测试表明，后续遍历速度提升1.6至15.7倍，且远距离或高负载场景下收益最大。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 高能物理分析往往多次处理相同数据以调试算法和测试新想法，但数据从远程存储读取时每次迭代都受网络延迟、带宽和共享存储吞吐量的影响，尤其是在负载较高时波动明显。

Method: 实现为XRootD客户端插件，无需服务器端部署或修改分析代码；利用分析机器的本地存储作为网络与内存之间的缓存层，仅缓存分析实际读取的数据，并可将缓存数据重建为分支对齐（branch-aligned）且重新压缩的形式，以消除后续遍历的大部分输入输出与解压成本。

Result: 在高能物理分析大挑战（Analysis Grand Challenge）的top夸克对分析及CMS开放数据（使用zlib和LZMA压缩）上测试。填充缓存相比直接读取几乎没有额外开销；后续遍历在字节缓存（byte cache）下快1.6至8.8倍，在重压缩缓存下快2.1至15.7倍。约1 TB缓存可支撑10-20 TB数据集；当远程数据源负载高或地理位置遥远时改进最大。

Conclusion: uCache以透明、无需修改代码的方式显著加速高能物理分析的重复数据访问，在远程存储场景下具有实用价值，尤其在网络条件不佳或存储负载较大的情况下效果尤为显著。

Abstract: High-energy physics analyses often process the same data as physicists refine algorithms and test new ideas. With data increasingly read from remote storage, each iteration is subject to network latency and depends on network bandwidth and shared-storage throughput, which can vary substantially under load. We present uCache (xrd-ucache), a transparent client-side cache implemented as an XRootD client plugin that requires neither server-side deployment nor changes to analysis code. It uses local storage on the analysis machine as a cache layer between the network and memory. The cache stores only the data actually read by an analysis. It can also rebuild cached data into a branch-aligned, recompressed form, eliminating most of the input/output and decompression costs of subsequent passes. We benchmark the cache using the Analysis Grand Challenge top quark pair analysis on public CMS Open Data compressed with zlib and LZMA. Filling the cache adds essentially no overhead compared with a direct read. Subsequent passes are 1.6-8.8 times faster from the byte cache and 2.1-15.7 times faster from the recompressed cache. For a typical analysis, a 1 TB cache suffices for datasets of 10-20 TB. The largest improvements occur when the remote data source is heavily loaded or geographically distant.

</details>

### [56] [Bose--Einstein Condensation without an Initial Low-Energy Concentration Assumption](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.01436)
*Siwei Luo,Jian-Guo Liu*

Main category: math.AP

TL;DR: 本文证明：在临界温度以下，对带低动量散射简并指数0≤η<1的空间均匀量子玻尔兹曼方程的保守各向同性测度解，若其半强收敛（semi-strong convergence）到玻色-爱因斯坦平衡，则必然在有限时间内产生玻色-爱因斯坦凝聚（BEC）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 量子玻尔兹曼方程（quantum Boltzmann equation）描述玻色气体的非平衡动力学，理解在临界温度以下系统如何在有限时间内形成玻色-爱因斯坦凝聚是该领域的重要理论问题。已有结果通常要求初始浓度在零能量附近集中的假设，本文旨在去除这一限制。

Method: 作者提出一种从松弛估计（relaxation estimate）到玻色-爱因斯坦平衡来获得凝聚的新证明方法。首先，松弛估计给出低于某一小能量水平的固定质量；随后利用越来越小能量尺度上的碰撞估计（collision estimates），并借由一个带固定上界的加权求和泛函累加各尺度的效应。若零能量处不形成原子，则正能量壳层中滞留的质量会迫使该加权和有限时间内超过其上界，从而产生凝聚。

Result: 对所有满足温度比T̄/T̄c<1的允许初始测度，存在保守解在有限时间后产生正的零能量原子（zero-energy atom），且无需任何零能量附近的初始浓度假设；该凝聚态经证明持续存在并收敛到平衡凝聚质量，同时完整解强收敛。

Conclusion: 在低动量散射简并指数0≤η<1条件下，半强收敛到玻色-爱因斯坦平衡这一条件足以蕴含有限时间的玻色-爱因斯坦凝聚，证明方法为从松弛估计推导凝聚提供了全新的分析框架。

Abstract: We prove that semi-strong convergence to a Bose--Einstein equilibrium below the critical temperature implies finite-time Bose--Einstein condensation (BEC) for conservative isotropic measure solutions of the spatially homogeneous quantum Boltzmann equation with low-momentum scattering degeneracy exponent $0\leqη<1$. For every admissible initial measure with $\overline T/\overline T_c<1$, there exists a conservative solution that has a positive zero-energy atom after a finite time without any assumption of initial concentration near zero energy. We also show that it persists and converges to the equilibrium condensate mass, while the full solution converges strongly. The proof introduces a method to obtain condensation from relaxation estimate to Bose--Einstein equilibrium. The relaxation estimate first gives a fixed amount of mass below a small energy level. We then use collision estimates at smaller and smaller energy scales and add their effects in a weighted sum functional with a fixed upper bound. If no atom forms at zero energy, enough mass remains in the positive-energy shells to force this sum to exceed its upper bound in finite time, which leads to condensation in finite time.

</details>

### [57] [Critical points of point charge potentials along lines](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.00336)
*Goncalo Oliveira*

Main category: math.CA

  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Gabrielov-Novikov-Shapiro 提出了关于点电荷势函数临界点数目的猜想（Conjecture 1.9），该问题涉及势函数的几何结构及其临界点分布的精细刻画。作者旨在严格证明该猜想，并验证其上界的最优性。

Abstract: We report a cryogen-free cryogenic current comparator (CCC) system for precision resistance measurements. Abstract: Long-lived particles are a generic prediction of Standard Model extensions that address the hierarchy problem, the origin of neutrino masses, the baryon asymmetry of the Universe, and the nature of dark matter. GRENDEL will exploit the substantial rock and concrete between the detector and LHC interaction point as passive shielding in addition to employing active background rejection. Located approximately 22 m above the interaction point and running largely transverse to the beam, GRENDEL will span around 110 m and cover the central region $|η| < 0.6$. GRENDEL will make use of extruded scintillator bars to achieve competitive sensitivity with other proposed detectors while requiring minimal infrastructure, and without the need for civil engineering. We present the physics case, detector concept, and projected sensitivities to heavy neutral leptons, dark scalars, and axion-like particles covering the $0.2-15$ GeV mass range with 3 $\mathrm{ab}^{-1}$ of HL-LHC data. The benchmarks span the neutrino, scalar, and axion portals, with production through exotic Higgs decays, $B$- and $D$-meson decays, and $W/Z$ decays.

Abstract: Foundation models are increasingly being pursued in particle and nuclear physics, but existing approaches remain strongly tied to individual experiments through detector-specific architectures or pre-training objectives, limiting their reuse across sensing modalities. Abstract: High energy neutrinos are copiously produced in proton-proton collisions at the Large Hadron Collider (LHC), located at CERN, near Geneva, Switzerland. In particular, a large neutrino flux is expected in the far-forward direction. This flux is concentrated near the beam axis, reaches energies up to the TeV scale, and for electron and tau neutrinos can be dominated by heavy flavour decays at the highest energies. These features open a new experimental window for studying neutrino interactions with dedicated forward detectors, in an energy range beyond the reach of traditional accelerator-based neutrino experiments. The new FASER and SND@LHC experiments reported the first direct observations of neutrino interactions at a hadron collider using the first data from Run 3, which started in 2022. Since then, the programme has advanced to the first measurements, including charged-current neutrino-nucleon cross sections and the detection of different neutrino flavours. This article describes the forward neutrino flux at the LHC, the acceptance and detector concepts of the Run 3 experiments, and summarises the current status of collider neutrino measurements. It also discusses prospects for extending this neutrino programme towards the HL-LHC era. The collected data will also provide constraints on very forward hadron production, and enable searches for feebly interacting particles.

Method: 采用sMDT漂移管室与新型薄间隙阻性板室（RPC）触发室集成的方案替代原有MDT室，于2021年1月开始串行生产。生产过程中实施严格的质量控制程序，包括对每根漂移管的单独测试、室几何尺寸测量、气密性验证，并在生产现场通过宇宙射线进行最终认证；同时建立两处生产现场共用的质量控制数据库和监测Web界面。

Result: sMDT室已在MPI Munich实现串行生产，并建立了一套完整的质量控制体系，涵盖从单管测试到整室宇宙线认证的全流程质量保障，同时部署了跨生产现场的共享质量控制数据库与监测界面。

Conclusion: 该文档为sMDT室的生产与质量控制流程提供了系统性记录，保证了探测器在HL-LHC运行环境下具备高可靠性和高机械精度，从而支持ATLAS缪子触发效率与计数率能力的提升目标。

Abstract: In order to improve the muon trigger efficiency and the rate capability of the ATLAS muon detectors for operation at the high luminosity upgrade of the Large Hadron Collider (HL-LHC), the Monitored Drift Tube (MDT) tracking chambers in the inner barrel layer of the ATLAS Muon Spectrometer will be replaced by small-diameter Muon Drift Tube (sMDT) chambers integrated with new thin-gap RPC trigger chambers. The sMDT chambers are in serial production since January 2021. This documentation provides insight into the drift tube production and every step performed in the chamber construction at the MPI Munich. The serial production involves a stringent quality control program to assure the reliability and high mechanical precision of the chambers. This program consists of tests of the individual drift tubes, measurements of the chamber geometry and proof of gas tightness. Final certification at the production site is provided using cosmic rays. The dedicated quality control database and monitoring Web interface that is used commonly for both production sites of the sMDT chambers is presented

</details>

Conclusion: QED核介质效应在EicC能量下对核子与原子核非微扰结构的提取具有显著影响，需要在实验数据分析中予以考虑。

Abstract: We evaluate quantum electrodynamics (QED) nuclear medium effects under experimental conditions at the future Electron-ion collider in China (EicC). We consider neutral-current and charged-current elastic scattering, as well as neutral-current and charged-current deep inelastic scattering. We compute cross-section corrections at first order in the opacity expansion and estimate kinematic modifications due to multiple rescattering within the nucleus. We perform the first calculations of charged-current deep inelastic scattering and polarized scattering. We present results for the anticipated $^{197}_{79}\mathrm{Au},~^{208}_{82}\mathrm{Pb},$ and $^{238}_{92}\mathrm{U}$ nuclei and extrapolate these calculations to the lighter $^{40}_{20}\mathrm{Ca}$ isotope. We find that QED nuclear medium effects are non-negligible when extracting the process-independent non-perturbative structure of nucleons and nuclei at the EicC.

</details>


### [67] [Fundamental geometric limitations on disentangling nuclear-surface properties in relativistic heavy ion collisions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.01229)
*Hadi Mehrabpour,Behnaz Behzadmoghaddam,S. M. A. Tabatabaee Mehr,Oscar Garcia-Montero,Li Yan*

Main category: nucl-th

TL;DR: 本文通过逐事件蒙特卡洛Glauber模拟研究相对论重离子碰撞中提取核表面弥散度（surface diffuseness）的局限性，发现该方法受几何驱动可观测量对表面弥散度与固有核变形（intrinsic nuclear deformation）相互交织响应的限制；椭球几何主导的可观测量与四极形变产生局部简并，而三角关联响应更独立，可保留额外信息。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 从相对论重离子碰撞中提取核表面弥散度存在固有困难，因为几何驱动的可观测量对表面弥散度和核固有形变的响应相互纠缠，需要系统理解这一限制机制。

Method: 采用逐事件蒙特卡洛Glauber模拟（event-by-event Monte Carlo Glauber simulations），系统检验Woods-Saxon表面弥散度a₀与四极形变参数β₂及八极形变参数β₃之间的局部关联；利用多维响应图谱（multidimensional response maps）、局部灵敏度和信息几何度量（information-geometric measures）以及贝叶斯分析（Bayesian analysis）量化这些效应。

Result: 椭球几何主导的可观测量对四极形变响应强烈，产生a₀–β₂局部简并，显著削弱其对表面弥散度的灵敏度；三角关联响应更独立，能保留额外信息，但其灵敏度也会受固有形变影响；在同时存在四极和八极形变时，局部简并和最小约束方向取决于核构型，表明提取a₀的限制无法用单一的全局参数关联描述。

Conclusion: 该研究阐明了固有多极形变对从相对论重离子碰撞确定核表面弥散度所施加的基本限制，并识别出能够提供更独立a₀信息的粒子多体关联（multiparticle correlations）。

Abstract: The extraction of the nuclear surface diffuseness from relativistic heavy ion collisions is limited by the intertwined responses of geometry-driven observables to surface diffuseness and intrinsic nuclear deformation. We investigate this limitation using event-by-event Monte Carlo Glauber simulations, focusing on the sensitivity of multiparticle correlations to the Woods--Saxon surface diffuseness $a_0$ in intrinsically deformed nuclei. We systematically examine the local correlations between $a_0$ and quadrupole and octupole deformation parameters, $β_2$ and $β_3$, and determine how these correlations affect the ability of different observables to constrain $a_0$. We find that observables dominated by elliptic geometry exhibit a strong response to quadrupole deformation, leading to a local $a_0$--$β_2$ degeneracy that substantially limits their sensitivity to the nuclear surface diffuseness. Triangular correlations provide a more independent response to the nuclear surface and therefore retain additional information on $a_0$, although their sensitivity can also be modified by intrinsic deformation. Extending the analysis to simultaneous quadrupole and octupole deformation shows that the local degeneracy and least-constrained directions depend on the nuclear configuration, demonstrating that the limitation on extracting $a_0$ is not described by a single global parameter correlation. We quantify these effects using multidimensional response maps, local sensitivity and information-geometric measures, and a Bayesian analysis of the resulting parameter constraints. The results clarify the fundamental limitations imposed by intrinsic multipole deformation on the determination of nuclear surface diffuseness from relativistic heavy ion collisions and identify multiparticle correlations that provide more independent information on $a_0$.

</details>

*Steven J. Yang,Govinda Anantha Padmanabha,D. Thomas Seidl,Nikolaos Bouklas*

Main category: physics.comp-ph

TL;DR: 本文提出一种基于输入凸神经网络（input convex neural networks）的混合凸神经势能方法，用于对随组分变化的材料家族进行模块化且数据高效的超弹性本构建模，在PolyJet 3D打印材料和合成Gent型材料基准上均展现出优于整体式网络的泛化能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统超弹性本构模型需要预先规定应变能密度函数形式并校准参数，但对力学行为随组分变化的材料家族而言，固定的本构形式难以覆盖全部行为，分别拟合的形式又无法直接预测新组分的响应。因此需要一种既能学习灵活应变能函数又能保持关键物理约束的数据驱动建模方法。

Method: 提出基于输入凸神经网络的混合凸神经势能架构：每个势能对多凸应变不变量保持凸性和单调性，采用条件网络（conditioning network）将材料描述符映射为混合权重，从而以模块化方式刻画材料家族。

Result: 在PolyJet 3D打印实验数据和合成Gent型基准两个数据集上，混合架构对训练中未见的材料描述符具有更好的泛化能力。PolyJet实验中混合架构对模型超参数敏感性更低，Gent型基准中在材料描述符空间数据稀疏时泛化更可靠。

Conclusion: 用一组共享的凸神经势能表示材料家族，为从有限数据中学习依赖描述符的本构行为提供了有效的结构先验。

Abstract: Hyperelastic constitutive models enable modeling large deformations in elastic solids. In common practice, a strain energy density function is prescribed in advance and model-specific parameters are calibrated from experiments. However, many applications require constitutive models for a family of related materials whose mechanical behavior varies with composition. A fixed constitutive model-form may not capture the full range of behavior across the family, while fitting separate forms does not provide a direct way to predict the response of new compositions. Recent work has developed data-driven constitutive models that learn flexible strain energy functions while incorporating key physical constraints. In this work, we propose using mixtures of convex neural potentials based on input convex neural networks as a modular and data efficient approach to modeling material families. Each potential is convex and monotonic with respect to polyconvex strain invariants, while a conditioning network maps material descriptors to mixture weights. We compare the approach with a monolithic partially input-convex neural network using experimental data from PolyJet 3D-printed materials and a synthetic Gent-type benchmark. Across both benchmarks, the mixture architecture generalized better to material descriptors not seen during training. In the PolyJet experimental benchmark, we showed that the mixture architecture is less sensitive to model hyperparameters, while in the Gent-type benchmark it generalized more reliably with sparse data in the material-descriptor space. These results suggest that representing a material family through a small set of shared convex neural potentials provides a useful structural prior for learning descriptor-dependent constitutive behavior from limited data.

</details>

### [69] [Reliable LLM-Generated Programs for High-Energy Physics Experiments through Graph-Grounded Software Knowledge](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.01095)
*Yue Sun,Tong Liu,Yipu Liao,Jingde Chen,Ke Li*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本文提出一种在生成代码前预先组织软件依赖关系并在推理时检索任务相关知识的方法，用于提升大语言模型（LLM）在粒子物理实验软件生态中的代码生成可靠性。以ROOT框架为测试平台，结合异构软件知识图谱的混合检索、技能筛选的工作流示例和执行引导修复，将275项ROOT任务的首轮执行成功率从58.5%提升至76.0%（Claude Code编排）和从51.3%提升至64.0%（独立编排），最终成功率分别从90.5%提升至96.0%和从78.9%提升至90.9%，而平均生成成本仅增加1.3%和3.2%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现代粒子物理实验的信息提取依赖多层分析流程，这些流程构建于庞大且高度互联的软件生态系统之上。通用大语言模型（LLM）因用户请求很少明确指定所需的API、依赖关系和使用约定，往往为此类任务生成不可靠的程序，因此需要一种方法在生成前组织软件关系并在推理时检索任务相关知识。

Method: 方法包含三部分：在异构软件知识图谱上进行混合检索（hybrid retrieval），以组织跨代码库的软件关系；检索技能筛选的工作流示例（skill-selected workflow examples），提供任务相关的生成范式；以及执行引导式修复（execution-guided repair），根据程序执行结果迭代修正生成代码。整个系统在开源ROOT框架上作为可复现的测试平台进行完整评估。

Result: 在275项ROOT任务基准上，接地系统将首轮执行成功率从58.5%提升至76.0%（Claude Code编排下）以及从51.3%提升至64.0%（独立编排下）；最终成功率从90.5%提升至96.0%和从78.9%提升至90.9%；每项成功任务的平均生成成本仅增加1.3%和3.2%。在强编码智能体下增益仍然保持。

Conclusion: 显式软件知识即使在已有智能体脚手架（agentic scaffolding）存在时仍然具有价值。由于该方法捕捉了大型代码库中普遍存在的软件关系，而非ROOT或特定模型特有的事实，因此应可迁移至其他实验框架和专有软件，尤其适用于文档稀疏或内部依赖复杂的场景。

Abstract: Extracting physics information from modern particle-physics experiments requires multistage analyses implemented on top of large and highly interconnected software ecosystems. General-purpose large language models (LLMs) often produce unreliable programs for such tasks because a user request alone rarely specifies the required APIs, dependencies, and usage conventions. We organize these software relations before generation and retrieve task-relevant knowledge at inference time. Using the open-source ROOT framework as a representative and reproducible testbed, we evaluate a complete grounding system that combines hybrid retrieval over a heterogeneous software knowledge graph, skill-selected workflow examples, and execution-guided repair. On a benchmark of 275 ROOT tasks, grounding improves first-attempt execution from 58.5% to 76.0% under Claude Code orchestration and from 51.3% to 64.0% under standalone orchestration. Final success increases from 90.5% to 96.0% and from 78.9% to 90.9%, respectively, while the average generation cost per successful task increases by only 1.3% and 3.2%. The gains persist under a strong coding agent, indicating that explicit software knowledge remains valuable even when agentic scaffolding is already in place. Because the method captures software relations common to large codebases rather than facts specific to ROOT or a particular model, it should transfer to other experiment frameworks and proprietary software, especially where documentation is sparse or internal dependencies are complex.

</details>

### [70] [On the Gram matrix of standard inner products of asymmetrically-weighted Hermite functions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.01144)
*Ruiyang Dai*

Main category: math.NA

TL;DR: 本文针对非对称加权（asymmetrically-weighted, AW）埃尔米特函数（Hermite functions）的标准L2内积所对应的无限维格拉姆矩阵（Gram matrix）展开系统研究，给出了其元素表示、Cholesky分解、逆矩阵的显式公式，并分析了有限维情形下该矩阵及其Schur补的渐近性质。这些结果进一步被应用于弗拉索夫–泊松（Vlasov-Poisson, VP）系统的伽辽金谱方法（Galerkin spectral methods）的分析与实现。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 该研究的分析动机源于等离子体物理（plasma physics）中的数值方法，特别是应用于VP系统的伽辽金谱方法。对这些数值方法的实施和理论分析而言，需要深入理解AW埃尔米特函数所生成的格拉姆矩阵的结构与渐近行为。

Method: 作者首先推导了无限维AW埃尔米特函数格拉姆矩阵的元素显式表示及其Cholesky分解，并证明该分解在缩放后的Bargmann–Fock基上具有自然解释；同时给出矩阵逆的显式公式。随后转向有限维格拉姆矩阵，分析其及Schur补的渐近性质。

Result: 获得了无限维格拉姆矩阵的元素、Cholesky分解与逆矩阵的闭式（explicit）表达式，建立了分解的Bargmann–Fock尺度化解释，并给出了有限维情形下矩阵及其Schur补的渐近分析结论。此外展示了上述公式与渐近结果如何用于VP系统伽辽金谱方法的具体实施与理论分析。

Conclusion: 所推导的格拉姆矩阵公式及渐近结果可为VP系统伽辽金谱方法提供理论基础和实现指导，表明该抽象线性代数结构在计算等离子体物理中具有实际应用价值。

Abstract: Let A denote the infinite Gram matrix associated with the standard L2 inner product of asymmetrically-weighted (AW) Hermite functions. We derive an explicit representation of its entries and its Cholesky factorization. We further show that this factorization admits a natural interpretation on a scaled Bargmann-Fock basis. An explicit formula for the inverse of A is also obtained. We then consider the corresponding finite Gram matrix and analyze its asymptotic property, as well as that of its Schur complement. The analysis is motivated by numerical methods for plasma physics, in particular Galerkin spectral methods applied to the Vlasov-Poisson (VP) system. As an application, we demonstrate how the derived Gram matrix formulas and asymptotic results can be exploited in the analysis and implementation of a Galerkin spectral method for the VP system.

</details>

### [71] [Families of relative periodic orbits in the planar three-body problem via consecutive alignments](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.01585)
*Joan Gimeno,Marc Jorba-Cuscó,Begoña Nicolás*

Main category: math.DS

TL;DR: 本文提出一种数值计算方法，用于计算和延拓平面牛顿三体问题中相对周期轨道（relative periodic orbits, RPOs）的单参数族。该方法利用连续共线构型（syzygies）将RPO的求解简化为低维非线性问题，并通过数值延拓和线性稳定性分析，得到包含Poincaré族、Hill族和双星型解在内的多种轨道族，展示了RPO的动力学多样性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 相对周期轨道是三体问题的重要解，具有在均匀旋转参考系中周期性、在惯性系中一般为准周期性的特征。由于直接计算RPO计算量大且维度高，需要发展更高效的数值方法以系统探索其解族结构和动力学性质，并为受限四体模型提供预设轨迹。

Method: 该方法利用连续的共线构型（syzygies）——即三体排成一线且速度满足相应对称条件的状态。通过匹配两次连续共线配置时的位置和动量，将RPO的计算简化为低维非线性问题；随后对其解进行数值延拓，并通过去除与守恒量和连续对称性相关的中性方向后的旋转单值矩阵（monodromy matrix）的非平凡特征值，确定线性稳定性。

Result: 该程序应用于多种质量分布和初始构型，生成了Poincaré族、Hill族和双星型解族。这些轨道族表现出从近圆到高偏心的运动转变、在共振和转向点附近发生稳定性变化，以及当旋转角为2π的有理倍数时出现绝对周期解。在Hill族中，延拓将卫星构型与环恒星运动相连，因为最小天体失去了对中间天体的引力束缚。在双星区域中还获得了环双星和环恒星构型。

Conclusion: 结果展示了相对周期轨道丰富的动力学多样性，提供了连贯的三体运动解，可作为受限四体模型中的预设轨迹使用。

Abstract: Relative periodic orbits (RPOs) are solutions of the three-body problem that are periodic in a uniformly rotating reference frame and, in general, quasi-periodic in inertial coordinates. We present a numerical procedure for computing and continuing one-parameter families of RPOs of the planar Newtonian three-body problem.
  The method exploits consecutive syzygies, understood here as configurations in which the three bodies are aligned and their velocities satisfy the corresponding symmetry conditions. Matching the positions and momenta at two consecutive alignments reduces the computation of RPOs to a low-dimensional nonlinear problem. Its solutions are then numerically continued, and linear stability is determined from the nontrivial eigenvalues of the rotated monodromy matrix after removing the neutral directions associated with conserved quantities and continuous symmetries.
  The procedure is applied to several mass distributions and initial configurations, producing families of Poincaré, Hill, and binary-type solutions. These families exhibit transitions from nearly circular to highly eccentric motion, changes of stability near resonances and turning points, and absolute periodic solutions when the rotation angle is a rational multiple of 2π. In the Hill families, the continuation connects satellite configurations with circumstellar motion as the smallest body loses its gravitational binding to the intermediate body. Circumbinary and circumstellar configurations are also obtained in the binary regime.
  The results illustrate the dynamical diversity of RPOs and provide coherent three-body motions that can be used as prescribed trajectories in restricted four-body models.

</details>

### [72] [Lipshitz-Sarkar refines mirrors more than Khovanov](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.00976)
*Yang-Hui He*

Main category: math.GT

TL;DR: 本文回应了Lipshitz与Sarkar在ICM 2018上提出的问题，通过构造一个素结（prime knot）及其镜像（mirror），二者在整数Khovanov同调（integral Khovanov homology）的每个双次数上都相同（包括挠群部分），但在模2系数下，第二Steenrod平方（second Steenrod square）的秩在指定双次数上分别为1和0，从而给出了肯定回答。文中还给出了复合结的例子。构造、搜索与计算借助chat-GPT 5.6 sol Pro完成，数据来源于Regina结数据库。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Lipshitz与Sarkar在ICM 2018公开问题中询问：当普通整数Khovanov同调无法区分某结与其镜像时，其同伦论细化（homotopy-theoretic refinement）是否仍能探测镜像反射（mirror reflection）。本文旨在为该问题提供一个肯定的实例回答。

Method: 构造并搜索一个素结，使得该结与其镜像在整数Khovanov同调的每一个双次数上（含挠部分）完全一致；随后对比二者在模$\mathbb{F}_2$系数下第二Steenrod平方的秩，在指定双次数上检验秩的差异（结为1，镜像为0）。计算与搜索借助chat-GTP 5.6 sol Pro在Regina结普查（Regina Census）数据中完成，并另构造了一个复合结实例加以佐证。

Result: 构造出一个素结及其镜像，二者整数Khovanov同调完全无法区分，但在$\mathbb{F}_2$系数下，第二Steenrod平方的秩在该结上为1、在其镜像上为0，从而证实同伦论细化确实能探测镜像反射。额外还给出了一组复合结的例子。

Conclusion: 对Lipshitz–Sarkar问题给出了肯定的回答：同伦论化的Khovanov同调具有超越整数Khovanov同调的镜像区分能力。该结果也展示了利用大型结数据库与自动化工具辅助数学发现的可操作路径。

Abstract: Lipshitz and Sarkar asked in an ICM 2018 question whether their homotopy-theoretic refinement of Khovanov homology can detect mirror reflection when ordinary integral Khovanov homology cannot. We give an affirmative answer by presenting a prime knot whose integral Khovanov homology agrees with that of its mirror in every bidegree, including torsion, and yet over $\mathbb{F}_2$, the second Steenrod square has rank one for the knot and rank zero for its mirror in a specified bidegree. We also give a composite example. The construction, search and computations are done with the help of {\it chat-GTP 5.6 sol Pro} combing over the {\it Regina Census} of knots.

</details>

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 风味非普适规范对称性在TeV能级的自发破缺既可生成标准模型费米子质量与混合角的分层结构，也可能诱导强一阶相变进而产生引力波信号。此前尚未系统评估此类信号能否在LISA观测中从仪器噪声与天体物理前景中分离和重建，本文旨在填补这一空缺。

Result: 研究表明最强基准信号可被成功重建；较弱信号与未解析河外致密双星前景存在显著简并。进一步发现，基于地面探测器观测导出的对前景的更紧先验可以降低这种简并并改善信号重建效果。

Conclusion: 风味解构模型可以产生LISA可观测的信号，同时突显了天体物理前景建模对于此类信号的识别与表征的重要性。

Abstract: We investigate the reconstruction of a stochastic gravitational-wave background generated by a first-order phase transition in flavour-deconstruction models using the Laser Interferometer Space Antenna (LISA). In these scenarios, the spontaneous breaking of an extended flavour-non-universal gauge symmetry at the TeV scale can both generate the observed hierarchies of Standard Model fermion masses and mixing angles and induce a strong first-order phase transition. We consider representative benchmark points of the model, compute the corresponding thermodynamic transition parameters, and construct the resulting gravitational-wave spectra using a state-of-the-art sound-wave template. We then inject these spectra into simulated LISA data and perform Bayesian inference with SGWBinner, jointly reconstructing the cosmological signal, instrumental noise, and astrophysical foregrounds. We find that the strongest benchmark signal can be successfully reconstructed, whereas weaker signals are substantially degenerate with the unresolved extragalactic compact-binary foreground. We further show that tighter prior information on this foreground, motivated by observations with ground-based detectors, can reduce this degeneracy and improve signal reconstruction. Our results demonstrate that flavour-deconstruction models can produce signals accessible to LISA, while highlighting the importance of astrophysical-foreground modelling for their identification and characterization.

</details>

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 小尺度上的大振幅原始曲率和张量扰动可通过多种源项诱导产生二阶张量-标量引力波，这类引力波是随机引力波背景（SGWB）观测的主要贡献源之一。为更好地理解并区分TSIGWs与其他SGWB源，需要对其谱特征进行深入研究。

Conclusion: 非高斯性对二阶TSIGWs谱结构有影响，而各向异性角功率谱为此类引力波源的识别提供了有前景的诊断工具。

Abstract: Large primordial curvature and tensor perturbations on small scales can generate second-order tensor-scalar induced gravitational waves (TSIGWs) through scalar-scalar, tensor-scalar, and tensor-tensor source terms. TSIGWs are recognized as one of the principal contributors to the stochastic gravitational wave background (SGWB) observations. We explore the energy density spectrum of second-order TSIGWs in the presence of non-Gaussian primordial curvature perturbations. Furthermore, we present the first calculation of the anisotropic angular power spectrum associated with second-order TSIGWs, which provides a promising tool to discriminate them from other SGWB sources.

</details>

### [75] [Detectability-Aware Screening of Post-Merger Gravitational-Wave Damping Anomalies in Real Detector Noise: A Graph-Spectral Approach with Empirical Null Calibration](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.00095)
*Ruslan Alaskarov*

Main category: astro-ph.IM

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种两阶段方法学概念验证，用于在真实LIGO Livingston探测器噪声中筛选简化的并合后引力波阻尼异常信号。信号以受控阻尼正弦代理注入，第一阶段通过Constant-Q变换的图特征值进行可检测性统计，第二阶段使用非线性最小二乘估计有效阻尼时间评分，在保留的真实噪声测试块上达到AUC=0.943、TPR=0.879（FPR=0.012），优于仅使用功率的基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在真实引力波探测中，并合后（post-merger）阻尼异常的统计可检测性和阻尼时间评分的可靠性尚未在真实探测器噪声条件下得到系统验证，需要一种方法学上的概念验证来评估此类信号在实际噪声校准下的可检测性能。

Method: 采用两阶段（two-stage）方法学流程：Stage 1通过对白化候选窗口的Constant-Q表示构建局部时频图，利用前导特征值作为可检测性统计量，并在真实探测器噪声上经验校准其零分布；Stage 2对通过Stage 1的候选使用非线性最小二乘（nonlinear least squares）和可解释的岭/能量衰减（ridge/energy-decay）估计器计算有效阻尼时间评分，阈值在最终保留测试前预先固定。

Result: 匹配滤波信噪比约定通过合成高斯噪声验证，图统计量通过功效比较（power-comparison）和瓦片洗牌（tile-shuffling）测试评估。在保留的真实噪声测试块上，非线性最小二乘Stage 2评分取得AUC=0.943，在FPR=0.012下TPR=0.879，优于经过校准的纯功率基线。

Conclusion: 该方法学证明在真实LIGO Livingston噪声条件下，两阶段框架能够以高于功率基线的性能有效筛选阻尼异常的并合后候选信号，为未来使用物理上完整的双中子星并合后波形的应用提供了方法学基础。

Abstract: A two-stage methodological proof of concept for screening simplified post-merger gravitational-wave damping anomalies in real LIGO Livingston noise is presented. The injected signal is a controlled damped-sinusoid proxy rather than a physically complete binary-neutron-star post-merger waveform; it is used to test statistical detectability and damping-time scoring under real-noise calibration. Stage 1 constructs a local time-frequency graph from Constant-Q representations of whitened candidate windows and uses the leading eigenvalue as a detectability statistic, with its null distribution calibrated empirically on real detector noise. Stage 2 estimates an effective damping-time score for Stage-1-passing candidates using nonlinear least squares and an interpretable ridge/energy-decay estimator, with thresholds fixed before final held-out evaluation. The matched-filter SNR convention is checked with synthetic Gaussian noise, while the graph statistic is assessed through power-comparison and tile-shuffling tests. On a held-out real-noise test block, the nonlinear least-squares Stage-2 score gives AUC = 0.943 and TPR = 0.879 at FPR = 0.012, outperforming a calibrated power-only baseline.

</details>

*Ewan McCulloch,Adam Nahum*

Main category: cond-mat.stat-mech

TL;DR: 本文研究带噪声分布式传感器阵列对扩散目标的贝叶斯追踪问题，将其映射为带有移动缺陷的KPZ生长（或等价的有向聚合物钉扎问题）。研究发现d=2和d=3维度下存在去钉扎相变，追踪成功相与失败相之间的转变涉及多种临界行为，其中一些无法用传统ε展开描述。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在先前一维情形研究的基础上，本文旨在推广到更高维度，理解贝叶斯最优与次优推断在分布式传感器追踪中的相结构，并揭示模型误设（model misspecification）如何驱动系统进入不同失败相。

Method: 采用弱耦合重整化群（weak-coupling renormalization group）理论分析，结合数值模拟，并在d=2和d=3维度下利用KPZ/Edwards-Wilkinson统计及有向聚合物（directed polymer）相关的最新严格结果对相图和相变进行系统研究。

Result: 在d=2中，贝叶斯最优追踪总是成功，但过度自信的次优推断可导致失败；在d=3中，追踪可成功，也可以两种方式失败（后验分布退局域化的无检测失败或局域化在错误位置的虚假检测失败）。三个相在双参数相图中沿贝叶斯最优线相交于Nishimori类多临界点，部分相变表现出异常临界行为，传统ε展开无法描述。

Conclusion: 本文的结果（包括钉扎相变处临界指数的标度关系和RG流结果）不仅适用于贝叶斯追踪问题，还可推广到2+1D或3+1D中涉及表面生长或有向聚合物的其他相变系统。

Abstract: We study Bayesian tracking of a diffusing target monitored by a noisy distributed sensor array. Building on an earlier mapping to KPZ growth with a moving defect (or an equivalent directed polymer pinning problem) we determine the phase structure, beyond the previously-studied one-dimensional case, for both Bayes-optimal and suboptimal inference. In $d=2$, theoretical analysis and numerical simulations both give a depinning transition between a successful tracking phase and a failure phase. Weak-coupling RG shows that Bayes-optimal tracking is always successful in $d=2$, but failure can arise from overconfident (suboptimal) inference. In $d=3$, tracking can succeed, or can fail in two distinct ways: the posterior probability distribution may delocalize (no detection), or may become sharply localized, but at the wrong position (a false detection). The two possibilities correspond to Edwards-Wilkinson or Kardar-Parisi-Zhang statistics for the log-posterior. The three phases meet at a Nishimori-like multicritical point on a Bayes-optimal line in a two-parameter phase diagram. (Model misspecification alone can drive depinning into either unpinned phase: underconfidence gives diffuse failure, while overconfidence gives localized-but-wrong failure.) We analyze the transitions between the various phases numerically and with renormalization group arguments. We show that some of these have unusual critical behavior, which the conventional $ε$ expansion fails to describe. Recent rigorous results for directed polymers indicate an alternative scenario. Many of our results, including a scaling relation for exponents at pinning transitions and results for RG flows, are relevant to other phase transitions that involve surface growth or directed polymers in 2+1D or 3+1D.

</details>


### [77] [Nonlinear Fluctuating Hydrodynamics from Interacting Noisy Quantum Matter](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.00159)
*Alexios Christopoulos,João Costa,Stefano Scopa,Jacopo De Nardis,Zala Lenarčič,Denis Bernard,Tony Jin*

Main category: cond-mat.stat-mech

TL;DR: 该论文从边界驱动的XXZ自旋链（含体相退相干）的微观Lindblad动力学出发，推导出描述其大尺度涨落的经典宏观流涨落理论（Macroscopic Fluctuation Theory, MFT），该流体力学描述携带密度相关的扩散率和迁移率，从而证明含噪声量子多体系统可实现真正相互作用扩散物质（interacting diffusive matter）的普适类。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 统计物理的核心难题之一在于缺乏对相互作用量子多体系统中非平衡稳态（non-equilibrium steady states）的普适刻画，该问题尚未得到系统解决。

Method: 以边界驱动的XXZ自旋链（含体相退相干）为范例模型，直接从其微观Lindblad动力学出发推导出描述大尺度涨落的经典宏观流涨落理论（MFT），并利用张量网络模拟（tensor-network simulations）对理论结果进行数值验证。

Result: 所得到的有效流体力学描述携带随密度变化的扩散率和迁移率，作为相互作用的指纹；该描述可精确计算稳态密度剖面、长程关联以及电流的全计数统计（full counting statistics），结果与张量网络模拟高度一致。

Conclusion: 含噪声的量子多体系统能够实现真正相互作用扩散物质的普适类，超越了对称简单排他过程（symmetric simple exclusion process）中恒定扩散率的类别，确立了MFT作为相互作用扩散量子系统普适框架的强有力地位。

Abstract: A universal characterization of non-equilibrium steady states in interacting quantum many-body systems remains one of the central challenges of statistical physics. Here, we address this problem for a paradigmatic model of diffusive interacting quantum matter---the boundary-driven XXZ spin chain with bulk dephasing---and derive, directly from its microscopic Lindblad dynamics, an emergent classical Macroscopic Fluctuation Theory (MFT) governing its large-scale fluctuations. Crucially, the resulting hydrodynamics carries a density-dependent diffusivity and mobility as the fingerprint of interactions. This effective description enables the exact computation of the stationary density profile, long-range correlations, and the full counting statistics of the current, in excellent agreement with tensor-network simulations. Our work demonstrates that noisy quantum many-body systems can realize the universality class of genuinely interacting diffusive matter, beyond the constant-diffusivity class of the symmetric simple exclusion process, and establishes MFT as a powerful universal framework for interacting diffusive quantum systems.

</details>


### [78] [Duality between the level statistics of Hermitian and non-Hermitian random matrices](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.00162)
*Ze Chen,Zhenyu Xiao,Yifei Liu,Shinsei Ryu*

Main category: cond-mat.stat-mech

TL;DR: 本文发现在大矩阵尺寸（large-$N$）极限下，厄米（Hermitian）与非厄米（non-Hermitian）随机矩阵的能级统计之间存在精确对偶关系，并以换位对称性（transposition symmetry）为组织原则，建立了Dyson三分类的非厄米对应物，同时导出了七个非标准Altland–Zirnbauer类的硬边统计（hard-edge statistics）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随机矩阵理论（random matrix theory）以对称性为组织原则来描述复杂量子系统的统计行为。此前非厄米随机矩阵在原点附近的谱密度等硬边统计大多仅能通过数值方法得知，缺乏系统性的解析框架，因此需要一个能联系厄米与非厄米体系的统一理论。

Method: 作者揭示厄米与非厄米随机矩阵在大$N$极限下的精确对偶性，该对偶逐对称类成立：两个由费米型非线性$\sigma$模型给出的复制配分函数（replica partition functions）可通过解析延拓（analytic continuation）相互关联。将该对偶应用于Wigner–Dyson三类与七个Altland–Zirnbauer类，分别推导相应统计量，并通过精确对角化（exact diagonalization）验证解析预测，借助物理模型检验普适性。

Result: 对三个Wigner–Dyson类，该对偶导出了非厄米随机矩阵的普适体元本征值对关联函数（bulk eigenvalue pair-correlation functions），建立了以换位对称性组织的Dyson三分类非厄米对应物；相应类的耗散谱形因子（dissipative spectral form factors）以闭式给出。对七个非标准Altland–Zirnbauer类，该对偶得到原点附近的精确谱密度，即非厄米硬边统计，其中多数此前仅知数值结果。

Conclusion: 数值确认与物理模型检验表明上述解析结果具有普适性，作者预期这些结果指向厄米与非厄米随机矩阵理论之间更深层的对应关系。

Abstract: Random matrix theory describes complex quantum systems statistically, with symmetry as its organizing principle. We uncover an exact duality between the level statistics of Hermitian and non-Hermitian random matrices in the large-$N$ (matrix size) limit. It acts class by class: the two replica partition functions, given by fermionic nonlinear $σ$ models, are related by analytic continuation. Applied to the three Wigner--Dyson classes, the duality yields the universal bulk eigenvalue pair-correlation functions of non-Hermitian random matrices. This establishes a non-Hermitian counterpart of Dyson's threefold way, organized by transposition symmetry: generic complex, complex symmetric, and complex self-dual matrices. The dissipative spectral form factors of these classes follow in closed form as well. Applied to the seven nonstandard Altland--Zirnbauer classes, the duality yields the exact spectral densities near the origin, the non-Hermitian hard-edge statistics. Most of these statistics were previously known only numerically. Exact diagonalization confirms the analytical predictions, and physical models demonstrate their universality. We expect these results to be the tip of a deeper correspondence between Hermitian and non-Hermitian random matrix theory.

</details>


### [79] [Exact joint eigenvalue densities of non-Hermitian random matrices are Calogero scattering states](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.00164)
*Zhenyu Xiao,Ze Chen,Yifei Liu,Shinsei Ryu*

Main category: cond-mat.stat-mech

TL;DR: 本文解决了任意尺寸下具有转置对称性（transposition symmetry）的非厄米矩阵（非厄米矩阵）的精确联合本征值密度这一长期未解问题，发现其与Calogero模型的散射态波函数相关联，并计算了复能级间距分布和两点谱关联函数，揭示了其中的幂律尾部特征。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 确定精确的联合本征值密度（joint eigenvalue densities）是随机矩阵理论（random matrix theory）的核心问题，但对于具有转置对称性的非厄米矩阵，该问题在任意矩阵尺寸下尚未得到解决，是领域内的长期开放问题。

Method: 作者将联合本征值密度与Calogero模型的散射态波函数（scattering-state wave functions）建立联系，其中Calogero模型是由逆平方势相互作用的一维粒子系统，耦合强度由对称性决定；密度表达式包含一个Vandermonde因子。

Result: 所得联合密度无法写成具有两两相互作用的特征值气体形式；进一步计算了复能级间距分布（complex level spacing distributions）和两点谱关联函数（two-point spectral correlation functions），发现其携带Coulomb气体中不存在的幂律尾（power-law tails）。

Conclusion: 研究结果揭示了随机矩阵、可积性（integrability）与对称性之间的深层联系，为理解这三者的相互作用提供了新的视角。

Abstract: Determining exact joint eigenvalue densities is central to random matrix theory. We solve this long-standing problem for non-Hermitian matrices with transposition symmetry (complex symmetric and complex self-dual) at arbitrary matrix size. Up to a Vandermonde factor, they are scattering-state wave functions of the Calogero model, a line of particles interacting through an inverse-square potential, with the coupling strength set by the symmetry. In contrast to many previously known joint densities, the densities cannot be written as a gas of eigenvalues with pairwise interactions. We further compute the complex level spacing distributions and two-point spectral correlation functions, which carry power-law tails, absent in a Coulomb gas. Our results shed light on the interplay among random matrices, integrability, and symmetry.

</details>


### [80] [A Human-AI Theorem Connecting Spontaneous and Field-Induced Mechanisms of Collective Behavior in One Dimension](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.00322)
*Weiguo Yin*

Abstract: Can an artificial intelligence (AI) generate a scientific hypothesis outside a human collaborator's active hypothesis space (AHS), and can human-AI research be organized to make such breakthroughs more likely? The emergence of the theorems from sustained human-AI collaboration suggests that involving AI throughout a systematic research program may incubate autonomous scientific breakthroughs.

Result: 反馈在不必改变平均热力学电流的情况下可增强储层参数估计（reservoir-parameter estimation）与时钟精度（clock precision）；该增强精度不受储层熵产生单独约束，其缺失成本由反馈装置的信息熵产生补给。在双量子点示例中，跳变条件反馈（jump-conditioned feedback）改善了温度测量（thermometry）与化学势传感（chemical-potential sensing），并稳定了以输出电流触发信号（ticks）定义的量子时钟。

Abstract: Feedback can convert continuously monitored quantum jumps into a thermodynamic resource.
