### [111] [Isospectral potentials with Dirac delta interaction: Constrained Spectra](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.00056)
*Kumar Abhinav,Biswanath Rath,Prasanta K. Panigrahi*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文研究在超对称（等谱）构造中通过不连续的超势引入狄拉克δ函数（Dirac delta function, DDF）量子势，而非将其简单作为附加项。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有方法通常将DDF作为外加项处理，本文旨在探索通过不连续超势在等谱构造中自然嵌入DDF的可能性及其对谱结构的限制效应。

Method: 构造由两个独立可解区域组成，在奇异点处通过由自伴随延拓（self-adjoint extension）所施加的匹配条件连接；这些边界条件对每个本征态施加独立的代数约束，通过正则化过程限制并截断谱。

Result: 存活束缚态必须为偶宇称，其数量由系统可用参数而非通常的量子化规则决定；谐振子与Rosen-Morse势等谱注入DDF展现出高度受限的谱，而DDF与Calogero型奇异性组合因正则化失效而基本不相容。

Conclusion: 局域奇异性能够控制并设计量子系统的离散谱，对具有高度局域相互作用的众多物理系统具有潜在应用价值。

Abstract: We study quantum potentials containing Dirac delta function (DDF) within its supersymmetric (isospectral) construction via a discontinuous superpotential, instead of being simply an added part. This construction comprises of two independently solvable sectors joined at the singular point by matching conditions imposed by the corresponding self-adjoint extension. Needing careful regularization, these boundary conditions impose independent algebraic constraints on each of the eigenstates, that restrict and truncate the spectrum. The number of surviving bound states, which are required to be parity-even, is thus fixed by the available system parameters rather than by the usual quantization rule. The specific cases of the Harmonic oscillator and the Rosen-Morse potential isospectrally infused with the DDF demonstrate such highly restrictive spectra, while the similar combination of the DDF with a Calogero-type singularity turns out to be largely incompatible as the regularization breaks down. Therefore, localized singularities are capable of controlling and engineering the discrete spectrum of quantum systems, with possible use in myriads of physical systems with very localized interactions.

</details>


### [112] [High-Rank Encoding Can Improve Approximate Quantum Error Correction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.00778)
*Bikun Li,Liang Jiang*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文指出传统量子码构造将纯逻辑态编码为纯码态，但该限制可能牺牲性能。研究表明内在编码随机性可以提升最优纠缠保真度（entanglement fidelity），并证明了在联合优化后，强加秩为一（rank-one）的编码器造成的损失在接近完美恢复时最多是二次量级，且该优势在小噪声扰动下依然保持。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统量子码构造将纯逻辑态编码为纯码态，但研究者怀疑这种限制可能并非最优，会牺牲编码性能。

Method: 从理论上分析编码随机性对最优纠缠保真度的影响，界定秩为一编码器带来的性能损失上界（证明其为联合优化后接近完美恢复情形下的二次量级损失），并构造显式噪声族以验证结论。

Result: 证明了秩为一编码器在接近完美恢复时造成的性能损失是二次量级（at most quadratic）；优化优势在小噪声扰动下稳健存在；显式噪声族表明需要更高秩（higher-rank）编码器才能任意逼近完美恢复，此时每个最优编码器都将纯输入映射到混合码态。

Conclusion: 编码过程中的内在随机性（更高秩编码器）可以超越传统纯态编码的性能限制，为量子码设计提供了更广阔的构造空间。

Abstract: Conventional quantum-code constructions encode pure logical states as pure code states, but this restriction can sacrifice performance. We show that intrinsic encoding randomness can improve optimal entanglement fidelity. We bound the loss from imposing a rank-one encoder and prove it is at most quadratic near perfect recovery after joint optimization. The optimized advantage survives small noise perturbations. An explicit noise family requires higher-rank encoders arbitrarily close to perfect recovery, with every optimal encoder mapping pure inputs to mixed code states.

</details>


### [113] [Anticoncentration and entanglement in Gaussian boson sampling](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.01241)
*Laura Shou,Adam Ehrenberg,Yu-Xin Wang,Joseph T. Iosue,Alexey V. Gorshkov*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文围绕高斯玻色采样（Gaussian boson sampling）中的反集中（anticoncentration）与纠缠（entanglement）两个性质展开，推导了对称高斯乘积的Hafnian二阶矩闭式表达式，精确定位了反集中转变位置；推导了Rényi-α Page曲线闭式表达式；并研究了不等压缩参数情形下平均Rényi-2熵Page曲线的估计与单调性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 高斯玻色采样在不同条件下的经典计算难度或易处理性可通过反集中与纠缠两个性质来刻画，但相关定量规律尚不明确，需要严格的数学推导来定位转变点并理解参数对行为的影响。

Method: 采用解析方法，推导对称高斯乘积的Hafnian二阶矩闭式表达式，据此定位作为压缩输入模式数量函数的反集中转变；推导Rényi-α Page曲线的闭式表达式；并针对不等输入压缩参数(s_i)_i，证明平均Rényi-2熵Page曲线的估计及关于|s_i|的单调性。

Result: 获得对称高斯乘积Hafnian二阶矩的闭式解，能精确刻画反集中转变与压缩输入模式数的函数关系；获得Rényi-α Page曲线的闭式表达式；在不等压缩参数情况下建立估计并证明Page曲线关于各|s_i|幅度具有单调性，从而将等压缩结果推广到不等压缩情形。

Conclusion: 本文的分析结果为高斯玻色采样中反集中与纠缠行为提供了精确刻画，并通过单调性证明实现等压缩到不等压缩结果的推广，有助于理解不同参数区间下高斯玻色采样的经典采样难度。

Abstract: Anticoncentration and entanglement are two properties used to study classical hardness or easiness of Gaussian boson sampling in varying regimes. In this paper, we consider three directions concerning anticoncentration and entanglement in Gaussian boson sampling: (1) We derive closed-form expressions for the second moment of hafnians of symmetric Gaussian products, and use this to precisely locate the anticoncentration transition as a function of the number of squeezed input modes. (2) We derive closed-form expressions for the Rényi-$α$ Page curves for Gaussian boson sampling. (3) We study unequal input squeezing parameters $(s_i)_i$, and prove estimates as well as monotonicity of the average-case Rényi-2 entropy Page curve in terms of the magnitudes $|s_i|$, which allow for extending equal squeezing results to unequal squeezing.

</details>


### [114] [A nonabelian anyon violates Haag duality](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.01267)
*Daniel Wallick,Henrik Wilming*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 该论文证明描述单一非阿贝尔任意子（nonabelian anyon）的超选择扇区违反Haag对偶（Haag duality），并进一步证明其关联基态不满足近似Haag对偶，从而否证了所有有能隙基态均满足该条件的猜想。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Haag对偶与量子信息论中纯化唯一性原理等价，是理解有能隙物相（gapped phases）中量子信息结构的关键概念。此前是否存在违反Haag对偶的有能隙物相尚属未知，且近似Haag对偶是否对所有有能隙基态成立存在公开猜想。

Method: 论文采用双路径论证：一方面给出基于物理直觉的简单论证，另一方面利用扇区理论（sector theory）提供严格证明。具体通过在Levin-Wen模型中构造实例，检验超选择扇区中的Haag对偶性质，并通过量子操控（quantum steering）检验基态的量子关联结构。

Result: 论文证明：(1) 单一非阿贝尔任意子的超选择扇区违反Haag对偶；(2) 该关联基态虽为纯态但不支持量子操控；(3) 该基态同时违反近似Haag对偶，否证了相关猜想；(4) 由此可推出存在在整个物相中每一点均违反Haag对偶的有能隙物相。

Conclusion: 该结果表明有能隙物相中确实存在Haag对偶在相空间每一点均失效的情形，这为理解物相中量子信息的根本结构提供了新约束，并揭示了非阿贝尔任意子与量子信息基本原理之间的深层联系。

Abstract: We show that a superselction sector describing a single nonabelian anyon violates Haag duality, which is equivalent to the fundamental quantum-information theoretic principle of uniqueness of purifications. We provide a simple physics argument as well as a rigorous proof using sector theory, and instantiate our result concretely in Levin-Wen models. We also show that the associated ground state does not allow for quantum steering despite being a pure state. Finally, we show that this ground state violates approximate Haag duality, disproving the conjecture that all gapped ground states satisfy this condition. Our result implies that there are gapped phases of matter where Haag duality fails at every point in the phase.

</details>


### [115] [Irreducibility and regularisation properties of Gaussian quantum Markov semigroups](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.01547)
*Franco Fagnola,Federico Girotti*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文研究了作用于连续变量量子系统的高斯量子马尔可夫半群（Gaussian quantum Markov semigroups, GQMSs）的正则性与不可约性，通过漂移矩阵与量子扩散矩阵的代数条件刻画其平滑效应，并给出了不可约性的多个等价代数判据，其中包括Hörmander条件的量子类比。一个核心发现是：与经典情形不同，在量子情形中不可约性严格强于保证正则化的条件。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 连续变量量子系统中高斯量子马尔可夫半群的正则化（regularisation）与不可约性（irreducibility）是理解其长期行为与结构的关键，但缺乏统一的代数刻画框架。作者旨在建立一套代数条件，将这两类性质与量子线性系统的可控性理论及无序子系统（decoherence-free subsystems）的结构联系起来。

Method: 作者首先为算子引入自然的正则性概念，将GQMSs的平滑效应（smoothing effects）刻画为涉及漂移矩阵（drift matrix）与量子扩散矩阵（quantum diffusion matrix）的代数条件。随后通过三个等价的代数判据表征不可约性：其一基于漂移与量子扩散矩阵，其二基于广义GKLS表示（GKLS representation）中出现的算子，其三为Hörmander条件（Hörmander's condition）的量子类比。

Result: 建立了正则性与不可约性的完整代数框架，证明了不可约性可通过多个等价条件刻画。一个核心结果是：与经典马尔可夫过程不同，GQMSs的可约性严格强于保证正则化的代数条件，即正则化并不蕴含不可约性。

Conclusion: 本文为可约高斯量子马尔可夫半群及更一般的连续变量量子马尔可夫半群的系统研究奠定了代数基础，揭示了量子情形下不可约性与正则性关系的本质差异。

Abstract: We study regularisation and irreducibility properties of Gaussian quantum Markov semigroups (GQMSs) acting on continuous-variable quantum systems. We first identify a natural notion of regularity for operators in this setting, which allows us to formulate and characterise the smoothing effects of GQMSs in terms of algebraic conditions involving the drift and quantum diffusion matrices. These conditions establish a connection with the controllability theory of quantum linear systems and with the structure of decoherence-free subsystems.
  We then characterise irreducibility through several equivalent algebraic criteria: one formulated in terms of the drift and quantum diffusion matrices, one in terms of the operators appearing in the generalised GKLS representation of the generator, and a third given by a quantum analogue of Hörmander's condition. A central and somewhat surprising consequence is that, in contrast with the classical case, irreducibility is strictly stronger than conditions ensuring regularisation. Our results provide an algebraic framework for analysing these properties and lay the groundwork for a broader study of reducible Gaussian quantum Markov semigroups and, more generally, more general relevant quantum Markov semigroups on continuous-variable systems.

</details>


### [116] [Tangent fermions can restore vacuum stability of discrete-time Dirac models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.01029)
*C. W. J. Beenakker*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文提出一种替代Dirac量子游走的离散化方案，利用切线型色散关系（tangent dispersion relation）和Cayley变换，在保持单一Dirac锥的同时消除真空不稳定性，并证明其Schwinger效应产生率与连续极限下单一Dirac锥的结果一致。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Dirac量子游走（Dirac quantum walk）在准能量—动量布里渊区中心与角点存在2μ质量间隙，Gupta和Short近期指出Dirac真空可在区域角点产生粒子-空穴对并向环境释放能量2μ，造成真空不稳定性。消除这种不稳定的现有方案会引入费米倍增（fermion doubling）及第二个低能Dirac锥，作者旨在寻找一种既能保持稳定性又不产生额外Dirac锥的替代方案。

Method: 作者采用切线型色散关系替代正弦型色散关系，并利用Cayley变换，将Floquet本征值e^{-iε}所在单位圆映射到无界能量E=2tan(ε/2)的实轴上。随后计算了切线费米子（tangent fermions）在均匀电场中的Schwinger效应，并对比两种标量势耦合方式（通过量子映射的厄米生成子耦合，以及分裂算符耦合以保持格点精确规范不变性）下的行为。

Result: 切线费米子在均匀电场中的粒子-空穴对产生率与连续极限下单一Dirac锥的结果一致。当标量势通过量子映射的厄米生成子耦合时，区域角点被精确解耦；当标量势以分裂算符方式耦合以保持格点精确规范不变性时，角点也有贡献，但其权重随格点常数呈二次衰减，与Dirac量子游走中区域角点不稳定性在连续极限下依然存在的情况形成对比。

Conclusion: 切线型色散关系的替代离散化方案能够在保持单一Dirac锥的同时提供真空稳定性，在连续极限下其物理行为与连续Dirac方程一致，克服了Gupta-Short方案中因费米倍增而产生的缺陷。

Abstract: The Dirac quantum walk (a 1+1 dimensional space-time discretization of the Dirac equation) has a $2μ$ mass gap both at the center and at the corner of the quasi-energy-momentum Brillouin zone. Gupta and Short recently noted [Quantum 9, 1845 (2025)] that the Dirac vacuum can create a particle-hole pair at the zone corner with the release of an energy $2μ$ to the environment. They removed this vacuum instability at the expense of fermion doubling, the appearance of a second low-energy Dirac cone. Here we show that an alternative discretization scheme, with a tangent rather than a sine dispersion relation, offers stability while retaining a single Dirac cone. The key step is the Cayley transformation from the unit circle of Floquet eigenvalues $e^{-i\varepsilon}$ to the real line of unbounded energies $E=2\tan(\varepsilon/2)$. We compute the Schwinger effect (particle-hole pair creation in a uniform electric field) for tangent fermions and show that the pair-production rate agrees with the continuum result for a single Dirac cone. The zone corner is exactly decoupled if the scalar potential is coupled through the Hermitian generator of the quantum map. If it is coupled as a split operator, in order to preserve exact gauge invariance on the lattice, the corner does contribute - with a weight that vanishes quadratically with the lattice constants, in contrast to the Dirac quantum walk where the zone-corner instability survives the continuum limit.

</details>


### [117] [Scattering and Tunneling in Real Quantum Mechanics](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.01066)
*Firdevs Karakus,Daniil Stepanenko,Igor Volovich*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 该论文在实量子力学（real quantum mechanics）中建立了演化方程的一般形式，研究了散射与隧穿问题。作者证明了实Kähler空间上Stone定理的类比命题，推导出相应的演化方程，其演化算子同时具有正交性和辛性。散射理论通过实波算子及对应S矩阵表述，所研究势类下的微分散射截面与标准复量子力学结果一致，隧穿概率亦完全相同。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 实量子力学是一种只使用实数的量子力学形式体系，其基本数学框架与标准复量子力学不同，因此需要建立对应的演化方程和散射理论，以考察实量子力学是否能复现标准量子力学的物理预言。

Method: 作者在实Kähler空间（real Kähler space）上证明了类比于Stone定理的结论，并据此推导出一般形式的演化方程，其演化算子同时满足正交条件与辛条件。散射理论采用实波算子（real wave operators）和相应的S矩阵（S-matrix）进行表述。

Result: 对于所考察的势类（class of potentials），实量子力学中的微分散射截面与标准复量子力学得到的微分散射截面等价；同时，实量子力学中的隧穿概率与复量子力学中的隧穿概率完全相同。

Conclusion: 研究结果表明，实量子力学在所考虑的散射与隧穿问题中能够复现标准复量子力学的物理结果，为实量子力学形式体系的可行性与等价性提供了理论支持。

Abstract: We establish the general form of the evolution equation and study scattering and tunneling in real quantum mechanics. We prove an analogue of Stone's theorem for a real Kahler space and derive the corresponding general evolution equation, whose evolution operator is both orthogonal and symplectic. We formulate scattering theory in terms of real wave operators and the associated S-matrix. For the class of potentials considered, we show that the differential scattering cross section is equivalent to that obtained in standard complex quantum mechanics. We also show that the tunneling probability in real quantum mechanics is identical to that in complex quantum mechanics.

</details>


### [118] [Superconducting Flux Memory for Cryogenic Applications](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.00772)
*Tony X. Zhou,John McFarland,Aruna N. Ramanayaka,Brian Sears,Colin Stack,Aref Fouladi,Robert Smith,Sambarta Rakshit,Zachary A. Stegen,Keith D. Hillaire,Moe Khalil,Robert M. Young,David G. Ferguson,Anthony J. Przybysz,John X. Pryzbysz,Mark Covington,Gregory R Boyd,Jeremy Clark,Aaron Pesetski*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文报道了用于超导电路的磁通存储器（flux memory）的开发，通过在片上超导回路中存储持续电流来为超导电路（如量子比特）提供磁通偏置。作者开发了三种类型的磁通存储器并进行了对比，展示了其可通过原位磁通探测器进行验证，且设定后无需外部直流控制源即可保持片上磁通偏置。该技术可呈二维排列以复用控制信号，将线路数量从N²器件缩减为2N条控制线，并成功用于偏置transmon量子比特，使其稳定运行20小时。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 超导量子电路（superconducting circuits）中的量子比特需要精确的磁通偏置才能调控其频率和耦合，而传统方案依赖大量外部直流控制线源，导致线路数量随器件规模平方增长。为突破这一可扩展性瓶颈，作者开发了片上磁通存储技术。

Method: 作者开发了三种类型的磁通存储器件，在片上超导回路中存储持续电流。利用原位磁通探测器对每种方案进行表征和对比，并通过关闭台式直流控制电源来验证磁通偏置的保持能力。此外，将磁通存储器应用于transmon量子比特的磁通偏置，并测量其频率可调性及稳定性。

Result: 三种磁通存储器方案均能成功存储片上磁通偏置，且在断电后偏置保持不变。利用transmon量子比特验证了磁通存储器可将量子比特状态调谐至目标频率，并在片上稳定保持20小时。

Conclusion: 磁通存储器可实现片上磁通的非易失性偏置，并可通过二维阵列布局将控制线数量由平方规模（N²）缩减为线性规模（2N），为超导量子电路的大规模扩展提供了可行路径。

Abstract: We report the development of flux memory for use with superconducting circuits. This technology stores persistent currents in superconducting loops on-chip to be used to provide flux biasing for superconducting circuits, like qubits. We developed three types of flux memory and draw comparisons among them for circuit design. We demonstrate the utility of flux memory by using an in-situ flux detector and characterize each approach and further demonstrate that once flux is set in a memory cell, benchtop DC control sources can be powered off, leaving the on-chip flux bias in place. We propose that flux memory can be arranged in a two-dimensional configuration to multiplex control signals and reduce how line counts scale (N^2 devices -> 2N control lines), and our experimental results pave the path to the proposed scalability. We demonstrate the use of flux memory to flux bias a transmon qubit and show the tunability of the qubit state to a target frequency which remained stable on-chip for 20 hours.

</details>


### [119] [Thermal screening and critical scaling of quantum energy teleportation in a harmonic chain](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.00928)
*Taisanul Haque*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文发展了一维谐振子链中量子能量隐形传态（quantum energy teleportation）的有限温度高斯态（Gaussian-state）表述。在Gibbs态下，优化测量—反馈协议简化为热两体关联函数；数值结果验证了临界及非临界条件下提取能量的指数屏蔽与零温临界极限下精确的d^{-4}渐近律，并揭示了中间距离处有效衰减指数的表观漂移源于交叉行为。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 量子能量隐形传态在零温基态研究的基础上，缺乏对有限温度下行为与临界性质的系统分析。作者旨在建立可解析处理的有限温度框架，阐明热涨落对能量提取的影响以及临界极限下的精确标度律。

Method: 采用一维谐振子链的高斯态有限温度表述，将Gibbs态下的优化测量—反馈协议约化为热两点关联函数。分别构造单位点测量协议与压缩高斯测量协议，并借助热关联长度的概念分析热力学极限与近临界极限。

Result: 在固定有限温度下，提取能量随距离呈指数屏蔽，受热关联长度控制（临界与非临界情形均成立）。在热力学极限之后取零温临界极限，得到精确渐近律E_ext ~ d^{-4}。数值结果确认两种情形并澄清了中间距离处有效衰减指数表观漂移的交叉本质。p-压缩增强提取能量而q-压缩抑制之，均不改变大距离标度行为。

Conclusion: 结果表明，量子能量隐形传态在有限温度下仍具有明确的尺度化规律，热关联长度与临界指数共同支配提取能量的远程衰减；压缩测量可调节提取效率但与远程标度解耦，为调控量子能量转移提供了理论指导。

Abstract: We develop a finite-temperature Gaussian-state formulation of quantum energy teleportation in the one-dimensional harmonic chain. For Gibbs states, the optimized measurement-feedback protocol reduces to thermal two-point functions. In the single-site protocol, the extracted energy is governed by a single correlator, which makes the thermodynamic and near-critical limits analytically tractable. At fixed finite temperature, the extracted energy is exponentially screened with distance and is controlled by a thermal correlation length for both critical and non-critical $α$. In the zero-temperature critical limit taken after the thermodynamic limit, we derive the exact asymptotic law $E_{\mathrm{ext}}\sim d^{-4}$. Numerical results confirm both regimes and resolve the crossover responsible for the apparent drift of the effective decay exponent at intermediate distances. We also analyze squeezed Gaussian measurements and show that they modify the extraction prefactor: $p-$squeezing enhances the extracted energy, whereas $q-$squeezing suppresses it, without changing the large-distance scaling.

</details>


### [120] [Information geometry of non-equilibrium quantum states: Mixed metric structures on an extended information manifold](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.00977)
*Kouji Kashiwa,Hidefumi Matsuda*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文提出一个信息几何框架来描述非平衡动力学中的量子态，以横向场伊辛链（transverse-field Ising chain）模型为载体，研究量子Fisher信息度量及其混合分量 $g_{ht}$，并分析其与纠缠熵和Loschmidt回声等非平衡指标的关联。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 非平衡动力学中的量子态缺乏统一的几何刻画手段，亟需建立一种能够系统描述量子态在参数-时间扩展流形上演化的框架，以弥补传统度量工具的不足。

Method: 将弹后哈密顿量参数 $h$ 与实时 $t$ 视为扩展信息流形 $(h,t)$ 的坐标，考察量子Fisher信息度量（quantum Fisher information metric），重点分析混合度量分量 $g_{ht}$ 及相关几何观测量，这些量用于表征流形上演化速度以及时间方向与参数畸变方向之间的对准程度。

Result: 几何观测量与两种广泛使用的非平衡动力学度量——纠缠熵（entanglement entropy）和Loschmidt回声（Loschmidt echo）——之间存在相关性。

Conclusion: 信息几何框架可为非平衡量子动力学提供新的表征工具，其几何观测量有望成为描述量子态非平衡演化的有效指标。

Abstract: We discuss an information-geometric framework for characterizing quantum states in non-equilibrium dynamics. Using the transverse-field Ising chain model as a laboratory, we investigate the quantum Fisher information metric with particular emphasis on the mixed metric component $g_{ht}$ and related geometric observables, where $h$ is a controllable post-quench Hamiltonian parameter and $t$ is real time. The framework treats $h$ and $t$ as coordinates of an extended information manifold $(h,t)$. The geometric observables characterize the speed of evolution on the manifold and the alignment between the temporal and parameter-deformation directions. Correlations between these geometric quantities and two widely used measures of non-equilibrium dynamics, the entanglement entropy and the Loschmidt echo, are analyzed.

</details>


### [121] [Depth-1 expanders on the unitary group and applications](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.01605)
*Anurag Anshu,Shankar Balasubramanian,Jonas Haferkamp,Aram W. Harrow,Xinyu Tan*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文构造了一个常数度和常数间隙的量子扩张器（quantum expander），作用于n个量子比特，其中的每个幺正变换可通过深度为1、一维的Pauli门或CNOT门电路实现。该扩张器被应用于两个方向：一是构造一族无挫折（frustration-free）的一维哈密顿量，其基态满足纠缠—间隙关系 S = Θ(Δ^{-1/2})，这被认为是最优的结果而此前一直未实现；二是提供一种流式协议（streaming protocol），用于测试与一类一维体积律（volume-law）纠缠态的接近程度。此外，作者还将量子扩张器推广到酉群（unitary group）上的常数度扩张器，每个幺正变换为单个T门、T†门或深度为1的Clifford电路，从而产生酉群稠密子群上的有间隙随机游走，优于Bourgain和Gamburd此前未控制间隙对维度依赖的结果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 量子扩张器在量子信息与量子计算中具有基础重要性，但此前已知的构造往往需要较高的电路深度或缺乏对间隙—维度依赖关系的精确控制。此外，关于一维哈密顿量基态是否可实现最优纠缠—间隙标度S = Θ(Δ^{-1/2})，此前一直是一个悬而未决的问题。本文旨在通过构造效率更高的量子扩张器来解决这些开放问题。

Method: 作者构造了一个常数度和常数间隙的量子扩张器，其中每个幺正变换由一维深度为1的Pauli门或CNOT门电路实现。在此基础上，利用该扩张器构造无挫折的一维哈密顿量族，并设计用于测试体积律纠缠态的流式协议。进一步地，作者将扩张器推广至酉群，其中每个幺正变换为单个T门、T†门或深度为1的Clifford电路，从而构造酉群稠密子群上的有间隙随机游走。

Result: 成功构造了所需的量子扩张器，实现了具有纠缠—间隙关系 S = Θ(Δ^{-1/2}) 的一维无挫折哈密顿量族，并给出了测试一维体积律纠缠态的流式协议。同时，将结果推广到酉群上的扩张器，改进了Bourgain和Gamburd的工作，弥补了其未控制间隙对维度依赖的问题。

Conclusion: 本文的量子扩张器构造为一维无挫折哈密顿量的纠缠—间隙下界提供了可实现的最优标度，并为酉群上的高效随机游走提供了新工具。该结果在量子哈密顿量复杂性、纠缠结构以及量子协议设计等多个方向上具有潜在影响。

Abstract: We construct a constant-degree and constant-gap quantum expander on $n$ qubits where each unitary can be implemented by a depth-$1$ and 1D circuit of Pauli or CNOT gates. We provide two applications of this expander. First, we use it to construct a family of frustration-free 1D Hamiltonians whose ground states obey the entanglement-gap relation $S = Θ(Δ^{-1/2})$; this is believed to be optimal, but achieving it had been open. Second, we use it to provide a streaming protocol that tests for closeness to a class of 1D volume-law entangled states.
  Moreover, we extend our quantum expander to a constant-degree and constant-gap expander on the unitary group where each unitary is a single $T$ gate, a single $T^{\dagger}$ gate, or a depth-$1$ Clifford circuit. This implies that a random sequence of unitaries from the expander yields a gapped walk on a dense subgroup of the unitary group. This improves upon previous work by Bourgain and Gamburd which did not control the dependence of the gap on the dimension.

</details>


### [122] [Observable- and state-selective prethermalization and bounds on prethermal lifetimes](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.01606)
*C. L. Sriram,Soumya Kanti Pal,Lea F. Santos*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文揭示即使存在能量尺度分离，预热化（prethermalization）平台并非必然出现；其出现与否取决于哈密顿量、可观测量和初始状态的共同作用，并通过Loschmidt回声（Loschmidt echo）给出任意有界可观测量的预热化寿命下界。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 预热化描述了平衡前长期存在的中介态，可在实验可观测动力学中占主导地位。作者旨在澄清"能量尺度分离是否足以保证预热化平台的出现"这一基本问题，并揭示其选择性机制的根源。

Method: 作者证明能量尺度的谱分离虽然产生动力学时间尺度的层次结构，但并不自动保证预热化平台的涌现；通过分析接近完全置换对称（permutation-symmetric）极限的系统进行机理推导，并以长程相互作用自旋模型（long-range interacting spin model）加以说明。此外，作者严格证明了在预热化动力学与精确动力学均为幺正的条件下，Loschmidt回声给出了任意有界可观测量的预热化寿命下界。

Result: 在同一哈密顿量下，某些可观测量可展现预热化行为，而其他可观测量则直接弛豫到平衡态。预热化平台的出现不仅依赖哈密顿量，还取决于可观测量的选择和初始态的制备。

Conclusion: 预热化行为具有可观测量与初始态依赖性（selectivity），这修正了以往仅基于哈密顿量能标分离来预测预热化的认识；Loschmidt回声可作为预热化寿命的普适探针，为实验与理论研究提供新的诊断工具。

Abstract: Prethermalization describes long-lived intermediate regimes that precede equilibrium and can dominate experimentally accessible dynamics. Here, we show that a separation of spectral energy scales, despite giving rise to a hierarchy of dynamical timescales, does not by itself guarantee the appearance of a prethermal plateau. Under the same Hamiltonian, some observables may exhibit prethermal behavior, while others relax directly toward equilibrium. We uncover the mechanism governing this selectivity, showing that the emergence of a prethermal plateau depends not only on the Hamiltonian, but also on the observable and the initial state. Our results apply broadly to systems close to a fully permutation-symmetric limit and are illustrated with a long-range interacting spin model. We further prove that the Loschmidt echo provides a lower bound on the prethermal lifetime of any bounded observable whenever the prethermal and exact dynamics are unitary.

</details>

# math.RT [[Back]](#toc)

### [123] [Scary Wheels (and Super Shrubs)](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.00726)
*Andrei Neguţ,Alexander Tsymbaliuk*

Main category: math.RT

TL;DR: 本文在回路实现（loop realization）中证明了类型 $B,C,D$ 的量子仿射超代数（quantum affine superalgebras）的洗牌实现（shuffle realization），即构造这些代数与合适的双重洗牌代数（double shuffle algebra）之间的同构，并引入了推广 Feigin-Odesskii 轮条件（wheel conditions）的消失条件来描述后者。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 对量子仿射超代数的代数结构及其实现进行系统性理解，并填补类型 $B,C,D$ 在洗牌代数框架下尚未建立完整同构的空白，同时为研究带参数的箭图（quivers with parameters）提供统一的实现框架。

Method: 通过构造类型 $B,C,D$ 量子仿射超代数与适当定义的双重洗牌代数之间的同构来证明洗牌实现；利用推广自 Feigin-Odesskii 轮条件的消失条件显式描述洗牌代数，其中新的关键特征是出现了所谓"恐怖轮"（scary wheel）这一涉及 7 个变量的二阶消失条件；此外充分发展了类型 $A$（仿射和环面）超情形中"灌木"（shrubs）理论，并借助该理论定义类型 $B,C,D$ 的量子环面超代数（quantum toroidal superalgebras）。

Result: 成功证明了类型 $B,C,D$ 量子仿射超代数的洗牌实现定理，引入了恐怖轮这一新型消失条件，发展了超类型 $A$ 的灌木理论，并建立了一个可在带参数的箭图广泛框架下表述和证明洗牌实现的通用方法。

Conclusion: 该工作不仅完成了特定类型量子仿射超代数的洗牌实现证明，还提供了一套适用于广泛箭图参数的统一理论框架，为后续在环面 Calabi-Yau 三流形（toric Calabi-Yau threefolds）相关洗牌代数研究中应用这些结果奠定了基础。

Abstract: We prove the shuffle realization for quantum affine superalgebras of types $B,C,D$ in the loop realization, in that we construct an isomorphism between each of these algebras and a suitably defined double shuffle algebra. We explicitly describe the latter shuffle algebra using certain vanishing conditions that generalize the Feigin-Odesskii wheel conditions; a novel feature is the appearance of a so-called scary wheel, which is a particular order $2$ vanishing condition involving $7$ variables. Along the way, we fully develop the theory of shrubs in super types $A$ (affine and toroidal), which are important combinatorial tools in the study of the shuffle algebras associated to toric Calabi-Yau threefolds. Our techniques also allow us to define quantum toroidal superalgebras of types $B,C,D$. More importantly, we provide a general framework to formulate and prove shuffle realizations in the wide generality of quivers with parameters.

</details>


<div id='nlin.PS'></div>

# nlin.PS [[Back]](#toc)

### [124] [Chemotaxis-induced linear instabilities and pattern formation in a reaction-diffusion model](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.01159)
*Mintu Karmakar,Abhik Basu*

Main category: nlin.PS

TL;DR: 本文研究了具有扩散和趋化（chemotaxis）相互作用的空间扩展Selkov糖酵解模型中的线性不稳定性和斑图形成（pattern formation），发现趋化强度可作为一种非单调控制参数，调控不稳定性类型、波长选取、重入式斑图转变以及非线性形态（斑点和条纹）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有研究对趋化作用如何影响反应-扩散（reaction-diffusion）系统中的不稳定性选择和斑图形成机理理解不足，尤其是趋化参数在非线性斑图形态转变中的角色尚不明确。

Method: 通过线性稳定性分析（linear stability analysis）识别两种不同的分岔，并辅以二维非线性偏微分方程（PDE）的大规模直接数值模拟（DNS）进行验证，同时利用线性振幅方程（amplitude equations）分析条纹态的稳定性。

Result: 发现零波矢—有限频率的Hopf分岔导致空间均匀振荡，以及有限波矢的Turing不稳定性产生静止的非均匀斑图；选取波矢随趋化参数非单调变化，且当趋化较强且两物种相互吸引或排斥时会出现波矢发散的反常不稳定性；DNS证实了上述不稳定性及重入行为，并揭示出稳态条纹以及斑点与条纹之间的形态转变。

Conclusion: 趋化作用可充当反应-扩散系统中不稳定性选取、波长选取、重入式斑图形成及非线性斑图形态的非单调控制参数，且斑点—条纹转变源于超越线性稳定性的非线性效应。

Abstract: We study linear instabilities and pattern formation in a spatially extended Selkov model for glycolysis with diffusion and chemotactic interactions between two species. Linear stability analysis reveals two distinct bifurcations: a zero-wavevector, finite-frequency Hopf bifurcation leading to spatially homogeneous oscillations, and a finite-wavevector Turing instability with a saddle-node bifurcation leading to stationary inhomogeneous patterns. The selected wavevector, $k_c$, depends sensitively on the nature and strength of chemotaxis and varies nonmonotonically with the chemotaxis parameters, allowing re-entrant transitions between homogeneous and patterned states. For sufficiently strong chemotaxis, when the two species mutually attract or mutually repel each other, an unusual instability emerges in which the preferred wavevector diverges as a finite threshold in chemotactic strength is approached. We complement the linear analysis with extensive direct numerical simulations (DNS) of the nonlinear partial differential equations in two dimensions. The DNS confirm the predicted instabilities and their nonmonotonic and re-entrant character, and also reveal spatially uniform oscillatory states for suitable parameters. The stationary patterns include spots and, unexpectedly, stable stripes. We further uncover transitions between spot and stripe morphologies controlled by chemotactic strength and diffusivity ratios. The stability of the striped states is consistent with linear amplitude equations, whereas the spot-stripe transition is attributed to nonlinear effects beyond linear stability. Our results demonstrate that chemotaxis can act as a nonmonotonic control parameter for instability selection, wavelength selection, re-entrant pattern formation, and nonlinear pattern morphology in reaction-diffusion systems.

</details>

### [125] [Odd-viscosity-induced instability in shear flows](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.01566)
*Yonatan Messica,Igor Gornyi,Dmitri B. Gutman*

Main category: physics.flu-dyn

TL;DR: 本文研究了奇黏度（odd viscosity）这一无耗散黏度分量对流体动力学稳定性的影响，发现尽管奇黏度不耗散能量，却能通过关键层（critical layer）中奇黏性力的放大作用，将剪切流中由非正常动力学（non-normal dynamics）引起的瞬态增长转化为指数增长的不稳定模式，从而在平面泊肃叶流（plane Poiseuille flow）中产生传统流体中不存在的新失稳机制。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 奇黏度是黏度张量中的无耗散分量，存在于打破时间反演对称性（time-reversal symmetry）的流体中。以往研究表明其守恒能量的特性，但其对流体动力学稳定性的潜在影响尚未被系统探讨，本文旨在建立奇黏度与流体不稳定性之间的关联。

Method: 作者推导了平面泊肃叶流的奇黏度推广的奥-索-斯夸尔方程（Orr–Sommerfeld–Squire equations），分析了奇黏性扰动下的线性稳定性；同时从一般非正常动力系统的角度出发，证明了一个能量守恒的扰动只有在原系统支持瞬态增长（transient growth）时才能使系统失稳，从而建立了从瞬态增长到指数不稳定性的统一理论框架。

Result: 研究发现了一种新失稳模式，该模式首先出现在展向（spanwise）扰动中，并通过关键层中奇黏性力的放大扩展到倾斜模态（oblique modes）。机理在于奇黏度在壁法向速度（wall-normal velocity）与涡量（vorticity）之间提供反馈，将传统流体中升力机制（lift-up mechanism）产生的瞬态增长转化为自持的指数增长模式。

Conclusion: 研究建立了瞬态增长、非正常性（non-normality）与无耗散力诱导的不稳定性之间的直接联系，证明奇黏度可在不违反能量守恒的前提下定性改变流体稳定性特性，其结论的适用范围超出了奇黏度流体力学本身。

Abstract: Odd viscosity is a nondissipative component of the viscosity tensor that arises in fluids with broken time-reversal symmetry. Despite conserving energy, we show that odd viscosity can qualitatively alter hydrodynamic stability by generating exponentially growing modes that are absent in conventional fluids. For plane Poiseuille flow, we derive the odd-viscous generalization of the Orr--Sommerfeld--Squire equations and find a new instability that first emerges for spanwise perturbations and extends to oblique modes through the amplification of odd-viscous forces in critical layers. The instability originates from the non-normal dynamics of shear flows: conventional fluids support transiently growing disturbances through the lift-up mechanism, while odd viscosity provides a feedback between wall-normal velocity and vorticity that converts this transient growth into a self-sustaining exponentially growing mode. More generally, we show that an energy-conserving perturbation can destabilize a non-normal dynamical system only when the unperturbed system supports transient growth. Our results establish a direct connection between transient growth, non-normality, and instability induced by nondissipative forces, with implications extending beyond odd-viscous hydrodynamics.

</details>

# physics.optics [[Back]](#toc)

### [126] [Strong suppression of the photonic density of states in three-dimensional disordered silicon networks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.00432)
*Abraham Aguilar Uribe,Francisco Hernández Alejandre,Mattis Reisner,Geoffroy Aubry,Luis S. Froufe-Pérez,Marian Florescu,Frank Scheffold*

Main category: physics.optics

TL;DR: 该研究通过直接激光写制版（direct laser-writing）和先进材料加工技术，用高折射率硅制备了自均匀非晶陀螺网络（self-uniform amorphous gyroid networks），并利用偏振分辨透射光谱（polarization-resolved transmission spectroscopy）在中红外波段观测到强透射抑制，结合大规模数值模拟证实了非晶三维介电材料中深光子赝隙（photonic pseudogap）的存在。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 光带隙不仅可以在晶态介电材料中打开，也可能存在于非晶结构中。自均匀非晶陀螺网络被提出为有前景的无序光子结构，但缺乏直接的实验验证，因此需要将该结构制备出来并从实验上确认其光子带隙的形成。

Method: 将直接激光写制版光刻（direct laser-writing lithography）与先进材料加工结合，将非晶陀螺网络结构制备为高折射率硅材料，并开发偏振分辨透射光谱方法以区分弹道（ballistic）与多次散射（multiply scattered）传输的贡献，进而识别底层传输机制；同时辅以大规模数值模拟。

Result: 中红外波段的谱学测量揭示了显著的透射极小值；观测到漫透射（diffuse transmission）的强烈抑制，直接实验证实了与光子态密度（photonic density of states）急剧降低相关的常规漫传输失效；结合大规模数值模拟结果，确立了非晶三维介电材料中深光子赝隙的存在。

Conclusion: 该项工作为非晶三维介电材料中的深光子赝隙提供了直接实验证据，并为观察无序诱导的局域化现象（disorder-induced localization phenomena），包括光的安德森局域化（Anderson localization of light），开辟了新的机遇。

Abstract: Photonic bandgaps can open not only in crystalline dielectric materials but also in amorphous structures. Self-uniform amorphous gyroid networks have been proposed as promising disordered photonic architectures. Motivated by numerical studies, we combine direct laser-writing lithography with advanced materials processing to fabricate these structures from high-refractive-index silicon. Spectroscopic measurements at mid-infrared wavelengths reveal pronounced transmission minima. To investigate the formation of photonic band gaps in amorphous structures, we develop polarization-resolved transmission spectroscopy that separates ballistic and multiply scattered contributions, enabling direct identification of the underlying transport regimes. We observe a strong suppression of diffuse transmission, providing direct experimental evidence for a breakdown of conventional diffuse transport associated with a strongly reduced photonic density of states. Together with large-scale numerical simulations, our results establish the presence of a deep photonic pseudogap in an amorphous three-dimensional dielectric material and open new opportunities for observing disorder-induced localization phenomena, including Anderson localization of light.

</details>


### [127] [Spatio-temporal coherent molding and retrieval of pulsed signals in optical waveguides](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.00856)
*Nika Durishvili,Ramaz Khomeriki,Vakhtang Jandieri,Douglas H. Werner,Daniel Erni,Jamal Berakdar*

Main category: physics.optics

TL;DR: 本文提出一种从时空演化态中高保真度提取相位加密输入信号的概念，通过在耦合波导阵列中施加符合检索概念的局域相位移动来实现，全波电磁模拟验证了该方案的有效性，可应用于超快相干编码与解码。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 耦合波导阵列可提高信息密度并支持编码，但包含多个波导模式频率的短脉冲在相干传播过程中相对相位发生混合，导致尚无法从传播后的信号中恢复编码输入态，因此需要一种高保真的相位加密信号检索方法。

Method: 作者提出一个用于从时空传播态中提取相位加密输入信号的概念，其实现方案为耦合波导阵列，检索机制通过符合所识别检索概念的局域相位移动（local phase shifts）来达成；随后对宽带光信号在介质耦合波导中的传播进行了三维全波电磁仿真验证。

Result: 三维全波电磁仿真结果表明，该方案能够以高保真度恢复信息，证实了所提检索机制的有效性与可行性。

Conclusion: 该研究指向潜在应用，例如对脉冲序列上印刻信息的超快相干编码与解码（ultrafast coherent coding and decoding）。

Abstract: Optical waveguides are key elements for high fidelity, long distance optical communications. Coupled waveguide arrays allow for higher information density, steerting the propagation direction, and for encoding information. However, due to the mixing of relative phases for short pulses containing multiple waveguide-mode frequencies, a process for retrieving an encoded input state once these signals undergo coherent propagation remains elusive. A concept is presented to extract with high fidelity the phase-encrypted input signal from spatio-temporal propagated states. As a realization, an array of coupled waveguides is suggested with the retrieval mechanism being realized by local phase shifts that comply with the identified retrieval concept. Three dimensional full-wave electromagnetic simulations for broadband optical signals in coupled dielectric waveguides confirm the validity of the scheme and the high fidelity of information retrieval pointing to potential applications, for instance in ultrafast coherent coding and decoding of information imprinted on pulse sequences.

</details>


### [128] [Topological charge switching in trapped polariton condensates](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.00979)
*Jaewon Kim,Hyun Gyu Song,Daegwang Choi,Yong-Hoon Cho*

Main category: physics.optics

TL;DR: 本文在光子晶体中通过激子极化激元（polariton）的非线性蓝移效应，实现了对束缚型连续谱束缚态（BIC）极化激元凝聚体中拓扑荷的主动切换，仅通过调节激发功率即可实现单模选择。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 光子系统中的拓扑荷（topological charge）为光信息处理提供了鲁棒自由度，但光子晶体平板中连续谱束缚态（BIC）所携带的拓扑荷受拓扑保护，难以主动重构，这构成了根本性的挑战。

Method: 在光子晶体中通过空间调制形成多个受限态，每个态携带不同的拓扑荷；利用极化激元蓝移（polariton blueshift）将狄拉克带隙（Dirac bandgap）内被束缚的凝聚体驱入泄漏带，从而仅通过调节激发功率即可实现单模选择。

Result: 成功演示了俘获BIC极化激元凝聚体中拓扑荷的切换，实现了仅通过激发功率调控即可进行的单模选择。

Conclusion: 该工作利用极化激元非线性，为光学可控调节拓扑荷提供了平台，拓展了拓扑荷在光信息处理中的可操作性。

Abstract: Topological charges in photonic systems provide a robust degree of freedom with direct applications in optical information processing. Bound states in the continuum (BICs) in photonic crystal slabs inherently carry such quantized topological charges, yet they are topologically protected, making active reconfiguration fundamentally challenging. Here, we demonstrate topological charge switching in trapped BIC polariton condensates in a photonic crystal, where spatial modulation of the photonic crystals supports multiple confined states, each carrying a distinct charge. Trapped condensates within the Dirac bandgap are expelled into the leaky band via polariton blueshift, enabling single-mode selection by solely tuning the excitation power. This work offers a platform for optically controlling topological charges by harnessing polariton nonlinearity.

</details>


### [129] [Exceptional Points in Photonics: From Non-Hermitian Physics to Applications](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.01239)
*Fan Zhang,Nikolay Solodovchenko,Dmitrii N. Maksimov,Xuchen Wang,Mingzhao Song,Filippo Capolino,C. T. Chan,Andrey Bogdanov*

Main category: physics.optics

TL;DR: 本文是一篇综述论文，为开放光子系统中反常点（exceptional points, EPs）物理提供了统一框架，系统区分了不同类型的反常简并，涵盖其谱拓扑、环绕动力学、高阶反常结构及其在各种光子技术中的应用。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 开放光子系统为非厄米物理（non-Hermitian physics）提供了多功能平台，而非对称哈密顿量中的反常点作为谱异点与可控本征态转换的关键机制，需要统一的分类框架来整合分散于不同算子与系统中的研究成果。

Method: 该综述按底层算子、谱变量、边界条件和实验可观测观测量，系统分类了各类反常简并，包括哈密顿量反常点、伴随散射零点的吸收反常点、实频率散射矩阵与Jones矩阵反常点、Bloch与Floquet反常点，以及开放量子系统中的Liouvillian反常点。

Result: 文中综述了反常点的谱拓扑、静态与动态环绕、高阶反常结构及其与连续谱中束缚态（bound states in the continuum）的共存现象，并梳理了其在传感、激光、相干吸收、方向性散射、偏振与波前控制、非线性光学、光存储、非互易光子学和量子光子学等领域的应用。

Conclusion: 作者批判性地评估了基于反常点的光子技术当前存在的局限性与实践挑战，重点关注鲁棒性、噪声、可扩展性及可实验测量的性能指标，并指出了未来发展方向。

Abstract: Open photonic systems provide a versatile platform for non-Hermitian physics, enabling control over complex spectra, transport, and light-matter interactions. Exceptional points (EPs), at which eigenvalues and eigenvectors coalesce and the governing operator becomes defective, play a central role because they combine branch-point spectral topology, nonanalytic perturbative response, and controllable eigenstate conversion. This Review provides a unified framework for EP photonics by systematically distinguishing exceptional degeneracies according to the underlying operator, spectral variable, boundary conditions, and experimentally accessible observables. We discuss Hamiltonian EPs, absorbing EPs associated with scattering zeros, real-frequency scattering-matrix and Jones-matrix EPs, Bloch and Floquet EPs, and Liouvillian EPs in open quantum systems. We review their spectral topology, static and dynamical encircling, higher-order exceptional structures, and coexistence with bound states in the continuum, together with applications in sensing, lasing, coherent absorption, directional scattering, polarization and wavefront control, nonlinear optics, optical storage, nonreciprocal photonics, and quantum photonics. We also critically assess the current limitations, practical challenges, and future perspectives of EP-based photonic technologies, with particular attention to robustness, noise, scalability, and experimentally measurable performance.

</details>

# gr-qc [[Back]](#toc)

### [130] [Guiding center quantization of a quantum Hall analog of Hawking radiation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.00158)
*Rodrigo Andrade e Silva,Ted Jacobson*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 本文重新审视了量子霍尔模拟霍金辐射现象：在四极电势作用下占据半平面的电子费米海中，手性边缘模产生模拟霍金辐射。研究表明，该现象可由经典引导中心理论（guiding center theory）的量子化精确描述，其量子动力学发生在非交换平面（non-commutative plane）上，电子在半平面内的局域化约束要求其在边缘方向全域延展，这一运动学约束直接导致向边缘两端传播的辐射。该辐射相对于实验室时间而言是热性的，对应着模拟闵可夫斯基时空中的模拟安鲁辐射（analog Unruh radiation）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 作者旨在厘清量子霍尔系统中模拟霍金辐射现象的物理本质和精确机制，回答该辐射是否可被经典引导中心理论的量子化框架所精确捕获，以及其真实物理对应物是霍金辐射还是安鲁辐射。

Method: 作者重新求解了四极电势下的半平面电子费米海问题，采用引导中心理论的量子化方法：将电子的回旋运动（gyroscopic motion）进行粗粒化，仅分辨漂移运动，从而在非交换平面上建立量子动力学。关键推导基于半平面约束与边缘方向全域延展之间的运动学矛盾。

Result: 该辐射沿边缘远离原点双向传播，并被证明在实验室时间下呈热谱。进一步发现，实验室时间（至多相差一个常数因子）等同于模拟闵可夫斯基时空中的boost角，从而表明该效应实际上对应于模拟安鲁辐射而非严格意义上的霍金辐射。

Conclusion: 量子化的引导中心理论能精确描述该模拟辐射现象，其根源在于非交换几何带来的运动学约束。该辐射的热谱在实验室系下成立，实质上是模拟安鲁辐射的体现，这一认识澄清了此前将此类效应归为模拟霍金辐射的表述偏差。

Abstract: We revisit the quantum Hall analog of Hawking radiation in which a Fermi sea of electrons occupying half of a plane, subjected to a quadrupolar electric potential, gives rise to analog Hawking radiation of chiral edge modes. We show that the phenomenon is accurately captured by quantization of the classical guiding center theory, where the gyroscopic motion of the electrons is coarse grained and only the drift motion is resolved. The quantum dynamics takes place on a non-commutative plane, which requires an electron localized in the half-plane to be supported everywhere along the edge direction. This kinematical constraint on the quantum state leads directly to the radiation, which propagates in both directions away from the origin along the edge. The radiation is thermal with respect to laboratory time, which is equal (up to a constant factor) to the boost angle in the analog Minkowski spacetime in which the chiral edge modes propagate, so in fact it corresponds to analog Unruh radiation.

</details>


### [131] [Gravitational waves from core collapse of rotating very-massive stars: 3D numerical relativity computation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.00200)
*Alan Tsz-Lok Lam,Masaru Shibata,Sho Fujibayashi*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 该研究通过二维轴对称与三维相对论数值模拟，研究了约200M⊙至1100M⊙的大质量恒星核心坍缩为黑洞的过程，发现当黑洞无量纲自旋超过0.8时，会形成质量超过黑洞质量10%的巨大吸积盘，此类盘易发生非轴对称形变并激发频率约10-50 Hz的引力波暴，可被爱因斯坦望远镜（Einstein Telescope）和宇宙探索者（Cosmic Explorer）在数Gpc距离上探测到。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探索旋转大质量恒星核心坍缩为黑洞过程中的引力波辐射特征，特别是非轴对称过程产生的引力波信号，以评估未来引力波探测器对这些事件的探测能力。

Method: 采用带旋转的大质量恒星核心坍缩模型，核心质量约为200、300、500和1100太阳质量（M⊙），使用二维轴对称和三维（3D）数值相对论（numerical relativity）模拟系统研究坍缩过程及随后黑洞周围的物质分布和动力学演化。

Result: 当形成的黑洞无量纲自旋（dimensionless spin）超过0.8时，周围会稳定形成大质量且致密的吸积盘，其质量超过黑洞质量的10%；这些盘易受非轴对称形变驱动，产生频率约为10-50 Hz的引力波暴（gravitational-wave bursts），可被爱因斯坦望远镜和宇宙探索者在数Gpc距离处探测到；轴对称坍缩产生的引力波信号振幅较低且频率较高，不同于非轴对称不稳定性产生的信号。

Conclusion: 旋转超大质量恒星核心坍缩为非轴对称吸积盘不稳定性提供了潜在的引力波源，其信号特征（约10-50 Hz）落在未来第三代地面引力波探测器（如爱因斯坦望远镜和宇宙探索者）的灵敏度范围内，使此类事件具有可探测前景。

Abstract: We numerically study the collapse of rotating, very-massive stellar cores with masses of $\approx 200$, $300$, $500$, and $1100M_\odot$ into black holes using both axisymmetric and three-dimensional (3D) numerical relativity. Our results indicate that when the dimensionless spin of the resulting black hole exceeds 0.8, a massive disk consistently forms around it. These massive, compact disks, carrying more than about 10\% of the black hole's mass, are prone to non-axisymmetric deformations that trigger gravitational-wave bursts with frequencies around 10-50 Hz. Such waves could be detected by the Einstein Telescope and Cosmic Explorer, even from sources a few Gpc away. We also summarize the gravitational-wave signals from axisymmetric collapse, which tend to have lower amplitudes and higher frequencies than those caused by non-axisymmetric instabilities.

</details>


### [132] [Unimodular Gravity with Arbitrary Diffusion Function: A Dynamical System Reconstruction Approach](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.00201)
*Gabriel Gómez,Guillermo Palma,Norman Cruz*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 本文在单模引力（unimodular gravity）框架内，采用动力学系统重建方法研究宇宙学扩散模型。通过将扩散扇区的对数斜率视为可逆动力学变量，作者系统重建了扩散函数，识别出新的物质-扩散标度解和纯扩散主导配置，后者无需显式宇宙学常数即可驱动晚期加速膨胀。进一步建立的基于扩散斜率动力学演化的重建形式体系，允许构造在宇宙历史中连接不同扩散区域的轨迹。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 旨在为单模引力中的扩散宇宙学提供系统的动力学系统重建框架，从而在无需引入显式宇宙学常数项的条件下解释晚期宇宙加速膨胀，并建立可行的扩散宇宙学系统的分类与构造方法。

Method: 将扩散扇区的对数斜率（logarithmic slope of the diffusion sector）设为可逆动力学变量，通过相空间（phase-space）结构系统重建扩散函数；分析物理上允许的定点（fixed points），并将局部行为推广至完整宇宙学演化；进一步基于扩散斜率的动力学演化建立重建形式体系，以构造连接不同扩散区域之间的轨迹。

Result: 识别出与幂律扩散扇区相关的新型物质-扩散标度解（matter–diffusion scaling solutions），以及能够驱动晚期加速膨胀的纯扩散主导构型；建立了从相空间动力学直接构造和分类单模引力中可行扩散宇宙学的系统框架。

Conclusion: 研究为单模引力中可行扩散宇宙学的系统构造与分类提供了完整框架，确立了从相空间动力学出发直接构建扩散宇宙学的标准化流程，并为超越渐近定点结构的全局宇宙学行为提供了探索途径。

Abstract: We investigate cosmological diffusion models in unimodular gravity within a dynamical systems reconstruction framework. By treating the logarithmic slope of the diffusion sector as an invertible dynamical variable, the diffusion function can be systematically reconstructed from the phase-space structure of the cosmological evolution. Under these conditions, we determine the physically admissible fixed points of the system, identifying novel matter--diffusion scaling solutions associated with power-law diffusion sectors, as well as purely diffusion-dominated configurations capable of driving late-time accelerated expansion without requiring an explicit cosmological constant term. The local behavior around the fixed points is then extended to the full cosmological evolution, providing a framework to explore the global implications of diffusion cosmologies. Beyond the asymptotic fixed-point structure, we further develop a reconstruction formalism based on the dynamical evolution of the diffusion slope, allowing for trajectories interpolating between different diffusion regimes during the cosmic history. Our results establish a systematic framework for constructing and classifying viable diffusion cosmologies in unimodular gravity directly from the phase-space dynamics.

</details>


### [133] [Ghost-free higher-gradient Newtonian gravity from the Second Law of Thermodynamics](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.00317)
*M. Pszota,P. Ván*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 本文提出从热力学第二定律推导高阶修正牛顿引力场方程的新途径，消除Ostrogradsky不稳定性，并给出Yukawa型修正的振幅求和规则约束。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 高阶修正的牛顿引力虽可消除点质量奇异性，但其拉格朗日动力学受Ostrogradsky不稳定性困扰，需要寻找无该不稳定性的替代推导框架。

Method: 将热力学第二定律应用于具有弱非局域状态空间的自引力流体，导出一阶弛豫方程，该方程为梯度流，以熵密度为Lyapunov函数；同时推导压力张量、能量流以及再现牛顿力定律的精确恒等式。

Result: 势函数满足一阶弛豫梯度流方程，弛豫谱在熵凹条件下对所有波数负定；推导出无变分原理所能产生的耗散自由体粘滞压力交叉耦合；Yukawa型修正受振幅求和规则∑iαi=-1约束，实验室逆平方律测试将最大内禀长度限制在4×10⁻⁵ m以下，并将复根区域识别为经典一致的Lee–Wick类扇区。

Conclusion: 热力学推导路径在构造上消除了Ostrogradsky不稳定性，为高阶引力修正提供了无奇异性的经典一致框架，并给出了可检验的实验约束。

Abstract: Higher-gradient modifications of Newtonian gravity remove the point-mass singularity, but their Lagrangian dynamics is haunted by the Ostrogradsky instability. We show that the same field equations follow from the Second Law of thermodynamics applied to a self-gravitating fluid with a weakly nonlocal state space, and that on this route the instability is absent by construction: the potential obeys a first-order relaxation equation that is a gradient flow, whose Lyapunov function is the entropy density, and whose relaxation spectrum is negative definite at every wavenumber precisely when the entropy is concave. The derivation also yields the pressure tensor and the energy current, an exact identity that reproduces Newton's force law without further input, and a dissipation-free cross-coupling to the bulk viscous pressure that no variational principle can produce. Regularity constrains the Yukawa-type corrections by an amplitude sum rule, $\sum_iα_i=-1$, so that laboratory tests of the inverse-square law bound the largest internal length below $4\times10^{-5}$~m, and identify the complex-root regime as a classically consistent Lee--Wick-like sector.

</details>


### [134] [A Fast and Scalable Transformer Pipeline for Binary Black Hole Detection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.00339)
*Chayan Chatterjee,Abigail Petulante,Haowei Fu,Yang Hu,Roy Lau,Karan Jani*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 本文提出 CASTOR，一种基于 Transformer 的双探测器引力波符合搜索（coincident search）管线，用于检测先进 LIGO 探测器的双黑洞引力波信号，支持通过时间滑移（time slides）廉价估计误报率（false-alarm rate），在 MLGWSC-1 基准数据集和约五个月的 O3b 真实数据上表现优异。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 未来十年致密双星并合（compact-binary coalescence）探测率预计将显著增长，需要开发快速、稳健且可扩展的引力波搜索替代方案，以取代传统的匹配滤波（matched filtering）方法。Transformer 模型虽在自然语言和音频处理领域引发革命，但其在引力波天文学中的应用仍基本处于空白状态。

Method: 引入 CASTOR，一种基于 Transformer 架构的符合搜索管线，用于从先进 LIGO 双探测器数据中检测双黑洞引力波信号。模型的关键特性是允许通过时间滑移廉价地估计误报率，无需重复评估神经网络。在 MLGWSC-1 挑战数据集以及约五个月的 O3b 真实观测应变数据上进行评估，并与另一 Transformer 架构 GW-Whisper（对 OpenAI 音频基础模型的领域适配）进行对比基准测试。

Result: 在基准数据集测试中，CASTOR 位列最灵敏的机器学习管线之一，并成功恢复了 GWTC-3 目录中训练范围内的大部分高置信事件。与 GW-Whisper 相比，CASTOR 在灵敏度上大幅领先，并将背景估计的计算成本降低约 20 倍。

Conclusion: CASTOR 为深度学习引力波搜索和实证背景估计提供了一种高度实用且可扩展的方案，适用于未来观测运行。与适配自音频领域的 Transformer 模型相比，领域专业化架构在引力波搜索中表现显著更优。

Abstract: With the projected increase in the detection rate of compact-binary coalescences in the coming decade, there is critical need to develop fast, robust, and scalable alternatives to matched filtering for gravitational-wave searches. Transformer models have revolutionized natural language and audio processing but their application to gravitational-wave astronomy is still largely unexplored. In this work, we introduce \castor, a transformer-based coincident search pipeline for detecting binary black hole gravitational-wave signals from Advanced LIGO detectors. One of the major features of our model is that it allows the false-alarm rate to be estimated via time slides cheaply without requiring repeated evaluations of the neural network. We evaluate \castor\ on datasets from the Machine-Learning Gravitational-Wave Search Challenge (MLGWSC-1) and on approximately five months of real O3b observing strain. When tested on benchmark datasets, \castor\ ranks among the most sensitive machine-learning pipelines and successfully recovers the majority of confident events from the GWTC-3 catalog that lie within its training range. We also benchmark \castor\ against another transformer architecture, GW-Whisper, a domain-adaptation of OpenAI's audio foundation model. We find that \castor\ substantially outperforms the repurposed audio model in sensitivity and also reduces the computational cost of background estimation by a factor of 20. Our results demonstrate a highly practical, scalable approach for deep-learning gravitational wave searches and empirical background estimation for future observing runs.

</details>


### [135] [Positive energy from timelike singularities](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.00423)
*Fernando Ruiz Ruiz*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 本文通过引入时间型调控截断边界处理奇异点，基于霍金与霍洛维茨提出的在壳哈密顿作用量定义引力能量，计算了具有时间平移不变性和类时奇异性的多种爱因斯坦方程解的引力能量，发现奇异点贡献的能量有限且为正，使得总引力能量为正。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 尽管霍金和霍洛维茨在三十年前就提出了经典物理作用的在壳哈密顿量（on-shell Hamiltonian）作为引力能量的定义，且该定义与Abbott-Deser和ADM表述一致，但将其应用于含类时奇异性的时空时存在困难，需要一种系统方法来评估这类时空的能量。

Method: 采用霍金-霍洛维茨在壳哈密顿量定义引力能量；针对类时奇异点，引入一个包围奇异点的时间型（timelike）调控截断边界，该边界与良定义的恒定时间片层叶状结构相容；所有计算在有限调控参数下进行，并在最终能量表达式中令该参数趋于零。

Result: 在所有考察的情形中，奇异点对能量的贡献有限且为正，其数值依赖于奇异点周围的几何结构，并使得总引力能量为正；在考察的解中，仅有不含奇异点的AdS孤子（AdS soliton）具有相对于AdS空间的负引力能量；带负质量的AdS和Schwarzschild黑洞分别具有与AdS和Minkowski空间相同的能量。

Conclusion: 该研究为含类时奇异性的渐近AdS与渐近平直时空提供了能量计算的可行框架，结果表明奇异点贡献正能量，从而支持总引力能量为正的结论。

Abstract: The energy of various asymptotically AdS and asymptotically flat solutions to the Einstein equations with time-translational invariance and a timelike singularity is computed, spaces with Kasner timelike singularities and black holes with negative masses among them. The definition used for gravitational energy is the on-shell Hamiltonian of the physical classical action proposed by Hawking and Horowitz thirty years ago and which agrees with the Abbott-Deser and ADM expressions. To deal with the singularity, a timelike regulating cut-off boundary surrounding the singularity compatible with a well-defined foliation in constant-time slices is introduced, so calculations are performed at a finite value of the regulating parameter, which is set to zero in the final expression for the energy. In all instances considered, the energy contribution from the singularity is finite and positive, its value depends on the geometry around the singularity and is such that the total gravitational energy is positive. Of the solutions reviewed, only the AdS soliton (which has no singularity) has negative gravitational energy relative to AdS space. The AdS and the Schwarzschild black holes with negative masses have respectively the same energies as AdS and Minkowski spaces.

</details>


### [136] [Dynamical evolution of quantum mutual information in Schwarzschild spacetime](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.00485)
*Guosen Ma,Fang Rao,Jingjing Hua,Xiaofen Huang*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 本文研究了Schwarzschild黑洞背景下三模高斯态（Gaussian states）基于Rényi-2熵的量子互信息（quantum mutual information）的动力学演化，发现物理不可达互信息随Hawking温度增加并趋于饱和，而物理可达互信息呈现非单调的温度依赖，并建立了子系统间互信息分布的若干约束关系。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 量子互信息是刻画量子系统间关联的基本量，但此前在弯曲时空（如Schwarzschild黑洞背景）中对其演化行为的研究尚不充分。现有的高斯纠缠（Gaussian entanglement）和转向（steering）在弯曲时空中表现出单调退化，量子互信息是否呈现不同行为值得探究。

Method: 以三模高斯态为研究对象，通过Rényi-2熵定义量子互信息，研究其在Schwarzschild黑洞背景中的动态演化。分析物理可达与物理不可达部分互信息随Hawking温度的变化，并推导用于刻画子系统间互信息分布的约束关系。

Result: 物理不可达的互信息随Hawking温度增大而增加并最终饱和；物理可达的互信息则呈现非单调温度依赖，先上升、达到峰值后下降并趋于有限渐近值。此外建立了若干约束关系以刻画子系统间互信息的分配规律。

Conclusion: 物理可达互信息的非单调行为不同于弯曲时空中高斯纠缠与转向的单调退化，表明互信息提供了额外的不寻常的量子关联信息，有助于深入理解Schwarzschild时空下连续变量系统量子关联的量子性。

Abstract: Quantum mutual information is a fundamental quantity for characterizing correlations among quantum systems. In this work, we investigate the dynamic evolution of the quantum mutual information based on Rényi-2 entropy for three-mode Gaussian states in the background of a Schwarzschild black hole. We find that the physically inaccessible mutual information increases with Hawking temperature and eventually saturates, while the physically accessible mutual information exhibits nonmonotonic temperature dependence: it first rises, reaches a peak, then declines toward a finite asymptotic value. This nonmonotonic behavior differs from the monotonic degradation typically observed for Gaussian entanglement and steering in curved spacetime. Furthermore, we establish several constraint relations governing the distribution of mutual information among subsystems. These findings contribute to understanding the quantumness of quantum correlations for continuous variable in Schwarzschild spacetime.

</details>


### [137] [Performance of quantum imaginarity in black hole spacetime](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.00573)
*Shiyan Li,Yimei Yuan,Xiaofen Huang*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 本文研究了三方量子混合态在Schwarzschild时空中的量子虚部性（quantum imaginarity）动力学演化行为，发现Hawking辐射会退化这一量子资源，且虚部性在演化早期出现陡变后缓慢趋近稳定值，并推导了表征虚部性跨Rindler视界非局域再分配的权衡关系。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 量子虚部性作为量子信息处理中的基本量子资源，其理解尚不充分。该研究旨在填补量子虚部性在黑洞时空（black hole spacetime）这一弯曲时空背景下动力学行为的空白，从而拓展对量子资源在引力环境下的行为认知。

Method: 作者在Schwarzschild时空中构建三方量子混合态模型，系统考察量子虚部性随Hawking辐射演化的动力学轨迹，并推导了描述虚部性在Rindler视界之间重新分布的权衡关系（trade-off relations）。

Result: 研究发现Hawking辐射会退化量子虚部性资源，与纠缠（entanglement）和相干性（coherence）在Hawking效应下的行为一致；虚部性在Hawking辐射早期阶段发生急剧变化，此后缓慢变化并渐近趋于稳态值；所导出的权衡关系刻画了虚部性跨Rindler视界的非局域再分配特征。

Conclusion: 该研究为理解黑洞时空中量子资源的行为提供了更深层次的洞察，表明量子虚部性在弯曲时空中的演化规律在定性与纠缠及相干性类似，但具有独特的早期陡变特征，有助于推进量子信息与引力物理交叉领域的发展。

Abstract: Quantum imaginarity is a fundamental quantum resource essential for quantum information processing. In this paper, we investigate the dynamical evolution behavior of quantum imaginarity for tripartite quantum mixed states in Schwarzschild spacetime. We find that Hawking radiation degrades the quantum resource of imaginarity, consistent with the behavior of entanglement and coherence under the Hawking effect. Interestingly, imaginarity exhibits a sharp change in the early stage of Hawking radiation, after which it varies slowly and asymptotically approaches a steady value. We further derive several trade-off relations that characterize the nonlocal redistribution of imaginarity across Rindler horizons. The findings of this study contribute to a deeper understanding of quantum resource behaviors in black hole spacetimes.

</details>


### [138] [Directional Response Optimization through Linear Recombination of Time-Delay Interferometry Channels in Space-based Gravitational Wave Detection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.00589)
*Heng-Sen Jiao,Jing-Rui Zhang,Hong-Bo Jin,Yun-Long Zhang*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 本文针对空间引力波探测器（如LISA、太极和天琴）的时间延迟干涉（time-delay interferometry, TDI）组合，构建了频域响应矩阵并通过对目标与抑制区域响应比的最大化（广义特征值问题）求解最优权重，在毫赫兹频段实现了天区零点和区域对比度增强。方法适用于近单色源（如银河系致密双白矮星双星）的定向窄带搜索。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 空间引力波探测器采用时间延迟干涉（TDI）消除不等臂配置下激光频率噪声，但不同TDI可观测量的天区响应存在差异，需在线性组合候选通道时增强某一目标天区平均响应的同时抑制另一区域的响应，以支持定向搜索。

Method: 构建TDI组合的频域响应矩阵，分别对目标天区和被抑制天区求平均，形成区域响应比值；通过求解最大化这一比值的广义特征值问题（generalized eigenvalue problem）获得最优线性组合权重。

Result: 在毫赫兹频段，以A、E、T通道、Sagnac组合α、β、γ以及16链路TDI为例，在所选频率附近可实现天区零点和大区域对比度，特征值ρ从较小基的O(10)到较大集合的O(10^2)量级。目标-抑制比（Target-to-Suppression Ratio, TSR）在设计频率处达到峰值并随频率偏移快速衰减。

Conclusion: 所提优化权重向量本质上是窄带的，适用于围绕选定频率和天区方向的定向搜索，尤其适合毫赫兹频段中如已分辨的银河系致密双白矮星双星等近单色（nearly monochromatic）引力波源。

Abstract: Space-based gravitational-wave detectors such as LISA, Taiji, and TianQin employ time-delay interferometry (TDI) to cancel laser-frequency noise for unequal-arm constellations. Since different TDI observables exhibit distinct sky responses, a linear combination of candidate channels can enhance the average response over one sky region while suppressing that of another. We construct a frequency-domain response matrix for TDI combinations, average it across target and suppressed sky regions, and derive the optimal weights via a generalized eigenvalue problem that maximizes the ratio between these two regional responses. At millihertz frequencies, examples with the $A$, $E$, and $T$ channels, the Sagnac combinations $α$, $β$, and $γ$, and 16-links TDI show that a sky-region null and a large regional contrast are possible near the chosen frequency, with eigenvalues $ρ$ ranging from $\mathcal{O}(10)$ for small bases to $\mathcal{O}(10^2)$ for the larger set. The method is therefore expected to be well suited to nearly monochromatic sources such as the resolved Galactic double white dwarf binaries in the millihertz band. The Target-to-Suppression Ratio (TSR) peaks near the design frequency and falls quickly away from it, so the optimized weight vector is inherently narrowband and suited to targeted searches around a chosen frequency and sky direction.

</details>


### [139] [Circulation and operational sparsity of analogue Hawking radiation in rotating acoustic horizons](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.00642)
*Fernando M. Belchior,Joao A. A. S. Reis,Edilberto O. Silva*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 本文研究二维旋转排水浴缸声学视界（acoustic horizon）的时间稀疏度（temporal sparsity），发现霍金温度仅由径向排水决定，与环量无关。当环量与排水比率为1时，热时钟稀疏度为0.44而基于谱的稀疏度为1.82，说明旋转增强通量并硬化谱，时间重叠依赖于操作时钟的选择。此外，分箱稀疏度定义在二维空间中具有对数红外敏感性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 时间稀疏度作为比较发射量子平均间隔与单量子特征时间尺度的操作量，可用于表征霍金辐射（Hawking radiation）的时间结构。此前该量尚未在旋转排水浴缸声学视界这个含超辐射（superradiance）的二维系统中被系统研究，而时空几何的环量可能显著影响辐射的时间统计学特性。

Method: 作者比较了几何黑体基准与灰体修正（greybody-corrected）结果：首先基于霍金温度固定、捕获宽度与透射谱依赖环量的物理事实建立黑体预测，随后求解径向散射问题，计算收敛的灰体修正数通量和能通量，并将超辐射扇区纳入分析。文中研究了两种时间稀疏度定义——基于热时钟的稀疏度与基于谱的稀疏度，并检验了分箱（binned）稀疏度定义在二维空间中的红外性质。

Result: 环量与排水比率等于1时，热时钟稀疏度为0.44，谱基稀疏度为1.82；前者在比率约为0.40处穿过时间重叠基准，后者在所有采样的环量比率下均保持大于1。分箱稀疏度在二维空间中表现出对数敏感性于红外截断。

Conclusion: 旋转通过增强数通量并硬化辐射谱来改变霍金辐射的时间分布，使时间重叠程度本质上依赖于用于分辨发射量子的操作时钟定义。这一发现表明旋转排水浴缸系统为考察黑洞类比系统（analog black hole systems）中时间统计学的测量依赖性质提供了新的视角。

Abstract: Temporal sparsity compares the mean interval between emitted quanta with a characteristic single-quantum timescale. We study this operational quantity for the rotating draining-bathtub acoustic horizon in two spatial dimensions. The Hawking temperature is fixed by the radial drain and does not depend on circulation, while the geometric capture width and the transmission spectrum do. A geometric black-body benchmark therefore predicts reduced temporal separation as circulation increases. We then solve the radial scattering problem and compute converged greybody-corrected number and energy fluxes, including the superradiant sector. At a circulation-to-drain ratio of unity, the thermal-clock sparsity is 0.44 whereas the spectrum-based sparsity is 1.82. The former crosses the temporal-overlap benchmark near a ratio of 0.40, while the latter remains above unity at all sampled circulation ratios. We also show that the binned sparsity definition is logarithmically infrared sensitive in two spatial dimensions. Rotation therefore enhances the number flux while hardening the spectrum, making temporal overlap intrinsically dependent on the operational clock used to resolve the emitted quanta.

</details>


### [140] [Perturbations and stability of black holes with static scalar hair in general GLPV theories](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.00664)
*Petarpa Boonserm,Antonio De Felice,Ratchaphat Nakarachinda,Shinji Tsujikawa,Pitayuth Wongjun*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 本文在四阶-五阶GLPV理论（含Horndeski）中推导了具有径向标量毛的静态球对称黑洞的背景方程及完整的奇偶宇称二次作用量，给出了无鬼条件与径向/角向eikonal特性，并发现两个分支的eikonal相速有限非零要求会选出Horndeski相关的相容性条件。在黑洞视界处，标量动能项有限非零时，所有非平凡分支均局部不稳定或具有退化张量锥；而在标量-Gauss-Bonnet黑洞中，小耦合条件下可构建满足全区域稳定性条件的解析幂律四阶beyond-Horndeski变形。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 旨在系统研究GLPV理论框架下径向标量毛黑洞的扰动稳定性和传播特性，考察beyond-Horndeski函数在视界附近及外部区域所施加的约束条件。核心动机是厘清四阶-五阶GLPV类中哪些黑解物理上可行、哪些受到稳定性限制，从而引导对扩张修正（beyond-Horndeski deformations）的合理构建。

Method: 方法上，作者首先在一般四阶-五阶GLPV理论（含Horndeski）中推导静态球对称径向标量毛黑洞的背景方程与完整二次作用量，涵盖奇偶宇称两个扇区。随后在正则非退化分支上跨Killing视界做形式化展开，导出局部无鬼条件和径向/角向eikonal色散特征，并考察张量模的径向速度一致性。通过要求两个角向分支的eikonal相速有限且非零，筛选出四阶与五阶beyond-Horndeski函数之间的Horndeski相容性条件。进一步将该条件用于构造标量-Gauss-Bonnet黑洞的解析幂律四阶beyond-Horndeski变形，并检验其局部稳定性及高频率梯度稳定性。

Result: 结果显示，两宇称扇区的张量模具有相同的径向平方速度；当径向坐标为类时方向时，一般的反对称混合会引致两个偶宇称角向分支出现非标准的极大多极标度行为。有限且非零的eikonal相速要求引出一致的四阶-五阶相容性条件，该条件对齐协变简并方向并允许局部的disformal映射到Horndeski。在简单外视界处，当标量动能项有限非零时，GLPV精确黑洞的所有非平凡分支均局部不稳定或具有退化张量锥。对于标量-Gauss-Bonnet黑洞，在足够小的耦合下，所构造的变形满足外部全域的无鬼和高频梯度稳定性条件。

Conclusion: 作者指出，视界处有限非零的标量动能（scalar kinetic term）几乎普遍地对GLPV黑解构成稳定性障碍，这一障碍很少例外，且延伸至更一般的位移对称和反射对称二次GLPV理论。在标量-Gauss-Bonnet情形下，当视界标量动能消失时，可由相容性条件唯一确定五次函数的四阶beyond-Horndeski变形在足够小耦合下全域稳定。对于线性Gauss-Bonnet耦合，作者估计了背景与稳定性展开失去微扰控制的内域尺度，并强调这并不意味着物理不稳定。

Abstract: We derive the background equations and complete odd- and even-parity quadratic actions for static, spherically symmetric black holes with radial scalar hair in general quartic-quintic Gleyzes-Langlois-Piazza-Vernizzi (GLPV) theories, including Horndeski. On regular, nondegenerate branches, the formulation applies across Killing horizons and yields local no-ghost conditions and radial and angular eikonal characteristics. Tensor modes in both parity sectors share the same squared radial speed. When the radial coordinate is timelike, generic antisymmetric mixing induces nonstandard large-multipole scaling for both even-parity angular branches. Requiring both branches to have finite, nonzero eikonal phase speeds selects the Horndeski-related compatibility condition between the quartic and quintic beyond-Horndeski functions. This condition aligns the covariant degeneracy directions and permits a local disformal map to Horndeski when regular and invertible. For a finite, nonzero scalar kinetic term at the horizon, every nontrivial branch of the exact quadratic GLPV black hole is locally unstable or has a degenerate tensor cone near a simple outer horizon. Apart from identified exceptions, this obstruction extends to general shift- and reflection-symmetric quadratic GLPV theories and the regular Horndeski-related branch of the quartic-quintic class. Finally, for scalar-Gauss-Bonnet black holes with a vanishing horizon scalar kinetic term, we construct analytic power-law quartic beyond-Horndeski deformations whose associated quintic function is fixed by the same condition. At sufficiently small coupling, all local no-ghost and high-frequency gradient-stability conditions hold throughout the exterior. For a linear Gauss-Bonnet coupling, we estimate an interior scale below which the background and stability expansions lose perturbative control; this does not imply a physical instability.

</details>


### [141] [Reissner-Nordstrom in the Bertotti-Robinson-Bonnor-Melvin electromagnetic field](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.00882)
*Marco Astorino*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 本文构造了嵌入Bertotti-Robinson（BR）均匀磁场中的磁Reissner-Nordstrom黑洞，并通过Harrison变换生成了位于Bertotti-Robinson-Bonnor-Melvin磁场中的Reissner-Nordstrom度规，进而讨论了其特例并展示了Schwarzschild-Bertotti-Robinson解各形式间的显式微分同胚等价性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 将黑洞嵌入外磁场环境并研究其几何与解的结构是广义相对论与高维引力的重要课题。作者希望利用Ernst方程的Harrison变换将已知的BR磁场中黑洞解推广至BR-Bonnor-Melvin磁场背景，并厘清各特解之间的局部等价关系。

Method: 以嵌入Bertotti-Robinson磁场的磁Reissner-Nordstrom黑洞为种子解（seed solution），利用Ernst方程的Harrison变换生成Reissner-Nordstrom度规在Bertotti-Robinson-Bonnor-Melvin磁场中的新解；随后讨论推广真空有毛I型Schwarzschild解的特例，并通过构造显式微分同胚（diffeomorphism）来证明不同形式Schwarzschild-Bertotti-Robinson解之间的局部等价性。

Result: 成功构造了位于BR-Bonnor-Melvin磁场中的Reissner-Nordstrom解，其涵盖了包括Schwarzschild等在内的真空有毛（hairy）解的特例，并给出了各种Schwarzschild-Bertotti-Robinson形式解之间的显式微分同胚映射。

Conclusion: BR磁场环境中的磁场化黑洞解可通过Harrison变换系统地生成，且不同参数化形式之间本质上是局部等价的引力几何，其等价性可由显式坐标变换加以证明。

Abstract: The magnetic Reissner-Nordstrom black hole embedded into the Bertotti-Robinson magnetic field is presented. Then it is used, as a seed, to generate, through the Harrison transformation of the Ersnt's equations, the Reissner-Nordstrom metric in the Bertotti-Robinson-Bonnor-Melvin magnetic field. Special cases generalising, the vacuum hairy type I Schwarzschild solution is discussed. The local equivalence between various forms of the Schwarzschild-Bertotti-Robinson is shown by exhibiting the explicit diffeomorphism.

</details>


### [142] [Polarization Images of Neutron Stars Illuminated by a Thin Accretion Disk: A Comparison with Black Holes](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.00934)
*Dan-Dan Feng,Guo-Ping Li,Chen-Yu Yang,Hong Lei*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 本文研究了受几何薄、光学薄吸积盘照亮的静态球对称中子星（neutron star）的线性偏振（linear polarization）图像。通过多方位状态方程构造平衡构型，并在几何光学近似下求解零测地线方程和平行输运方程，发现总偏振强度与总强度正相关、在星面附近最大，随观测者倾角增大图像对称性逐渐破坏；磁场主要影响偏振矢量方向而非强度分布。引入净电矢量位置角（net electric vector position angle）χ_net及第二方位傅里叶模∠β_2刻画偏振方向空间结构，并与Schwarzschild黑洞偏振图像比较，显示两者在强偏振区位置及中心无信号区尺寸上存在显著差异，证明线性偏振图像可为区分中子星与黑洞提供超越总强度图像的信息。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 总强度图像难以有效区分中子星与黑洞这类致密天体，线性偏振图像可能提供互补信息；作者旨在通过数值求解验证偏振图像能否作为区分二者的新观测诊断工具。

Method: 采用多方状态方程（polytropic equation of state）构造中子星平衡构型；在几何光学近似下求解零测地线方程与偏振矢量平行输运方程，计算静态球对称中子星受薄吸积盘照射下的线性偏振图像；引入χ_net与第二方位傅里叶模∠β_2表征偏振方向的空间分布，并与Schwarzschild黑洞结果进行对比。

Result: 总偏振强度与总强度总体呈正相关，且在中子星表面附近达到最大；随观测倾角增大，偏振图像对称性逐渐破坏。磁场构型主要改变偏振矢量方向，对总偏振强度分布影响有限。与Schwarzschild黑洞相比，两类致密天体在强偏振区域的位置以及中央无偏振信号区域的大小上呈现明显差异。

Conclusion: 线性偏振图像包含超出总强度图像的信息，有望为从观测上区分中子星与黑洞提供新型判别手段。

Abstract: We investigate linear polarization images of static, spherically symmetric neutron stars illuminated by a geometrically and optically thin accretion disk. Neutron star equilibrium configurations are constructed with a polytropic equation of state, and the null geodesic equations and the parallel transport equation for the linear polarization vector are solved in the geometric optics approximation. The numerical results show that the total polarized intensity is generally positively correlated with the total intensity and reaches its maximum near the neutron star surface. As the observer inclination increases, the symmetry of the polarization images is progressively broken. In addition, the magnetic field configuration mainly affects the direction of the polarization vectors, while its influence on the overall polarized intensity distribution is comparatively limited. To further characterize the spatial structure of the polarization direction, we introduce the net electric vector position angle $χ_{\mathrm{net}}$ and the second azimuthal Fourier mode $\angleβ_2$. A comparison with polarization images of a Schwarzschild black hole reveals clear differences between the two types of compact objects in the locations of strongly polarized regions and the size of the central region without a polarization signal. These results show that linear polarization images provide information beyond total intensity images for distinguishing neutron stars from black holes.

</details>


### [143] [Constraining AGN Disk Properties with Gravitational Waves from Inspiraling Stellar-Mass Binary Black Holes in Hierarchical Triple Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.00945)
*Jie Wu,Mengfei Sun,Jin Li,Zhoujian Cao*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 本研究探讨了空间引力波探测器能否通过观测在活动星系核（AGN）盘内围绕克尔超大质量黑洞（SMBH）旋进的恒星质量双黑洞（BBH）信号来约束局部气体密度。研究者在包含广义相对论修正和气体动力学摩擦（DF）的外轨道演化下构造探测器帧波形，并通过Fisher矩阵分析发现，对于GW190521类系统，LISA一年观测可将盘面密度约束在σρ~10⁻¹²–10⁻¹⁰ g·cm⁻³量级。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 空间引力波探测器能在恒星质量双黑洞合并前长时间观测其信号，使微弱的环境扰动得以累积。对于嵌入活动星系核盘中的双黑洞，局部气体密度是刻画超大质量黑洞环境和致密天体迁移的关键参数，因此有必要探究能否利用此类信号约束该密度。

Method: 研究者以广义相对论修正和气体动力学摩擦（dynamical friction, DF）演化外轨道，构建包含双黑洞旋进、de Sitter进动、DF相位修正及运动源效应的探测器帧波形。采用Fisher矩阵计算对采样系统进行统计不确定性和系统误差的估计。

Result: 较大的气体密度总体上能提升多个源参数和外轨道参数的统计精度，但在忽略DF时也会增大系统误差。以LISA对GW190521类系统的观测为例，一年观测可将盘面密度约束在σρ~10⁻¹²至10⁻¹⁰ g·cm⁻³范围内。

Conclusion: 此类约束有望将双黑洞合并环境与星系核的气体结构及支持黑洞增长的条件联系起来，表明只要有自洽的气体效应建模，分层级（hierarchical）双黑洞旋进即可用于探测活动星系核盘环境。

Abstract: Space-based gravitational-wave detectors can observe stellar-mass binary black holes (BBHs) long before merger, allowing weak environmental perturbations to accumulate. For binaries embedded in active galactic nucleus (AGN) disks, the local gas density characterizes the environment of the supermassive black hole (SMBH) and compact-object migration. We study whether such signals can constrain this density when a stellar-mass BBH orbits a Kerr SMBH. We evolve the outer orbit with relativistic corrections and gaseous dynamical friction (DF), and construct the detector-frame waveform including BBH inspiral, de Sitter precession, DF phase correction, and moving-source effects. Using Fisher-matrix calculations for sampled systems, we estimate statistical uncertainties and systematic errors. Larger gas densities generally improve the statistical precision of several source and outer-orbit parameters, but also increase systematic errors when DF is omitted. For favorable GW190521-like systems observed by LISA for one year, the disk density can be constrained at the level of $σ_ρ\sim10^{-12}\text{--}10^{-10}\,{\rm g\,cm^{-3}}$. Such constraints would connect BBH merger environments to the gas structure of galactic nuclei and the conditions that support black hole growth. These results indicate that hierarchical BBH inspirals can probe AGN disk environments, provided that gas effects are modeled consistently.

</details>


### [144] [Near-extremal asymptotics and strong cosmic censorship for black holes immersed in a Chaplygin-like dark fluid](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.00980)
*Hao-Peng Yan,Zeng-Yi Zhang,Xiang-Qian Li,Xiao-Jun Yue*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 本文研究了浸没在Chaplygin型暗流体（CDF）中的电中性黑洞上无质量标量扰动的强宇宙监督（SCC）。研究给出了极值和Nariai视界边界的闭式参数化，结合解析与全局拟正则模计算，确定了谱间隙并评估了整个三视界域内SCC的Christodoulou表述，发现潜在的SCC违背被限制在靠近极值态的窄区域内。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 强宇宙监督（strong cosmic censorship, SCC）是广义相对论中关于内视界稳定性的核心猜想，此前对de Sitter黑洞中SCC违背的研究需要推广到更广泛的暗流体背景。理解Chaplygin型暗流体（CDF）环境中黑洞扰动的拟正则模谱及其与SCC的关系，有助于检验该猜想在更一般物质场背景下的普适性。

Method: 研究针对浸没在Chaplygin型暗流体（CDF）中电中性黑洞的无质量标量扰动展开，该物质分布给出渐近de Sitter外部并支持内部Cauchy视界。作者推导了极值及Nariai视界边界的闭式参数化（closed-form parametrization），给出视界分裂与Cauchy视界表面引力的近极值渐近显式结果，并结合解析拟正则谱与全局拟正则模（quasinormal mode, QNM）数值计算确定谱间隙。

Result: 基波（fundamental mode）近极值阻尼率趋于Cauchy视界表面引力，而高角动量多极项保留了对CDF极值几何的显式依赖。潜在SCC违背被限制在靠近极值态的窄区域内；随着有效宇宙学尺度增大，该区域下边界依次由de Sitter模、光子球（photon-sphere）模和de Sitter模控制，其归一化宽度呈现非单调变化。

Conclusion: 在Chaplygin型暗流体黑洞背景中，强宇宙监督的Christodoulou表述仅在靠近极值态的窄区域存在潜在违背可能。该区域的边界行为受不同模式的竞争控制，其宽度随有效宇宙学尺度呈非单调变化，揭示了暗流体参数与SCC成立性之间的复杂耦合关系。

Abstract: We investigate strong cosmic censorship (SCC) for massless scalar perturbations of electrically neutral black holes immersed in a Chaplygin-like dark fluid (CDF). The matter distribution produces an asymptotically de Sitter exterior while supporting an inner Cauchy horizon. We derive a closed-form parametrization of the extremal and Nariai horizon boundaries. This analytic control yields explicit near-extremal asymptotics for the horizon splitting and Cauchy-horizon surface gravity, together with an analytic expression for the leading near-extremal scalar quasinormal spectrum. The fundamental near-extremal damping rate approaches the Cauchy-horizon surface gravity, whereas higher angular multipoles retain explicit dependence on the CDF extremal geometry. Combining these analytic results with global quasinormal-mode calculations, we determine the spectral gap and assess the Christodoulou formulation of SCC throughout the three-horizon domain. Potential SCC violation is confined to a narrow region near extremality. As the effective cosmological scale increases, the lower boundary of this region is controlled successively by de Sitter, photon-sphere, and de Sitter modes, while its normalized width varies nonmonotonically.

</details>


### [145] [Dissipative Multi-Field Dynamics from Non-Hermitian Inflationary Potentials](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.00992)
*S. D. Campos*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 本文发展了一种针对复标量场暴胀（inflation）的微扰框架，结合非最小引力耦合与非厄米势，在可观测宇宙微波背景辐射（CMB）时区自然退化为保守的双场模型，并保持α-吸引子（α-attractor）类预测满足普朗克2018与BICEP/Keck约束；暴胀末期的轨迹弯曲激活非厄米扇区触发几何再加热（geometric reheating），使曲率谱与随机引力波背景因复质量本征值引入的可计算阻尼因子而修正，在高频引力波谱（f > 10^2 Hz）出现可探测的抑制特征。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探索非厄米势与引力耦合在早期宇宙暴胀动力学中的角色，同时保持与现有CMB观测约束的一致性，并提出可被未来引力波探测器检验的新预测。

Method: 建立包含复暴胀子、非最小引力耦合和非厄米势的微扰理论框架；通过约化到保守双场模型来分析CMB时区，并利用复质量本征值推导非幺正演化下的阻尼因子以修正曲率谱与引力波背景。

Result: CMB尺度观测如n_s、r和f_NL仍与Planck 2018及BICEP/Keck数据一致；但非幺正演化在高频引力波谱（f > 10^2 Hz）中产生显著抑制，可被爱因斯坦望远镜（Einstein Telescope）与大爆炸观测者（Big Bang Observer）等未来探测器检验。

Conclusion: 该框架表明非厄米效应在CMB尺度上不改变观测结果，却在引力波高频段留下独特印记，为区分这类模型提供可实验验证的探针。

Abstract: In this work, we develop a perturbative framework for inflation driven by a complex inflaton with non-minimal gravitational coupling and a non-Hermitian potential. During the observable cosmic microwave background radiation era, the dynamics reduce to an effectively conservative two-field model, preserving the predictions of the $α$-attractor class and satisfying Planck 2018 and BICEP/Keck constraints on $n_s$, $r$, and $f_{\mathrm{NL}}$. Near the end of inflation, trajectory bending activates the non-Hermitian sector, triggering geometric reheating. The resulting non-unitary evolution modifies the curvature spectrum and stochastic gravitational-wave background through a calculable damping factor determined by the complex mass eigenvalues. While cosmic microwave background-scale observables remain essentially unchanged, a distinctive suppression emerges in the high-frequency gravitational-wave spectrum ($f > 10^2$ Hz), potentially testable by future detectors such as the Einstein Telescope and the Big Bang Observer.

</details>


### [146] [Charging higher-dimensional spacetimes with a generalized Kerr-Schild transformation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.01012)
*Aravindhan Srinivasan,Marcello Ortaggio*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 本文通过广义Kerr-Schild变换（沿零测地向量场k），从真空种子构造高维爱因斯坦-麦克斯韦（-陈-西蒙斯）解，系统分类出三类解族，并发现所有情形下陈-西蒙斯项在壳上为零。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探索在高维时空中通过Kerr-Schild变换从真空解出发构造带电爱因斯坦-麦克斯韦（-陈-西蒙斯）解的一般性框架，以期更系统地理解此类引力-电磁耦合解的构造机制。

Method: 采用广义Kerr-Schild变换，沿满足光学约束且为Weyl对齐零方向的测地零向量场k进行；假设向量势A与k对齐，根据k的膨胀、扭转与剪切性质进行分类分析，并另外放宽A的对齐假设考察六维带电Taub-NUT度规的推广。

Result: 得到三个截然不同的解支：当k膨胀且扭转时剪切必须为零（含带电Taub-NUT度规）；当k膨胀且无扭转时分为两支（Robinson-Trautman电真空解带非零Maxwell场（剪切为零）或带零场的剪切解）；当k非膨胀时退化为一类Kundt解。所有情况下陈-西蒙斯项在壳上恒为零。此外，放宽A的对齐条件后获得六维带电Taub-NUT度规，其磁场强度为两个不同Kähler 2-形式的线性组合。

Conclusion: 广义Kerr-Schild变换在给定假设下可从真空种子系统构造出涵盖Robinson-Trautman、Kundt及带电Taub-NUT等多类高维爱因斯坦-麦克斯韦（-陈-西蒙斯）解，表明该变换是构造此类解的通用工具。

Abstract: We explore the construction of higher-dimensional Einstein-Maxwell(-Chern-Simons) solutions from vacuum seeds by means of a generalized Kerr-Schild transformation along a geodesic null vector field $\mathbf{k}$. Assuming the vector potential $\mathbf{A}$ to be aligned with $\mathbf{k}$, and $\mathbf{k}$ to be a Weyl aligned null direction satisfying the ``optical constraint'', we arrive at three distinct branches of solutions. If $\mathbf{k}$ is expanding and twisting, then its shear must vanish -- this branch includes certain charged Taub-NUT metrics. If $\mathbf{k}$ is expanding and twistfree, one finds two subfamilies: Robinson-Trautman electrovac solutions with a non-null Maxwell field if the shear is zero, or shearing solutions with a null field. Finally, the case when $\mathbf{k}$ is non-expanding reduces to a subset of the known Kundt solutions. In all cases, the Chern-Simons term turns out to be identically zero on-shell. In passing, by relaxing the alignment assumption on $\mathbf{A}$, we also obtain a six-dimensional extension of a charged Taub-NUT metric for which the magnetic part of the field strength is a linear combination of two distinct Kähler 2-forms, as opposed to the previously known examples in more than four dimensions.

</details>


### [147] [Ultraviolet Closure in Asymptotically Weyl-Invariant Gravity](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.01047)
*Daniel Coumbe*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 本文在Palatini表述中研究渐近外尔不变引力（asymptotically Weyl-invariant gravity, AWIG），通过指数n在爱因斯坦型红外极限（n→1）与外尔不变紫外极限（n→2）之间插值。作者证明最小标量Palatini f(R)扇区的精确外尔不变性唯一选定R²作用作为紫外端点，并分析了严格紫外理论中可容许的有限圈对消项，发现所有发散壳上（on-shell）对消项在任意圈阶下均归结为原始√-gR²项加拓扑Euler密度，为紫外行为的改善提供了非平凡证据。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 爱因斯坦引力（Einstein gravity）在紫外区域存在可重整化性缺失问题，外尔不变引力（Weyl-invariant gravity）被认为是改善紫外行为的候选理论。作者希望研究从红外爱因斯坦型行为过渡到紫外外尔不变行为的插值模型中，紫外端点理论的结构性质及其重整化行为。

Method: 作者采用Palatini表述（Palatini formulation）构造渐近外尔不变引力理论，引入插值指数n（从n=1的爱因斯坦型红外区过渡到n=2的外尔不变紫外区）。将指数解释为依赖于曲率的有效物理量后，约束了一类光滑自治流的唯象类。随后在规则分支（R≠0）及受限无挠、偶宇称、纯曲率构造的扇区内，系统分析任意圈阶所有可容许的发散壳上对消项。

Result: 首先，在四维空间中，最小标量Palatini f(R)扇区的精确外尔不变性唯一确定R²作用为紫外端点。其次，在严格紫外理论的受限扇区内，所有可容许的发散壳上对消项在任意圈阶下均约化为原始√-gR²项加上拓扑Euler密度（Euler density）。

Conclusion: 该条件性结果虽未确立完整的非壳上（off-shell）微扰重整化性，但为相对于爱因斯坦引力具有改善的紫外行为提供了非平凡证据，并识别出未来进行第一性原理重整化群（renormalization group）计算的具体目标。

Abstract: We investigate asymptotically Weyl-invariant gravity (AWIG) in the Palatini formulation, defined by an exponent n that interpolates between an Einstein-like infrared regime, where n goes to one, and a Weyl-invariant ultraviolet regime, where n goes to two. In four dimensions, we show that exact Weyl invariance within the minimal scalar Palatini f(R) sector uniquely selects the R^2 action as the ultraviolet endpoint. Interpreting the exponent as an effective curvature-dependent quantity, we constrain a phenomenological class of smooth autonomous flows compatible with the required infrared and ultraviolet endpoints. We then analyse the strict ultraviolet theory. On the regular branch R not equal to zero, and within a restricted torsionless, parity-even sector constructed purely from curvature, all admissible divergent on-shell counterterms reduce at arbitrary loop order to the original \sqrt{-g}R^2 term plus a topological Euler density. Although this conditional result does not establish full off-shell perturbative renormalizability, it provides nontrivial evidence for improved ultraviolet behaviour relative to Einstein gravity and identifies a concrete target for a future first-principles renormalization-group calculation.

</details>


### [148] [High-Frequency Gravitational-Wave Transduction in a SQUID-Terminated Superconducting Cavity](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.01054)
*H. Hadil,Amin Rezaei Akbarieh*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 本文提出了SQUID端接超导微波腔作为兆赫兹至吉赫兹频段高频引力波（gravitational waves）窄带参量传感方案的完整理论框架，推导了腔模谱与谐振频率响应，引入了两个独立响应内核，并在耗散条件下将单模动力学约化到Mathieu型参量放大器形式，给出了光子产生、增益与不稳定性阈值等关键量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 兆赫兹至吉赫兹（MHz–GHz）频段的高频引力波超出了传统干涉仪（conventional interferometers）的探测能力，亟需发展新的探测机制与理论描述。

Method: 利用腔边界条件以及约瑟夫森电感（Josephson inductance）对磁通的依赖关系，推导四分之一波长模谱与对物理线长、相速度及SQUID感性长度的本征频率一阶响应。将受扰动力学投影到静态腔模上，引入谐振频率响应与光子对产生响应两个独立内核；在ΩGW≈2ωn附近将隔离单模动力学约化为Mathieu型参量放大器，并在含耗散条件下求解光子数、正交方差、增益与不稳定性阈值。

Result: 谐振频率响应与光子对产生响应在一般情况下彼此不同，仅当模归一化与空间起伏变化可忽略时二者重合。自发光子产生随引力波应变平方增长且对代表性参数而言极其微弱，而带相干探针的相位敏感响应可随应变线性增长。

Conclusion: 该框架为窄带引力波转换提供了理论描述，但定量灵敏度估计仍依赖器件特定的机电响应标定、模隔离验证以及关于损耗与噪声的完整处理。

Abstract: High-frequency gravitational waves in the MHz--GHz range require detection strategies beyond conventional interferometers. We study the response of a SQUID-terminated superconducting microwave cavity as a narrowband parametric transducer. The cavity boundary conditions and the flux dependence of the Josephson inductance are used to derive the quarter-wave mode spectrum and the first-order eigenfrequency response to changes in the physical line length, phase velocity, and SQUID inductive length. By projecting the perturbed dynamics onto the static cavity modes, we show that the resonance-frequency response and the photon-pair-production response are generally distinct. We therefore introduce two independent kernels, $R_n^ω$ and $R_n^{\rm pair}$, which coincide only when changes in mode normalization and spatial profiles can be neglected.
