Near $Ω_{\rm GW}\simeq 2ω_n$, the isolated-mode dynamics reduce to a Mathieu-type parametric amplifier, allowing the photon number, quadrature variances, gain, and instability threshold to be obtained in the presence of dissipation. Spontaneous photon production scales quadratically with the gravitational-wave strain and is extremely small for representative parameters, whereas phase-sensitive responses with a coherent probe can scale linearly with strain. The framework therefore provides a theoretical description of narrowband gravitational-wave transduction, while a quantitative sensitivity estimate requires device-specific calibration of the mechanical--electromagnetic response, verification of mode isolation, and a complete treatment of loss and noise.

</details>


### [149] [Colored-Noise-Induced Horizon Fluctuations Near a Double Root in an Effective Black-Hole Geometry](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.01063)
*Kashif Ammar Yasir*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 本文构建了一个有效的、准静态球形随机半经典模型，基于量子朗之万方程（quantum Langevin equation）的响应—噪声（response–noise）结构，研究应力张量涨落对视界几何、热力学及辐射统计的观测效应。在平均度规与视界局部色噪声源独立变化的设定下，作者重建了每一实现下的时移函数（lapse），定位外部捕获视界（outer trapping horizon），并由其斜率计算绝热的Hayward–Kodama温度。当平均时移趋近内外根合并时，其逆斜率充当几何磁化率（geometric susceptibility），使固定源协方差产生增强的视界涨落、视界—温度协方差以及正偏斜的灰体滤波光度。该模型是将色度规噪声映射至关联几何、热及辐射统计的单次实现映射，并明确区分了库强度与近临界放大，但其定位是随机半经典测试平台，而非Unruh态响应与噪声核的微观评估。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 平均半经典爱因斯坦方程无法捕捉应力张量涨落携带的信息，而涨落的可观测效应取决于几何的响应与噪声本身。作者旨在建立一个有效模型来研究此类涨落的观测后果，特别是当几何接近临界点时的放大效应。

Method: 作者构建了一个准静态球形模型，其结构由量子朗之万方程的响应—噪声结构组织。平均场穿（mean dressing）与视界局域化的色噪声（colored noise）源被独立变化；对每个实现重建时移函数，确定其外部捕获视界，并从根处斜率求得绝热Hayward–Kodama温度，构建从色度规噪声到相关几何、热与辐射统计的单次实现映射。

Result: 当平均时移趋近内外根合并（inner–outer root merger）时，逆斜率表现为几何磁化率：在固定源协方差下，视界涨落被增强，出现视界—温度协方差及正偏斜的灰体滤波光度（greybody-filtered luminosity）。模型明确分离了储库强度（reservoir strength）与近临界放大的贡献。

Conclusion: 该模型作为随机半经典测试平台（stochastic-semiclassical testbed）提供了从色度规噪声到关联几何、热与辐射统计的显式映射，但并非对Unruh态响应与噪声核的微观评估，其价值在于提供可计算的临界放大机制演示。

Abstract: Stress-tensor fluctuations carry information that is absent from the mean
  semiclassical Einstein equation, but their observable effect depends as much
  on the response of the geometry as on the noise itself. We formulate an
  effective, quasistationary spherical model organized by the response--noise
  structure of a quantum Langevin equation. A prescribed mean dressing and a
  horizon-localized colored source are varied independently. For every source
  realization we reconstruct the lapse, locate its outer trapping horizon, and
  evaluate the adiabatic Hayward--Kodama temperature from the slope at that same
  root. As the mean lapse approaches an inner--outer root merger, its inverse
  slope acts as a geometric susceptibility: a fixed source covariance produces
  enhanced horizon fluctuations, horizon--temperature covariance, and a
  positively skewed, greybody-filtered luminosity. The specific advance is a
  single-realization map from colored metric noise to correlated geometric,
  thermal, and radiative statistics, together with an explicit separation of
  reservoir strength from near-critical amplification. The model is a
  stochastic-semiclassical testbed, not a microscopic evaluation of Unruh-state
  response and noise kernels.

</details>


### [150] [Relative-Phase Control of Common-Bridge Survival in Collisions of Radially Excited Boson Stars](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.01134)
*Bo-Xuan Ge*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 本文通过对径向激发玻色子星（radially excited boson stars）的正碰撞（head-on collisions）进行数值研究，揭示了相对相位（relative phase）主要控制碰撞后公共桥（common bridge）的存活时间而非其初始形成，且相位偏移越大，桥的持续越短，这一抑制效应在不同构型和标量势（scalar potentials）下均成立。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 此前研究对相对相位在玻色子星碰撞中，特别是对桥结构的形成与持久性的具体作用尚未有系统刻画。本文旨在厘清相对相位究竟是影响桥的初始形成还是后续存活，以加深对强非线性引力动力学中碰撞过程的理解。

Method: 作者系统变化两碰撞玻色子星之间的相对相位，并跟踪接触后桥结构的演化过程；研究覆盖了多种玻色子星构型与标量势设定，以检验所得结果是否具有普适性。

Result: 在基准情形下，所有被采样的相位值均能形成公共桥，但较大相位偏移会减小桥的持续时长；这一抑制效应在不同构型和标量势下均一致地观察到。

Conclusion: 相对相位的作用主要体现在控制桥的存活（survival）而非其初始形成阶段。该结论对多种构型和标量势均适用，表明相位控制桥寿命的机制具有较强的普适性。

Abstract: We show that the relative phase primarily controls the survival, rather than the initial formation, of the common bridge in head-on collisions of radially excited boson stars. We vary the relative phase and track the post-contact bridge evolution across different boson-star configurations and scalar potentials. In the benchmark, all sampled phases form a common bridge, while larger phase offsets reduce its persistence; the same suppression persists across the configurations and potentials studied.

</details>


### [151] [Josephson interferometry in an Oppenheimer--Snyder-like scale-dependent black-hole spacetime](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.01177)
*Ali Ovgun,Reggie C. Pantig,G. Lambiase*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 该论文提出了一个协变（covariant）框架，用于描述由类 Oppenheimer–Snyder 坍缩（Oppenheimer–Snyder-like collapse）产生的尺度依赖外部时空中的约瑟夫森输运（Josephson transport）、超导干涉（superconducting interference）与分流噪声（shunt noise）。研究涵盖静态约瑟夫森频率的标度因子、径向零测地线传播核、近极端对数增强向极控制的交叉行为、红移后的分流结方程、双结干涉包络，以及在 Hartle–Hawking 态下的超导排除层与参数无关的渐近噪声谱，并推导出阴影–约瑟夫森一致性关系（shadow–Josephson consistency relation）及灵敏度界限。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 将约瑟夫森电动力学（Josephson electrodynamics）与引力坍缩外部时空的尺度依赖几何相结合，为研究强引力场中宏观量子相干现象建立统一的协变描述。

Method: 采用协变动力学框架，将约瑟夫森输运、干涉与噪声表述于 Kerr–Schild 类坍缩外部时空中；通过推导径向零传播时间核（radial-null travel-time kernel）分析传播效应，并在无自感极限下求解直流与微波驱动下的双结干涉包络。

Result: 静态约瑟夫森频率与输运电流（以 Killing 时间参照）含一个标度因子（lapse factor），功率含两个；局部近极端对数增强在固定发射位移下交叉到极点控制的极端行为。无自感极限下，静态标度失衡仅改变瓣幅不移动瓣中心，而微波诱导平移需动力学通量闭合（dynamical fluxoid closure）。在 Hartle–Hawking 态中，Tolman 红移产生超导排除层与标度无关的渐近噪声谱，低频极限满足无参数的涨落–延迟关系，并导出了阴影–约瑟夫森一致性关系。

Conclusion: 该框架将强引力坍缩几何与约瑟夫森宏观量子器件行为系统耦合，揭示了标度因子、红移与传播效应对输运、干涉和噪声的定量影响，并给出可检验的一致性关系与灵敏度约束，为理论黑洞物理与凝聚态类比提供桥梁。

Abstract: We develop a covariant framework for Josephson transport, superconducting interference, and shunt noise in the scale-dependent exterior generated by an Oppenheimer--Snyder-like collapse. Static Josephson frequencies and transported currents referred to Killing time acquire one lapse factor, whereas power acquires two. For a junction comoving with the collapsing surface, the coordinate-time phase rate differs from the frequency received at infinity because of null propagation and Doppler effects. We derive the exact radial-null travel-time kernel and show that its local near-extremal logarithmic enhancement crosses over, at fixed emission offset, to pole-controlled extremal behavior. We also obtain the redshifted resistively and capacitively shunted-junction equation and the dc and microwave-driven two-junction interference envelopes. In the negligible-total-inductance limit, static lapse imbalance modifies lobe amplitudes without shifting their centers, whereas microwave-induced translations require dynamical fluxoid closure. In a Hartle--Hawking state, Tolman redshift produces a superconducting exclusion layer and a lapse-independent asymptotic shunt-noise spectrum; its low-frequency limit obeys a parameter-free fluctuation--delay relation. Finally, we derive a shadow--Josephson consistency relation and sensitivity bounds on the running parameter.

</details>


### [152] [Deviations from Kerr: Polar critical curves and photon rings in a class of separable spacetimes](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.01290)
*Hao-Peng Yan,Xiang-Qian Li,Xiao-Jun Yue*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 本文为面向极轴观测者（polar observers）的黑洞临界曲线（critical curves）与光子环（photon rings）建立了超越Kerr度规的统一解析描述，推导了包含临界半径、Lyapunov指数、时间延迟和旋转参数在内的紧凑精确表达式及一般一阶公式，并揭示了弱偏差情形下这些观测量的组合仅探测临界轨道处的有限个局部形变值及其径向导数。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 黑洞临界曲线和光子环是对强场几何的互补探针，现有描述主要围绕Kerr度规展开，缺乏对超越Kerr的一般时空的统一解析框架，也缺少与Kerr预测的直接对比方法。

Method: 在具有可分离零测地线（separable null geodesics）的静态轴称时空径向类中，面向极轴观测者建立统一的解析描述。通过零动力学选择的自然光学组合组织时空依赖性，并引入"Kerr相对表示"以适配对Kerr的弱偏离；在此框架下推导观测量的精确表达式与一阶一般公式。

Result: 导出了极轴临界半径、Lyapunov指数、时间延迟与旋转参数的紧凑精确表达式，以及可输入特定度规形变函数（deformation functions）即可应用的一般一阶公式。弱偏差分析表明极轴观测量的组合仅探测临界轨道处的有限局部形变值及其径向导数，而非完整径向轮廓。

Conclusion: 该工作为超越Kerr的强场几何观测提供了统一的解析工具，同时揭示了极轴观测量对形变函数的局部敏感特性，有助于明确哪些时空信息可被这些可观测信号实际约束。

Abstract: Black-hole critical curves and photon rings provide complementary probes of strong-field geometry, motivating a unified analytic description of these observables beyond Kerr and a direct comparison with the Kerr predictions. We develop such a description for polar observers in a radial class of stationary, axisymmetric spacetimes with separable null geodesics. The spacetime dependence is organized through natural optical combinations selected by the null dynamics and a Kerr-relative representation suited to weak deviations from Kerr. We derive compact exact expressions for the polar critical radius, Lyapunov exponent, time delay, and rotation parameter, together with general first-order formulas that can be applied once the deformation functions of a specific metric are supplied. The weak-deviation analysis shows that the combined polar observables probe only a finite set of local deformation values and radial derivatives at the critical orbit, rather than the full radial profiles.

</details>


### [153] [Gravitational-wave signatures of primordial black hole clusters and the imprint of an early dense-core collapse](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.01331)
*Marc Barceló-Sastre,Juan García-Bellido*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 本文利用修改后的N体模拟（N-body simulation）代码nbody6++gpu，研究初始无自旋的原初黑洞（primordial black hole, PBH）星团如何在动力学过程中获得自旋和质量特征，并提出核-晕（core-halo）结构模型来解决致密核心坍缩与长期存活之间的张力，预测了可被LISA探测的双分量随机引力波背景。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 原初黑洞预计以基本无自旋且（在非高斯原初扰动下）强烈成团的方式形成，因此任何观测到的自旋都必然来自动力学过程。当前引力波观测已对PBH群体自旋、质量及随机背景信号敏感，需要量化研究初始无自旋PBH星团在何种条件下能够发展出这些可观测特征。

Method: 使用经修改以处理近抛物线动力学俘获并合的nbody6++gpu进行直接N体模拟，将核心与晕两个动力学机制作为独立模块分别模拟。核心模块包含4000个PBH，Plummer半径取3×10⁻⁶至10⁻⁴ pc，研究其数日至数年时间尺度上的坍缩与并合行为，并将核心并合信号红移化为可探测的随机背景。

Result: 产生30–50倍太阳质量范围内均方根自旋χ_rms约为0.1–0.2需要初始Plummer半径约10⁻⁵ pc，而宇宙学时间尺度的存活要求秒差距量级星团，两者存在尖锐张力。核心模块坍缩产生的并合轨道高度非圆（e→1）且无量纲轨道角动量低，导致残骸自旋系统性地低于准圆并合的χ≈0.7；核心并合红移后产生的随机背景峰值位于LISA可及的10⁻⁶–10⁻⁴ Hz频带。数据显示自旋与质量均支持在约50倍太阳质量处截断的原初质量函数。

Conclusion: 这种张力可通过PBH形成具有致密暴命核心嵌入扩展晕层的结构自然化解。该图景预测了可观测量之间的明确分工：自旋由原初坍缩设定，可探测的并合由长期晕层决定，并产生双分量随机背景。

Abstract: Primordial black holes (PBHs) are expected to be born essentially non-spinning and, if the primordial curvature fluctuations possess non-Gaussian tails, strongly clustered. Any spin measured in a PBH population must therefore be dynamically generated. Using direct $N$-body simulations with \textsc{nbody6++gpu}, modified to treat nearly parabolic dynamical-capture mergers, we quantify the conditions under which an initially non-spinning PBH cluster develops the spin, mass and stochastic-background signatures that gravitational-wave observations are now sensitive to. We find a sharp tension: producing a root-mean-square spin $χ_{\rm rms}\sim0.1 - 0.2$ in the $30 - 50\,M_\odot$ range requires initial Plummer radii of order $10^{-5}\,{\rm pc}$, whereas survival over cosmological times requires parsec-scale clusters. This tension is resolved naturally if PBHs form with a compact, short-lived core embedded in an extended halo, and we therefore simulate the two regimes as independent blocks. The core block ($4000$ PBHs, Plummer radii $3\times10^{-6} - 10^{-4}\,{\rm pc}$) collapses in days to years, producing a burst of mergers whose orbits are strongly non-circular ($e\to1$) and of low dimensionless orbital angular momentum, so that remnant spins are systematically below the quasi-circular value $χ\simeq0.7$. Redshifted from their formation epoch, the core mergers deposit a stochastic background peaking in the $10^{-6}$--$10^{-4}\,{\rm Hz}$ band accessible to LISA. The picture predicts a specific division of labour between observables, spins set in the primordial collapse, detectable mergers set by the long-term halo, and a two-component stochastic background, and, in particular, that both the spin and the mass data favour a primordial mass function truncated near $50\,M_\odot$.

</details>


### [154] [Scalar wave scattering by black holes embedded in dark matter halos](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.01391)
*Shi-Qian Hu,Federico Piazza*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 本文研究了嵌入暗物质晕中的静态黑洞对无质量标量波的散射问题。晕采用Hernquist分布建模，其爱因斯坦方程存在精确解。通过零测地线、glory近似、分波展开和复角动量（CAM）方法进行分析，发现暗物质晕对光环半径的影响较弱，但对临界与glory碰撞参数产生位移；波分析表明黑洞干涉图样得以保留，晕的致密度是主要修正因素，晕的尺度半径居次要地位。大部分干涉极值位移可由等效频率移的真空Schwarzschild解重现，但残差揭示出无法吸收的真实的晕贡献。CAM重建表明仅需少数Regge极即可在大角度实现高精度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 黑洞在自然界中并不孤立存在，而是常常嵌入星系暗物质晕中。理解暗物质环境如何影响黑洞的引力波散射和透镜观测特征，对于天体物理黑洞观测和暗物质环境探测具有重要意义。作者选择Hernquist分布描述暗物质晕，因为该模型与Einstein方程存在精确解，便于进行严格的理论分析。

Method: 研究采用多种互补的分析工具：零测地线（null geodesics）进行几何分析，glory近似、分波展开（partial wave expansion）以及复角动量（complex angular momentum, CAM）方法进行波散射分析。暗物质晕由Hernquist profile建模，其对应的Einstein方程具有精确解，从而保证了计算的严格性。

Result: 几何分析显示晕的存在对光环（light ring）半径影响较弱，但会使临界碰撞参数和glory碰撞参数发生位移。波分析显示黑洞特征干涉图样得以保留，晕的致密度（compactness）是主导修正因素，而晕的尺度半径（scale radius）影响较小；足够致密的晕还会引起可观的引力红移。大部分干涉极值位移可由选取适当ωM_BH的真空Schwarzschild解重现，但存在无法被这种有效频率移吸收的残余，表明晕存在真实的贡献。CAM重建显示仅需少量Regge极即可在大角度获得高精度的散射截面。

Conclusion: 暗物质晕对黑洞散射的影响主要体现在碰撞参数的位移和引力红移效应上，干涉图样的基本结构得以保持。虽然大部分效应可通过等效Schwarzschild频率移近似描述，但残余部分揭示了暗物质环境的本质性贡献，说明这类效应在未来的天文观测中不可完全忽略。

Abstract: We study the scattering of massless scalar waves by static black holes embedded in dark matter halos. The halo is modelled by means of a Hernquist profile, for which the Einstein equations admit an exact solution. We analyse the scattering using null geodesics, the glory approximation, the partial wave expansion, and the complex angular momentum (CAM) method.
  The geometric analysis shows that the presence of the halo changes the light ring radius weakly but shifts the critical and glory impact parameters. The wave analysis shows that the characteristic black hole interference pattern is preserved, with halo compactness providing the leading correction and the halo scale radius playing a secondary role. For sufficiently compact halos, the environment also induces a sizeable gravitational redshift.
  Most of the displacement of the interference extrema can be reproduced by a vacuum Schwarzschild solution with an appropriately chosen $ωM_{\rm BH}$. A non-vanishing residual, however, reveals a genuine halo contribution that cannot be absorbed into such an effective frequency shift. Finally, we perform the CAM reconstruction of the scattering cross section and find that only a few Regge poles are required to achieve high accuracy at large angles.

</details>


### [155] [Bounded Electromagnetic Power Flux at Naked Kasner Singularities](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.01536)
*Franco Fiorini,P. A. González,Yerko Vásquez*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 本文研究了经典电磁场与裸奇点（naked singularity）可以共存的部分罕见情形：在Kasner时空中的裸奇点附近，电磁功率通量在任意开邻域内有界。作者利用Plebanski–Tamm映射，将弯曲时空中的Maxwell理论映射为平坦空间中各向异性有效介质问题，发现了正则静电场构型以及振幅收敛的电磁波，其功率通量在接近奇点时保持有界且定义良好。在某些情形下，该奇点类似于完美反射的镜面；在另一些情形下，非零的有限功率通量可解释奇点如何吸收或辐射电磁场。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统观点通常认为强曲率或裸奇点附近电磁场会表现出发散等奇异行为，但本文旨在寻找并阐释个别例外情形，即经典电磁场在裸奇点邻域内依然可能保持有界功率通量并稳定共存，从而丰富对强引力背景下电磁现象的理解。

Method: 作者选择Kasner时空中的裸奇点作为研究对象，借助Plebanski–Tamm映射，将弯曲时空中的Maxwell理论等价转化为平坦空间中有效各向异性介质内的电磁问题，借此分析静电场构型及电磁波的传播行为。

Result: 研究发现了若干正则静电场的配置，同时也找到一类电磁波，其振幅在接近奇点时使得功率通量保持良好定义且有界。进一步的分析表明，在某些情形下裸奇点如同完美反射镜；在另一些情形下，非零的有限功率通量提示奇点可吸收或发射辐射场。

Conclusion: 该研究表明经典电磁场与裸奇点可在特定时空结构中稳定共存，奇点甚至可以扮演反射器或辐射源的角色，这对强引力环境下经典电磁理论的适用范围与行为给出了新的例证和简单解释。

Abstract: In some occasions, it would appear that classical electromagnetic fields could be able to coexist with strong curvature, naked singularities, in the sense that the electromagnetic power flux would be bounded in any open neighborhood of them. We illustrate this rare behavior by considering the naked singularity present in Kasner spacetimes. There, using the Plebanski--Tamm map between Maxwell theory in curved spacetime and the associated description corresponding to an effective anisotropic medium in flat space, we found not only some regular electrostatic field configurations, but also electromagnetic waves whose amplitudes lead to a well defined, bounded power flux as they approach the singularity. Sometimes, in these unusual situations, the singularity resembles a mirror, perfectly reflecting its environs; in other occasions, the existence of a non-null, finite power flux as the waves travel to or from the singular plane, offers a very simple explanation as to how the singularity might absorb or create radiation fields.

</details>


### [156] [Enabling gravitational-wave astronomy with spin-precessing black holes on generic orbits](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.01568)
*Aldo Gamboa,Lorenzo Pompili,Alessandra Buonanno,Luca Sebastiani,Raffi Enficiaud,Michael Boyle,Lawrence E. Kidder,Harald P. Pfeiffer,Antoni Ramos-Buades,Mark A. Scheel*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 本文提出了SEOBNRv6EPHM——有效单体（effective-one-body, EOB）SEOBNR家族中首个支持任意轨道和旋进（spin precession）的通用轨道引力波模型，覆盖从旋到并合再到衰荡（inspiral-merger-ringdown）的全过程，并扩展至动力学俘获和散射事件。该模型在1437个近圆（quasi-circular, QC）和87个偏心（eccentric）数值相对论（numerical relativity, NR）模拟上的中位失配度低于1%，与QC模型SEOBNRv5PHM精度相当，比现有最先进的通用轨道模型TEOBResumS-Dalí中位提升4倍，且计算速度更快，首次实现了同时考虑偏心率和旋进效应的引力波分析。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在稠密恒星环境或层级三体系统中形成的双黑洞（binary black holes, BBHs）可能在偏心轨道上并合并携带任意取向的自旋，在引力波信号中留下独特印记，但现有模型无法同时准确处理偏心率和自旋旋进效应。因此需要开发一个通用轨道、支持自旋旋进的模型，并进行系统性精度评估。

Method: 在EOB框架内构建SEOBNRv6EPHM模型，描述通用轨道双黑洞的动力学和多重极（multipolar）引力波信号，覆盖并合全过程并扩展到动力学俘获与散射场景。利用1437个近圆和87个偏心自旋旋进BBH的NR模拟进行首次系统精度评估，并通过注入-恢复研究（injection-recovery studies）验证对真实GW事件的分析。

Result: 模型的波形中位失配度低于1%，与QC模型SEOBNRv5PHM精度相当，比TEOBResumS-Dalí提升中位因子4。在QC极限下比SEOBNRv5PHM快约2-3倍，比TEOBResumS-Dalí快一个数量级。对十一个GW事件的分析中，GW200129的偏心证据得到加强，该结果得到注入-恢复研究支持。

Conclusion: SEOBNRv6EPHM首次实现了既准确又高效地同时考虑偏心率和自旋旋进的引力波数据分析，将偏心推断的计算成本降至当前近圆分析的水平，并能在散射场景中重现NR模拟中的非微扰现象。

Abstract: Binary black holes (BBHs) formed in dense stellar environments or in hierarchical triples can coalesce on eccentric orbits and carry spins of arbitrary orientation, leaving distinctive imprints on their gravitational-wave (GW) emission. We present SEOBNRv6EPHM: the first generic-orbit, spin-precessing model in the effective-one-body SEOBNR family, whose waveforms have underpinned LIGO-Virgo GW analyses since 2011. The model describes the dynamics and multipolar GW signal of generic BBHs, covering the inspiral-merger-ringdown of coalescing binaries and extending to dynamical captures and scattering encounters. We perform the first systematic accuracy assessment of a generic-orbit model against numerical relativity (NR) waveforms of spin-precessing BBHs, using 1437 quasi-circular (QC) and 87 eccentric simulations: median waveform mismatches remain below $1 \%$, matching the accuracy of the QC model SEOBNRv5PHM, and improving on the state-of-the-art generic-orbit model TEOBResumS-Dalí by a median factor of $ 4 $. The model also reproduces the non-perturbative phenomenology observed in NR simulations of generic-spin BBH scattering. It is $\sim 2 - 3$ times faster than SEOBNRv5PHM in the QC limit, and up to an order of magnitude faster than TEOBResumS-Dalí, bringing eccentric inference to the cost of current QC analyses. As a proof of principle, we analyze eleven GW events and focus on GW200129, strengthening its evidence for eccentricity $-$ a result supported by injection-recovery studies with synthetic NR signals of eccentric, spin-precessing BBHs. SEOBNRv6EPHM thus enables, for the first time, accurate and efficient GW analyses that jointly account for eccentricity and spin precession.

</details>


<div id='physics.app-ph'></div>

# physics.app-ph [[Back]](#toc)

### [157] [Quasi-normal-mode signatures of periodicity and hyperuniformity](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.01395)
*V. Romero-García,M. Martí-Sabaté,V. F. Dal Poggetto,L. M. García-Raffi,M. Lázaro*

Main category: physics.app-ph

TL;DR: 本文提出将准正则模（quasi-normal mode）谱作为表征有限隐身超均匀（stealthy hyperuniform）材料波动性质的补充框架，分析了周期结构和隐身超均匀结构中复本征频率随隐身参数（χ）的变化，发现超均匀程度改变时准正则模谱呈现系统性的演化，揭示了仅通过结构因子（structure factor）无法捕捉的信号，为比较有限离散异质材料的散射与共振动力学提供了统一框架。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统上，超均匀材料通过结构因子来量化长波长密度涨落的抑制，此方法依赖于扩展构型或超胞（supercell）的统计性质，无法直接描述有限结构在散射实验中的共振响应。作者因此希望引入一种能够直接在实空间表征有限体系、并与散射响应相关联的框架。

Method: 文中引入准正则模谱作为有限隐身超均匀材料波动特性的互补表征工具；对周期结构和隐身超均匀结构，在一系列隐身参数（χ）范围内分析其复本征频率，并考察散射体空间组织如何编码在准正则模的频率与空间分布中。

Result: 研究发现，随着超均匀程度的改变，准正则模谱呈现系统性变化，揭示了仅凭结构因子无法捕捉的特征；共振态（resonant states）的演化直接建立了结构关联与有限体系散射响应之间的联系。

Conclusion: 准正则模可作为超均匀性（hyperuniformity）的实空间与开放体系表征手段，为从散射和共振动力学角度比较各类离散异质材料提供了统一框架。

Abstract: Hyperuniform materials occupy an intermediate regime between periodic order and conventional disorder, characterized by a suppression of long-wavelength density fluctuations, commonly quantified by the structure factor. This characterization, however, relies on the statistical properties of an extended configuration or supercell representation and does not directly describe the resonant response of a finite structure, as typically encountered in scattering experiments. Here, we introduce the quasi-normal-mode spectrum as a complementary framework for characterizing the wave properties of finite stealthy hyperuniform materials. We analyze the complex eigenfrequencies of both periodic and stealthy hyperuniform structures over a range of the stealthiness parameter ($χ$), and investigate how the spatial organization of scatterers is encoded in both frequency and spatial profiles of their quasi-normal modes. We find systematic changes in the quasi-normal modes spectrum as the degree of hyperuniformity is varied, revealing signatures that are not captured solely by the structure factor. In particular, the evolution of the resonant states provides a direct connection between structural correlations and the scattering response of finite systems. Our results establish quasi-normal modes as a real-space and open-system characterization of hyperuniformity and provide a unified framework for comparing discrete heterogeneous materials from the perspective of their scattering and resonant dynamics.

</details>


<div id='hep-ph'></div>

# hep-ph [[Back]](#toc)

### [158] [Reining in an Agentic Harness for High Energy Physics](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.00107)
*Tony Menzo,George T. Fleming,Konstantin T. Matchev,Stephen Mrenna,Alexander Roman*

Main category: hep-ph

TL;DR: 本文针对高能物理（high energy physics, HEP）领域智能体系统（agentic systems）科学能力难以跨大语言模型（LLM）、供应商和框架复用的问题，提出将工作流中的稳定部分提升为带版本的科学操作（versioned scientific operations），并通过通用协议（common protocols）暴露，结合社区维护的注册表（registry）实现可发现与可引用，最终构建便携且社区维护的HEP智能体框架。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 智能体系统已覆盖高能物理中理论、现象学和实验等多类任务，但其科学能力在不同大语言模型、供应商和框架之间难以复用，阻碍了能力的迁移与标准化。

Method: 提出将稳定工作流组件提升为带版本的科学操作并通过通用协议公开；将通用框架通过特定任务工具集与技能集加以专业化；引入社区维护的注册表以支持能力发现与引用；针对操作之间的规范、假设与有效域不一致问题，探讨机器可读科学契约（machine-readable scientific contracts）作为解决方案。

Result: 识别了独立开发的操作之间在约定、假设与有效域方面存在不匹配，并讨论了机器可读科学契约作为潜在解决途径；提出了包含设计原则与评估指南的近期实现路径。

Conclusion: 上述设计原则与评估指南为实现一个便携、社区维护的高能物理智能体框架提供了近期可行路线。

Abstract: Agentic systems now address tasks across theoretical, phenomenological, and experimental high energy physics (HEP), but their scientific capabilities remain difficult to reuse across different large language models, providers, and harnesses. We argue that stable parts of these workflows should be promoted into versioned scientific operations and exposed through common protocols. Existing general-purpose harnesses can then be specialized for HEP through task-specific sets of tools and skills, while community-maintained registries would make these capabilities discoverable and citable. We identify mismatches in conventions, assumptions, and domains of validity among independently developed operations as a potential obstacle to their composition, and discuss machine-readable scientific contracts as one possible solution. These design principles and evaluation guidelines provide a near-term path toward a portable and community-maintained agentic harness for HEP.

</details>


### [159] [Neutrino Electroweak Couplings and the Neutrino Fog at Belle II](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.00121)
*Carlos Henrique de Lima,David McKeen,Afif Omar,Douglas Tuckler*

Main category: hep-ph

TL;DR: 本文研究Belle II实验中的单光子过程e^+e^-→γνν̄，表明完整数据集可使中微子有效弱混合角的统计精度达到3.6%相对误差，可与现有其他能标的中微子探针竞争；极化束流（Chiral Belle升级）可分离中性流和带电流贡献，为研究中微子规范相互作用的赝标结构提供新途径，同时该过程是暗玻色子搜索的不可约本底。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Belle II的单光子过程e^+e^-→γ+不可见态既能探测不可见新态，又能检验中微子的电弱耦合。此前对该过程是否可用于精确测量中微子电弱耦合参数及分析其作为暗玻色子搜索本底的影响尚未充分研究。

Method: 理论上研究e^+e^-→γνν̄过程，评估Belle II在完整预期数据集下的灵敏度，分析极化束流（Chiral Belle升级方案）对中性流与带电流贡献的分离能力，并考察该过程在不同亮度下对暗玻色子搜索本底的贡献。

Result: Belle II完整数据集可将中微子敏感的等效弱混合角测至3.6%相对统计精度，竞争性强；极化束流能够分别独立提取中性流和带电流贡献，揭示中微子规范相互作用的赝标结构；当亮度超过1 ab⁻¹时，该过程形成"中微子雾"，成为暗玻色子搜索的不可约本底。

Conclusion: 单光子过程在Belle II中具有双重意义：既是测量中微子电弱耦合参数的有效物理通道，又是高亮度下暗玻色子搜索不可忽视的背景来源，需要综合考虑其对新物理探测前景的影响。

Abstract: The mono-photon process $e^+e^- \to γ+\mathrm{invisible}$ at Belle II probes both invisible new states and the electroweak couplings of neutrinos. In this work, we study the process $e^+e^- \to γν\barν$, finding that Belle II with the full expected dataset can determine the neutrino-sensitive effective weak mixing angle to a 3.6$\%$ relative statistical precision, competitive with existing neutrino probes at other energy scales. Polarized beams, as proposed for the Chiral Belle upgrade, separate the neutral- and charged-current contributions and provide independent access to each, giving a handle on the chiral structure of the neutrino gauge interactions. The same process is an irreducible background to dark boson searches, producing a neutrino fog once the luminosity exceeds 1 ab$^{-1}$.

</details>


### [160] [Searching for Axions on a Higher Note: Third-Harmonic Generation from Colliding High-Intensity Laser Beams](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.00123)
*Ashis Paul,Leone di Mauro Villari,Davide Tedeschi,Carino Ferrante,Luca Di Luzio,Andrea Marini*

Main category: hep-ph

TL;DR: 本文提出一种基于三阶谐波产生（third-harmonic generation）的实验方案，用以探测轴子类粒子（axion-like particles），该方案利用两束非共线偏振高强激光（峰值强度约10^24 W/cm^2）激发轴子场，从而产生轴子诱导的三阶谐波信号，并可在宽范围的轴子质量与耦合参数下获得可探测信号。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有轴子探测手段对参数空间的覆盖有限，作者希望利用新一代高功率激光设施，探索尚未被涉足的轴子参数区域，为下一代激光基轴子搜索铺路。

Method: 从轴子修正的麦克斯韦方程组出发，解析推导两束非共线、偏振高强激光束产生的轴子诱导三阶谐波场，并可通过调节两束光之间的夹角实现轴子-光子转换率的共振增强。

Result: 利用当前最先进的拍瓦（petawatt）级激光设施即可在宽广的轴子质量与耦合范围内获得可探测的三阶谐波信号，且共振增强机制不依赖于实验装置的物理体积。

Conclusion: 所提构型能够探测轴子参数空间中尚未被研究的区域，可作为下一代高功率激光基轴子搜索的实验方案基础。

Abstract: We propose a new laboratory strategy to generate and detect axion-like particles via third-harmonic generation induced by two non-collinear, polarised high-intensity laser beams of peak intensity of the order of $10^{24}\,\mathrm{W/cm^2}$, where the third-harmonic signal is generated by the axion field. Starting from the axion-modified Maxwell equations, we analytically derive the axion-induced third-harmonic field, and show that by using state-of-the-art petawatt laser facilities, a detectable signal can be obtained over a broad range of axion masses and couplings. A key feature of the setup is that the axion-photon conversion rate can be resonantly enhanced by tuning the angle between the two beams through a mechanism that does not depend on the physical volume of the apparatus. The proposed configuration may therefore probe an unexplored region of axion parameter space and pave the way for next-generation high-power laser-based axion searches.

</details>


### [161] [The recasting bound atlas on pair production of vector-like quarks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.00127)
*Thomas Flacke,Manuel Kunkel*

Main category: hep-ph

TL;DR: 本文对矢量型夸克（vector-like quark）对产生过程进行了全面的重铸（recasting）研究，覆盖其衰变至第三代夸克与标准模型玻色子或奇异顶夸克耦合标量子的所有通道，导出了43种衰变通道组合的重铸界限，并发布了包含重铸结果和示例代码的数据库，以便将限制应用于不同模型而无需重新运行重铸工具。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 矢量型夸克的寻找是超越标准模型物理的重要前沿方向，但已有的重铸分析往往针对特定衰变通道，缺乏对多种衰变组合的系统性覆盖。作者希望提供一个综合性的重铸研究框架，为不同模型提供便捷的约束应用途径。

Method: 系统考虑矢量型夸克衰变为第三代夸克与标准模型玻色子或奇异顶夸克耦合标量子的情况，逐一推导全部43种衰变通道组合的重铸界限；利用各通道效率，通过对每个信号区域（signal region）的贡献进行加总来获得多通道组合限制；将全部结果和示例代码整理成公开数据库。

Result: 成功得到了所有43种衰变通道组合的联合限制，建立了包含每个通道效率的重铸结果数据库，并通过对多种示例模型施加界限验证了该数据库的适用性与实用性。

Conclusion: 该重铸数据库可帮助不同理论模型直接获取矢量型夸克对产生的实验约束，避免了针对每种模型重复运行完整重铸工具的计算开销，从而极大提升了模型约束分析的效率与可扩展性。

Abstract: We present a comprehensive recasting study of pair production of vector-like quarks. We consider decays of vector-like quarks into third generation quarks and Standard Model bosons or exotic top-philic scalars. We derive recasting bounds on all 43 combinations of decay channels. Using the efficiencies of each channel, we obtain combined limits across decay channels by adding up the contributions to each signal region. A data base of recasting results along with example codes is published, allowing for application to different models without the need for rerunning recasting tools. We demonstrate the utility of the data base by setting bounds on several sample models.

</details>


### [162] [Dynamics and Frequency Conversion of Accreting Axion Clouds](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.00128)
*Ximeng Li,Zhen Zhong,Yifan Chen,Yue Zhao,Hidetoshi Omiya,Vitor Cardoso*

Main category: hep-ph

TL;DR: 本文研究了轴子（axion）场围绕致密天体（compact objects）通过自相互作用驱动的环境波弛豫形成指数增长引力云的过程。在振幅接近轴子衰变常数时，非线性效应变得显著，作者识别出由引力精细结构常数（gravitational fine-structure constant）和云增长速率决定的两种末期演化区域：Bosenova区（伴随爆炸性轴子爆发的坍缩）和饱和区（自相互作用诱导的轴子发射与吸积相平衡）。后者中，发射的轴子辐射呈现稳定的离散谱线，位于束缚态能量的奇数倍，可直接探测轴子势超出其二次极小值的全局结构。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 理解轴子云非线性演化的末期行为，特别是自相互作用在强失稳和稳定态之间如何决定最终归宿；同时希望通过观测到特征性的离散谱线来揭示轴子势的全局结构和底层自相互作用形式，进而探测其紫外完备性（ultraviolet completion）。

Method: 通过理论分析研究轴子波在致密天体周围自相互作用驱动的弛豫过程，依据引力精细结构常数和云增长率的取值划分演化区域，并对单余弦势（single-cosine potential）和类QCD轴子势（QCD axion-like potential）两种势模型分别预测发射光谱特征。

Result: 识别出两种不同演化区域：Bosenova区与饱和区。在饱和区，轴子辐射具有稳定的离散谱线，频率位于束缚态能量的奇数倍；单余弦势和类QCD轴子势预测出发射谱存在显著差异，可区分不同轴子自相互作用结构。

Conclusion: 饱和区的离散谱线直接反映轴子势的全局形状，为通过地面探测来自致密天体的相对论性轴子通量来研究轴子自相互作用的底层结构和紫外完备性提供了原理上可行的途径。

Abstract: Axion fields can form exponentially growing gravitational clouds around compact objects through self-interaction-driven relaxation of ambient axion waves. As the field amplitude approaches the axion decay constant, nonlinear effects become important. We identify two distinct regimes of late-time evolution, determined by the gravitational fine-structure constant and the cloud growth rate: a Bosenova regime, characterized by collapse accompanied by explosive axion bursts, and a saturation regime, in which self-interaction-induced axion emission balances accretion. In the latter regime, the emitted axion radiation exhibits stable discrete spectral lines at odd multiples of the bound-state energy, directly probing the global structure of the axion potential beyond its quadratic minimum. We show that single-cosine potentials and QCD axion-like potentials predict distinct emission spectra, enabling probes of the underlying axion self-interaction structure and its ultraviolet completion through terrestrial detection of relativistic axion fluxes from compact objects.

</details>


### [163] [Anomaly detection for multijet scenarios](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.00132)
*Gregor Kasieczka,Sung Hak Lim,Louis Moureaux,Tore von Schwartz,David Shih,Chitrakshee Yede*

Main category: hep-ph

TL;DR: 本文展示了将喷注重子结构物理（jet substructure physics）中的递归软衰减（recursive soft drop）方法与CATHODE异常检测技术相结合，可同时对未态含任意数量喷注（jets）的信号进行异常检测（anomaly detection），大幅扩展了此类搜索的适用范围。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LHC上尚未发现超出标准模型（beyond the Standard Model）的物理信号；现有基于弱监督（weak supervision）的异常检测方法虽取得成功，但受限于依赖共振子（resonance）衰变为双喷注（pair of jets）的假设，适用范围有限。

Method: 将喷注重子结构物理中成熟的递归软降（recursive soft drop）思想与CATHODE异常检测技术相结合，实现对未态含任意数量喷注信号的联合异常检测。

Result: 该方法能够同时处理未态含任意数量喷注的信号，显著扩大了异常检测搜索的覆盖范围。

Conclusion: 将递归软降与CATHODE结合是扩展弱监督异常检测适用范围的有效途径，可为寻找超出标准模型新物理信号提供更具普适性的检测工具。

Abstract: Signals of physics beyond the Standard Model continue to resist discovery at the LHC. Recent years have seen the proliferation of new anomaly detection techniques, promising discovery with significantly fewer model assumptions than traditional approaches. Strategies based on weak supervision have been especially successful, but so far were largely limited in scope by their reliance on a resonance manifesting a decay into a pair of jets. In this work, we demonstrate that a well-established idea from jet substructure physics - recursive soft drop - in combination with the CATHODE technique for anomaly detection can be used to simultaneously perform anomaly detection for signals with an arbitrary number of jets in the final state, greatly increasing the scope of such searches.

</details>


### [164] [Two-Stage Machine Learning Strategy for Scalar and Vector Leptoquark Discrimination at the LHC](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.00270)
*Ernesto Arganda,Martín de los Rios,Andres D. Perez,Rosa M. Sandá Seoane,Alejandro Szynkman*

Main category: hep-ph

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于两阶段机器学习推理管线的框架，用于在大型强子对撞机上区分标量轻子夸克（SLQ）和矢量轻子夸克（VLQ）信号，并通过模拟伪实验验证了该方法的稳健性和统计一致性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 轻子夸克（leptoquark, LQ）是许多超出标准模型理论中预言的新粒子，其自旋性质（标量或矢量）是理解其本质及背后物理机制的关键；然而现有方法在LHC复杂末态下难以有效区分SLQ与VLQ假设。

Method: 采用两阶段推理管线：第一阶段训练分类器将标准模型背景与混合LQ信号区分开，并据此推断信号产额；第二阶段利用该产额信息引导标量与矢量质量假设，训练第二个分类器专门区分SLQ与VLQ场景。随后从分类器输出构造检验统计量（test statistic），并利用参考概率密度函数进行解释。该方法应用于包含强子衰变tau轻子、多喷注及缺失横动量（missing transverse momentum）的现实LHC末态。

Result: 通过模拟伪实验（pseudo-experiments）评估，该策略在广泛的轻子夸克质量和信号强度范围内提供了稳健且统计一致的SLQ与VLQ判别程序。研究发现自旋识别能力与发现潜力紧密相关，表明确定新发现轻子夸克的自旋性质所需的样本量并不显著大于实现发现本身所需的样本量。

Conclusion: 所提出的机器学习框架能够在大质量范围内有效区分SLQ与VLQ信号，而且自旋鉴别所需的统计量级与发现新粒子的需求相当，这为未来LHC实验中轻子夸克自旋性质的确定提供了一条实用且高效的路径。

Abstract: We present a machine learning framework for the characterization of leptoquark (LQ) signals at the Large Hadron Collider, focusing on the discrimination between scalar (SLQ) and vector (VLQ) hypotheses. The method is based on a two-stage inference pipeline that combines a classifier trained to separate Standard Model backgrounds from a mixed LQ signal with a second classifier designed to distinguish between SLQ and VLQ scenarios, using the signal yield inferred from the first-stage classifier to guide the corresponding scalar and vector mass hypotheses. A test statistic is constructed from the classifier outputs and interpreted using reference probability density functions. The approach is applied to realistic LHC final states with hadronically decaying tau leptons, multiple jets, and missing transverse momentum, and its performance is assessed using simulated pseudo-experiments. We show that the proposed strategy provides a robust and statistically consistent procedure to discriminate between SLQ and VLQ signals across a wide range of masses and signal stregths. We find that the spin identification power closely follows the discovery potential, demonstrating that determining the spin nature of a newly discovered LQ does not require substantially larger datasets than those needed for discovery itself.

</details>


### [165] [Mixing-angle sensitivity of inclusive $Ξ_{bc} $ and $Ω_{bc}$ lifetimes](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.00660)
*Hai-Yang Cheng,Chia-Wei Liu,Fanrong Xu*

Main category: hep-ph

TL;DR: 该研究对 Ξ_bc^+、Ξ_bc^0 和 Ω_bc^0 的重子（baryon）寿命进行了系统计算，重点考察自旋零与自旋一 bc-双夸克（diquark）构型间的混合效应。采用均匀袋模型（homogeneous bag model, HBM）和非相对论夸克模型（nonrelativistic quark model, NRQM）两种框架评估强子矩阵元，得出寿命三元组并揭示纯自旋构型无法完整描述混合角依赖的结论。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 双重味重子的寿命研究对理解强子内部动力学具有重要意义，而 bc-双夸克自旋混合对重子寿命的影响此前尚未被充分阐明。

Method: 在两种夸克模型（均匀袋模型 HBM 和非相对论夸克模型 NRQM）中评估相关强子矩阵元（hadronic matrix elements），并对自旋零与自旋一 bc-双夸克构型间的混合角进行参数化扫描，以基准混合角进行数值计算。

Result: 在基准混合角下，HBM 与 NRQM 分别给出两组寿命三元组（皮秒量级）；相较于未混合的自旋一参考点，Ξ_bc^0 与 Ω_bc^0 的寿命下降约 17%–19%。寿命曲线的极值出现在混合角 |φ_B|≈27°–63° 处，而非纯自旋 S_bc=0 或 S_bc=1 的构型。

Conclusion: 仅考虑纯自旋极限（spin-zero 与 spin-one）不足以刻画混合角对寿命的完整影响；必须纳入张量混合构型才能准确预测双重味底-粲重子的寿命行为。

Abstract: We study the inclusive lifetimes of $Ξ_{bc}^{+}$, $Ξ_{bc}^{0}$, and $Ω_{bc}^{0}$, with emphasis on the mixing between the spin-zero and spin-one $bc$-diquark configurations. The relevant hadronic matrix elements are evaluated in both the homogeneous bag model (HBM) and the nonrelativistic quark model (NRQM). At the benchmark mixing angles of $-23.0^\circ$ for $Ξ_{bc}$ and $-23.5^\circ$ for $Ω_{bc}$, the corresponding lifetime triplets are $\left(τ_{Ξ_{bc}^{+}},τ_{Ξ_{bc}^{0}},τ_{Ω_{bc}^{0}}\right)=\left(0.421^{+0.093}_{-0.093},0.075^{+0.035}_{-0.010},0.223^{+0.074}_{-0.043}\right)\,\mathrm{ps}$ in the HBM and $\left(τ_{Ξ_{bc}^{+}},τ_{Ξ_{bc}^{0}},τ_{Ω_{bc}^{0}}\right)=\left(0.367^{+0.076}_{-0.070},0.100^{+0.032}_{-0.014},0.229^{+0.066}_{-0.042}\right)\,\mathrm{ps}$ in the NRQM. Relative to the unmixed spin-one reference points, the $Ξ_{bc}^{0}$ and $Ω_{bc}^{0}$ lifetimes decrease by approximately $17\%$-$19\%$. Neither the minimum nor the maximum of the central lifetime curves occurs at a pure $S_{bc}=0$ or $S_{bc}=1$ configuration; the extrema instead lie at mixed angles with $\left|φ_{\mathcal{B}}\right|\simeq27^\circ$-$63^\circ$. The two pure-spin limits alone are therefore insufficient to characterize the full mixing-angle dependence.

</details>


### [166] [Saturation effects in diffractive deep inelastic scattering](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.01163)
*Jani Penttala,Christophe Royon,Jarno Vierros*

Main category: hep-ph

TL;DR: 本文研究光子诱导过程中c和b夸克产生作为胶子饱和（gluon saturation）探针的可能性，重点针对LHC超外围碰撞（ultra-peripheral collisions, UPCs）中的高能区域。通过比较包含及不包含饱和效应时使用非线性Balitsky–Kovchegov方程与线性Balitsky–Fadin–Kuraev–Lipatov方程演化的偶极子振幅，预测包容性与衍射性重夸克产生截面。饱和模型中相干衍射事件的占比约为20%，约为基于部分子分布函数加阴影效应预测值的两倍，表明UPC中衍射性重夸克产生的测量可为区分量子色动力学的线性与非线性区域提供灵敏探针。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 高能重离子与质子物理中，胶子饱和是一个关键但尚未被充分实验确认的现象。利用UPC中光子诱导过程产生重夸克，有望提供对胶子饱和效应的独特且灵敏的检验途径。

Method: 论文比较了两种理论框架下的理论预言：采用非线性Balitsky–Kovchegov方程（含饱和效应）与线性Balitsky–Fadin–Kuraev–Lipatov方程（不含饱和效应）分别对偶极子振幅（dipole amplitude）进行演化，进而计算包容性与衍射性重夸克（c和b夸克）产生的截面及事件占比。

Result: 在饱和模型中，相干衍射事件的比例被预测约为20%，该值约为基于部分子分布函数（parton distribution functions）与阴影效应（shadowing）预言值的两倍。

Conclusion: UPC中对衍射性重夸克产生的测量可能为区分量子色动力学（quantum chromodynamics）中的线性和非线性区域提供灵敏的探针，有助于实验检验胶子饱和现象。

Abstract: We study the production of $c$ and $b$ quarks in photon-induced processes as a probe of gluon saturation, with a focus on the high-energy region accessible in ultra-peripheral collisions (UPCs) at the LHC. We compare predictions for inclusive and diffractive heavy quark production with and without saturation effects by using the nonlinear Balitsky--Kovchegov and the linear Balitsky--Fadin--Kuraev--Lipatov equations for the evolution of the dipole amplitude. The percentage of coherent diffractive events is predicted to be $~20\%$ in the saturation model, which is approximately twice as large as predictions based on parton distribution functions and shadowing. This indicates that measurements of diffractive heavy quark production in UPCs may provide a sensitive probe for distinguishing linear and nonlinear regimes of quantum chromodynamics.

</details>


### [167] [Angular correlations and polarization in $Z$+jet production at the LHC](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.01203)
*Francesco Giovanni Celiberto,Francesca Lonigro*

Main category: hep-ph

TL;DR: 本文研究了大强子对撞机（LHC）上Drell–Yan加喷注产生过程中的高能QCD动力学，在次领头对数精度下重整化求和（resummation）高能对数，并与次领头阶（NLO）固定阶计算一致结合，发展了JETHAD–DYnamis–POWHEG策略以获得真实轻子级预言，确立Z+喷注产生作为Run 3和HL-LHC高能QCD动力学的高精度探针。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LHC上的精密研究日益需要对超越固定阶微扰论的QCD辐射进行理论控制，特别是在大快度分离（rapidity separation）的终态中，高能对数能产生量级达百分之十的修正（如希格斯产生中最近观测到的），因此需要系统性地纳入这些修正以提高精度。

Method: 研究以Drell–Yan加喷注产生过程为对象，重点分析Z玻色子终态及其角分布与极化结构。高能对数在次领头对数（next-to-leading logarithmic）精度下被重整化求和，并与次领头阶（NLO）固定阶信息一致合并；框架进一步解析过程的方位角谐波含量，并将JETHAD–DYnamis–POWHEG策略发展为保留自旋关联、衰变角信息和实验可及运动学的真实轻子级预言。

Result: 除包容性快度和横动量谱外，该框架能够解析过程的方位角谐波结构，其模式因领头阶（LO）到次领头阶（NLO）发射动力学的转变而被重塑，并提供兼具自旋关联与衰变角信息的轻子级预测。

Conclusion: 该工作将Z+喷注产生确立为Run 3和HL-LHC时期高能QCD动力学的高精度探针（precision probe），为电弱尺度观测量中的高能对数效应提供了系统的理论控制手段。

Abstract: Precision studies at the LHC increasingly require theoretical control over QCD radiation beyond fixed-order perturbation theory, particularly in final states characterised by large separations in rapidity. High-energy logarithms can generate corrections at the ten-percent level even for electroweak-scale observables, as recently observed in Higgs production, motivating their systematic inclusion in precision analyses. We investigate this regime through Drell$-$Yan plus jet production, with particular emphasis on $Z$-boson final states and their angular and polarisation structure. High-energy logarithms are resummed at next-to-leading logarithmic accuracy and consistently combined with NLO fixed-order information. Beyond inclusive rapidity and transverse-momentum spectra, the framework resolves the azimuthal harmonic content of the process, whose pattern is reshaped by the transition from leading- to next-to-leading-order emission dynamics. We further develop the JETHAD$-$DYnamis$-$POWHEG strategy towards realistic lepton-level predictions, retaining spin correlations, decay-angle information, and experimentally accessible kinematics. This programme establishes $Z$+jet production as a precision probe of high-energy QCD dynamics for Run~3 and the HL-LHC.

</details>


### [168] [Fermionic Dark Matter Absorption and the High-Energy Event in LUX-ZEPLIN](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.01592)
*Yuanchao Lou,hih-Ting Lu*

Main category: hep-ph

TL;DR: 本研究提出LUX-ZEPLIN（LZ）实验中观察到的高能核反冲窗口候选事件可由费米子暗物质在氙核上的中性流（neutral-current）吸收过程自然解释，但重铸KamLAND数据的新约束与这一解释存在显著矛盾。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LZ实验在$248\pm32.5\ \mathrm{keV}_{\mathrm{nr}}$高能核反冲窗口中报告了单个候选事件，而低能谱与背景预期一致，需要对此异常信号提出物理解释并检验其可行性。

Method: 作者构建了暗物质在氙核上的中性流吸收理论框架：对$m_\chi\simeq247\ \mathrm{MeV}$的暗物质，相干吸收产生单能核反冲，但在此动量转移下过程进入非相干（incoherent）区域，单核子散射产生宽谱反冲；并利用单个有效场论（EFT）耦合计算事件率，同时对标KamLAND数据对中子发射道进行重铸（recasting）分析。

Result: 单个有效场论耦合可同时产生LZ窗口内的一个事件并满足邻近能区的零观测约束，所需单核子吸收截面为$\sigma_{\chi N}^{\mathrm{NC}}=1.07\times10^{-46}\ \mathrm{cm}^2$，对应有效场论能标$\Lambda\simeq11.5\ \mathrm{TeV}$。然而KamLAND中子发射道数据排除了该基准参数空间，与LZ过剩解释存在显著张力。

Conclusion: LZ过剩的暗物质中性流吸收解释受到大体积闪烁体探测器已有约束的严重挑战，需通过未来专门的高能分析来分辨这一张力并验证该解释。

Abstract: The LUX-ZEPLIN (LZ) experiment has reported a single candidate event in the high-energy nuclear recoil window $248\pm32.5\ \mathrm{keV}_{\mathrm{nr}}$ with an exposure of $2.80\ \mathrm{ton}\cdot\mathrm{yr}$, while the low-energy spectrum remains consistent with background expectations. We demonstrate that this excess can be naturally explained by the neutral-current absorption of fermionic dark matter on xenon nuclei. For a dark matter mass $m_χ\simeq 247\ \mathrm{MeV}$, the coherent absorption process produces a monoenergetic nuclear recoil at $E_R \simeq 248\ \mathrm{keV}_{\mathrm{nr}}$. At this momentum transfer, the absorption process enters the incoherent regime, where scattering off individual nucleons produces a broad recoil spectrum extending from $\sim 200\ \mathrm{keV}$ to $100.2\ \mathrm{MeV}$. We show that a single effective field theory coupling can simultaneously produce one event in the $248\pm32.5\ \mathrm{keV}_{\mathrm{nr}}$ window while remaining consistent with the non-observation of events in neighboring energy regions. The required single-nucleon absorption cross section is $σ_{χN}^{\mathrm{NC}} = 1.07\times10^{-46}\ \mathrm{cm}^2$, corresponding to an effective field theory scale $Λ\simeq 11.5\ \mathrm{TeV}$. However, a recasting analysis of KamLAND data on the neutron-emission channel $χ+{}^{12}\mathrm{C} \to ν+ n + {}^{11}\mathrm{C}^*$ excludes this benchmark parameter space, establishing a significant tension between the LZ excess interpretation and existing constraints from large-volume scintillator detectors. We discuss the implications of this tension and prospects for resolving it with future dedicated high-energy analyses.

</details>


### [169] [The Type-I Seesaw as a Relativistic Fermionic Proximity Effect: Spectral Moments, EFT Consistency, and Sterile-Sector Reconstruction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.00888)
*Jianlong Lu*

Main category: hep-ph

TL;DR: 本文将第一类跷跷板机制（type-I seesaw）重新表述为活性扇区两点核中相对论性费米子邻近效应（proximity effect）。通过积分掉惰性（sterile）Majorana配对场，得到一个非局域的Nambu-Gorkov嵌入自能，其低能展开产生相关的轻子数破坏与守恒的矩塔结构。作者证明了核的块Hankel正定性与维数重标度混合Nambu-Hankel序列的正定性，并推导出矩阵Cauchy-Schwarz不等式及孤立非简并极点留数的非线性相容性恒等式，最终给出树级有效场论（EFT）一致性检验与矩阵束重构方案。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 活性中微子（active neutrinos）不具有可重整化的规范不变Majorana质量项，需通过引入惰性场来解释其极轻质量，即第一类跷跷板机制。本文将这一机制从紫外模型视角重新表述为活性扇区两点核的低能邻近效应结构，旨在揭示惰性场积分后所遗留的数学结构（如极点留数、矩塔与Hankel正定性），从而建立系统的、可检验的树级有效场论一致性判据。

Method: 研究采用活性场经轻子数守恒的Yukawa杂化与惰性Majorana配对核耦合的形式化方案。通过积分掉惰性场获得非局域Nambu-Gorkov嵌入自能，并在惰性Takagi基下计算其反常与正常味响应函数A(Q²)与B(Q²)。对二者进行低能矩展开，并与Weinberg系数和六维动力学算符匹配。利用块Hankel序列与维数重标度混合Nambu-Hankel序列的正定性分析，推导矩阵不等式、极点留数相容性恒等式，并构建可见极点与聚合留数的矩阵束重构算法

Result: 反常与正常响应函数的低能展开分别产生相关的轻子数破坏与守恒矩塔，其前几阶矩对应于Weinberg系数与六维动力学算符系数。两类矩塔共享同一套惰性质量集，其正正常支撑提供共同零化元，但在精确简并情形下反常留数可能抵消。证明正常块Hankel正定性和维数重标度混合序列正定性，由此导出守恒与破坏数据之间的矩阵Cauchy-Schwarz不等式，可揭示被简并正常留数遮蔽的配对方向。孤立非简并极点留数满足非线性相容性恒等式。单代精确例表明静态Schur补核与物理Takagi极点质量不同。

Conclusion: 研究表明第一类跷跷板的邻近效应结构不需要费米面或凝聚体的存在，且Nambu加倍不引入新物理自由度。该框架提供树级EFT一致性测试以及从可见极点重构质量与留数的矩阵束方法；但唯一确定性紫外拉氏量仍需要关于惰性基多重度的额外信息。该结果深化了对跷跷板机制低能唯象的可检验结构理解。

Abstract: We formulate the type-I seesaw as a relativistic fermionic proximity effect in the exact active-sector two-point kernel. Active neutrinos have no renormalizable gauge-invariant Majorana mass and couple by lepton-number-conserving Yukawa hybridization to a sterile Majorana pairing kernel. Integrating out the sterile fields gives a nonlocal Nambu-Gorkov embedding self-energy. In a sterile Takagi basis its anomalous and normal flavor responses are $\mathcal{A}(Q^2)=\sum_i M_i y_i y_i^T/(Q^2+M_i^2)$ and $\mathcal{B}(Q^2)=\sum_i y_i y_i^\dagger/(Q^2+M_i^2)$, respectively. Their low-energy expansions generate correlated lepton-number-violating and lepton-number-conserving moment towers. The leading moments reproduce the Weinberg coefficient and the dimension-six kinetic operator. Both towers share one sterile mass set; its positive normal support supplies a common annihilator, although anomalous residues can cancel at exact degeneracy. We prove normal block-Hankel positivity and positivity of a dimensionally rescaled mixed Nambu-Hankel sequence. The latter gives matrix Cauchy-Schwarz inequalities between lepton-number-conserving and lepton-number-violating data and can reveal paired directions hidden by a degenerate normal residue. Isolated nondegenerate pole residues satisfy an additional nonlinear compatibility identity. These results yield tree-level EFT consistency tests and a matrix-pencil reconstruction of visible pole and aggregate-residue data; selecting a unique ultraviolet Lagrangian requires additional information about sterile-basis multiplicities. An exact one-generation example distinguishes the static Schur-complement kernel from the physical Takagi pole mass. The proximity structure applies without a Fermi surface or condensate, and Nambu doubling introduces no new physical degrees of freedom.

</details>


### [170] [Radiative Corrections and Reheating in Supergravity Models of Inflation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.00221)
*John Ellis,Tony Gherghetta,Kunio Kaneta,Wenqi Ke,Keith A. Olive*

Main category: hep-ph

TL;DR: 本文研究了类Starobinsky暴胀模型中的辐射修正（radiative corrections）效应，聚焦于N=1无标度超引力（no-scale supergravity）框架下的暴胀模型。研究表明暴胀子与物质场的耦合会产生圈图修正，其中与超引力耦合相关的修正可忽略，但利用Planck数据对GUT双线性耦合、真空期望值（vevs）及规范玻色子质量给出了有意义的约束，未来CMB测量可能进一步收紧这些限制。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 暴胀子（inflaton）与物质场的耦合对于再加热（reheating）过程是必要的，但这类耦合会对暴胀子势产生圈图（loop）修正，可能影响暴胀的可观测预言。因此需要系统评估这些辐射修正的效应及其对模型参数空间的约束。

Method: 作者在N=1无标度超引力框架下构造类Starobinsky暴胀模型，分别考察暴胀子与超对称标准模型（MSSM）场及大统一理论（GUT）场耦合所产生的圈图修正，并结合Planck卫星的CMB数据对模型参数施加约束。

Result: 研究发现，暴胀子与标准模型（MSSM）场通过超引力耦合产生的修正可以忽略不计；然而，GUT层次的双线性耦合、真空期望值及规范玻色子质量受到Planck数据的显著约束，且当前约束可能在未来的CMB观测中进一步收紧。

Conclusion: 类Starobinsky暴胀模型的辐射修正在不同耦合途径下影响差异显著，Planck数据为GUT相关参数提供了有价值的约束，未来更高精度的CMB测量有望增强对这些参数的限制能力。

Abstract: We consider the effects of radiative corrections in Starobinsky-like models of inflation, concentrating on models of inflation formulated in N=1 no-scale supergravity. Inflaton couplings to matter fields are necessary for reheating and these have an impact on loop corrections to the inflaton potential. Whilst corrections due to the supergravity couplings of the inflaton to Standard Model (MSSM) fields are negligible, we use {\it Planck} data to obtain interesting constraints on GUT bilinear couplings, vevs, and gauge boson masses that could be sharpened by future CMB measurements.

</details>


### [171] [Status of the strong $CP$ problem](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.00620)
*Wen-Yuan Ai*

Main category: hep-ph

TL;DR: 本文是一篇教学型综述（pedagogical review），系统阐述了量子色动力学中强CP问题（strong CP problem）的物理学基础，总结了其经典解决方案——佩切伊-奎因机制（Peccei-Quinn mechanism）及QCD轴子（QCD axion）的理论与唯象学现状，同时涵盖近年提出的替代性解决方案及其新视角。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 强CP问题仍是粒子物理标准模型（Standard Model）中尚未解决的重要开放问题之一，其经典解法（Peccei-Quinn机制）虽广为接受，但近年来关于其基础及替代方案的讨论日益增多，亟需系统梳理当前的理论与唯象学进展。

Method: 本文采用教学式综述（review）的方式展开：首先以教育性（pedagogical）的视角阐述强CP问题背后的基础物理原理，随后系统梳理该问题的经典解（Peccei-Quinn机制与QCD轴子预言），并涵盖近年涌现的替代性解法与对问题基础的新视角。

Result: 文章汇总了强CP问题的当前理论状态（theoretical status）与唯象学状态（phenomenological status），包括Peccei-Quinn机制和QCD轴子作为最知名方案的内容，以及近年来受到再度关注的替代解和新基础视角。

Conclusion: 强CP问题虽已在Peccei-Quinn机制框架下获得经典解答，但该问题的基础性与替代解仍在积极研究之中；本综述旨在为读者提供理解这些物理原理及其现状的综合性教学参考。

Abstract: The strong $CP$ problem remains one of the major open questions in the Standard Model of particle physics. Its best-known solution is the Peccei--Quinn mechanism, which predicts a new pseudoscalar boson, the QCD axion. In recent years, alternative solutions and new perspectives on the foundations of the strong $CP$ problem have also received renewed attention. In this review, we provide a pedagogical account of the underlying physics and summarise the current theoretical and phenomenological status of the problem.

</details>


### [172] [Understanding IR singularities in the Loop-Tree Duality](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.00869)
*Germán Rodrigo,Leandro Cieri,Prasanna K. Dhani,Roger J. Hernández-Pinto,Jorge J. Martínez de Lejarza,Salvador A. Ochoa-Oregon,Konstantinos Pyretzidis,Selomit Ramírez-Uribe,David F. Rentería-Estrada,Andrés E. Rentería-Olivo,José Ríos-Sánchez,German F. R. Sborlini,Surabhi Tiwari,Juan P. Uribe-Ramírez*

Main category: hep-ph

TL;DR: 本文综述了环-树对偶（Loop-Tree Duality）表示在散射振幅中的最新进展，重点阐述了红外与阈值奇点的起源和消除机制，提出了一种高效构造有限积分的新策略，并介绍了基于量子比特（qubit）和量子电路（quantum circuit）编码因果与奇点结构的互补视角。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 环-树对偶表示的主要优势在于能使散射振幅中红外奇点和阈值奇点的起源变得尤为透明，因此有必要系统回顾并推进关于这些奇点如何在散射和真空振幅层面产生并相互抵消的理解。

Method: 本文采用综述性方法，回顾了环-树对偶表示中奇点产生与抵消的最新理论进展，提出了一种高效构造有限积分的新策略，并将其与基于量子比特和量子电路来编码底层因果结构与奇点结构的互补方法相结合进行讨论。

Result: 研究梳理了散射和真空振幅中奇点的涌现与抵消机制，提出了高效构造有限积分的创新方案，并展示了用量子比特与量子电路编码底层因果和奇点结构的可行性视角。

Conclusion: 环-树对偶表示不仅深化了对红外与阈值奇点起源和消除的理解，其与量子信息视角（量子比特、量子电路）的结合也为未来构造有限振幅积分提供了富有前景的互补路径。

Abstract: One of the main advantages of the Loop-Tree Duality representation of scattering amplitudes is that it makes the origin of infrared and threshold singularities particularly transparent. This talk reviews recent progress in describing how singularities emerge and cancel at the level of scattering and vacuum amplitudes, discusses a novel strategy to efficiently construct finite integrals, and presents a complementary perspective based on encoding the underlying causal and singular structure in terms of qubits and quantum circuits.

</details>


### [173] [Gravitational Waves from Reheating beyond Instantaneous Thermalization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.01480)
*Kyohei Mukaida,Tenta Tsuji*

Main category: hep-ph

TL;DR: 本文研究微扰再加热（perturbative reheating）过程中产生的引力波（gravitational-wave），不假设膨胀子（inflaton）衰变产物瞬间热化。通过介质中的近共线劈裂级联（受LPM效应支配）和热尺度附近的弹性散射实现热化，指出完全热化前的非热硬粒子群体会通过硬-软散射产生额外引力波成分，其谱指数在注入尺度转折以下为Ω_GW∝f^(1/2)，且各向同性化过程改变了红外区域的谱形。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 此前研究通常假设膨胀子衰变产物瞬时热化，但实际物理过程中存在有限的热化时间窗口，在此期间的非热粒子群体会产生区别于真空涨落背景的引力波信号，需要更准确的理论描述。

Method: 放弃瞬时热化假设，引入受朗道-庞漠兰楚克-米格达尔效应（Landau-Pomeranchuk-Migdal effect）支配的介质中近共线劈裂级联机制来描述高能粒子热化过程，并考虑热尺度附近的弹性散射引起的各项同性化效应。

Result: 发现完全热化前的非热硬粒子群体通过硬-软散射产生额外引力波分量，其谱满足Ω_GW∝f^(1/2)（在注入尺度转折之下）；各向同性化抹去真空韧致辐射软极点背后的方向信息，使深红外谱从Ω_GW∝f变为Ω_GW∝f^3。

Conclusion: 再加热期间的非瞬时热化过程会显著影响引力波谱形，导致与瞬时热化假设下不同的红外行为和额外的引力波分量，这对借助引力波信号探测宇宙早期再加热动力学具有重要意义。

Abstract: We study gravitational-wave production during perturbative reheating without assuming instantaneous thermalization of the inflaton decay products. The injected energetic particles thermalize through an in-medium cascade of nearly collinear splittings subject to the Landau-Pomeranchuk-Migdal effect and are ultimately isotropized by elastic scatterings near the thermal scale. The non-thermal hard population present before complete thermalization produces an additional gravitational-wave component through hard-soft scatterings, with $Ω_{\mathrm{GW}}\propto f^{1/2}$ below the injection-scale turnover. The same isotropization erases the directional information underlying the vacuum $1/k$ bremsstrahlung soft pole, changing the deep-infrared spectrum from $Ω_{\mathrm{GW}}\propto f$ to $Ω_{\mathrm{GW}}\propto f^3$.

</details>


### [174] [Higgsino Dark Matter Interpretation of the LUX-ZEPLIN 248 keV Nuclear-Recoil Event](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.01583)
*Katherine Freese,Dionysios P. Theodosopoulos*

Main category: hep-ph

TL;DR: 本文提出将Higgsino暗物质作为LUX-ZEPLIN（LZ）实验中观测到的248 keV核反冲事件的潜在解释。质量约1 TeV的近纯Higgsino通过Z玻色子的非对角耦合自然实现非弹性暗物质截面，预测结果在δ~350 keV处接近LZ发表的90%置信区间。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LZ实验报告了一个248 keV核反冲事件，但对多种稀有本底过程和探测器效应进行详细研究后未能识别出合理的解释来源，因此需要提出新的粒子物理解释方案。

Method: 作者提出质量约1 TeV的近纯Higgsino作为暗物质候选体，利用两个近简并中性Majorana态之间通过非对角Z耦合实现的非弹性暗物质（inelastic dark matter）机制，并利用电弱相互作用固定而非自由调节Higgsino与核子的非弹性散射截面。

Result: 预测的Higgsino非弹性散射截面在质量劈裂δ~350 keV时接近LZ发表的90%双侧置信区间，表明该方案与实验观测一致。

Conclusion: Higgsino暗物质可以成为LZ 248 keV事件的一个可行的理论解释，且其预测截面对应一个相对确定的参数区域。

Abstract: We propose Higgsino dark matter as a potential interpretation of the $248~\mathrm{keV}$ nuclear-recoil event of interest reported by the LUX-ZEPLIN (LZ) experiment. LZ studied several rare background processes and detector effects in detail, but did not identify any as a likely explanation of the event. A nearly pure Higgsino with mass $m_{\widetilde H}\sim1~\mathrm{TeV}$ naturally realizes inelastic dark matter through the off-diagonal $Z$ coupling of two nearly degenerate neutral Majorana states separated by a mass splitting $δ$. The same electroweak interaction fixes the inelastic Higgsino-nucleon scattering cross section, rather than leaving it as a free parameter. We show that the predicted Higgsino inelastic scattering cross section approaches the published LZ two-sided 90% confidence interval for $δ\sim350~\mathrm{keV}$.

</details>

### [175] [Algebra objects in direct limit completions of compact Lie group duals and the classification of $c=1$ vertex operator algebras](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.00347)
*Sebastiano Carpi,Tiziano Gaudio,Luca Giorgetti*

Main category: math.QA

TL;DR: 本文证明复约化仿射代数群G的对称张量范畴的Ind完备化中，任何至多可数维的简单交换代数对象必同构于某个约化子群H的齐次空间上的正则函数代数O(G/H)，并将此结果应用于顶点算子代数扩展理论，在假设V_{L_2}的A_5-轨道体的强有理性的前提下，分类了中心荷c=1的简单酉Virasoro顶点算子代数L(1,0)的所有满足特定谱条件的不一定有理的简单CFT型preunitary扩展。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 该研究的动机有两个层面：一是从范畴论层面理解复约化群的表示范畴中交换代数对象的结构，二是将此类代数分类结果推广到顶点算子代数（vertex operator algebra）扩展理论，以期对c=1强有理顶点算子代数实现完整分类，从而建立与共形网（conformal net）领域Feng Xu结果的顶点算子代数类比。

Method: 论文首先在复约化仿射代数群G的有限维有理表示构成的对称张量范畴C_G的Ind完备化（Ind-completion）中，对至多可数维的简单交换代数对象进行结构分类，证明其必为某个约化代数子群H的齐次空间上的正则函数代数形式。随后将这一范畴论结果引入顶点算子代数扩展理论，结合Xue（Feng Xu）的共形网结果框架，利用A_5-轨道体的强理性假设和字符的同余子群模性（congruence subgroup modularity），对L(1,0)满足谱条件的扩展进行分类。

Result: 该论文得到的主要结果包括：(1) C_G的Ind完备化中任何至多可数维的简单交换代数对象都与O(G/H)同构（其中H为G的约化代数子群）；(2)在V_{L_2}^{A_5}强有理性的假设下，分类了满足特定谱条件的简单CFT型preunitary的L(1,0)扩展；(3)所有强有理preunitary的L(1,0)扩展因字符的模性自动满足该谱条件；(4)作为推论，在不依赖V_{L_2}^{A_5}强有理性的前提下得到了c=1强有理顶点算子代数的完整分类。

Conclusion: 本文建立了群表示范畴中交换代数对象的分类定理，并将其成功应用于顶点算子代数扩展的分类，最终在V_{L_2}^{A_5}强有理性的条件下完成了c=1强有理顶点算子代数的分类，这一结果实现了Feng Xu在共形网框架中的分类定理在顶点算子代数理论中的对应版本。

Abstract: Let $G$ be a complex reductive affine algebraic group with its symmetric tensor category $\mathcal{C}_G$ of finite-dimensional rational representations. We prove that every simple commutative algebra object with at most countable dimension in the direct limit completion $\operatorname{Ind}(\mathcal{C}_G)$ of $\mathcal{C}_G$ is isomorphic to the algebra $\mathcal{O}(G/H)$ of regular functions on the homogeneous space $G/H$, for some reductive algebraic subgroup $H$ of $G$. Then we apply this result to the theory of vertex operator algebra extensions. In particular, assuming the strong rationality of the $ \operatorname{A}_5$-orbifold of the vertex operator algebra $V_{\mathcal{L}_2}$ associated with the rank-one root lattice $\mathcal{L}_2:= \sqrt{2}\mathbb{Z}$, we classify all the not necessarily rational simple CFT type preunitary vertex operator algebra extensions of the simple unitary Virasoro vertex operator algebra $L(1,0)$ with central charge $c=1$ satisfying a certain spectrum condition. This result is the vertex operator algebra analogue of a conformal net result by Feng Xu. Every strongly rational preunitary vertex operator algebra extension of $L(1,0)$ satisfies the above spectrum condition because of the congruence subgroup modularity property of its characters. As a consequence, we get a complete classification result for strongly rational $c=1$ vertex operator algebras, up to the strong rationality of $V_{\mathcal{L}_2}^{\operatorname{A}_5}$.

</details>

### [176] [Operator-valued maximal $f$-divergences for completely positive maps](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.00554)
*Rui Okayasu*

Main category: math.OA

TL;DR: 本文为冯·诺依曼代数间的完全正（CP）映射引入了一种取值于算子代数、与算子凸函数f相关的算子值极大f-散度（operator-valued maximal f-divergence），并证明了其多个关键性质，包括对共同CP上界的独立性、次可加性、单调性、鞅收敛定理与下半连续性。该构造在正规正泛函情形下恢复Hiai的极大f-散度，并解决了Hiai遗留的任意冯·诺依曼代数上联合弱下半连续性问题；对eta(t)=t log t得到算子值Belavkin–Staszewski（BS）相对熵，并在正规通道情形下证明其与Hollands–Ranallo的BS通道散度在扩展范数意义上一致。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 经典极大f-散度（maximal f-divergence）仅对正泛函（positive functionals）定义，缺乏对更一般CP映射之间的算子值推广；同时Hiai关于联合弱下半连续性的问题在任意冯·诺依曼代数上仍未解决，需要构造新的算子值框架来完善散度理论并与已知的BS通道散度建立联系。

Method: 文章引入一种算子值极大f-散度，针对冯·诺依曼代数间的完全正映射构造，关联定义在(0,+∞)上的算子凸函数f，其取值于余定义域冯·诺依曼代数扩展下半有界自伴部分；通过证明其对公共CP上界的不依赖性、联合次可加性、单预复合与正规后复合下的单调性、正规CP映射的鞅收敛定理以及点σ-弱拓扑下的联合下半连续性来确立该构造的数学性质。

Result: 对正规正泛函而言，该构造恢复了Hiai的极大f-散度，由此建立其任意冯·诺依曼代数上的联合弱下半连续性，回答了Hiai留下的开放问题。当f取eta(t)=t log t时得到算子值BS相对熵；对σ-有限余定义域的正规通道，证明BS通道散度与算子值散度的扩展范数一致。有限维例子与有限指标条件期望情形下，BS情形给出Jones–Kosaki指标的显式公式（取对数形式）。

Conclusion: 该算子值极大f-散度统一并推广了已有散度理论，填补了负定域冯·诺依曼代数间CP映射算子值散度框架的空白，并建立了与BS通道散度的等价性，同时通过显式公式将有限指标条件期望与Jones–Kosaki指标联系起来。

Abstract: We introduce an operator-valued maximal f-divergence for completely positive (CP) maps between von Neumann algebras, associated with an operator convex function f on (0,+infinity). The construction takes values in the extended lower-semibounded self-adjoint part of the codomain von Neumann algebra. We prove independence of the common CP upper bound used in its computation, joint subadditivity, monotonicity under unital precomposition and normal postcomposition, a martingale convergence theorem for normal CP maps, and joint lower semicontinuity in the point-sigma-weak topology.
  For normal positive functionals, our construction recovers Hiai's maximal f-divergence. As a consequence, we establish its joint weak lower semicontinuity for arbitrary von Neumann algebras, answering a question left open by Hiai.
  For eta(t)=t log t, we obtain an operator-valued Belavkin--Staszewski (BS) relative entropy. Moreover, for normal channels with sigma-finite codomain, we prove that the BS channel divergence of Hollands and Ranallo coincides with the extended norm of our operator-valued divergence.
  Finally, we give finite-dimensional examples and obtain an explicit formula for a finite-index conditional expectation. In the BS case, this formula reduces to the logarithm of the Jones--Kosaki index.

</details>

### [177] [Smoothed truncated Coulomb potential for periodic Gaussian-basis Hartree--Fock exchange](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.00203)
*Gengzhi Yang,Aamy Bakry,Hong-Zhou Ye*

Main category: physics.chem-ph

TL;DR: 本文引入光滑截断库仑（smoothed truncated Coulomb, sTC）势，通过对尖锐截断边界进行实空间高斯卷积平滑，并用单一无量纲参数η控制平滑窗口宽度，实现周期性高斯基组电子排斥积分（ERIs）的双空间算法，从而在赝势和全电子计算中系统逼近传统截断库仑（TC）势的精度，显著改善热力学极限收敛并降低有限尺寸误差。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 截断库仑（truncated Coulomb, TC）势虽能降低周期性哈特里-福克（Hartree-Fock, HF）计算的有限尺寸误差并加速热力学极限收敛，但在高斯基组中使用时，电子排斥积分（electron repulsion integrals, ERIs）的评估（尤其针对非球形截断域和全电子计算）存在显著复杂性。

Method: 引入光滑截断库仑（smoothed truncated Coulomb, sTC）势作为TC势的系统可改进近似：通过实空间高斯卷积（real-space Gaussian convolution）平滑尖锐截断边界，以单一无量纲参数η控制平滑窗口宽度并收紧以逼近TC参考；基于此平滑性设计了评估周期性高斯基组ERIs的双空间（dual-space）算法，无需大平面波基组或新的分子积分核，可适用于赝势和全电子计算，包括维格纳-赛茨（Wigner-Seitz）胞截断边界。

Result: 在绝缘体、半导体、层状、金属和分子晶体体系上的基准测试表明：赝势计算中sTC-HF结果紧密复现TC结果，并将TC质量的计算扩展到全电子场景；相比探针电荷Ewald方法，sTC在所有测试体系中显著改善热力学极限收敛，同时在全电子计算中保持实用性（而直接TC计算在该场景下计算代价高）。

Conclusion: sTC势是一种系统可改进、计算可行的TC势替代方案，兼具体系普适性与全电子兼容性，为周期性量子化学计算中的有限尺寸修正提供了有效途径。

Abstract: Truncated Coulomb (TC) potentials reduce finite-size errors and accelerate thermodynamic-limit convergence in periodic Hartree--Fock (HF) calculations, but their use with Gaussian basis sets is complicated by the evaluation of electron repulsion integrals (ERIs), particularly for nonspherical truncation domains and all-electron calculations. We introduce the smoothed truncated Coulomb (sTC) potential as a systematically improvable approximation to a parent TC potential. A real-space Gaussian convolution smooths the sharp truncation boundary, and a single dimensionless parameter, $η$, controls the width of the smoothing window, which can be tightened to systematically approach the TC reference. The smoothing by sTC enables a dual-space algorithm for evaluating periodic Gaussian-basis ERIs that requires neither a large plane-wave basis nor new molecular integral kernels and is applicable to both pseudopotential and all-electron calculations, including the important Wigner--Seitz-cell truncation boundaries. Benchmarks spanning insulating, semiconducting, layered, metallic, and molecular-crystal systems show that sTC-based HF closely reproduces TC results in pseudopotential calculations and extends TC-quality calculations to all-electron settings. Across these systems, sTC substantially improves thermodynamic-limit convergence relative to the probe-charge Ewald method while remaining practical for all-electron calculations in which direct TC-based calculations are computationally challenging.

</details>

### [178] [Calabi-Yau Threefold Singularities and their Universal Quilts](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.00021)
*Elizabeth Gasparim,Carlos Simpson,Bruno Suzuki,Rashid Talha*

Main category: math.AG

TL;DR: 本文针对三维环状卡拉比-丘奇点（toric CY3 singularities）引入"万有拼块"（universal quilt）概念，并构造了当万有拼块本身为光滑卡拉比-丘三维流形（smooth Calabi-Yau threefolds）时的具体例子。这类拼块被设计用于包含给定奇点的所有互不矛盾的消解（crepant resolutions）。作者还提出了实现算法来计算任意平面多边形的所有三角剖分（triangulations），并引入"拼块叠"（quilt stack）概念，以探讨给定奇点之前最可能的光滑配置问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 环状卡拉比-丘奇点的几何结构及其消解之间的关系是该领域的重要问题，但目前缺少一种统一包含所有互不矛盾的消解（crepant resolutions）的构造框架。作者希望寻找一个自然的光滑对象，能够同时承载给定奇点的全部互不矛盾的消解结构，并进一步探讨奇点之前可能的光滑配置。为此需要一种系统性算法来列举所有可能的相关组合结构。

Method: 本文首先引入万有拼块（universal quilt）这一概念，用于环状奇点框架下统一所有互不矛盾的消解；随后为构造这类拼块，开发了一种能够计算任意平面多边形所有三角剖分（triangulations）的算法。此外，作者定义了拼块叠（quilt stack）概念，以形式化探讨奇点对应的最可能光滑配置。

Result: 作者成功构造了万有拼块为光滑卡拉比-丘三维流形（smooth Calabi-Yau threefolds）的具体实例，从而验证了所引入概念的有效性。

Conclusion: 万有拼块与拼块叠为理解环状三维卡拉比-丘奇点与其各种光滑消解之间的整体关系提供了新的几何与组合工具，并为寻找给定奇点之前的光滑配置提供了系统性路径。

Abstract: We introduce the notion of universal quilt for toric CY3 singularities and construct examples when the universal quilts are themselves smooth Calabi-Yau threefolds. Such quilts are designed to contain all of the crepant resolutions of the given singularity. To obtain universal quilts we implement an algorithm that computes all triangulations for any given planar polygon. We also introduce the notion of quilt stack, to address the question of finding the most likely smooth configuration preceding a given singularity.

</details>


### [179] [Virasoro Constraints for Orbifold Curves](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.01300)
*Jianxun Hu,Pengrong Jin,Hua-Zhong Ke,Jingwei Lu,Hsian-Hua Tseng,Longting Wu*

Main category: math.AG

TL;DR: 本文证明了所有光滑射影有效轨道曲线上相对Gromov–Witten理论的Virasoro约束，作为推论得到绝对形式的Jiang–Tseng Virasoro猜想。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Virasoro约束是Gromov–Witten理论中的重要结构性恒等式，此前对更一般几何对象（如轨道曲线）的相对理论尚未被证明。

Method: 通过建立光滑射影有效轨道曲线在普通点处带相对条件的相对Gromov–Witten理论，并证明其满足Virasoro约束。

Result: 证明了相对Gromov–Witten理论的Virasoro约束，并由此导出绝对Jiang–Tseng Virasoro猜想作为推论。

Conclusion: 该结果将Virasoro约束理论推广到轨道曲线的相对Gromov–Witten理论，填补了此前的空白。

Abstract: We prove the Virasoro constraints for the relative Gromov--Witten theory of all smooth projective effective orbifold curves, allowing relative conditions at ordinary points. The absolute Jiang--Tseng Virasoro conjecture for smooth projective effective orbifold curves follows as a corollary.

</details>
