### [180] [Polarisation-mediated underscreening from weakly bonded ion clusters](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.00178)
*David Ribar,Jake W. Felber,Clifford E. Woodward,Jan Forsman*

Main category: cond-mat.soft

TL;DR: 本文提出高离子强度水溶液中离子形成松散簇（cluster）会降低有效离子强度并产生簇极化（cluster polarization），进而导致带同种电荷表面或粒子间出现更长程的排斥相互作用。作者推导了簇拓扑结构通过电荷加权二阶矩完全进入解析体相关系的结论，并用经典聚合物密度泛函理论（cDFT）数值模拟验证，定性再现了"反常欠屏蔽"（anomalous underscreening）现象，但定量预测的屏蔽长度增长弱于实验观测。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 实验观测到的"反常欠屏蔽"现象尚无满意解释，作者先前工作认为离子成簇可降低有效离子强度、减慢屏蔽长度衰减，但未能完全解释实验。本研究旨在探究成簇之外的另一贡献——簇极化，以检验其能否产生更长程的同种电荷排斥相互作用，从而更完整地解释该现象。

Method: 作者首先推导出解析体相关系，其中任意簇拓扑的极化响应仅由其电荷加权结构因子（charge-weighted second moment of intramolecular charge structure factor）的第二矩决定。随后基于星型簇模型（star-like clusters），即卫星离子通过简谐弹簧弱键合到公共中心离子，利用经典聚合物密度泛函理论（classical polymer Density Functional Theory, cDFT）进行数值计算，在不同盐浓度下计算表面相互作用和屏蔽长度。模型中假设卫星离子冕区净电荷为零，以避免高电荷簇带来的显著自能成本。

Result: 簇极化效应能在浓缩盐溶液中产生同种电荷表面或粒子之间更长程的排斥相互作用。在假设属于簇的离子比例随盐浓度增加的前提下，数值结果可以定性复现"反常欠屏蔽"——有效屏蔽长度在单价盐浓度约1 M处出现极小值。然而定量上，该模型预测的屏蔽长度超过阈值后的增长弱于实验表面力测量通常获得的结果。

Conclusion: 簇的形成降低了名义离子强度且簇极化贡献不会逆转屏蔽长度的衰减，但产生更长程的排斥相互作用，定性上与反常欠屏蔽一致。尽管定量偏差仍存在，本文为理解高浓度盐溶液中"反常欠屏蔽"提供了一个包含簇极化效应的解析与数值框架，可作为后续改进模型的出发点。

Abstract: We explore the hypothesis that ions form loosely connected clusters at high ionic strength in aqueous solutions, and that these clusters have relevance to the experimentally observed phenomenon usually referred to as "anomalous underscreening". Cluster formation lowers the ionic strength below its nominal value, slowing the decay of the screening length but not reversing it. Here we focus on an additional contribution, cluster polarisation. We demonstrate that this effect produces longer-ranged repulsive interactions between like-charged surfaces or particles in concentrated salt solutions. We derive an analytical bulk relation in which the entire architecture of a cluster enters through a single quantity, the charge-weighted second moment of its intramolecular charge structure factor, which quantifies the polarisation response of an arbitrary cluster topology. We also make numerical calculations using classical polymer Density Functional Theory, cDFT, for a model based on star-like clusters in which satellite ions are weakly bonded by a harmonic spring to a common central ion. The corona of satellite ions is assumed to be net neutral, since the formation of highly charged clusters would be accompanied by a significant self-energy cost. Using this model, we calculate surface interactions and screening lengths at various overall salt concentrations. We show that, under the assumption that the fraction of ions belonging to clusters increases with salt concentration, one may qualitatively arrive at "anomalous underscreening", i.e., an effective screening length that displays a minimum at an overall (monovalent) salt concentration of about 1 M. Quantitatively, we note that the predicted growth of the screening length beyond this threshold value is weaker than typically found by experimental surface force measurements.

</details>


### [181] [Using microrheology to study dynamical heterogeneities](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.00331)
*Antonio M. Puertas,Thomas Voigtmann*

Main category: cond-mat.soft

TL;DR: 该研究通过朗之万动力学（Langevin dynamics）模拟和模式耦合理论（mode coupling theory），利用单个示踪粒子（tracer particle）的被动微流变学（passive microrheology）方法，监测硬胶体接近玻璃转变密度时的动力学异质性（dynamical heterogeneities）。研究发现示踪粒子的非高斯参数（non-Gaussian parameter）在尺寸耦合交叉区域达到最大，而动力学磁化率对示踪粒子尺寸和浴密度依赖性较弱。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 动力学异质性是过冷液体（supercooled liquids）的核心特征之一，其性质已通过理论、模拟和实验广泛研究。该工作旨在提出一种新的途径——利用单个示踪粒子的被动微流变学来探测动力学异质性，从而为研究玻璃转变附近的软物质体系提供新视角。

Method: 采用朗之万动力学模拟与模式耦合理论相结合的方法，在高于玻璃转变密度的硬胶体（hard colloids）体系中追踪单个被动示踪粒子的轨迹。将动力学异质性研究中常用的多种观测量（observables）改编为可从单粒子轨迹计算的形式，涵盖示踪粒子的耦合行为、非高斯参数、动力学磁化率和不同迁移率区域等方面。

Result: 示踪粒子动力学从小尺寸时与浴体系（bath）最解耦（decoupled）的区域过渡到大尺寸时的强耦合区域。理论和模拟均表明，非高斯参数在两种区域交叉的临界尺寸处达到最大值，且强烈依赖浴密度。动力学磁化率对示踪粒子尺寸和浴密度依赖性较小。微流变学估算的迁移率区域尺寸随浴密度增加而减小，与体相团簇分析（cluster analysis）结果相反。

Conclusion: 单示踪粒子被动微流变学为探测玻璃转变附近的动力学异质性提供了一种可行的替代方法。然而，该方法与体相分析在迁移率区域尺寸随密度变化趋势上的差异表明，被动微流变学观测到的现象与本体动力学异质性可能存在不同的物理关联机制，需要进一步研究。

Abstract: Dynamical heterogeneities are one of the hallmarks of supercooled liquids, and their properties and relevance have been studied with theory, simulations and experiments. In this work, we propose to monitor the dynamics of tracer particles (passive microrheology) to analyze the dynamical heterogeneities in a system of hard colloids close to the glass transition density, using Langevin dynamics simulations and mode coupling theory. Different observables, typical in the study of the dynamical heterogeneities are adapted to be calculated from the trajectory of a single tracer particle. The tracer dynamics shows a transition from a regime where it is most decoupled from the bath for small tracer size to a strong coupling regime for large tracers. Both theory and simulations show that the non-Gaussian parameter of the tracer is maximal for tracer sizes at the crossover between both regimes, and is highly dependent on the bath density. The dynamic susceptibility is also studied, but this parameter shows a minor dependence on both the tracer size or the bath density. Finally, the existence of regions with different mobility is also studied with microrheology. Although the tracer trajectory indeed shows stages with increased mobility, the estimated size of the regions decreases with the bath density, contrary to the results from cluster analysis in the bulk.

</details>


### [182] [Nonlinear force response of modular lattice-based metamaterials](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.00395)
*Jochem G. Meijer,Armin Yousefi,Francois Barthelat,Heinrich M. Jaeger*

Main category: cond-mat.soft

TL;DR: 本文研究基于晶格的力学超材料（lattice-based mechanical metamaterials），通过模块化架构的组合与串联，利用局部失稳（instability）行为调控全局力学响应。作者展示模块间相互作用可产生单个模块不具备的涌现力响应（emergent force responses），包括瞬态弱化与增强的力衰减效果；部分设计在循环加载下还展现出训练行为（training behavior），揭示出依赖加载历史的力学响应。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 晶格型超材料为轻量化平台提供了利用局部失稳控制全局力学响应的可能性，适用于能量路由、振动隔离和冲击减缓等场景。尽管通过几何设计已能实现对变形和屈曲（buckling）顺序的控制，但对非线性耦合单元在大应变率范围下的行为及其历史依赖响应（history-dependent response）仍缺乏深入研究，这正是本研究试图填补的空白。

Method: 研究者构建了基于晶格的非线性屈曲力学超材料，通过模块化架构（modular architectures）设计来实现行为调控。方法核心在于将模块单元进行串联组合，使各模块在交互作用下产生协同效应，并针对不同设计在循环加载条件下考察其屈曲状态转换与历史依赖行为。

Result: 实验发现，模块化单元串联后产生的相互作用导致了单个模块所不具备的涌现力学行为，包括瞬态弱化和增强的力衰减。此外，部分选定设计在循环加载下展现出训练行为，可在不同的屈曲状态之间转换，呈现出明显的历史依赖力学响应。

Conclusion: 研究证明，失稳驱动的模块化超材料不仅可通过几何设计进行编程与调谐，还可通过加载历史实现对非线性应力响应（nonlinear stress-response）的控制与设计，为力学系统非线性响应设计提供了新途径。

Abstract: Lattice-based metamaterials provide lightweight platforms where local instabilities can govern the global mechanical response, enabling applications in energy routing, vibration isolation, and impact mitigation. Although much progress has been made in controlling deformation and buckling sequences through geometric design, the behavior of coupled nonlinear units over a large range of strain rates and their history-dependent response is less explored. Here, we investigate lattice-based mechanical metamaterials whose nonlinear buckling behavior can be harnessed through modular architectures. By combining modular units in series, we show that their interaction gives rise to emergent force responses, including transient weakening and enhanced force attenuation, that are absent in the individual modules. Furthermore, selected designs exhibit training behavior under cyclic loading, transitioning between distinct buckling states and revealing a history-dependent mechanical response. Our results demonstrate that modular, instability-driven metamaterials can be programmed and tuned not only through geometry but also through loading history, opening new avenues for designing a nonlinear stress-response in mechanical systems.

</details>


### [183] [Bond-number-controlled durability of cohesive granular materials under repeated vibration](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.00587)
*Hikari Yokota,Rei Kurita*

Main category: cond-mat.soft

TL;DR: 本研究使用由黏性颗粒与非黏性颗粒组成的混合颗粒体系，在保持键强度近似不变的条件下调控黏性键数量（cohesive bond number），并通过重复振动实验测量失效寿命，发现失效循环数 $N_f$ 与混合比 α 显著相关，寿命随黏性键数量增加而持续增长，表明机械刚度与抗重复扰动耐久性对黏性网络具有不同的依赖关系。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 黏性颗粒材料（cohesive granular materials）的机械稳定性既取决于颗粒间键的强度，也取决于构成承力网络的键的数量，但这两者难以在实验中分离，因为传统控制参数（如液体含量）会同时改变二者。因此需要一种能够独立控制黏性键数量的实验方法。

Method: 采用由黏性颗粒与非黏性颗粒组成的混合颗粒体系，在保持键强度近似不变的前提下，通过改变混合比 α 来控制黏性键数量；随后对体系施加重复振动，测量其失效所需的振动循环数 $N_f$，并考察其与混合比及峰值加速度 G（以重力加速度归一化）之间的关系。

Result: 实验发现失效循环数 $N_f$ 强烈依赖于混合比 α，数据可由 $\ln N_f \propto α^2/G$ 很好地重合，其中 $α^2$ 代表黏性接触的比例。值得注意的是，虽然杨氏模量在刚度阈值之上几乎不依赖于 α，但体系寿命仍随 α 增大而显著上升。

Conclusion: 该结果表明机械刚度（mechanical rigidity）与抵抗重复扰动的耐久性（durability）对黏性网络具有截然不同的依赖关系，从而确立键数量为黏性颗粒材料耐久性的关键控制参数。

Abstract: Cohesive granular materials derive their mechanical stability not only from the strength of individual interparticle bonds but also from the number of bonds forming the load-bearing network. However, these two effects are difficult to separate experimentally because conventional control parameters, such as liquid content, generally alter both simultaneously. Here, we use a mixed granular system composed of cohesive and noncohesive grains to control the cohesive bond number while keeping the bond strength approximately unchanged. We investigate the failure lifetime under repeated vibration and find that the number of cycles to failure, $N_f$, depends strongly on the mixing ratio $α$. The data are well collapsed by $\ln N_f \propto α^2/G$, where $α^2$ represents the fraction of cohesive contacts and $G$ is a peak acceleration normalized by gravitational acceleration. Remarkably, although the Young's modulus is nearly independent of $α$ above the rigidity threshold, the lifetime continues to increase strongly with $α$. This demonstrates that mechanical rigidity and durability against repeated perturbations exhibit distinct dependences on the cohesive network. These results identify bond number as a key control parameter for the durability of cohesive granular materials.

</details>


### [184] [A deviatoric-stress closure for constitutive modeling of viscoelastic dynamics](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.00635)
*Souta Miyamoto,Shotaro Moro,Takeshi Sato,Shota Kato,Katsuaki Tanabe,John J. Molina,Takashi Taniguchi*

Main category: cond-mat.soft

TL;DR: 本文提出一种仅基于可观测流变数据的本构方程（constitutive equation）构建方法：用偏应力张量（deviatoric stress tensor）替代附加应力张量，通过符号回归（symbolic regression）推断闭合方程，并施加物质客观性（material objectivity）和线性Maxwell响应约束，从而避免传统方法中不可测的各向同性应力（isotropic stress）问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 标准流变学测量只能获得选定的应力分量，这使得从实验可观测变量推断张量形式的本构方程面临困难。作者旨在构建一种仅依赖实验可观测量的本构框架，以绕开不可测的各向同性应力带来的不确定性问题。

Method: 提出以迹为零的偏应力张量而非附加应力张量书写本构方程，利用剪应力、第一和第二法向应力差（normal stress differences）以及单轴伸长应力构建无各向同性应力的偏应力状态。通过符号回归推断偏应力动力学闭合关系，并施加物质客观性约束及给定的线性Maxwell响应条件。

Result: 从Giesekus和Larson模型的应力响应中推断出的两个闭合表达式，成功预测了未经训练的瞬态流变响应（包括平面伸长及剪切与单轴伸长的混合流动），其形变速率约为逆松弛时间（inverse relaxation time）量级。在训练区范围内和线性响应区内，稳态流变函数与原模型一致；但在超出训练窗口的高形变速率下，出现偏差和发散响应。

Conclusion: 所提偏应力本构框架为从可观测实验数据出发构建线性和非线性黏弹性（viscoelastic）动力学模型提供了一条实用路径，同时也明确了其在强形变条件下的适用边界。

Abstract: Standard rheological measurements yield only selected stress components; thus, inferring tensorial constitutive equations from experimentally accessible observables is complicated. We propose a constitutive formulation written in terms of a deviatoric stress tensor, whose trace is zero, rather than the extra stress tensor. From rheometric data including shear stress, first and second normal stress differences under shear, and elongational stress under uniaxial elongation, we can construct a deviatoric stress state without the indeterminate isotropic stress. The deviatoric-stress dynamics is represented by a closure inferred through symbolic regression, constrained to satisfy material objectivity and a given linear Maxwell response. To demonstrate the proposed formulation, two closures inferred from stress responses of the Giesekus and Larson models successfully captured untrained transient-flow responses under planar elongation and mixed shear/uniaxial elongations at deformation rates around an inverse relaxation time. Steady rheological functions of the closures agreed with the original models in the linear-response regime and over a deformation-rate range connected to the training data, whereas deviations and divergent responses appeared at larger deformation rates outside the training regime. These results demonstrate that the proposed deviatoric-stress formulation provides a practical route for constitutive modeling of observable linear and nonlinear viscoelastic dynamics, while clarifying its range of validity under strong deformation.

</details>


### [185] [Optimizing diffusion-limited transport, with applications to electrochemical systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.00849)
*Manu Mannattil,L. Mahadevan*

Main category: cond-mat.soft

TL;DR: 论文研究扩散受限条件下（diffusion-limited）电荷输运的失效问题，提出了在浓度约束下最大化电荷转移的动态电流协议，发现最优协议具有"bang-ride"结构，即先以最大可行电流运行直到边界浓度达到临界值，再逐步降低电流维持该浓度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 实验表明电化学系统中的电荷输运可能因扩散无法在边界快速补充或移除物质而失效，导致边界浓度达到临界最小或最大值。作者希望确定在这些约束下如何设计电流协议以最大化电荷转移量。

Method: 作者通过数学优化方法，在预设的浓度约束条件下推导实现最大电荷转移的动态电流协议。所得最优协议呈"bang-ride"结构：初始阶段使用最大可行电流，当地边界浓度达到临界值后，电流则被逐步减小以维持临界浓度。在半无限域中解析推导了最优电流，并将框架扩展到有限域和多层域（multilayer domain）情形。

Result: 作者在半无限域中解析地得出了最优电流表达式，并获得了与系统无关的上界，用以描述该协议相比恒定电流（constant-current）运行的改善幅度。框架还可推广至有限域和多层域结构，且同样适用于充电与放电过程。

Conclusion: 所提出的最优电流控制框架适用于多种几何构型及充放电双向过程，证明了"bang-ride"策略在扩散受限系统中的普适性。

Abstract: Transport in certain systems fails when diffusion cannot replenish or remove material from a boundary rapidly enough, causing the boundary concentration to reach a critical minimum or maximum. Motivated by experiments in electrochemical systems that demonstrate this diffusion-limited failure of charge transport, we determine dynamic current protocols that maximize charge transfer subject to a prescribed concentration constraint. The optimal protocol has a "bang-ride" structure: the maximum feasible current is used until the boundary concentration reaches its critical value, after which the current is progressively reduced to maintain that value. For semi-infinite domains, we derive the optimal currents analytically and obtain system-independent upper bounds on their improvement over constant-current operation. We also extend the framework to finite and multilayer domains and show that the same formulation applies to both charging and discharging.

</details>


### [186] [Mass-conserving growth percolation in polymer gelation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.01044)
*Ameya Rege,Lorenz Ratke*

Main category: cond-mat.soft

TL;DR: 本文提出将聚合凝胶化（polymer gelation）建模为局部质量守恒的“生长-逾渗”（growth-percolation）过程，利用Voronoi捕获区定义单个核的有限材料储备，从而将粒子尺寸与材料消耗耦合，并在不预设饱和状态的情况下自然捕捉凝胶点及凝胶后老化行为。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统的逾渗（percolation）模型通常独立预设颗粒尺寸，无法反映凝胶化中聚合物富集域持续生长并形成跨系统网络时材料消耗对颗粒尺寸的反作用。因此需要一种将粒子尺寸与材料消耗耦合的描述框架。

Method: 该框架将凝胶化表述为局部质量守恒的“生长-逾渗”过程：Voronoi捕获区为每个核定义有限材料储存库；局部消耗决定演变中的过饱和度（supersaturation）与极限颗粒尺寸；颗粒接触生成动态网络，其首个跨越团簇（spanning cluster）定义凝胶点。

Result: 三种生长机制——界面控制、扩散控制和聚合物球（polymer-blob）控制——产生截然不同的凝胶化动力学，但共享相同可达到的最终状态。此外，成核的空间涨落导致凝胶时间呈现分布特征。

Conclusion: 该框架将凝胶化与饱和状态相分离，并能自然捕捉凝胶后老化（post-gel aging），即持续生长引发的结构增粗与拓扑成熟。这为理解凝胶化动力学提供了超越传统逾渗模型的新视角。

Abstract: Polymer gelation involves the emergence of a system-spanning network from growing polymer-rich domains, yet conventional percolation models typically prescribe particle size independently of material consumption. We formulate gelation as a locally mass-conserving growth-percolation process in which Voronoi capture zones define finite material reservoirs for individual nuclei. Local depletion determines the evolving supersaturation and limiting particle size, while particle contacts generate a dynamic network whose first spanning cluster defines the gel point. Three mechanisms, namely, interface-, diffusion-, and polymer-blob-controlled growth produce distinct gelation kinetics while sharing the same accessible final state. Spatial fluctuations in nucleation further generate a distribution of gelation times. The framework separates gelation from saturation and naturally captures post-gel aging as continued growth and topological maturation.

</details>


### [187] [Intruder dynamics in granular media under localized surface loading](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.01266)
*E. M. Franklin,B. Darbois Texier,A. Seguin,D. D. Carvalho,Y. Bertho*

Main category: cond-mat.soft

TL;DR: 本文通过实验研究颗粒介质中球形侵入体在局部表面过载作用下的动力学行为，发现表面载荷会诱导侵入体在通过负载区域下方时产生瞬态减速，并通过特征时间尺度和基于过载的弗劳德数（Froude number）使减速动力学坍缩为通用曲线，据此建立了考虑表面应力传递的模型。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 颗粒介质（granular medium）中侵入体动力学在地表下运动、植物根系生长及地下运输等场景中具有重要应用价值。以往研究主要关注自由表面条件下的侵入行为，而对局部表面过载（localized surface overload）如何影响侵入体动力学的问题缺乏系统理解，因此需要对该效应进行定性与定量表征。

Method: 采用实验方法，在颗粒介质中施加水平恒定力驱动球形侵入体运动，并同时施加局部表面过载。通过改变过载大小与侵入体深度，系统测量侵入体的速度/加速度变化；引入特征时间尺度和过载型弗劳德数进行无量纲化分析，验证数据是否坍缩为主曲线（master curves）；随后构建将表面应力传递纳入考虑的力学模型，并与实验数据对比以检验其准确性。

Result: 实验表明，自由表面下侵入体在准静态（quasistatic）区间内呈恒定加速度运动，而存在表面载荷时，侵入体在通过载荷下方区域时出现显著瞬态减速。该减速幅度随过载增大而增大，在大过载下达到饱和，且随侵入体深度增加而减小。无量纲化后，减速动力学可坍缩为主曲线；所建模型部分捕捉了减速现象，并揭示减速取决于过载大小与载荷作用面积两个参数。

Conclusion: 该研究提供了量化局部表面应力如何影响颗粒介质中地下侵入体动力学的理论框架，为理解与预测涉及颗粒环境中的运动、根系生长及地下运输等过程奠定了实验与建模基础。

Abstract: We experimentally investigate the dynamics of a spherical intruder driven horizontally at a constant force in a granular medium subjected to a localized surface overload. While intruder motion beneath a free surface exhibits constant acceleration in the quasistatic regime, the presence of a surface load induces a pronounced transient deceleration when the intruder passes below the loaded region. The magnitude of this deceleration increases with the applied overload and saturates at large overloads, while it decreases with intruder depth. Introducing a characteristic timescale and an overload-based Froude number, we show that the deceleration dynamics collapse onto master curves. We further develop a model incorporating stress transmission from the surface, which partially captures the intruder deceleration. In this approach, this deceleration is shown to depend on two parameters: the overload and the area on which this overload is applied. These results provide a framework to quantify how localized surface stresses influence subsurface intruder dynamics, with implications for locomotion, root growth, and underground transport in granular media.

</details>


### [188] [Cell size and confinement drive asymmetric cell division through a cortical instability](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.01393)
*Da Gao,Guoye Guan,Chao Tang,Rui Ma*

Main category: cond-mat.soft

TL;DR: 本研究提出细胞分裂的不对称性可在未极化母细胞中自发产生，源于细胞皮层中依赖曲率的主动应力（curvature-dependent active stresses）在受限空间内触发的对称性破缺失稳，并通过秀丽隐杆线虫（C. elegans）胚胎发育成像数据验证了模型预测。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 不对称细胞分裂（asymmetric cell division）是生物学中的基本问题。传统观点认为分裂的不对称性源于母细胞先前的极化（polarization），而本研究旨在探索不对称性是否可不依赖分子极性线索（polarity cue）自发产生。

Method: 通过理论建模分析细胞皮层（cell cortex）中依赖曲率的主动应力在受限空间内的力学行为，揭示自发对称性破缺（spontaneous symmetry breaking）失稳机制；将模型预测与C. elegans胚胎发生（embryogenesis）中连续分裂过程的成像数据进行比较验证。

Result: 研究发现减小细胞尺寸或增强机械束缚（mechanical confinement）均可触发收缩环（contractile ring）偏离赤道面的对称性破缺失稳，从而产生体积不等的子细胞；当存在极性线索时，该失稳与线索协同决定分裂不对称性。C. elegans胚胎中测得的分裂不对称性随细胞缩小而增大，且在胚胎受到机械压缩时进一步增强，均与模型预测一致。

Conclusion: 不对称细胞分裂可脱离分子极性线索而由几何与力学因素自发产生，表明细胞尺寸与机械环境是调控分裂不对称性的关键因素；极性线索的作用是与这一力学失稳协同作用以编程分裂结果。

Abstract: Asymmetric cell division -- in which a mother cell divides into two daughter cells of unequal size -- is a fundamental problem in biology. It is believed that the asymmetry originates from the prior polarization of the mother cell. Here we show that division asymmetry can occur spontaneously even in unpolarized mother cells. Specifically, curvature-dependent active stresses in the cell cortex can lead to this symmetry breaking without any molecular polarity cue if the mother cell is confined within a restricted space. Either reducing the cell size or tightening mechanical confinement triggers the same spontaneous symmetry-breaking instability, in which the contractile ring slips off the equator to yield daughters of unequal volume. In the presence of a polarity cue, this instability cooperates with the cue to program the division asymmetry. The model prediction is compared with the imaging data of C. elegans embryogenesis, in which successive cell divisions in a confined eggshell lead to smaller and smaller cell sizes. The measured division asymmetry indeed increases as the cells shrink, and is further amplified when the embryo is mechanically compressed, both in agreement with the model prediction.

</details>

### [189] [The Radius of the Neutron Star PSR J0614-3329 from NICER Data](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.00965)
*M. C. Miller,A. J. Dittmann,I. M. Holt,F. K. Lamb,C. Chirenti,Z. Arzoumanian,J. Berteaud,S. Bogdanov,K. C. Gendreau,A. K. Harding,W. C. G. Ho,C. Kalapotharakos,S. M. Morsink,P. S. Ray,R. A. Remillard,Z. Wadiasingh,M. T. Wolff*

Main category: astro-ph.HE

TL;DR: 本文基于中子星内部成分探测器（Neutron star Interior Composition Explorer，NICER）对318 Hz脉冲星PSR J0614-3329的X射线数据进行建模分析，最优模型包含三个均匀温度圆形热点，给出该中子星赤道圆周半径的对称68%置信区间为9.88–12.77 km，并讨论了与XMM-Newton数据联合拟合的不一致问题及其对致密物质性质的启示。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 中子星半径测量，特别是利用NICER的X射线数据获得的测量结果，为核饱和密度以上冷催化物质（cold, catalyzed matter）的状态方程模型提供了宝贵信息，因此有必要对更多中子星进行此类分析以约束致密物质性质。

Method: 对NICER获取的PSR J0614-3329（由射电观测确定质量约1.4–1.5个太阳质量）的318-Hz脉冲星X射线数据进行贝叶斯建模分析，探索具有三个均匀温度圆形热点的模型；此外还进行了NICER数据与X射线多镜望远镜卫星（X-ray Multi-Mirror，XMM-Newton）数据的联合拟合。

Result: 最优模型给出赤道圆周半径的对称68%置信区间为9.88–12.77 km。联合拟合中，能同时最优拟合两套数据集的模型在采用标准假设（即XMM-Newton背景由周围场观测得知）下会系统性地低估XMM-Newton数据。

Conclusion: 作者讨论了该结果结合其他中子星数据对中子星核心致密物质性质的约束意义，提示NICER数据与XMM-Newton数据联合分析中背景假设需要进一步审视。

Abstract: Neutron star radius measurements, particularly using X-ray data collected with the Neutron star Interior Composition Explorer (NICER), have provided invaluable information for models of cold, catalyzed matter at densities above that of nuclear saturation. Here we present an analysis of NICER data on the 318-Hz pulsar PSR J0614-3329, which has a mass ~1.4-1.5 solar masses determined from radio observations. The best-fitting model we explore has three uniform-temperature circular hot spots and yields a symmetric 68% credible range for the equatorial circumferential radius of 9.88-12.77 km. We also explore joint fits to the NICER data and the X-ray Multi-Mirror (XMM-Newton) data on this pulsar, but find that the models that best fit both data sets systematically underpredict the XMM-Newton data when, as is standard, it is assumed that the XMM-Newton background is known from observations of surrounding fields. Finally, we discuss the implications of our results, combined with data on other neutron stars, for the properties of the dense matter in neutron star cores.

</details>


### [190] [Constraints on Buchdahl-Inspired Gravity from Future Pulsar Timing near Sgr A*](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.01153)
*Jian-Ming Yan,Tao Zhu,Zong-Kuan Guo,Zhao Li*

Main category: astro-ph.HE

TL;DR: 本文基于未来银心附近脉冲星计时观测，评估其对由单参数ε刻画的Buchdahl型R²引力的约束能力。研究采用自洽的计时框架，通过Fisher矩阵预报发现短轨道周期和高偏心率可显著提升ε的测量灵敏度；对S2类轨道的假想脉冲星，统计灵敏度达σ_ε~10⁻⁴，远低于当前S2的95%置信区间半宽（|ε|≈0.56）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 人马座A*（Sgr A*）附近的脉冲星计时观测为探测超大质量黑洞周边的引力物理提供了独特窗口，因此有必要评估此类观测对修正引力的约束潜力。本文旨在量化脉冲星计时对Buchdahl型R²引力的测量灵敏度，为未来的银河系中心脉冲星搜索提供目标选择和观测设计指导。

Method: 采用自洽的计时框架（timing framework），将轨道动力学与光传播延迟（light-propagation delays）集成，并在整个观测跨度上保留完整的计时解。通过Fisher矩阵预报（Fisher-matrix forecasts），系统性地考察代表脉冲星的ε测量精度如何依赖于轨道几何（即轨道周期和偏心率）。在弱场、静态、球对称（weak-field, static, spherically symmetric）的计时模型假设下进行基准比较，并以S2类轨道（P_b=16年，e=0.88）的假想脉冲星为例。

Result: 研究发现，较短轨道周期和较高偏心率显著增强ε的灵敏度，这与近拱点（periastron）附近观测贡献占主导相一致。对S2类轨道假想脉冲星，获得统计灵敏度σ_ε~10⁻⁴，该量级与1PN（1-post-Newtonian）展开的自然截断尺度相当。在此理想化假设下，统计尺度比当前S2的95%置信区间半宽（|ε|≈0.56）低约数个数量级，尽管该比较在置信水平差异下仅为启发性的。

Conclusion: 这些趋势为未来银心脉冲星搜索提供了定量指导，包括目标选取与观测活动（campaign）设计。需注意，σ_ε~10⁻⁴不应被理解为对真实Sgr A*系统的完整预报，因为其基于理想化假设和新颖性模型的有效展望存在局限。

Abstract: Future pulsar timing observations near Sgr~A* offer a unique probe of gravitational physics in the vicinity of a supermassive black hole. We forecast the ability of such measurements to constrain a Buchdahl-inspired $R^2$ gravity, parameterized by a single deviation parameter $ε$, using a timing framework that self-consistently integrates orbital dynamics with light-propagation delays and preserves the full timing solution across the observing span. Through Fisher-matrix forecasts for a representative pulsar, we systematically isolate how the precision on $ε$ depends on orbital geometry. We find that shorter orbital periods and higher eccentricities significantly enhance sensitivity, consistent with a substantial contribution from observations near periastron. As a benchmark comparison, we further consider a hypothetical pulsar on an S2-like orbit ($P_b=16~{\rm yr}$, $e=0.88$) and obtain a statistical sensitivity of $σ_ε\sim 10^{-4}$ within the adopted weak-field, static, and spherically symmetric timing model. This sensitivity is comparable to the natural order-of-magnitude truncation scale of the 1PN expansion and should not be interpreted as a complete forecast for the real Sgr~A* system. Under the adopted idealized assumptions, the characteristic statistical scale is several orders of magnitude below the current S2 95\% confidence interval half-width ($|ε|_{\rm S2}^{\rm 95\%}\approx 0.56$), though this comparison is heuristic given the differing confidence levels. These trends provide quantitative guidance for target selection and campaign design in future Galactic-center pulsar searches.

</details>

### [191] [Synchronization Pathways and Resilience in Power Grids](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.00910)
*Cook Hyun Kim,Jihye Kim,Sangjoon Park,B. Kahng*

Main category: nlin.AO

TL;DR: 本文通过一个由实证电网数据驱动的最小模型，揭示了电力网络中同步集群（synchronized cluster）成核与传播的微观机制，识别出两种截然不同的种子集群类型与传播路径，并发现该路径选择—持续规则同样适用于欧洲电网的数据衍生退火表示（annealed representation），为维持电网稳定运行提供了基于路径的框架。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 确保可持续能源供应需要维持电网稳定，而电网转子动力学由摆动方程（swing equation）描述——即具有功率与总耦合强度相关性的二阶Kuramoto模型（second-order Kuramoto model）。然而，同步集群的成核与传播所涉及的微观机制至今仍缺乏理解，这是动机的核心所在。

Method: 采用由实证电网数据驱动并基于Adhoc势能方法（Adhoc potential method）构建的最小模型，系统研究同步集群的成核与传播过程，并检验外部扰动后的恢复路径。此外，研究还验证了所发现的规则在欧洲电网数据衍生的退火表示中是否同样成立。

Result: 识别出两种不同的种子集群类型与传播路径：对于非对称功率分布，群体驱动（population-driven）的种子集群在功率分布中心成核并通过转子累积（rotor accretion）向外围尾部传播；对于对称分布，耦合驱动（coupling-driven）的种子集群则在尾部成核并通过集群合并（cluster merger）向中心向内传播。两者产生不同的序参量（order-parameter）模式，而惯性（inertia）决定种子集群是否以不同角速度持续存在。相同的传播路径同样支配外部扰动后的恢复过程，且选择—持续规则在欧洲电网数据衍生退火表示中得到验证。

Conclusion: 研究结果可为维持电网稳定运行提供策略参考；更一般地，该基于路径的框架将同步（synchronization）问题重新定义为聚焦于集群形成与恢复动力学，而非仅仅依赖静态判据，从而提供了新的理解视角。

Abstract: Ensuring a sustainable energy supply requires maintaining power-grid stability. Rotor dynamics are governed by the swing equation, which takes the form of a second-order Kuramoto model with a correlation between power and total coupling strength. Yet the microscopic mechanisms that nucleate and propagate synchronized clusters remain poorly understood. Using a minimal model motivated by empirical grid data and the \Adhoc potential method, we reveal two distinct seed cluster types and propagation pathways: a population-driven seed cluster at the center of the power distribution propagating to its tails by rotor accretion, and, for a symmetric distribution, coupling-driven seed clusters at its tails propagating inward to the center by cluster merger. These differences generate distinct order-parameter patterns, while inertia controls whether the seed clusters persist with distinct angular velocities. The same propagation pathways also govern recovery following external disturbances. We further confirm that the same selection--persistence rule holds in data-derived annealed representations of European power grids. Therefore, our results can inform strategies for sustaining stable power-grid operation. More generally, our pathway-based framework reframes synchronization by emphasizing the dynamics of cluster formation and recovery rather than relying solely on static criteria.

</details>

### [192] [Dimensional reduction associated with unchanged direction trajectories in flat fiber Robertson Walker spacetimes](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.00229)
*D. de la Fuente,R. M. Rubio,J. Torrente*

Main category: math.DG

TL;DR: 本文在（伽利略或洛伦兹）具有欧几里得纤维的罗伯逊-沃克时空（Robertson-Walker spacetimes）中建立了新的降维（dimensional reduction）结果，证明每个未变方向观测者都可以嵌入到一个三维完全测地类时子流形中。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在广义相对论与几何框架下，罗伯逊-沃克时空是宇宙学模型的重要基础。该工作旨在通过降维手段简化高维时空中的观测者运动分析，为后续物理或几何推论奠定结构性前提。

Method: 作者针对（伽利略或洛伦兹型）具欧几里得纤维的罗伯逊-沃克时空结构，运用微分几何工具证明：每一"未变方向观测者"（unchanged direction observer）均能嵌入某个三维完全测地的类时（timelike）子流形。

Result: 成功建立了新的降维定理：在该类时空中，任意保持不变方向的观测者的运动轨迹可限制在三维完全测地类时子流形内，从而实现从高维到三维的降维嵌入。

Conclusion: 该结果为罗伯逊-沃克时空中的观测者运动提供了一种降维刻画，有望在宇宙学模型或相对论性几何分析中简化相关问题的处理。

Abstract: In this article, we establish a new dimensional reduction result in (Galilean or Lorentzian) Robertson Walker spacetimes with Euclidean fiber. In this context, we prove that every unchanged direction observer can be embedded into a three dimensional totally geodesic timelike submanifold.

</details>

### [193] [Modernization and Statistical Validation of a Multilayer TRIM.SP Code for Low-Energy Muon and Ion Implantation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.00094)
*Zaher Salman,Ryan M. L. McFadden,Thomas Prokscha*

Main category: physics.acc-ph

TL;DR: 该论文描述了对本地多层TRIM.SP程序进行的分阶段现代化改造，命名为TRIM.SP-NL。改造内容涵盖行为保持的重构、局部簿记与初始化缺陷修正、支持每层超过五种元素、可复现构建模式及运行时可选随机数生成器（RNG）接口。通过精确回归测试与统计比较验证了新后端性能与正确性，并配套开发了Node.js图形界面，最终形成开源的TRIM.SP-NL Workbench。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有TRIM.SP实现对多层结构支持有限（每层不超过五种元素），且存在簿记与初始化缺陷，同时需要更灵活高效且可复现的随机数生成与构建方式。作者希望通过系统化改造提升程序的可用性、正确性与性能，并降低对专有或不可复现环境的依赖。

Method: 采用分阶段现代化策略，包括行为保持的重构（behavior-preserving refactoring）、修正局部簿记（bookkeeping）与初始化缺陷、扩展至每层五种以上元素的处理能力、引入可复现构建模式（reproducible build modes），以及提供运行时可选随机数生成器接口。RANLUX作为默认参考后端，PCG32与xoshiro256**为备选加速方案，保持输入卡（input-deck）格式不变。验证方式结合精确回归测试与13个代表性多层靶配置上的统计比较，共覆盖143个能量-配置点、每后端429次模拟。

Result: 在测试套件中，PCG32与xoshiro256**分别将运行时间缩短至原基准的约1/1.98与1/2.01（即加速约2倍）。植入、背散射与透射分数，以及平均植入深度、射程离散（range straggling）和代表性深度剖面均未显示出系统性随机数生成器依赖。此外，开发了基于Node.js的图形界面，用于设置、扫描与结果收集，与Fortran引擎共同构成开源工具包。

Conclusion: TRIM.SP-NL通过模块化改造与多RNG后端支持，在不改变输入格式的前提下实现了显著性能提升与更高灵活度，同时通过严格的回归与统计测试保证了物理结果不受随机数生成器选择的影响，为一个可复现、可维护且用户友好的开源多层离子输运模拟工作台奠定了基础。

Abstract: We describe the staged modernization of a local multilayer TRIM.SP implementation, denoted TRIM.SP-NL. The work included behavior-preserving refactoring, correction of localized bookkeeping and initialization defects, support for more than five elements per layer, reproducible build modes, and a runtime-selectable random-number-generator (RNG) interface. The RANLUX RNG remains the default reference backend, while PCG32 and xoshiro256** provide faster alternatives without changing the input-deck format. The code validation combined exact regression testing with statistical comparisons over 13 representative target multilayer configurations, comprising 143 energy-configuration points per backend and 429 simulations in total. Across the test suite, PCG32 and xoshiro256** reduced runtime by factors of 1.98 and 2.01, respectively. The implanted, backscattered, and transmitted fractions, as well as the mean implantation depths, range straggling, and representative depth profiles showed no systematic RNG dependence. Together with a Node.js-based graphical interface for setup, scans, and result collection, the Fortran engine forms the open-source TRIM.SP-NL Workbench.

</details>

### [194] [AutoXRD: Autonomous LLM Agents and Comprehensive Evaluation for Powder Diffraction Analysis](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.00070)
*Yuetong Wu,Maojun Sun*

Main category: cond-mat.mtrl-sci

TL;DR: 本文提出AutoXRD——一个自主大语言模型（LLM）智能体框架，将粉末X射线衍射（XRD）分析组织为逐步精修流程，并对输出施加晶体学与物理约束检查。同时引入双轨基准XRDBench（QA与E2E），对十种近期LLM在1340个模型-任务运行上进行评估，结果显示模型平均仅得57.8/100，GPT-5.6系列表现最佳，但整体在动作选择、定量推理等环节仍显不足。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 粉末XRD是材料表征的核心手段，但其可靠的端到端自动化仍具挑战性。XRD智能体需同时具备证据解读、精修软件操作、耦合参数的有序管理，以及区分数值优化与物理有效性的能力，尚无系统性框架与评测基准满足这一需求。

Method: 构造AutoXRD框架，将XRD分析组织为逐步精修（stepwise refinement），使动作基于实测证据并应用确定性晶体学与物理检查方才接受结果。构建XRDBench双轨基准：XRDBench-QA含100个有界诊断任务以分离科学推理能力，XRDBench-E2E含34个可执行工作流以测试端到端分析能力。对十种LLM在1340次模型-任务运行上进行系统评估，并执行消融实验与执行轨迹分析。

Result: 模型整体平均仅得57.8/100，从XRDBench-QA的61.9降至XRDBench-E2E的53.7。模型在精修历史评估与结果接受上表现最佳，但在精修动作选择、相定量、指标化（indexing）及Rietveld精修上明显薄弱。GPT-5.6系列表现领先，其中GPT-5.6 Sol以81.1居总分首位。消融实验表明AutoXRD的全部六个组件均能一致提升性能；执行轨迹分析揭示了耦合参数控制、定量推理、证据保留与工作流终止方面的系统性失败。

Conclusion: AutoXRD框架设计得到消融结果支持，但仍需更强的科学约束、不确定性感知决策与更高效的规划以克服当前LLM在复杂XRD端到端分析中的核心局限。为自动化材料表征智能体的持续发展提供了可量化的评测基础与改进方向。

Abstract: Powder X-ray diffraction (XRD) is central to materials characterization, yet reliable end-to-end automation remains challenging. An XRD agent must interpret diffraction evidence, operate refinement software, manage coupled parameters in a defensible order, and distinguish numerical improvement from physical validity. In this paper, we propose AutoXRD, an autonomous large language model (LLM) agent framework that organizes powder-XRD analysis as stepwise refinement, grounds actions in observed evidence, and applies deterministic crystallographic and physical checks before accepting results. We further introduce XRDBench with two complementary tracks. XRDBench-QA contains 100 bounded diagnostic tasks that isolate scientific reasoning and decision-making, whereas XRDBench-E2E contains 34 executable workflows that test whether agents can compose these capabilities into complete analyses requiring file inspection, crystallographic-software execution, iterative refinement, evidence preservation, and reporting. We evaluate ten recent LLMs across 1,340 model--task runs. Models average only 57.8 out of 100, falling from 61.9 on XRDBench-QA to 53.7 on XRDBench-E2E. They perform best on refinement-history assessment and result acceptance, but remain substantially weaker on refinement-action selection, phase quantification, indexing, and Rietveld refinement. GPT-5.6 Sol achieves the highest overall score of 81.1, GPT-5.6 Terra the highest XRDBench-E2E point estimate of 81.0, and GPT-5.6 Luna the best score--cost trade-off. Ablations show that all six AutoXRD components consistently improve performance, supporting the framework design. Finally, execution-trace analysis reveals recurring failures in coupled-parameter control, quantitative reasoning, evidence preservation, and workflow termination, motivating stronger scientific constraints, uncertainty-aware decisions, and more efficient planning.

</details>


### [195] [Fermi-Level Metal-d Character of Group-6 Bis-Hexahapto Bilayer Graphene](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.00083)
*Mingguang Chen*

Main category: cond-mat.mtrl-sci

TL;DR: 本文通过密度泛函方法系统研究了双六合（bis-hexahapto，η6-η6）配位结合的Cr、Mo、W双层石墨烯插层化合物。结果显示，与离子型石墨插层化合物（如CaC6、C12Ca、C12Li）不同，第6族过渡金属系统在费米能级附近的态密度以金属d轨道为主导（约73%），仅保留15-23%的间隙态（interstitial state）谱权重，因此不具备超导石墨插层化合物所需的碳衍生层间带（interlayer band）特征。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 石墨插层化合物（graphite intercalation compounds）的超导性依赖于由电正性插层物提供电荷所填充的碳衍生层间带，而双六合配位通过共价键将过渡金属结合到两层石墨烯上，电荷转移极少——这种机制可能破坏插层石墨超导所依赖的电荷授予过程。作者旨在探究第6族过渡金属（Cr、Mo、W）插层双层石墨烯是否保留离子型参考体系中层间带的碳p轨道特征，以判断此类体系是否具备超导相关的电子结构。

Method: 研究采用第一性原理计算，对双层石墨烯中第6族金属（Cr、Mo、W）的插层体系（C12M）进行了系统分析。计算方法包括：通过与离子型对照体系（体相CaC6及同晶胞C12Ca/C12Li）比较费米能级附近的投影态密度（projected density of states）；采用原子掩蔽（atom-masking）方式在间隙区域分析谱权重；评估不同堆叠顺序（AA与Bernal）的能量稳定性，以及有相对孤立原子和体相金属的热力学稳定性比较。

Result: 结果显示，在每个画廊一个金属（C12M）时，离子型对照组在费米能级处的间隙谱权重保留率为68%（CaC6）和45%（C12Li），而第6族体系仅为15%、22%和23%（分别为Cr、Mo、W）。费米能级±0.15 eV内的投影态密度在离子型参考体系中由碳p轨道主导，而在三种第6族双层体系中均由金属d轨道主导（约73%）。几何上，η6-η6配位要求AA堆叠，使原有Bernal堆叠偏好被逆转400-600 meV。所有体系均为金属性，但承载费米面的电子态成分不同。热力学分析表明，有序相相对体相金属为亚稳态（每原子+2.9-5.0 eV），但相对孤立原子为束缚态（每原子-1.6-4.1 eV）；不稳定顺序Cr最小、W最大，与反应活性Cr>Mo>W一致。C12Cr为金属性，W单独携带0.65 μB磁矩。

Conclusion: 双六合配位插层实现了强的层间键合和确定的AA堆叠次序，但并未产生与超导石墨插层化合物相关的层间带特征。第6族金属插层体系在费米能级附近的电子结构以金属d轨道为主导而非碳p轨道，这使得此类体系不具备石墨插层超导所需的电子结构前提。

Abstract: Superconductivity in graphite intercalation compounds requires occupancy of a carbon-derived interlayer band filled by charge from an electropositive intercalant. Bis-hexahapto (eta6-eta6) coordination binds transition metals covalently to two graphene sheets with little charge transfer -- the mechanism that defines this chemistry removes the donation on which intercalated-graphite superconductivity rests. We ask, for group-6 Cr, Mo and W in bilayer graphene, whether Fermi-level states retain the interstitial, carbon-p character of ionic references, with bulk CaC6 and same-cell C12Ca/C12Li controls. Electron-phonon coupling is not computed. At one metal per gallery (C12M), the ionic controls retain 68 and 45 per cent, respectively, of gallery spectral weight in atom-masked interstitial regions at EF; the group-6 systems retain 15, 22 and 23 per cent. The contrast is compositional: group-6 galleries carry more absolute interstitial weight than C12Li, but projected density of states within +/-0.15 eV of EF is carbon p dominated in the ionic references and metal d dominated (~73 per cent) in all three group-6 bilayers. Every system studied is metallic; they differ in what carries the Fermi surface. Geometrically, eta6-eta6 coordination requires AA stacking and reverses the intrinsic Bernal preference by 400-600 meV wherever metal is present. Ordered phases are metastable against bulk metal (+2.9-5.0 eV per atom) but bound against isolated atoms (-1.6-4.1 eV); instability ordering (Cr least, W most) matches Cr > Mo > W reactivity. C12Cr is metallic; tungsten alone carries 0.65 muB. Bis-hexahapto intercalation delivers strong interlayer bonding and a definite AA registry, but not the interlayer-band character associated with superconducting graphite intercalation compounds.

</details>


### [196] [Magnetic frustration and non-collinear textures in layered Gd magnets](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.00141)
*Vladislav Borisov,Rohit Pathak,Sagar Sarkar,Anna Delin,Olle Eriksson*

Main category: cond-mat.mtrl-sci

TL;DR: 本文基于电子结构理论与原子自旋动力学（atomistic spin dynamics）的尺度桥接模拟，研究了GdRu₂Si₂、GdRu₂Ge₂等Gd基层状稀土磁体的磁性性质，并通过对结构家族的系统对比，揭示磁阻挫（magnetic frustration）与偶极相互作用对非共线及斯格明子（skyrmion）相稳定性的关键作用，同时提出碱金属替代Gd的化学调谐方案以产生新型稳定斯格明子。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 理解层状稀土磁体中斯格明子相形成的微观机制，并探索通过化学调控设计具有更宽带外磁场稳定窗口的新型斯格明子材料的可能性。

Method: 采用基于电子结构理论和原子自旋动力学的尺度桥接模拟（scale-bridging simulations），对GdRu₂Si₂、GdRu₂Ge₂及GdAu₂Si₂、GdAu₂Ge₂、GdAg₂Si₂、GdAg₂Ge₂等Gd基层状化合物进行系统性计算与对比分析。

Result: 确认磁阻挫与偶极相互作用对非共线磁序和斯格明子相稳定性的重要性；预测通过调节交换相互作用（exchange interaction）平衡与磁各向异性（magnetic anisotropy）特征可实现化学调谐；提出GdKRu₄Si₄、GdRbRu₄Si₄、GdCsRu₄Si₄及GdYRu₄Si₄等部分碱金属取代Gd的化合物，可能形成有序层状结构并产生宽磁场范围内稳定的新型斯格明子。

Conclusion: 该结构族化合物为斯格明子相提供了可化学调谐的磁性平台，碱金属取代策略有望在较宽外磁场范围内实现新型拓扑磁结构的稳定化。

Abstract: Using scale-bridging simulations based on electronic structure theory and atomistic spin dynamics, we investigate the magnetic properties of skyrmionic GdRu$_2$Si$_2$ and GdRu$_2$Ge$_2$ layered rare-earth magnets and similar Gd-based compounds (GdAu$_2$Si$_2$, GdAu$_2$Ge$_2$, GdAg$_2$Si$_2$ and GdAg$_2$Ge$_2$). By studying the trends across this structural family, we confirm the importance of magnetic frustration and dipolar interactions for the stability of non-collinear and skyrmion phases. Furthermore, our calculations predict promising opportunities for chemical tuning of these magnets in terms of the balance between various exchange interactions and the character of the magnetic anisotropy. These changes lead to the formation of new types of skyrmions that are stable in a wide range of applied external magnetic field. In particular, we propose partial alkali-metal substitution of Gd in GdRu$_2$Si$_2$ leading to GdKRu$_4$Si$_4$, GdRbRu$_4$Si$_4$, GdCsRu$_4$Si$_4$ as well as GdYRu$_4$Si$_4$ compounds, and suggest that it is likely to result in an ordered layered structure, similarly to previously reported iron pnictides like CaKFe$_4$As$_4$.

</details>


### [197] [Size-Dependent Growth Rates Amplify Infinitesimal Asymmetry in Nanocrystals](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.00145)
*Sam Oaks-Leaf,David T. Limmer*

Main category: cond-mat.mtrl-sci

TL;DR: 本文发展了一种尺寸依赖的动力学构造（size-dependent kinetic construction），揭示了即使在所有对称相关晶面遵循相同微观生长定律的条件下，微小的籽晶非对称性也能被放大为强烈不对称的纳米晶形态。空间不均一的成核速率和边界限制生长使晶面生长速度依赖尺寸，确定性形状演化将这些局部速率转化为全局形状对称性破缺。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统动力学Wulff构造（kinetic Wulff construction）预测晶面等价面共享固定生长速率时会产生对称的非平衡形状，但实际纳米晶通过有限晶面生长，其成核势垒和配体覆盖度取决于晶面尺寸，现有理论无法解释对称条件下出现的不对称形态。

Method: 作者建立了尺寸依赖的动力学构造模型，该模型考虑了空间不均一的成核速率与边界限制生长机制，使晶面生长速率成为尺寸的函数。通过在二维正方晶格和三角晶格以及三维FCC籽晶上对形状演化进行确定性模拟来验证机制。

Result: 数值演示表明即使所有对称相关晶面均遵守相同的微观生长定律，微小的籽晶不对称性也可被放大为强烈不对称的纳米晶形态。在三维情形中，立方八面体（cuboctahedra）可依据哪些晶面面积扰动被放大而演化成棒状或四面体状结构。

Conclusion: 该机制揭示了尺寸依赖的晶面生长如何导致持续的各向异性生长（persistent anisotropic growth），为理解纳米晶形态对称性破缺提供了新的理论框架。

Abstract: The kinetic Wulff construction predicts symmetric nonequilibrium shapes when crystallographically equivalent facets share a fixed growth rate. However, nanocrystals grow through finite facets whose nucleation barriers and ligand coverages depend on facet size. Here we develop a size-dependent kinetic construction and show that as a consequence infinitesimal seed asymmetries can be amplified into strongly asymmetric nanocrystal morphologies even when all symmetry-related facets obey the same microscopic growth law. Spatially heterogeneous nucleation rates and boundary limited growth generate facet growth velocities that depend on size, and deterministic shape evolution translates these local rates into global shape symmetry breaking. We illustrate the mechanism of persistent anisotropic growth on square and triangular lattices in two dimensions and on FCC seeds in three dimensions, where cuboctahedra can evolve toward rods or tetrahedra depending on which facet-area perturbations are amplified.

</details>


### [198] [Cryogenic Enhancement of Electron Spin Polarization from a Strained GaAs/GaAsP Superlattice Photocathode](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.00212)
*Matt Grau,Colin Kirk,Greg Blume,John Hill,Sushil Poudel,Alimohammed Kachwala,Marcy Stutzman,Joseph Michael Grames,Sylvain Marsillac,Matt Poelker*

Main category: cond-mat.mtrl-sci

TL;DR: 本文报道了利用金属有机化学气相沉积（metalorganic chemical vapor deposition, MOCVD）生长的应变GaAs/GaAsP超晶格（superlattice）光电阴极，在干冰温度（195 K）下实现了95.0%的电子自旋极化率，超过了过去二十年约92%的GaAs基光电阴极极化水平，并提出适度冷却至195K是获得更高极化GaAs基电子源的实用途径。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: GaAs基光电阴极的电子自旋极化率在过去二十年中一直徘徊在92%左右，亟需突破这一平台期以获得更高极化的电子源，用于高能物理等领域中的自旋相关实验。

Method: 采用MOCVD生长的应变GaAs/GaAsP超晶格光电阴极，用97.8%圆偏振光激发，并在295 K、273 K、195 K、77 K四个温度下测量极化率和量子效率（quantum efficiency）光谱，通过降温系统考察不同退极化（depolarization）机制的贡献。

Result: 在195 K下报道了95.0±0.8(统计)±2.4(系统)%的自旋极化率，峰值极化波长处量子效率为0.7%。极化率从295 K时的91.2%升至195 K时的最大值，光谱峰从775 nm移至739 nm（78 meV），与带隙加宽一致。降温抑制了热化输运通道，而能量依赖的热电子弛豫仍存在并决定低温饱和。

Conclusion: 适度冷却至195 K（干冰即可实现）是获得更高极化GaAs基电子源的实用途径；极化增益并非源于表面能量过滤机制，降温对热化退极化通道的抑制是极化提升的主要因素，完整冷却循环后极化可恢复且保持稳定。

Abstract: We report electron spin polarization of 95.0 +/- 0.8 (stat) +/- 2.4 (sys)% from a strained GaAs/GaAsP superlattice photocathode grown by metalorganic chemical vapor deposition (MOCVD) and cooled to dry-ice temperature (195 K). We achieved this polarization with 97.8% circularly polarized excitation light and a quantum efficiency of 0.7% at the peak polarization wavelength. This measurement exceeds the values of previously reported GaAs-based photocathode polarizations, which have clustered near 92% for two decades. We vary the temperature of the cathode and measure the polarization and quantum-efficiency spectra at 295 K, 273 K, 195 K, and 77 K. The polarization rises from 91.2(1)% at 295 K to its maximum at 195 K, while the spectral peak shifts from 775 nm to 739 nm (78 meV) over the full temperature range, tracking the widening band gap. The spectra probe two depolarization mechanisms: a thermalized transport channel that is suppressed on cooling, and energy-dependent hot-electron relaxation that persists and sets the low-temperature saturation. The polarization recovers after a full cooling and warmup cycle and is stable while the quantum efficiency decays, which disfavors surface energy filtering as the origin of the gain. These results indicate that modest cooling to 195 K, for which dry ice suffices, is a practical route to higher-polarization GaAs-based electron sources.

</details>


### [199] [Multi-image Overlap Stitching and Automatic Image Construction for Coherent X-ray Imaging](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.00341)
*Starr Boney,Umeshika Dissanayaka,Lillian Rutowski,Aaron George,Min Gyu Kim*

Main category: cond-mat.mtrl-sci

TL;DR: 本文提出一种名为MOSAICX的自动化拼接工作流，用于大规模直空间相干X射线成像（direct-space coherent X-ray imaging）数据，可自动对齐并组合数百张局部重叠图像，生成覆盖大面积区域的复合材料图像。该方法在反铁磁拓扑绝缘体MnBi₂Te₄的434张直接相干X射线成像（direct-CXI）图像上得到验证，成功还原了扫描区域的完整磁畴与畴壁结构。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 直空间实时相干X射线成像（direct-space and real-time coherent X-ray imaging）能够可视化超出单幅图像视场（field of view）的磁畴结构与动力学，但大面积测量通常需要光栅扫描，产生数百张必须精确对齐与合并后才能进行定量分析的局部重叠图像，缺乏高效的自动化处理方案。

Method: 工作流包含图像居中（image centering）、掩蔽（masking）、裁剪（trimming）、二值化（binarization）、分层拼接（hierarchical stitching）和后处理（post-processing）六个步骤。图像先组合为列重构（column reconstructions），再拼接为单一的大面积复合材料图像。

Result: 该方法应用于包含434张反铁磁拓扑绝缘体MnBi₂Te₄直空间相干X射线成像图像的实验数据集，成功重建出扫描区域内完整的磁畴与畴壁（domain-wall）景观，同时抑制了成像伪影和探测器缺陷。

Conclusion: MOSAICX提供了一种处理大型直空间相干X射线成像数据集的高效途径，使超出现有单次测量视场的磁畴结构可视化与分析成为可能。

Abstract: Direct-space and real-time coherent X-ray imaging (direct-CXI) enables visualization of magnetic-domain structures and dynamics over length scales exceeding the field of view of a single image. However, large-area measurements typically require raster scanning, producing hundreds of partially overlapping images that must be accurately aligned and combined before quantitative analysis can be performed. Here, we present Multi-image Overlap Stitching and Automatic Image Construction for coherent X-ray imaging (MOSAICX), an automated stitching workflow for large-area direct-space coherent X-ray imaging. The workflow consists of image centering, masking, trimming, binarization, hierarchical stitching, and post-processing. To demonstrate the method, we apply it to a dataset comprising 434 direct-CXI images of the antiferromagnetic topological insulator MnBi$_2$Te$_4$. The images are first combined into column reconstructions and subsequently stitched into a single large-area composite image. The resulting reconstruction reveals the complete magnetic-domain and domain-wall landscape over the scanned region while suppressing imaging artifacts and detector defects. The presented workflow provides an efficient approach for processing large direct-CXI datasets and enables visualization and analysis of magnetic-domain structures beyond the field of view of individual measurements.

</details>


### [200] [Numerical study of the grain-growth-induced non-parabolic kinetics of a solid-state reaction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.01002)
*Ya. A. Nikiforov,S. A. Chizhik,N. I. Baklanova*

Main category: cond-mat.mtrl-sci

TL;DR: 本文开发了一个移动边界扩散模型（moving-boundary diffusion model），用于描述固态扩散偶（solid-state diffusion couples）中反应层的非抛物线增长，模型中同时考虑了连续生成产物层的局部晶粒长大历史以及体扩散与晶界扩散。数值模拟揭示了三种不同的动力学阶段，并解释了瞬时生长指数如何随过程演变而连续变化。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 固态扩散偶中反应层的非抛物线增长现象常见，但微观结构演变与其动力学之间的关联尚未被充分理解。以往的解释通常将其归因于晶粒长大导致晶界扩散贡献逐渐减弱，但缺乏同时纳入局部晶粒长大历史与两种扩散机制的定量模型。

Method: 建立移动边界扩散模型，在描述产物层生长的同时追踪连续形成产物的局部晶粒长大历史，并同时考虑体扩散（bulk diffusion）与晶界扩散（grain-boundary diffusion）两种扩散路径。通过数值模拟分析产物层厚度增长与有效扩散系数在反应过程中的时空演变。

Result: 数值模拟显示反应可经历三个不同的动力学阶段：以晶界扩散为主的初始近似抛物线阶段、有效扩散系数强烈空间变化导致瞬态亚抛物线阶段、以及以体扩散为主的后续近似抛物线阶段。亚抛物线阶段的幅度和持续时间取决于晶界扩散与体扩散的相对大小及晶粒长大动力学。在有限实验时间区间内对模拟层厚数据进行拟合可获得定义良好的表观指数（apparent exponent）。

Conclusion: 瞬时生长指数（instantaneous growth exponent）在整个反应过程中连续演变，并非唯一的动力学常数，表明该过程不能由单一幂律唯一表征。然而，在实际有限时间窗口内的拟合仍可产生看似满足单一幂律的表观指数，这揭示了瞬态过程何以在宏观观测中呈现单一幂律的表象。

Abstract: Non-parabolic growth of reaction layers is frequently observed in solid-state diffusion couples, but the relationship between microstructural evolution and the resulting kinetics remains incompletely understood. This effect is commonly attributed to grain growth, which can progressively reduce the contribution of fast grain-boundary diffusion, causing the effective diffusivity of a polycrystalline product layer to evolve during reaction. This work develops a moving-boundary diffusion model to describe product-layer growth while accounting for the local grain-growth history of the continuously formed product, as well as both bulk and grain-boundary diffusion. Numerical simulations show that the reaction can pass through three distinct kinetic regimes: an initial approximately parabolic regime dominated by grain-boundary diffusion, a transient sub-parabolic regime associated with strong spatial variation of the effective diffusivity, and a subsequent approximately parabolic regime dominated by bulk diffusion. The magnitude and duration of the sub-parabolic regime depend on the relative grain-boundary and bulk diffusivities and on the kinetics of grain growth. The instantaneous growth exponent therefore evolves continuously and does not represent a unique kinetic constant. Nevertheless, fitting simulated layer-thickness data over finite experimental time intervals produces well-defined apparent exponents, demonstrating how a transient process can appear to obey a single power law.

</details>


### [201] [XUV Transmission Spectroscopy Using a Tabletop High-Harmonic Source](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.00511)
*Ryunosuke Takahashi,Soudai Sakoda,Kaede Yamada,Jumpei Horai,Shigetoshi Tomita,Yuto Shiokawa,Nobuhisa Ishii,Hiroki Wadati*

Main category: cond-mat.mtrl-sci

TL;DR: 本研究构建了一套以Yb:KGW激光器为驱动源、氩气为非线性介质的高次谐波产生（HHG）系统，观测到最高达70.6 eV（第59次谐波）的光子能量。该系统应用于Mg薄膜和Si3N4薄膜的极紫外（XUV）透射测量，并通过X射线光电子能谱（XPS）表征Mg薄膜表面化学状态，探讨表面氧化与XUV透射之间的关系。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 高次谐波产生（high-harmonic generation, HHG）可提供极紫外（XUV）波段相干超短脉冲光源，在时间分辨光谱和材料表征领域具有应用潜力，因此需要构建并验证可用于薄膜材料XUV表征的HHG系统。

Method: 以Yb:KGW激光系统（PHAROS, Light Conversion）作为驱动源、Ar气作为非线性介质构建HHG系统；利用该系统分别测量Mg薄膜和Si3N4薄膜的XUV透射率；同时采用X射线光电子能谱（XPS）对Mg薄膜表面化学状态进行表征，并将XPS结果与HHG光源测得的透射结果进行比较分析。

Result: 观测到的高次谐波最高光子能量达70.6 eV，对应第59次谐波阶次；成功测量了Mg薄膜和Si3N4薄膜的透射谱，并通过XPS与透射测量的对比，揭示了Mg薄膜表面状况（包括表面氧化）与其XUV透射之间的关系。

Conclusion: 研究结果表明，所构建的基于Ar气的HHG光源可有效应用于XUV波段薄膜透射特性的表征，验证了该系统的实用性。

Abstract: High-harmonic generation (HHG) provides a coherent ultrashort-pulse light source in the extreme- ultraviolet (XUV) region and has potential applications in time-resolved spectroscopy and materials characterization. In this study, we constructed an HHG system using a Yb:KGW laser system (PHAROS, Light Conversion) as the driving source and Ar gas as the nonlinear medium. High-order harmonics were observed up to a photon energy of 70.6 eV, corresponding to the 59th harmonic order. As an application of the developed XUV source, we measured the transmission of a Mg thin film and a Si3N4 membrane. In addition, the surface chemical state of the Mg thin film was characterized by X-ray photoelectron spectroscopy (XPS), and the results were compared with the transmission measured using the HHG source. This comparison was used to examine the relationship between the surface condition, including surface oxidation, and the XUV transmission of the Mg thin film. These results demonstrate the applicability of the developed Ar-based HHG source to the characterization of thin-film transmission in the XUV spectral region.

</details>


### [202] [Optical-Phonon-Enabled Large Lattice Thermal Conductivity Anisotropy in Hexagonal Perovskites Cs$BX_3$ ($B$ = Mg, Cd; $X$ = Cl, Br, I)](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.00512)
*Lingzhi Cao,Ying Song,Zhonghao Xia,Jianye Liu,Jiangang He*

Main category: cond-mat.mtrl-sci

TL;DR: 本文通过第一性原理（first-principles）计算研究了六方钙钛矿（perovskite）CsBX₃体系（B=Mg, Cd; X=Cl, Br, I）的晶格热输运性质，发现其面内与面外晶格热导率在300 K下呈现2.6–7.5倍的显著各向异性，尽管其弹性性质接近各向同性。机理分析表明中频光学声子（optical phonon）提供了高效的面外热输运通道，突破了传统上热输运由声学声子主导的认知。研究指出共享面八面体框架（face-sharing octahedral framework）是构建力学近各向同性非范德华晶体中强热导率各向异性的可行平台。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 具有强各向异性晶格热导率的材料对热管理应用至关重要，但传统上此类行为通常与层状或准一维范德华晶体及高弹性各向异性相关联。作者旨在探索能否在力学性质接近各向同性、非范德华晶体中发现显著的热导率各向异性，从而拓宽材料设计空间。

Method: 采用第一性原理计算（first-principles calculations）系统研究六方钙钛矿CsBX₃（B=Mg, Cd; X=Cl, Br, I）的晶格热输运。通过计算300 K下面内与面外晶格热导率，并结合弹性常数（C₃₃/C₁₁比值）评估弹性各向异性程度，进而分析各频率区间的声子色散对热输运的贡献机制。

Result: 在300 K下，计算得到的面内与面外晶格热导率分别为0.13–0.83和0.34–6.26 Wm⁻¹K⁻¹，各向异性比达到2.6–7.5。值得注意的是，该显著各向异性是在弹性常数比C₃₃/C₁₁仅为0.994–1.842（即弹性接近各向同性）的条件下实现的。机理层面，中频光学声子被识别为面外方向高效热输运通道的主要载体。

Conclusion: 该研究揭示了热输运可由光学声子主导这一反常规现象，并确立共享面八面体框架为在力学近各向同性、非范德华晶体中工程化强热导率各向异性的有前景结构平台，为热管理材料的设计提供了新思路。

Abstract: Materials exhibiting strongly anisotropic lattice thermal conductivity are desirable for thermal-management applications, yet such behavior is commonly associated with layered or quasi-one-dimensional van der Waals crystals and highly anisotropic elastic properties. Here, we investigate lattice thermal transport in the hexagonal perovskites Cs$BX_3$ ($B=$ Mg, Cd; $X=$ Cl, Br, I) using first-principles calculations. At 300~K, the calculated in-plane and out-of-plane lattice thermal conductivities range from 0.13--0.83 and 0.34--6.26~Wm$^{-1}$K$^{-1}$, respectively, corresponding to anisotropy ratios of 2.6--7.5. This pronounced anisotropy is remarkable given the relatively modest elastic anisotropy, characterized by $C_{33}/C_{11}$ = 0.994--1.842. Our analysis reveals that medium-frequency optical phonons provide an efficient out-of-plane heat-transport channel, contrary to the conventional picture in which heat transport is dominated by acoustic phonons. These findings identify face-sharing octahedral frameworks as a promising platform for engineering strong thermal-conductivity anisotropy in mechanically near-isotropic, non--van der Waals crystals.

</details>


### [203] [Lead-free piezoelectric perovskites for arterial-pulse e-skin: from configurational complexity to equivariant machine-learning potentials](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.00580)
*Sanaa Ismail,Hassan M. E. Azzazy,Zi-Kui Liu*

Main category: cond-mat.mtrl-sci

TL;DR: 本文综述了无铅压电材料（lead-free piezoelectric materials）在动脉脉搏柔性传感应用中的研究进展，并提出利用等变机器学习原子间势（equivariant machine-learning interatomic potentials）结合多层级泛函体系和有限温晶格动力学，以解决成分—温度—功能之间的计算难题，推动无铅脉搏传感器的临床化。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 心血管疾病是全球主要死亡原因，连续无创监测动脉脉搏是临床优先需求。当前性能最优的压电陶瓷含铅，其毒性与RoHS/REACH监管要求相冲突；而无铅候选材料BZT-BCT在可穿戴应用中面临灵敏度—柔韧悖论及多项计算壁垒，亟需系统性推进其设计与验证。

Method: 论文首先综述了无铅压电材料、准同型相界（morphotropic phase boundary）物理及已制备的柔性器件；随后提出以等变机器学习原子间势为核心的计算框架，并将其与分级泛函层级（tiered functional hierarchy）及有限温晶格动力学（finite-temperature lattice dynamics）耦合，用以全面采样310 K下的构型系综。

Result: 论文指出BZT-BCT在三临界准同型相界附近具有d33≈620 pC/N的高压电系数，可与软性PZT媲美且保持生物相容性，但其可穿戴应用受制于无序固溶体的构型组合爆炸、廉价密度泛函方法（density-functional approximations）的带隙误差以及0 K计算与310 K工作温度间的温差三个计算瓶颈。

Conclusion: 作者认为等变机器学习原子间势与多层级泛函和有限温度效应的结合，有望实现在体温条件下对完整构型系综的高效探索，弥合当前计算预测与临床可行无铅脉搏传感器之间的差距。

Abstract: Continuous, non-invasive monitoring of the arterial pulse is a clinical priority for cardiovascular disease, the leading cause of global mortality. Flexible piezoelectric electronic skins can transduce the 1-10 kPa pressure wave into a self-powered voltage, but the best-performing piezoceramics are lead-based, and their toxicity is incompatible with skin contact and with tightening RoHS/REACH regulation. Among lead-free alternatives, the BaTiO$3$-based solid solution BZT-BCT reaches $d{33} \approx 620$ pC/N near its tricritical morphotropic phase boundary, rivalling soft PZT while remaining biocompatible. Exploiting this in a wearable confronts a sensitivity-flexibility paradox and three computational walls: the combinatorial explosion of atomic configurations in a disordered solid solution, the band-gap error of affordable density-functional approximations, which corrupts leakage and insulation estimates, and the 0 K nature of standard calculations against a 310 K operating temperature. We review lead-free piezoelectrics, morphotropic-boundary physics and fabricated flexible devices, then argue that equivariant machine-learning interatomic potentials --- coupled to a tiered functional hierarchy and finite-temperature lattice dynamics --- can survey the full configurational ensemble at body temperature and close the gap to a clinically viable lead-free pulse sensor.

</details>


### [204] [GW and Bethe-Salpeter Theory for Molecular Polaritons, Quasiparticles, and Excitons](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.00594)
*Soohaeng Yoo Willow,Gi Beom Sim,Tae Hyeon Park,Tae In Kim,D. ChangMo Yang,Mikuláš Matoušek,Jiří Brabec,Libor Veis,Chang Woo Myung*

Main category: cond-mat.mtrl-sci

TL;DR: 本文研究了单模光学腔中分子的电子自能进入机制，使用偶极规范Pauli-Fierz哈密顿量和相干态QED Hartree-Fock参考态，揭示了腔通过三种通道影响体系：静态偶极自能（DSE）对参考轨道能的移动、对屏蔽相互作用的直接增强，以及携带双线性电子-光子耦合的极化激元极点。通过与腔Δ方法阶梯基准对比，系统评估了QED-GW方法在电离势（IP）和电子亲和能（EA）上的表现及其腔诱导偏移的误差特征。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 电子自能是准粒子理论的核心，但光学腔如何进入电子自能这一问题尚未明晰。作者旨在厘清腔耦合进入自能的机理路径，并对QED-GW方法的适用性和局限性进行系统基准测试。

Method: 采用偶极规范下的Pauli-Fierz哈密顿量，以相干态QED Hartree-Fock为参考态。将QED-GW方法计算的电离势（IP）和电子亲和能（EA）与腔Δ方法阶梯（从QED-HF到关联波函数方法）进行对比，并对耦合强度和失谐量进行扫描分析。

Result: 腔通过三条通道进入电子自能：参考轨道能的静态偶极自能（DSE）移动、对屏蔽相互作用的直接DSE增强、以及携带双线性电子-光子耦合的极化激元极点。QED-GW在闭壳层分子中系统高估了腔诱导的IP红移，但对EA移动几乎定量复现；对离子型分子这一排序反转，与已发表的QED耦合簇结果一致。光谱函数中出现权重随λ²标度的极化激元复制光发射边带，误差以λ的二次方为主导且由DSE驱动而非共振效应。

Conclusion: 腔效应通过DSE通道（非共振）主导QED-GW误差，该误差对λ二次方依赖。在静态屏蔽相互作用中裸光子交换抵消了直接相互作用中对应的DSE贡献，而交换和极化激元屏蔽修正保留；在Bethe-Salpeter方程中最低激发的净效应仅在氨分子中显著。涉及非束缚阴离子的激子结合能强烈依赖基组，应视为电子-空穴相互作用的诊断量而非基组收敛的分子量。

Abstract: The electron self-energy is central to quasiparticle theory, yet how an optical cavity enters it remains unclear. We address this question for a molecule in a single-mode cavity using the dipole-gauge Pauli-Fierz Hamiltonian and a coherent-state QED Hartree-Fock reference. The cavity enters through three channels: the static dipole self-energy (DSE) shift of reference orbital energies, direct DSE augmentation of the screened interaction, and the polariton pole carrying the bilinear electron-photon coupling. We benchmark QED-$GW$ ionization potentials (IPs) and electron affinities (EAs) against a cavity $Δ$-method ladder from QED-HF to correlated wave-function methods, whose cavity-induced shifts agree within 1 meV where directly comparable. For closed-shell molecules with unbound anions, $GW$ systematically overestimates cavity-induced IP redshifts, whereas EA shifts are reproduced nearly quantitatively, although this does not imply comparable accuracy for absolute EAs. For ionic molecules with bound anions, this ordering reverses, consistent with published QED coupled-cluster results. Coupling and detuning scans show that the error is predominantly quadratic in $λ$ and DSE-driven rather than resonant. The spectral function develops a polariton-replica photoemission sideband with weight scaling as $λ^2$. In the static screened interaction used in the Bethe-Salpeter equation, bare-photon exchange cancels the matching DSE contribution to the direct interaction, while exchange and polariton-screening corrections remain. Their net effect on the lowest excitation is appreciable only for ammonia in the molecules studied. Exciton-binding energies involving unbound anions are strongly basis-dependent and should therefore be viewed as diagnostics of electron-hole interactions rather than basis-converged molecular quantities.

</details>


### [205] [Overcoming the Efficiency-Stability Trade-off in Spin-Orbit Torque Devices with Thermally Robust BCC NiW Alloys](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.00623)
*Yu-Ming Pan,Chen-Yi Wei,Yi-Cheng Tsou,Tsung-Yu Pan,Guang-Yu Guo,Chih-Huang Lai*

Main category: cond-mat.mtrl-sci

TL;DR: 本研究通过工程化体心立方（BCC）镍掺杂钨（NiW）合金作为自旋流源，解决了传统重金属中自旋霍尔效率、热结构稳定性和垂直磁各向异性之间的固有矛盾，实现了超低临界电流密度的确定性磁化翻转，为高能效自旋轨道矩磁随机存储器（SOT-MRAM）提供了可扩展且热稳健的材料平台。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 高性能自旋轨道矩（spin-orbit torque, SOT）磁性存储器的发展受限于传统重金属中自旋霍尔效率、热结构稳定性与垂直磁各向异性（perpendicular magnetic anisotropy）之间的持续性权衡（trade-off）。作者旨在通过材料工程突破这一瓶颈。

Method: 作者设计了体心立方（body-centered-cubic, BCC）Ni掺杂W合金作为自旋流源，构建Ni₃₀W₇₀/CoFeB异质结构；采用谐波霍尔（harmonic Hall）和铁磁共振（ferromagnetic resonance）测量表征自旋霍尔角和界面自旋透明度；并结合第一性原理（first-principles）计算揭示其内在自旋霍尔电导率增强的物理机制。

Result: Ni₃₀W₇₀/CoFeB异质结构实现了临界电流密度低至1.78 MA/cm²的确定性垂直磁化翻转，约为β-W的三分之一，同时保持高各向异性场（8,500 Oe）和57.9的热稳定性因子。该合金在450 °C退火后仍保持结构完整性和相邻CoFeB层的垂直磁各向异性；测得自旋霍尔角为-0.39，界面自旋透明度为0.75。第一性原理计算显示，W-rich BCC NiW合金中费米能级位于自旋-轨道耦合（spin-orbit coupling）诱导的带隙内，从而增强内在自旋霍尔电导率。

Conclusion: BCC NiW合金被确立为一种可扩展、热稳健的材料平台，适用于高能效的自旋轨道力矩磁随机存取存储器（SOT-MRAM），能够满足后端集成（back-end-of-line integration）所需的严苛热加工条件。

Abstract: The development of high-performance spin-orbit torque (SOT) magnetic memories is fundamentally constrained by a persistent trade-off between spin Hall efficiency, thermal structural stability, and perpendicular magnetic anisotropy in conventional heavy metals. Here, we overcome this limitation by engineering body-centered-cubic (BCC) Ni-doped W alloys as highly efficient and thermally robust spin-current sources. Ni$_{30}$W$_{70}$/CoFeB heterostructures achieve deterministic out-of-plane magnetization switching at an ultra-low critical current density of 1.78 MA/cm$^2$, nearly threefold lower than that of $β$-W, while maintaining a high anisotropy field of 8,500 Oe and a thermal stability factor of 57.9. The BCC Ni$_{30}$W$_{70}$ alloy preserves its structural integrity and the perpendicular magnetic anisotropy of the adjacent CoFeB layer after annealing at 450 $^\circ$C, demonstrating robustness under the stringent thermal processing conditions relevant to back-end-of-line integration. Harmonic Hall and ferromagnetic resonance measurements reveal a large spin Hall angle of -0.39 and a high interfacial spin transparency of 0.75, demonstrating efficient spin-current generation and interfacial transmission. First-principles calculations further reveal enhanced intrinsic spin Hall conductivity in W-rich BCC NiW alloys, associated with the Fermi level lying within a spin-orbit-coupling-induced band gap. These findings establish BCC NiW alloys as a scalable and thermally resilient material platform for energy-efficient SOT-MRAM.

</details>


### [206] [Phase Switchable Photocatalytic Water Splitting via a Paraelectric-Ferroelectric Transition in Zr2Ge2S6 Monolayer: A Comprehensive Theoretical Insights](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.00724)
*Jubair Hossan Abir,Tauhidur Rahman,Md. Tanvir Khan,S. S. B. Pallab,Raihana Shams Islam,Saleh Hasan Naqib*

Main category: cond-mat.mtrl-sci

TL;DR: 本文通过第一性原理计算，证明Zr2Ge2S6单层材料可以通过顺电-铁电相变有效调控光催化水分解活性和能量转换效率，其太阳能制氢效率从顺电相的7.71%提升至铁电相的15.31%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 光催化水分解（photocatalytic water splitting, PWS）是应对全球能源危机和产生可再生清洁氢燃料的重要技术，虽然众多二维（2D）材料被提出作为潜在的光催化剂，但受限于性能调控挑战，有效调控光催化反应并提高能量转换效率的策略仍然有限。

Method: 采用第一性原理计算（first-principles calculations），研究Zr2Ge2S6单层材料通过顺电-铁电相变（paraelectric-ferroelectric phase transition）对光催化活性和能量转换效率的调控效应，并分析了结构稳定性、力学性能、带隙、带边位置及载流子利用等多方面特性。

Result: Zr2Ge2S6单层材料表现出优异的结构稳定性、良好的力学性能、合适的带隙、最优带边位置及广谱光吸收。在铁电相中该材料具有更高的氧化电位和对光生空穴更强的驱动力以促进析氧反应（oxygen evolution reaction, OER）；而顺电相则为光生电子提供更大的还原电位和驱动力以促进析氢反应（hydrogen evolution reaction, HER）。太阳能制氢效率从顺电相的7.71%提升至铁电相的15.31%。

Conclusion: 该理论研究不仅强调了铁电极化在光催化水分解中的关键作用，还为通过铁电切换调控二维铁电材料的光催化性能提供了一种有效策略。

Abstract: Photocatalytic water splitting (PWS) is a promising technology for addressing the global energy crisis and producing renewable and clean hydrogen fuel. Although numerous 2D materials have recently been proposed as potential photocatalysts, effective strategies for regulating photocatalytic reactions and improving energy conversion efficiency remain limited due to performance regulation challenges. Here, using first-principles calculations, we demonstrate that the photocatalytic activity and energy conversion efficiency of a Zr2Ge2S6 monolayer can be effectively tuned through a paraelectric-ferroelectric phase transition. The Zr2Ge2S6 monolayer exhibits excellent structural stability, favorable mechanical properties, a suitable band gap, optimal band edge positions, and broad-spectrum light absorption. Moreover, the Zr2Ge2S6 monolayer exhibits a higher oxidation potential and a stronger driving force for photogenerated holes to promote oxygen evolution reaction (OER) in the ferroelectric phase. In contrast, the paraelectric phase provides photogenerated electrons with a greater reduction potential and driving force for hydrogen evolution reaction (HER). The solar-to-hydrogen conversion efficiency is also strongly influenced by the phase transition, increasing from 7.71% in the paraelectric phase to 15.31% in the ferroelectric phase because of the improved carrier utilization. Our theoretical investigation not only highlights the crucial role of ferroelectric polarization in photocatalytic water splitting but also provides an effective strategy for tuning the photocatalytic properties of 2D ferroelectric materials through ferroelectric switching.

</details>


### [207] [Agentic programs: an emerging form of scientific software in computational materials science](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.00795)
*Yunsung Lim,Haekwan Jeon,Jaesun Kim,Jisu Kim,Seungwu Han*

Main category: cond-mat.mtrl-sci

TL;DR: 本文提出"代理程序（agentic programs）"这一新兴科学软件形态，将其定义为确定性算法与有界大语言模型判断（bounded LLM-based judgment）相结合的产物。作者以DeMARS系统为例，展示其如何用于从实验测量的无序晶体结构构建原子级模型。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统的计算材料科学将算法任务交由计算机执行而科学判断保留给人类，这种分工模式制约了自动化水平。作者认为近期基于大语言模型（LLM）的智能体工具使一种新型科学软件成为可能，值得系统阐述。

Method: 作者提出"代理程序"的概念框架，涵盖四个核心要素：确定性算法与有界LLM判断的结合、任务特定验证（task-specific verification）、阶段性成熟（episodic maturation）以及生产环境中的完全委托（complete delegation）。并以DeMARS系统作为该概念的具体化实例。

Result: 文章以DeMARS作为代理程序概念的具体验证，该系统能够从实验测量的无序晶体结构出发自动构建原子级模型，体现了LLM判断与确定性算法的融合。

Conclusion: 基于LLM的智能体工具将催生一种新的科学软件形态——代理程序，它可承担包括科学判断在内的完整生产任务，为计算科学实现更深层次的自动化和委托提供了可行路径。

Abstract: Computational materials science has traditionally delegated algorithmic tasks to computers while leaving scientific judgments to humans. We argue that recent LLM-based agent harnesses enable an emerging form of scientific software, agentic programs, that combine deterministic algorithms with bounded LLM-based judgment, task-specific verification, episodic maturation, and complete delegation in production. We illustrate this concept with DeMARS, an agentic program for constructing atomistic models from experimentally measured disordered crystal structures.

</details>


### [208] [Magnetic quantum defects in a uniaxial antiferromagnetic insulator](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.00801)
*Shangfei Wu,Laur Peedu,Zhihao Wang,Xuecong Wang,Xianghan Xu,Kai Du,Sang-Wook Cheong,Aleksei Boldin,Joosep Link,Ivo Heinmaa,Raivo Stern,Sai Mu,Urmas Nagel,Toomas Rõõm,Girsh Blumberg*

Main category: cond-mat.mtrl-sci

TL;DR: 利用偏振分辨拉曼光谱（polarization-resolved Raman spectroscopy）和太赫兹吸收光谱（terahertz absorption spectroscopy），在单轴反铁磁绝缘体Cr₂O₃中发现三种由铬空位（chromium vacancy）诱导的激发，其中能量最低的64 cm⁻¹模式被解释为铬空位态内局域磁矩的局域磁激发，表明Cr₂O₃中的磁性缺陷态具有量子应用潜力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 点缺陷已被成功应用于量子比特（quantum qubit）、单光子发射器（single-photon emitter）和量子计量传感器（nanoscale sensor）等多种量子技术，但其进一步发展面临关键挑战，即如何发现和探索满足量子应用标准的合适缺陷-宿主材料系统。

Method: 采用偏振分辨拉曼光谱和太赫兹吸收光谱对单轴反铁磁绝缘体Cr₂O₃进行实验表征，并结合缺陷超胞（defect supercell）第一性原理计算进行理论解读。

Result: 在Cr₂O₃中发现三种由铬空位诱导的激发，能量尺度为几十毫电子伏特，且具有二重简并性。其中能量最低的模式位于64 cm⁻¹，尖锐且对沿易轴方向（easy-axis）的外磁场敏感，在约6T的旋磁翻越（spin-flop）区域附近，该模式从64 cm⁻¹软化至27 cm⁻¹。

Conclusion: 实验与计算结果表明，64 cm⁻¹处的模式源于铬空位态中局域磁矩的局域磁激发，确立了Cr₂O₃中的磁性缺陷态具有量子应用的潜力。

Abstract: Point defects have been successfully utilized in various quantum technologies, serving as quantum qubits for quantum computation, single-photon emitters for quantum communication, and nanoscale sensors for quantum metrology. However, their further development faces key challenges, particularly in discovering and exploring suitable defect-host systems that meet the necessary criteria for quantum applications. Here, using polarization-resolved Raman spectroscopy and terahertz absorption spectroscopy, we discover three distinct chromium-vacancy-induced excitations in the uniaxial antiferromagnetic insulator, Cr$_2$O$_3$. These vacancy-induced excitations have an energy scale of a few tens of millielectronvolts and are twofold degenerate, and the lowest one at 64 $cm^{-1}$ is sharp and sensitive to the external magnetic field along the easy-axis direction, particularly close to the spin-flop regime around 6T, where the mode softens from 64 to 27$cm^{-1}$. Based on the defect supercell first-principles calculations, we interpret the mode at 64 $cm^{-1}$ as a local magnetic excitation of the local moment within the chromium vacancy state. Our results establish that the magnetic defect states in Cr$_2$O$_3$ have potential for quantum applications.

</details>


### [209] [Metastable defects in III-Nitrides](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.00835)
*A. E. Belyaev*

Main category: cond-mat.mtrl-sci

TL;DR: 本文是一篇综述性文章，系统总结了III族氮化物（III-nitrides）中亚稳态（metastable states）的相关概念、机制、实验特征、理论方法及器件影响，并提出了在GaN、AlGaN和InGaN基器件中避免亚稳态缺陷负面效应的一系列策略。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: III族氮化物（AlN、GaN、InN及其合金）中因存在多种竞争晶体结构、缺陷构型和相行为，会产生强烈的亚稳态，这些态对光学、电学乃至力学性能均有显著影响，然而相关系统性梳理仍较欠缺，因此需要一篇简洁、结构化的综述来整合该领域的核心知识。

Method: 本文采用综述式（review-style）结构化论述方式，涵盖亚稳态的主要概念、内在机制、实验可探测信号（experimental signatures）、理论方法以及器件层面的含义，并针对性地提出工程建议。

Result: 研究总结出避免亚稳态缺陷影响的三条核心途径：通过生长和掺杂控制（growth and doping control）阻止缺陷形成；借助退火（annealing）或光学激活（optical activation）稳定其电子构型；设计器件架构以最小化由场或温度诱导的转变。这些措施可保障载流子密度（carrier density）一致性、提高迁移率（mobility），并增强基于GaN、AlGaN和InGaN器件的光学与电学可靠性。

Conclusion: III族氮化物中亚稳态的材料物理问题可通过调控生长过程、稳定电子结构及优化器件设计三类协同策略加以有效缓解，由此实现高性能和高可靠性的III族氮化物基器件。

Abstract: Metastable states in III-nitrides (AlN, GaN, InN and their alloys) are an important topic across materials science, device physics, and epitaxial growth because these compounds exhibit multiple competing crystal structures, defect configurations, and phase behaviors that strongly affect optical, electrical, and mechanical properties. The paper is a concise, structured review-style summary covering the main concepts, mechanisms, experimental signatures, theoretical approaches, and device implications. Actions that should be proposed to avoid effect of metastable defects in III-Nitride based devices are discussed. Avoiding metastable defect effects in III nitride devices requires preventing their formation (through growth and doping control), stabilizing their electronic configuration (by annealing or optical activation), and designing device architectures that minimize field- or temperature induced transitions. These combined actions ensure consistent carrier density, higher mobility, and superior optical and electrical reliability in GaN , AlGaN , and InGaN based devices.

</details>


### [210] [Independent Tuning of Surface Acoustic-Waves and Spin-Waves via Buffer-Layer Engineering in Co2FeGe Heusler Thin Films](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.00864)
*A. V. Achuthan,A. Vovk,S. Bunyaev,B. Postolnyi,P. Štrichovanec,P. A. Algarabel,K. Załęski,J. P. Araujo,G. N. Kakazei,A. Trzaskowska*

Main category: cond-mat.mtrl-sci

TL;DR: 本文通过引入Cr和W缓冲层，在MgO(001)衬底上生长的Co2FeGe全Heusler磁性薄膜中实现了声学和磁学激发的独立调控。研究表明W缓冲层可使Rayleigh声表面波频率降低16%，同时将自旋波群速度提高约34%，证明了缓冲层工程可有效用于混合自旋声学器件设计。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 理解和控制磁性薄膜中的声学与自旋波激发对于发展磁子器件（magnonic devices）和自旋声学器件（spin-acoustic devices）至关重要，需要在单一材料体系中实现对这些激发的独立调控。

Method: 采用Cr和W缓冲层工程在MgO(001)衬底上生长Co2FeGe全Heusler薄膜，利用布里渊光散射（Brillouin light scattering, BLS）光谱和铁磁共振（ferromagnetic resonance, FMR）技术表征Rayleigh与Sezawa声表面波（surface acoustic waves, SAWs）以及Damon-Eshbach和垂直驻立自旋波（perpendicular standing spin-wave, PSSW）模式，并结合有限元模拟（finite-element simulation）验证声学数据。

Result: 声学色散强烈依赖于缓冲层材料：W缓冲层使Rayleigh声表面波频率相较无缓冲层薄膜降低约16%，主要归因于质量负载效应和声阻抗变化；同时缓冲层通过不同的物理机制将自旋波群速度提高约34%。有限元模拟结果与实验声学数据高度吻合。

Conclusion: 缓冲层工程是实现弹性和磁学激发独立调控的有效策略，为混合自旋声学技术提供了通用平台。

Abstract: Understanding and controlling acoustic and spin-wave excitations in magnetic thin films is critical for the development of magnonic and spin-acoustic devices. We report on the use of Cr and W buffer layers to independently modify the acoustic and magnetic excitations in Co2FeGe full-Heusler thin films grown on MgO(001). Using Brillouin light scattering (BLS) spectroscopy and ferromagnetic resonance (FMR), we probed Rayleigh and Sezawa surface acoustic waves (SAWs) alongside Damon-Eshbach and perpendicular standing spin-wave (PSSW) modes. Our results show that acoustic dispersion depends strongly on the buffer material; W-buffered films exhibit a pronounced 16% reduction in Rayleigh SAW frequency compared to buffer-free films, primarily due to mass loading and acoustic impedance shifts. While the buffer layers significantly shift acoustic frequencies, they simultaneously modify the dynamic magnetic response (increasing spin-wave group velocity by ~34%) through different physical mechanisms. Finite-element simulations show excellent agreement with the experimental acoustic data. These findings demonstrate that buffer-layer engineering is an effective strategy for the independent tailoring of elastic and magnetic excitations, providing a versatile platform for hybrid spin-acoustic technologies.

</details>


### [211] [Large Exchange Magnetostriction in a Kagome Antiferromagnet at Room Temperature](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.00887)
*Jie Du,Liang Yao,Hang Li,Xiaodong Zhou,Yuan Yao,Xuekui Xi,Yong-Chang Lau,Wenhong Wang*

Main category: cond-mat.mtrl-sci

TL;DR: 本文报道了kagome反铁磁材料YMn6Sn6中观测到的大磁致伸缩（magnetostriction）效应：在室温下体积磁致伸缩超过400 ppm，且在高达9 T的磁场下近乎完全可逆、几乎无磁滞，源于层内铁磁与层间交换相互作用的竞争。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 高性能磁致伸缩材料的研发对传感、驱动和微机电系统（microelectromechanical systems）等技术的发展至关重要。然而，虽然少数铁磁体中已观察到显著的磁致伸缩效应，但针对更广泛反铁磁体系的系统性探索仍然有限，研究者因此希望在反铁磁材料中寻找新的磁致伸缩候选体系。

Method: 本研究在kagome反铁磁体YMn6Sn6中施加磁场，观察体系从螺旋磁基态向共线场极化态的场诱导演化过程，并通过实验测量（晶格应变与体积磁致伸缩）与理论计算相结合的方法，揭示磁致伸缩的微观起源及相应的晶格畸变路径。

Result: 实验观察到体系在上述磁态转变过程中伴随大的各向异性晶格应变，室温下体积磁致伸缩超过400 ppm；磁致伸缩响应在高达9 T的磁场下近乎完全可逆且几乎无磁滞。理论与实验联合表明，该效应源于层内铁磁与层间交换相互作用之间的竞争，并由此产生强方向依赖性的晶格畸变路径。

Conclusion: 该研究将kagome螺旋磁体确立为一种可调控的低耗散磁弹功能平台，可用于敏感磁机械应用；其近乎无磁滞的特性有效抑制了通常与畴壁钉扎（domain-wall pinning）相关的能量耗散。

Abstract: The pursuit of high-performance magnetostrictive materials is crucial for advancing technologies in sensing, actuation, and microelectromechanical systems. Although pronounced magnetostrictive effects have been observed in a few ferromagnets, a systematic exploration of magnetostriction across a broader range of antiferromagnets remains limited. Here, we report the observation of large magnetostriction in the kagome antiferromagnet YMn6Sn6. Under a magnetic field, the system undergoes a field-induced evolution from a helical magnetic ground state toward a collinear field-polarized state, accompanied by a large anisotropic lattice strain and a substantial volume magnetostriction exceeding 400 ppm at room temperature. Remarkably, the magnetostrictive response remains nearly fully reversible up to 9 T with nearly hysteresis-free behavior, effectively minimizing the energy dissipation commonly associated with domain-wall pinning. Combined experimental measurements and theoretical calculations reveal that the large, nearly hysteresis-free magnetostriction originates from the competition between intralayer ferromagnetic and interlayer exchange interactions. This exchange-driven magnetoelastic coupling further gives rise to a strongly direction-dependent lattice distortion pathway. This work establishes kagome helimagnets as a tunable platform for low-dissipation magnetoelastic functionalities and responsive magnetomechanical applications.

</details>


### [212] [Fourier Neural Operators for Composition-Driven Crystal Structure Discovery](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.00900)
*Zhijie Yu,Jingyu Li,Yang Huang,Jingrun Chen*

Main category: cond-mat.mtrl-sci

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于傅里叶神经算子（Fourier Neural Operator, FNO）的晶体场求解器，将其与条件变分自编码器耦合，构建了一个"生成-求解"框架，可从指定的化学式和晶格参数生成周期性的数密度场和电子密度场，再通过峰检测、位置与权重优化实现原子重构，最终在104种化学式中生成了多样且有效的晶体结构。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 晶体材料发现对能源、电子和催化领域至关重要，但巨大的化学与结构空间使得穷举筛选不可行。现有基于体素（voxel）的方法受限于三维卷积神经网络的局部感受野和高维变分自编码器的后验坍缩（posterior collapse）问题，亟需一种能捕捉周期性晶体场中长程关联的新方法。

Method: 作者开发了基于FNO的晶体场求解器，通过全局傅里叶模式捕获周期性晶体场中的长程关联。在此基础上构建了一个耦合的生成-求解框架：条件变分自编码器在低维基系数空间中生成候选晶格参数，随后依次进行密度场预测、峰检测、位置优化和权重优化以重构原子结构，最后通过体素级过滤、机器学习原子间势驰豫和第一性原理计算对重构结构进行筛选。

Result: 该框架在104种化学式上生成了新颖结构，重构精度具有竞争力，同时展示了较高的生成多样性和结构有效性。通过将FNO扩展到周期性晶体场并与成分条件化晶格生成相耦合，为从指定化学成分进行晶体结构发现提供了可扩展的路径。

Conclusion: 该工作表明，基于全局傅里叶模式的方法能够有效克服局部卷积的局限，与低维生成模型耦合后可实现从化学组成到可行晶体结构的规模化生成与筛选，为晶体结构发现提供了一种可扩展的解决方案。

Abstract: Crystalline materials discovery is essential for energy, electronics, and catalysis, but the vast chemical and structural space makes exhaustive screening infeasible. Existing voxel-based methods are limited by the local receptive fields of three-dimensional convolutional neural networks and the posterior collapse of high-dimensional variational autoencoders. Here, we develop a Fourier Neural Operator (FNO)-based crystal-field solver that maps a prescribed chemical formula and lattice parameters to periodic number-density and electron-density fields. By operating on global Fourier modes, the solver captures long-range correlations in periodic crystal fields beyond conventional local convolutions. Building on this solver, we construct a coupled generation-solving framework in which a conditional variational autoencoder generates diverse candidate lattice parameters in a low-dimensional basis-coefficient space, followed by density-field prediction and atomic reconstruction through peak detection, position optimization, and weight optimization. The reconstructed structures are further screened using voxel-level filtering, machine-learning interatomic-potential relaxation, and first-principle calculations. The framework generates novel structures across 104 chemical formulas with competitive reconstruction accuracy, demonstrating high generative diversity and structural validity. By extending Fourier neural operators to periodic crystal fields and coupling them with composition-conditioned lattice generation, our approach provides a scalable route to crystal structure discovery from prescribed chemical compositions.

</details>


### [213] [Element priors and target support shape chemical transfer in materials graph networks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.00915)
*Ran Zhao,Kangming Li*

Main category: cond-mat.mtrl-sci

TL;DR: 本研究通过保留元素划分（held-out-element splits）和增量目标支持（incremental target support）实验，系统分离了材料图神经网络（graph neural networks）在化学迁移中两条路径——静态元素关系与目标结构监督——的各自作用，揭示目标支持能将迁移从依赖静态元素预定义关系转向从含目标环境的学习。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 材料图神经网络经常需要迁移到训练数据中覆盖较弱的化学区域，这种迁移既可能依赖元素间的预定义关系，也可能依赖含目标结构的监督证据，但两者通常相互纠缠，难以厘清各自贡献。研究者希望通过实验设计将两条路径分离，以深入理解其作用机制。

Method: 采用保留元素划分和增量目标支持两种实验策略分离影响因素；通过匹配扰动（matched perturbations）检验表征所引入的元素共享效应是否超越输入维度或数值形式的影响；使用免标签相似度图先验（label-free similarity-graph prior）测试对零样本（zero-shot）误差的改善；在ALIGNN和CGCNN两个模型上比较one-hot、k-hot和连续输入（continuous inputs）的表现，并通过冻结初始元素投影检验其贡献。

Result: 在没有含目标训练结构时，形成能误差强烈依赖于元素表征，尤其对H、O和F元素；匹配扰动显示表征诱导的元素共享效应独立于输入维度和数值形式，标签无关的相似度图先验可降低部分零样本误差。加入少量含目标结构后，误差显著下降，且one-hot、k-hot和连续输入在ALIGNN与CGCNN上的差异收窄。校准仅能解释部分恢复效果，而在ALIGNN的六个分割中，冻结初始元素投影仍保留了五个中的大部分增益。

Conclusion: 目标支持将化学迁移的主导机制从依赖静态元素关系转向从含目标环境中学习，这一发现对模型设计和数据扩充策略具有指导意义。

Abstract: Materials graph neural networks must often transfer to chemical regions weakly represented in training data. Such transfer can rely on predefined relations among elements or supervised evidence from target-containing structures, but these pathways are usually entangled. Here, held-out-element splits and incremental target support separate their roles. Without target-containing training structures, formation-energy errors depend strongly on the element representation, particularly for H, O and F.
