Matched perturbations show that representation-induced sharing matters beyond input dimension or numerical form, while a label-free similarity-graph prior reduces selected zero-shot errors. Adding a few target-containing structures sharply lowers errors and contracts differences among one-hot, k-hot and continuous inputs across ALIGNN and CGCNN. Calibration explains only part of this recovery, and freezing the initial element projection preserves most gains in five of six ALIGNN splits. Target support therefore shifts chemical transfer from reliance on static element relations toward learning from target-containing environments.

</details>


### [214] [Beyond Panchromatic Absorption: Deciphering the Excited-State Maze from Light Absorption to Photocatalysis in Dye-Sensitized MOFs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.00931)
*Manuela L. Kim,Mauricio E. Calvo,Katsuya Teshima,Fabio La Mattina,Eugenio H. Otal*

Main category: cond-mat.mtrl-sci

TL;DR: 本文通过多光谱框架（包括二维光致发光PL/PLE映射和波长分辨连续波X波段光-EPR）解码重氮敏化UiO-66中光激发态的物理命运，区分了“有效红移”与“无效发射性红移”，并提出新的光学重叠描述符S_Exc量化敏化剂设计中吸收红移与电荷转移效率之间的权衡。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 金属有机框架（metal–organic frameworks, MOFs）是有前景的光催化剂，可通过连接官能化扩展可见光吸收；但吸收边红移并不保证催化效率提升。因此需要建立方法以揭示光物理机制，识别哪些吸收带对应有效的电荷转移路径。

Method: 采用多光谱框架，结合二维光致发光（photoluminescence , PL/PLE）映射与波长分辨连续波X波段光-EPR（photo-EPR）光谱；将可见吸收与光EPR和PLE作用光谱关联，并提出一种能定量刻画吸收-效率权衡的新光学重叠描述符（S_Exc）。

Result: 高活性UiO-66-Anisole（相对TiO2活性97%）中，光EPR沿循新吸收带，确认激发填充产生持久自旋分离态的电荷转移路径；而低活性UiO-66-β-萘酚（活性4%）的扩展可见吸收仅被PLE追踪而非光EPR，表明激发被限制在刚性分子内氢键锁定的酮腙互变异构体局部态中。S_Exc描述符可定量捕捉该系列中的这种权衡关系。

Conclusion: 光敏剂设计应抑制刚性互变异构陷阱并瞄准特定的电荷转移流形（λ≤500 nm），而非仅追求最大化表观全色吸收谱宽。

Abstract: Metal--organic frameworks (MOFs) are promising photocatalysts whose visible-light absorption can be extended through linker functionalization; however, a red-shifted absorption edge does not guarantee enhanced efficiency. Here, we establish a multi-spectroscopic framework to decipher the photophysical fate of photoexcited states in diazo-sensitized UiO-66 using 2D photoluminescence (PL/PLE) mapping and wavelength-resolved continuous-wave X-band photo-EPR. By correlating visible absorption with photo-EPR and PLE action spectra, we distinguish a \textit{productive red shift} from a \textit{non-productive emissive red shift}. In highly active UiO-66-Anisole (97\% activity relative to \ch{TiO2}), photo-EPR tracks the new absorption band, confirming that excitation populates a charge-transfer pathway yielding persistent, spin-separated states. Conversely, poorly active UiO-66-$β$-naphthol (4\%) exhibits an extended visible absorption tracked by PLE but not photo-EPR. This reflects excitation trapping in a localized state caused by an \textit{ortho}-$\mathrm{OH}$ group forming a rigid intramolecular hydrogen bond, which locks the keto-hydrazone tautomer and disrupts the conjugated azo bridge. A new optical overlap descriptor ($S_{\mathrm{Exc}}$) quantitatively captures this trade-off across the series. We demonstrate that photosensitizer design must suppress rigid tautomeric traps and target specific charge-transfer manifolds ($λ\le 500$~nm) rather than merely maximizing apparent panchromatic absorption breadth.

</details>


### [215] [Weisfeiler-Lehman subtree encoding for Bayesian optimization of atomic configurations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.00953)
*Akira Kusaba,Tatoshi Yonemori,Tetsuji Kuboyama,Yoshihiro Kangawa*

Main category: cond-mat.mtrl-sci

TL;DR: 本文提出将Weisfeiler-Lehman (WL)子树核作为原子构型的编码方式引入贝叶斯优化（Bayesian optimization, BO）框架中，将构型视为元素标记图并按共享的局部结构模式数量度量相似性。该方法可被简化等价为局部拓扑模式L²归一化直方图特征的内积，因此在现有BO框架中仅需作为普通描述符引入。在立方BC₂N的基态构型搜索基准中，WL编码在5轮独立实验中几乎立刻达到基态（平均108±5次评估），而one-hot基线需要280±122次评估。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 贝叶斯优化原子构型的效率在很大程度上取决于构型编码方式，现有one-hot等基线编码方式搜索效率较低，需要开发更能刻画构型间局部结构相似性的编码方案，以加速基态构型搜索。

Method: 将WL子树核引入基于贝叶斯优化的构型搜索框架：首先将原子构型表示为元素标记图（element-labeled graphs），通过WL子树核度量构型间的局部结构模式共享程度；由于该核可被显式特征（局部拓扑模式的L²归一化直方图）的内积再现，编码便以普通描述符形式直接嵌入现有BO框架。并用通用机器学习原子间势（universal machine-learning interatomic potential）对立方BC₂N进行基态构型搜索基准验证。

Result: 在立方BC₂N基态构型搜索中，五个独立轮次均从共享的100个随机初始化样本出发，WL编码几乎立即达到基态，平均仅需108±5次评估即可收敛，而one-hot基线需要280±122次评估。WL驱动的采样器首先穷尽简并基态组，然后按能量升序自下而上发现亚稳简并组。

Conclusion: WL子树核编码显著提升了贝叶斯优化在原子构型基态搜索中的效率，且因其显式特征形式可无缝融入现有BO框架；该编码还能按照能量从低到高的顺序系统地探索简并构型空间。

Abstract: The efficiency of Bayesian optimization (BO) of atomic configurations depends strongly on how configurations are encoded. We introduce the Weisfeiler-Lehman (WL) subtree kernel, which views configurations as element-labeled graphs and measures their similarity by how many local structural patterns they share, into Bayesian-optimization-based configuration search. Because this kernel is reproduced as the plain inner product of explicit features (L$^2$-normalized histograms of local topological patterns), introducing it reduces to introducing the corresponding features: the encoding enters existing BO frameworks as an ordinary descriptor. In a benchmark ground-state configuration search of cubic BC$_2$N evaluated with a universal machine-learning interatomic potential, the WL encoding reached the ground state almost immediately after a shared random initialization of 100 samples in every one of five independent rounds (108$\pm$5 evaluations on average), whereas the one-hot baseline required 280$\pm$122 evaluations; the WL-driven sampler first exhausted the degenerate ground-state group and then discovered the metastable degenerate groups from the bottom up, in order of increasing energy.

</details>


### [216] [First-Principles Electronic Structure Calculation of Crystals in Laboratory Magnetic Fields](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.01053)
*Sichao Wang,Chengye Lü,Xingao Gong,Yingwei Chen,Hongjun Xiang*

Main category: cond-mat.mtrl-sci

TL;DR: 该研究首次构建了基于规范包含高斯型原子轨道的磁性Bloch基组，用于在实验可达磁场强度下对晶体材料进行第一性原理电子结构计算。该框架大幅减少所需基函数数量并保持稀疏性，显著降低成本，并通过再现石墨烯的朗道能级谱（Landau-level spectrum）验证了其正确性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 外磁场能定性重塑晶体的电子结构，从而支撑量子霍尔物理、朗道能级谱及场致拓扑相的研究。然而，在实验室尺度磁场下进行第一性原理处理受限于磁通量子化效应，磁性晶胞包含大量化学晶胞，使得实空间和平面波计算成本过高，亟需新的高效计算方法。

Method: 作者构建了一种磁性Bloch基组，由线性组合的规范包含高斯型原子轨道（gauge-including Gaussian-type atomic orbitals）构成。这些轨道引入了磁场平移对称性（magnetic translation symmetry）所要求的磁场相位因子。该框架所需的基函数数量远少于对应磁性超胞的实空间或平面波表示，同时保留原子中心基组的稀疏性，从而大幅降低计算成本。

Result: 该框架通过第一性原理再现了石墨烯的朗道能级谱，验证了方法的正确性与实用性。数值结果证实该方法能以较低计算代价在实验可达磁场强度下准确描述晶体的磁场响应。

Conclusion: 该研究提供了一条在实验可达外磁场条件下模拟晶体材料的实用第一性原理路径，为在实验室尺度磁场下研究电子结构及场致拓扑相开辟了新的计算方法。

Abstract: External magnetic fields can qualitatively reshape the electronic structure of crystals, underpinning quantum Hall physics, Landau-level spectra and field-induced topological phases. Their first-principles treatment at laboratory-scale fields is, however, hindered by magnetic-flux quantization, which requires magnetic unit cells with areas inversely proportional to the applied field. Such cells contain a large number of chemical unit cells, rendering real-space and plane-wave calculations prohibitively expensive. Here we, for the first time, construct a magnetic Bloch basis built from linear combinations of gauge-including Gaussian-type atomic orbitals, which incorporate the magnetic-field phase factors required by magnetic translation symmetry. The framework requires far fewer basis functions than real-space or plane-wave representations of the same magnetic supercell and retains the sparsity of an atom-centred basis, together substantially reducing computational cost. We validate the framework by reproducing Landau-level spectrum of graphene from first principles. This approach provides a practical route to simulations of crystalline materials under experimentally accessible magnetic fields.

</details>


### [217] [Exchange striction determines how fast antiferromagnetic insulators demagnetize](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.01069)
*Aleksandr Buzdakov,Ravi Kaushik,Nikolai Khokhlov,Sergey Artyukhin,Alexey Kimel*

Main category: cond-mat.mtrl-sci

TL;DR: 本文通过时间分辨二次谐波（second-harmonic generation）实验和第一性原理（first-principles）计算揭示，反铁磁绝缘体超快退磁速率由交换伸缩效应（exchange striction）决定：Cr2O3在晶格被驱动至Neel温度以上后2皮秒内完成退磁，比结构相似的FeBO3快两个数量级，其原因是Cr-Cr短接触使交换耦合（exchange coupling）对原子位移的敏感度高出约十倍，从而拓宽了声子衰变为磁振子对（magnon pairs）的相空间。交换耦合对离子位移的导数可作为可计算参数，用于预测反铁磁绝缘体的退磁速率并筛选候选材料。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 反铁磁体兼具太赫兹（terahertz）自旋动力学与对外杂散场不敏感的特性，其有序态的操控速度限制了器件运行上限。然而，飞秒光学脉冲退磁不同反铁磁绝缘体的时间尺度从皮秒跨越到纳秒，目前没有已知的材料参数能解释这种差异或预测新化合物的行为。

Method: 采用时间分辨二次谐波实验测量退磁动力学；利用第一性原理计算追踪交换伸缩效应；构建了包含从头计算参数的自旋-晶格（spin-lattice）模拟以定量验证。

Result: Cr2O3在晶格被驱动至Neel温度（Néel temperature）以上后2皮秒内退磁，比结构相似的FeBO3快两个数量级；第一性原理计算将差异归因于交换伸缩：短的Cr-Cr接触使交换耦合对原子位移的敏感度高出十倍，并拓宽了声子衰变为磁振子对的相空间；自旋-晶格模拟成功再现了实测比值的数量级。

Conclusion: 交换耦合对离子位移的导数确立了为可计算参数，可预测绝缘反铁磁体的退磁速率，并为热辅助反铁磁存储（thermally assisted antiferromagnetic memory）提供合成前筛选候选材料的实用途径，推进了对绝缘反铁磁体超快控制的理解。

Abstract: Antiferromagnets combine terahertz spin dynamics with insensitivity to stray fields, and how quickly their order can be manipulated sets the speed limit on device operation. Femtosecond optical pulses demagnetize antiferromagnetic insulators on timescales that span picoseconds to nanoseconds across compounds, and no material parameter is known that accounts for the spread or predicts where a new compound will fall. In a compensated antiferromagnet, no angular momentum needs to leave the spin system, so the rate is set by energy flow from the lattice into the spins. Time-resolved second-harmonic generation experiments show that Cr2O3 demagnetizes within 2 ps once the lattice is driven above the Neel temperature, two orders of magnitude faster than the structurally similar FeBO3. First-principles calculations trace the disparity to exchange striction: short Cr-Cr contacts make the exchange coupling tenfold more sensitive to atomic displacements and widen the phase space for phonon decay into magnon pairs. Spin-lattice simulations with ab initio parameters reproduce the order of magnitude of the measured ratio. The derivative of the exchange coupling with respect to the ionic displacement thus emerges as a computable parameter that predicts how fast an insulating antiferromagnet can be demagnetized. The results advance our understanding of ultrafast control in insulating antiferromagnets, and suggest a practical pathway to screen candidate materials for thermally assisted antiferromagnetic memory before synthesis.

</details>


### [218] [Text-guided flow matching enables sample-efficient crystal structure generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.01076)
*Wentao Li*

Main category: cond-mat.mtrl-sci

TL;DR: 本文介绍了TFMat，一个基于文本条件化的流匹配（flow-matching）框架，利用结构化材料语言作为语义先验（semantic prior）引导晶体流生成器（CrystalFlow）。在Perov-5、Carbon-24和MP-20基准测试中，TFMat相比CrystalFlow提升单候选匹配率，并在20个候选中达到92.04%的MP-20匹配率；在从头生成（de novo generation）中改善了元素计数与密度分布对齐，同时保持组合选择输出的粗粒度性质一致性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 晶体生成器已能提出周期性结构，但其控制接口与材料设计中使用的混合描述符匹配不良。文本虽然能紧凑地结合组分、对称性、原型和性质线索，但此前尚不清楚此类信息能否引导基于流的晶体生成。

Method: 引入TFMat，一个文本条件化的流匹配框架，将结构化材料语言用作CrystalFlow生成器的语义先验，使文本提示能够参与并指导晶体结构的生成过程。

Result: 在Perov-5、Carbon-24和MP-20三个晶体结构预测基准上，TFMat相较CrystalFlow提升了单候选匹配率，在20个候选中达到92.04%的MP-20匹配率；在从头生成任务中改善了元素计数和密度分布对齐，同时在组合选择输出中保留了粗粒度性质一致性。

Conclusion: 结构化文本可作为一种可检查的控制层，将人类可读的材料设计意图转化为候选晶体，供后续的模拟与验证环节使用。

Abstract: Crystal generators can now propose periodic structures, but their control interfaces remain poorly matched to the mixed descriptors used in materials design. Text provides a compact way to combine composition, symmetry, prototype and property cues, yet it has not been clear whether such information can steer flow-based crystal generation. Here we introduce TFMat, a text-conditioned flow-matching framework that uses structured materials language as a semantic prior for a CrystalFlow generator. Across Perov-5, Carbon-24 and MP-20 crystal structure prediction benchmarks, TFMat improves one-candidate match rates over CrystalFlow and reaches a 92.04% MP-20 match rate with 20 candidates; in de novo generation, it improves element-count and density distribution alignment while retaining coarse property consistency in composition-selected outputs. These results position structured text as an inspectable control layer for translating human-readable materials intent into candidate crystals for downstream simulation and validation.

</details>


### [219] [Trap Dynamics and Conductivity Changes in AlGaN/GaN Heterostructures Under Ultrasonic Loading](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.01080)
*Vladyslav Kaliuzhnyi,Mykola Tymochko,Oleksandr Gudymenko,Oleg Olikh,Alexander Belyaev*

Main category: cond-mat.mtrl-sci

TL;DR: 本文研究了超声振动对MOCVD生长的AlGaN/GaN异质结构中电子输运的影响，结合变温霍尔效应与高分辨X射线衍射测量，发现超声加载通过声学驱动的亚稳态DX中心重排，引发持续声电导（persistent acoustoconductivity）及晶格参数变化，进而增加载流子浓度、降低迁移率。该发现为动态变形条件下的陷阱（trap）动力学提供新见解，并提示超声处理可作为抑制载流子俘获、提升GaN基器件性能与可靠性的潜在途径。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: AlGaN/GaN异质结构是高功率电子器件的关键材料，但其在缺陷处的电子俘获严重限制了器件的可靠性。作者旨在探究超声振动对这类结构中电子输运的影响，以理解动态变形条件下陷阱态的行为，并寻求可行的缓解策略。

Method: 研究对象为MOCVD生长的AlGaN/GaN异质结构，实验采用变温霍尔效应（temperature-dependent Hall effect）测量和高分辨X射线衍射（high-resolution X-ray diffraction）两种手段，系统考察超声加载前后材料的电学与结构响应。

Result: 实验显示，超声加载引发持续声电导现象及晶格参数变化，其机制归于超声驱动的亚稳态DX中心重排。该重排导致载流子浓度上升而迁移率下降，反映出声学应变对缺陷态的调制作用。

Conclusion: 研究揭示了超声激励下陷阱态动态响应机制，为理解缺陷俘获行为提供了新的物理视角；同时指出超声处理有望成为一种抑制俘获效应、增强GaN基器件性能与可靠性的实用手段。

Abstract: AlGaN/GaN heterostructures are critical for high-power electronic devices but suffer from electron trapping at defects, limiting reliability. We investigate how ultrasonic vibrations affect electron transport in MOCVD-grown AlGaN/GaN heterostructures by combining temperature-dependent Hall effect and high-resolution X-ray diffraction measurements. Here we demonstrate that ultrasonic loading induces persistent acoustoconductivity and lattice parameter changes, attributed to acoustically driven rearrangement of metastable DX centers. This leads to increased carrier concentration and decreased mobility, reflecting defect state modulation by acoustic strain. These findings provide new insights into trap dynamics under dynamic deformation and suggest ultrasonic treatment as a potential approach to mitigate trapping effects, thereby enhancing the performance and reliability of GaN-based devices.

</details>


### [220] [Pressure-regulated mechanochemistry at lithium metal-sulfide electrolyte interfaces](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.01088)
*Kunik Jang,Jaehwan Choi,Jang Wook Choi,Yousung Jung*

Main category: cond-mat.mtrl-sci

TL;DR: 该论文利用压敏感知的、电荷分辨的机器学习分子动力学模拟，结合密度泛函理论（DFT）验证，系统研究了堆叠压力的大小和负载几何如何调控Li||Li6PS5Cl界面处的相间层形成，发现压力效应是非单调的，并将外加压力确立为一种耦合相间化学、离子输运和缺陷演化的机械化学过程变量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 堆叠压力通常被认为仅是维持全固态锂金属电池中物理接触的手段，但它也可能改变反应性固-固界面的化学性质。作者旨在揭示压力大小和加载几何如何影响界面相间层的形成机制。

Method: 采用压敏感知的、电荷分辨的机器学习分子动力学模拟，并通过DFT进行验证，分析了压力大小与加载几何对Li||Li6PS5Cl界面相间层形成的影响。该方法还结合了电荷分辨动力学来识别早期相间层中的化学环境。

Result: 压力响应呈非单调特征：1千巴（kbar）压缩加速PS4分解和Li2S类有序化，而10–100千巴压缩则限制结构重排和长程结晶。电荷分辨动力学识别出与后续Li2S类有序化相关的硫中心、富锂早期相间层环境。在相同标称压力下，单轴加载比等静压加载加速界面反应更快；压力还以缺陷位置依赖的方式影响空洞闭合与死锂扩散。

Conclusion: 研究确立了外加压力作为一项机械化学过程变量，它同时耦合界面相间化学、离子输运和缺陷演化，为解释硫化物固态电池中的压力效应提供了机理框架。

Abstract: Stack pressure is commonly treated as a means of maintaining physical contact in all-solid-state lithium-metal batteries, but it can also alter the chemistry of reactive solid-solid interfaces. Here, using pressure-aware, charge-resolved machine-learning molecular dynamics validated against DFT, we determine how pressure magnitude and loading geometry regulate interphase formation at Li||Li6PS5Cl interfaces. The response is nonmonotonic: compression at 1 kbar accelerates PS4 decomposition and Li2S-like ordering, whereas 10-100 kbar compression restricts structural rearrangement and long-range crystallization. Charge-resolved dynamics further identify sulfur-centered, lithium-rich early-interphase environments associated with subsequent Li2S-like ordering. Uniaxial loading accelerates interfacial reaction relative to isostatic loading at the same nominal pressure. Pressure also changes void closure and dead-lithium spreading in a defect-location-dependent manner. These results establish applied pressure as a mechanochemical process variable coupling interphase chemistry, ion transport and defect evolution, providing a mechanistic framework for interpreting pressure effects in sulfide solid-state batteries.

</details>


### [221] [Autonomous discovery of new structure-plausibility laws for explainable and rapid crystal diagnosis and screening](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.01209)
*Zhilong Song,Lixue Cheng*

Main category: cond-mat.mtrl-sci

TL;DR: 该论文提出了一种基于自主智能体（autonomous agents）主动证伪方法发现的八条无机结构合理性规则（PRIS），用于在昂贵的密度泛函理论（DFT）计算或实验评估之前筛选晶体候选结构。PRIS编码了五种物理化学机制，其筛选性能远优于传统距离截断方法，并能解释合成难度、GNoME中低对称结构富集现象及伪造晶体报告中的错误元素归属问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 晶体生成器与工具使用型智能体（tool-using agents）产生的候选结构数量远超DFT能量计算、声子计算或实验评估所能处理的速度，因此决定哪些候选值得投入昂贵评估成为瓶颈。然而现有的大多数筛选仅检测原子重叠，无法提供化学层面的失败原因，亟需一种既能高效筛选又能解释失败机理的合理性规则。

Method: 智能体通过生成、测试并主动反证（actively refute）两百万条候选法则，最终提炼出八条无机结构合理性规则（PRIS）。这些法则编码了五种物理化学机制：短程排斥（short-range repulsion）、离子接触与堆积、静电平衡（electrostatic balance）、键价守恒（bond-valence conservation）以及晶体学位点复杂性（crystallographic site complexity）。

Result: 实验结构对PRIS法则满足率为82%至99%，而同时对鲍林规则（Pauling's rules）第2至5条的满足率仅6.5%；最严格法则集能检测出87.9%的破损晶体结构，而距离截断法仅能检测1.6%至3.2%。PRIS合理性评分与可合成性线性相关，其衍生合成评分（PSS）可筛除83.7%难以合成的结构，同时保留80.7%的实验结构。在性质条件反演生成中，PRIS与PSS可将DFT验证队列减少67.3%，同时保留99.2%满足目标体模量的候选。

Conclusion: PRIS将筛选从二元的通过或不通过判断转变为提供化学失败原因的解释性工具，能够解释GNoME中稀有低对称结构的富集原因，并揭示伪造晶体报告中错误元素分配隐藏于看似合理的坐标背后的现象。该工作表明自主智能体能通过主动反证发现指导计算与实验的物理化学规律。

Abstract: Crystal generators and tool-using agents propose structures faster than density functional theory (DFT) energy and phonon calculations or experiments can assess them. Deciding which candidates merit expensive assessment is therefore the bottleneck, yet most screens test little beyond atomic overlap and give no chemical reason for failure. Here, our agents generate, test and actively refute two million candidate laws, leaving eight Plausibility Rules for Inorganic Structures (PRIS). These laws encode five mechanisms: short-range repulsion, ionic contact and packing, electrostatic balance, bond-valence conservation and crystallographic site complexity. Experimental structures satisfy our law sets at 82--99%, but satisfy Pauling's rules 2--5 together at only 6.5%. The strictest set detects 87.9% of damaged crystal structures, whereas distance cutoffs detect only 1.6--3.2%. PRIS plausibility is linearly correlated with synthesizability, so the PRIS-derived synthesis score (PSS) explainably screens 83.7% of hard-to-synthesize structures while retaining 80.7% of experimental structures. In a property-conditioned inverse-design run, PRIS and PSS can reduce the DFT validation queue by up to 67.3% and keep 99.2% of the candidates whose DFT-validated bulk moduli reach the design target. Beyond screening, PRIS explains why GNoME remains enriched in rare low-symmetry structures and reveals how wrong-element assignments in falsified crystal reports hide behind plausible coordinates. PRIS moves screening from a pass-or-fail verdict to a chemical reason for failure, showing that autonomous agents can discover, by active refutation, physicochemical laws that guide calculations and experiments.

</details>


### [222] [Geometry-Controlled Magnetic and Electronic Landscapes in Anisotropic van der Waals Materials](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.01223)
*Maciej Śmiertka,Ewelina Cybula,Oliwia Janikowska,Bartosz Hołyński,Gayatri,Grzegorz Krasucki,Mariusz Hasiak,Kseniia Mosina,Zdenek Sofer,Adam Babiński,Maciej R Molas,Paulina Plochocka,Michał Baranowski*

Main category: cond-mat.mtrl-sci

TL;DR: 本文提出一种称为"几何电子学"（geometronics）的新概念，通过衬底几何结构局部重定向各向异性晶体，将均匀外部扰动转化为可编程的磁性与电子景观，并在CrSBr双层上实现了反铁磁与铁磁相共存及可切换激子势阱。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 范德华材料中电子结构的工程化通常依赖于组分、应变、静电门控和异质结构组装等手段，但缺乏不改变材料本身即可实现空间可编程电子与磁性景观的通用方法。

Method: 将反铁磁半导体CrSBr双层转移至倒金字塔形纳米压痕上，利用衬底几何结构局部改变晶体内各向异性方向，从而使晶体相对于外部磁场的局域取向驱动反铁磁相与铁磁相在同一连续晶体中共存。

Result: 所生成的磁性景观产生了深度达10至12 meV的可切换激子势阱，并通过空间分辨光谱直接可视化验证。

Conclusion: 几何电子学为在不修改材料本身的前提下确定性编程电子与磁性景观提供了通用途径，确立了衬底形貌作为利用层状范德华材料内在各向异性的新设计原则。

Abstract: Electronic structure in van der Waals materials is commonly engineered through composition, strain, electrostatic gating and heterostructure assembly. Here we introduce geometronics, a concept in which substrate geometry locally reorients an anisotropic crystal, transforming homogeneous external perturbation into programmable magnetic and electronic landscapes. We demonstrate this concept using a bilayer of the antiferromagnetic semiconductor CrSBr transferred onto an inverted pyramidal nanoindentation, where the local crystal orientation with respect to the external magnetic field drives the coexistence of antiferromagnetic and ferromagnetic phases within a single continuous crystal. The resulting magnetic landscape creates a switchable excitonic potential well of up to 10--12 meV, directly visualised by spatially resolved spectroscopy. More generally, geometronics provides a universal route for deterministically programmed electronic and magnetic landscapes without modifying the material itself. It therefore establishes substrate topography as a new design principle that exploits the intrinsic anisotropy of layered van der Waals materials.

</details>


### [223] [Thickness-Dependent Orbital-to-Spin Torque Signatures in Cr/Gd/Co Thin Films](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.01302)
*Tiago de Oliveira Schneider,Michel Heidkamp,Luana Caron,Inga Ennen,Matthias Opel,Alexey Arzumanov,Lambert Alff,Markus Meinert*

Main category: cond-mat.mtrl-sci

TL;DR: 该研究通过结合电学谐波霍尔测量（harmonic Hall measurements）与磁强测量（magnetometry），系统研究了Cr(10nm)/Gd(t_Gd)/Co(3nm)/TaO_x及其倒置堆叠结构中轨道力矩（orbital torque）的生成行为，并通过截面化学成分测绘揭示了显著的界面互混现象。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 本研究旨在系统探索Cr/Gd/Co多层膜及其倒置堆叠中的轨道力矩生成机制，揭示稀土层Gd厚度变化及堆叠顺序反转对类阻尼（damping-like, DL）与类场（field-like, FL）力矩效率的影响。

Method: 结合电学谐波霍尔测量与磁强测量获取自旋力矩效率数据，利用截面化学成分映射（cross-sectional chemical composition mapping）获得磁强数据的微观解释，分别采用朴素层模型（naive-layer model）与考虑界面混合的合金模型（alloy model）两种描述方案来阐释Gd插层厚度依赖性。

Result: 数据显示存在温度依赖的磁补偿点（magnetic compensation points），元素分析表明Gd与Co层之间存在明显的界面互混。提取的电场归一化类阻尼与类场力矩效率（ξ_DL^E与ξ_FL^E）对Gd插层厚度表现出不同的依赖关系。值得注意的是，倒置堆叠顺序时FL贡献发生符号翻转，而DL贡献在谐波霍尔测量中符号不变。

Conclusion: Gd插层的厚度与界面互混对该体系的轨道力矩生成具有重要影响；FL力矩随堆叠反转改变符号而DL力矩符号不变这一现象，为理解轨道力矩生成机制中不同贡献的对称性特征提供了新的实验证据。

Abstract: We studied orbital-torque generation in Cr(10nm)/Gd($t_{\mathrm{Gd}}$)/Co(3nm)/TaO$_x$ and the inverted stack Co(3nm)/Gd($t_{\mathrm{Gd}}$)/Cr(10nm) with $t_{\mathrm{Gd}}$ from 0 to $5\,\mathrm{nm}$ by combining electrical harmonic Hall measurements with magnetometry. A detailed understanding of the magnetometric data is obtained by cross-sectional chemical composition mapping. The data show temperature-dependent magnetic compensation points, while elemental analysis provides evidence of pronounced intermixing, in particular of Gd and Co layers. From the harmonic Hall dataset we extract the damping-like (DL) and field-like (FL) torque efficiencies normalized to the applied electric field, $ξ_{\mathrm{DL}}^{E}$ and $ξ_{\mathrm{FL}}^{E}$, and interpret their dependence on the Gd interlayer thickness using two different descriptions: (i) a naive-layer model and (ii) an alloy model that accounts for interfacial mixing. Notably, upon reversing the stack order, the FL contribution changes sign, whereas the DL contribution does not change sign within the harmonic Hall measurements.

</details>


### [224] [Simulating Continuous-Rotation 3D Electron Diffraction: A Multislice and Bloch Wave Framework](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.01378)
*Małgorzata K. Cabaj,Jacob Madsen,Toma Susi,Lukáš Palatinus,Paul B. Klar*

Main category: cond-mat.mtrl-sci

TL;DR: 本文基于abTEM Python包构建了一套计算流水线，用于模拟连续旋转三维电子衍射（3D ED）实验数据，可基于Bloch波或多层切片（multislice）方法进行计算，并通过多种化学体系验证了该方法的准确性与鲁棒性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 实测三维电子衍射（3D ED）实验的强度与计算值之间存在偏差，需要一套模拟流程来评估并量化各种结构参数和实验参数的影响，以改善理论与实验的一致性。

Method: 基于abTEM Python包构建计算流水线，支持Bloch波和multislice两种理论框架模拟连续旋转3D ED数据；任意取向的multislice计算通过大超胞（large supercells）和开窗（windowing）实现。研究关键模拟参数的收敛性并建立适用于准确高效模拟的参数组合。

Result: 通过立方硅及其拉伸和剪切变体案例，分析了电子动能、样品厚度、取向和对称性对模拟衍射强度的影响。进一步在包括立方SrTiO₃、单斜α-甘氨酸C₂H₅NO₂和三斜蓝晶石Al₂SiO₅在内的七种代表性化合物上验证了方法的适用性与鲁棒性。

Conclusion: 该三维电子衍射数据模拟框架为研究衍射强度对实验中难以系统性变化的实验参数的依赖关系提供了有效途径。

Abstract: To improve the agreement between measured and calculated intensities of three-dimensional electron diffraction (3D ED) experiments, a simulation pipeline is needed to assess and quantify the influence of various structural and experimental parameters. We present a computational pipeline, built upon the abTEM Python package, to simulate continuous-rotation 3D electron diffraction data based on either the Bloch wave or the multislice formalism. Multislice calculations in arbitrary orientations are achieved through large supercells and windowing. We investigate the convergence of key simulation parameters and test their consistency, establishing suitable parameters for accurate and efficient simulations. The pipeline's applicability and robustness are demonstrated through case studies on cubic silicon as well as stretched and sheared variants, analyzing the influence of electron kinetic energy, sample thickness, orientation, and symmetry on simulated diffraction intensities. Finally, we investigate a representative selection of seven compounds, including cubic $\mathrm{SrTiO_3}$, monoclinic $α$-glycine $\mathrm{C_2H_5NO_2}$, and triclinic kyanite $\mathrm{Al_2SiO_5}$ to validate the method. This framework for the simulation of 3D electron diffraction data establishes an approach to investigate the dependence of diffracted intensities on experimentally relevant parameters which are difficult to systematically investigate in experiments.

</details>


### [225] [Polar nanoregions and reentrant-like ferroelectric behavior in SrTiO$_3$](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.01446)
*Yuan-Jie Sun,Fei Yang,Long-Qing Chen*

Main category: cond-mat.mtrl-sci

TL;DR: 本文基于实空间成像实验中发现的SrTiO₃量子顺电体系中局域极性织构在中间温度（60–65 K）出现强度最大值的反常再入型行为，采用自洽相场理论阐明其源于极性模与反铁畸变（antiferrodistortive, AFD）模式间的类挠曲电耦合及有限波矢软化机制，并预测弱应变SrTiO₃中存在非常规的再入型相序列。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 近期SrTiO₃的实空间成像实验[Nature 656, 54 (2026)]揭示了局域极性织构在降温过程中的反常行为：其强度并非单调增强，而是在中间温度（约60–65 K）出现峰值后向量子顺电基态减弱。这种再入式减弱挑战了传统范式——即热涨落被抑制时有序倾向通常应增强——因此需要新的理论来解释该异常现象的微观起源。

Method: 本文采用自洽相场理论（self-consistent phase-field theory）进行研究，通过考虑本征极性涨落与AFD涨落的相互作用，分析类挠曲电耦合（flexoelectric-like coupling）如何杂化极性模与AFD模式。理论框架结合了模式杂交、有限波矢（finite-q）软化以及Brazovskii型不稳定性的分析。

Result: 理论表明，类挠曲电耦合强烈杂化极性模与AFD模。当未耦合模在约52 K处交叉时杂交最强，使较低杂交分支在有限波矢壳层上出现极小值，触发Brazovskii型不稳定性，显著增强极化关联并产生纳米尺度极性织构。远离交叉温度时，两模式失谐增大，杂交及相关有限q软化减弱，从而自然解释了局域极性织构仅在中温窗口增强的异常现象。此外，理论预测弱应变SrTiO₃从零温加热时呈现铁电→顺电→极性纳米区→顺电的非常规再入型演化序列。

Conclusion: 该工作揭示了SrTiO₃中极性纳米区（polar nanoregion）形成的微观机制，为实验中发现的局域极性织构局限于中间温度窗口提供了超越量子涨落解释的理论方案，并提供了可检验的新预测。

Abstract: Recent real-space imaging in quantum paraelectric SrTiO$_3$ [Nature 656, 54 (2026)] reveals that local polar textures do not continuously grow upon cooling, but reach a maximum intensity at intermediate temperatures around 60-65~K and weaken again toward the quantum paraelectric ground state. Such a reentrant-like weakening of local polar textures challenges the conventional paradigm in which ordering tendencies generally strengthen as thermal fluctuations are suppressed. Here we employ a self-consistent phase-field theory showing that this anomalous behavior naturally arises from the interplay between intrinsic polar and antiferrodistortiv (AFD) fluctuations. We demonstrate that flexoelectric-like coupling strongly hybridizes the polar and AFD modes. As the uncoupled polar and AFD modes cross near 52~K, their hybridization is maximized, driving the lower hybridized branch to develop a minimum on a finite-wave-vector shell. This finite-$q$ softening triggers a Brazovskii-type instability, strongly enhancing polarization correlations and producing nanoscale polar textures. Away from the crossing temperature, the two modes become increasingly detuned, weakening their hybridization and the associated finite-$q$ softening. These results reaveal the origin of the formation of polar nanoregions in SrTiO$_3$, naturally explaining the unexpected confinement to an intermediate-temperature window and providing a mechanism beyond the quantum-fluctuation-based interpretation suggested by experiment. Furthermore, we predict an unconventional reentrant-like sequence in weakly strained SrTiO$_3$, evolving from ferroelectric to paraelectric, polar-nanoregion, and eventually paraelectric regimes upon heating from zero temperature.

</details>


### [226] [Accelerating dynamic simulations of photoexcited materials and their evolution by electron-informed machine learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.01492)
*Yunzhe Jia,Fankai Xie,Yunfei Bai,Miao Liu,Cui Zhang,Sheng Meng*

Main category: cond-mat.mtrl-sci

TL;DR: 本文开发了非绝热激发态机器学习分子动力学（EMLMD）模拟框架，通过从高精度实时含时密度泛函理论（rt-TDDFT）基准模拟中校准非平衡电子信息（如电子温度），重建激发态势能面，并原生纳入激发态电子-声子耦合与声子非简谐效应。该框架在保证第一性原理精度的同时实现大规模激发态原子演化模拟，成功解析了铋光诱导相变中的相干声子竞争及硒光致非晶化中的微观动力学。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 电子激发引发的非绝热耦合电子-核动力学是众多光诱导功能现象的微观机制基础，但直接从头算模拟的计算代价过高，阻碍了大规模激发态动力学研究。

Method: 构建激发态机器学习分子动力学框架，从rt-TDDFT基准模拟中严格校准光激发后的非平衡电子温度等电子信息，据此准确重建激发态势能面（PES），并天然纳入标准机器学习分子动力学所缺失的激发态电子-声子耦合和光诱导声子非简谐性。

Result: 大规模EMLMD模拟在铋中解析了时间及动量分辨的声子动力学，揭示了光诱导相变过程中光生相干声子与热声子的竞争；同时同步解析了硒光致非晶化中原子尺度的微观动力学及全局结构重排。

Conclusion: EMLMD在精度与效率之间实现平衡，为复杂激发态分子动力学研究中的关键挑战提供了通用解决方案。

Abstract: Nonadiabatic coupled electron-nuclear dynamics upon electronic excitation underpin the microscopic mechanism and rational modulation of diverse photoinduced functional phenomena in materials, yet their direct first-principles simulations remain computationally demanding. Here we develop a framework for nonadiabatic excited-state machine-learning molecular dynamics (EMLMD) simulations, where the nonequilibrium electronic information upon photoexcitation such as electron temperature is rigorously calibrated from high-precision real-time time-dependent density functional theory (rt-TDDFT) benchmark simulations, enabling accurate reconstruction of excited-state potential energy surfaces (PES). This framework natively incorporates the excited-state electron-phonon couplings and intrinsically captures photoinduced phonon anharmonicity, both of which are missing in standard machine learning molecular dynamics, thus delivering first-principles-level accuracy for excited-state atomic evolutions. Large-scale EMLMD simulations resolve time- and momentum-resolved phonon dynamics in photoexcited materials, directly uncovering the competition between photogenerated coherent phonons and thermal phonons during photoinduced phase transition of bismuth. It also simultaneously resolves elusive atomic-scale microscopic dynamics and global structural rearrangement for selenium photoamorphization. Balancing high accuracy and efficiency, EMLMD offers a versatile paradigm to tackle key challenges in the study of complex excited-state molecular dynamics.

</details>


### [227] [A relational fabrication-to-modeling database for memristor devices](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.01500)
*Lai Gan,Guoyang Huang,Deepika Yadav,Spyros Stathopoulos,Ben D. Rowlinson,Themis Prodromakis*

Main category: cond-mat.mtrl-sci

TL;DR: 本文介绍了首个公开的氧化物忆阻器（oxide-based memristor）大规模关系型数据库，整合了6,190个器件、161,006项已验证实验和超过1.69亿条电气点记录，涵盖制造、晶圆映射、电气表征与建模数据，以规范化的SQLite格式公开并提供模式文档与图形界面支持。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 忆阻器/阻变存储器（RRAM）器件的性能高度依赖制备工艺、晶圆位置及测量协议，但缺乏将实验元数据与电气测量相结合且保持实验流程关联的公开大规模数据集，制约了数据驱动的忆阻器技术研究。

Method: 构建了一个规范化的关系数据库，将TiN/HfOx/TiN、Pt/TiOx/AlOy/Pt和Pt/TiOx/Pt三种叠层结构中6,190个忆阻器器件的制造信息、晶圆映射、电气表征及建模数据进行系统整合；数据库以标准化且带索引的SQLite文件发布，配套模式文档、图形用户界面和实证建模示例。

Result: 数据库包含161,006项已验证实验和超过1.69亿条电气点记录，可描述电阻开关的形成过程（electroforming）、电流-电压非线性、存储窗口（memory window, R_off/R_on）、开关动力学及短期易失性（short-term volatility）等关键行为特征。

Conclusion: 该数据库支持溯源查询（provenance-aware querying）、统计分析和数据驱动的忆阻器应用开发，为未来忆阻器技术的研发提供了公开可复用的数据基础设施。

Abstract: Resistive random access memory (RRAM) devices, also known as memristors, are highly dependent on fabrication, location on the wafer, and measurement protocols. However, openly available large-scale memristor datasets remain scarce, particularly those that combine experimental metadata with electrical measurements and preserve explicit links across the experimental workflow. Here, we present a comprehensive relational database of automated electrical characterization data from oxide-based memristors. The database links 6,190 memristor devices across TiN/HfOx/TiN, Pt/TiOx/AlOy/Pt, and Pt/TiOx/Pt stacks to 161,006 validated experiments and over 169 million electrical point records. It integrates fabrication, wafer mapping, electrical characterization, and modeling to describe electroforming, current-voltage non-linearity, memory windows (R_off/R_on), switching dynamics, and short-term volatility. Released as a normalized and indexed SQLite database with schema documentation, graphical user interfaces, and examples of empirical modeling, this resource supports provenance-aware querying, statistical analysis, and data-driven applications for the development of future memristor technologies.

</details>


### [228] [First-principles optical response of shock-compressed LiF: Quasiparticle, excitonic, and ionic-temperature effects](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.01512)
*M. S. Fadeev*

Main category: cond-mat.mtrl-sci

TL;DR: 本文采用DFT+G₀W₀+BSE方法并结合QMD考虑离子温度效应，系统性计算了LiF在常压与冲击压缩条件下的折射率色散及随密度与温度的变化关系，在常压和冲击实验中均获得了与观测值接近的结果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LiF在冲击压缩实验中常用作光学窗口，但其折射率在不同压力、温度和波长条件下的准确理论预测仍具挑战，此前第一性原理计算的精度有待提升。

Method: 采用DFT+G₀W₀+BSE组合方法计算准粒子能带与激子效应，通过QMD引入离子温度效应，分别计算常压下的光子能量色散n(ω)与k(ω)以及冲击压缩下532 nm和1550 nm处的n(ρ,T)；并计算准粒子带结构、态密度及结构弹性基准。

Result: 常压下G₀W₀给出14.25 eV的准粒子带隙（实验值14.2 eV），BSE复现12.5 eV的主激子峰（实验值12.6 eV）；主Hugoniot上至140 GPa时，1550 nm处的n(ρ)与冲击数据吻合最佳，优于此前第一性原理计算；约110–120 GPa以上QMD产生相对冷曲线的下降趋势；压力下带隙增大，约50 GPa处转变为Γ→L间接带隙。

Conclusion: 准粒子和激子效应对准确的光学色散不可或缺，HSE杂化泛函无法再现光学谱；QMD结果提示离子动力学可能偏离线性Gladstone-Dale关系；约1400 GPa探测计算得到约24 eV的带隙，不支持带隙闭合的金属化路径。

Abstract: We investigate the refractive index $n$ of LiF using DFT+G$_0$W$_0$+BSE, with ionic-temperature effects included through QMD. We calculate photon-energy dispersions $n(ω)$ and $k(ω)$ at ambient pressure and $n(ρ,T)$ at 532 and 1550 nm under shock compression, where $ρ$ and $T$ vary together. Quasiparticle band structures at ambient and compressed conditions and the ambient-pressure orbital-projected density of states connect the optical response to the electronic structure. At ambient pressure, G$_0$W$_0$ yields a quasiparticle gap of 14.25 eV, close to the experimental 14.2 eV, while BSE reproduces the main excitonic feature at 12.5 eV, close to the observed 12.6 eV. Quasiparticle and excitonic effects are thus essential for accurate optical dispersion; the hybrid HSE functional does not reproduce the optical spectra. Along the principal Hugoniot up to 140 GPa, $n(ρ)$ agrees closely with shock data at 1550 nm and acceptably at 532 nm, and agrees better overall than earlier first-principles calculations. Above approximately 110-120 GPa, QMD produces a downturn relative to cold $n(ρ)$ curves, suggesting that ionic dynamics may contribute to deviations from the linear $n$-$ρ$ Gladstone-Dale relation. QMD-sampling and finite-k-grid uncertainties are estimated, and transition-peak broadening is assessed using a recently proposed criterion. The gap increases under pressure, with a transition to a $Γ\to\mathrm L$ indirect gap near 50 GPa. An exploratory calculation at approximately 1400 GPa finds a gap of about 24 eV, disfavoring gap-closure metallization. Structural and elastic benchmarks of the underlying models are also provided.

</details>


### [229] [No source-free exchange-correlation magnetic fields in non-collinear spin DFT](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.01533)
*Ester Livshits,Roi Baer*

Main category: cond-mat.mtrl-sci

TL;DR: 本文证明非共线自旋密度泛函理论（non-collinear spin density functional theory）中交换关联磁场（xc magnetic field）无散条件 $\nabla\cdot\mathbf{B}_{\mathrm{xc}}=0$ 并非精确条件，它违反全局自旋旋转对称性。通过构造无源（source-free）交换关联泛函并与局域共线（locally collinear）泛函对比，发现在Mn₂体系中无源构造给出正确的反铁磁态和耦合符号，但对称性破坏仍导致非物理结果：刚体自旋旋转改变交换关联能约1 eV，弱均匀场感生磁化方向垂直于外场。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 检验非共线自旋密度泛函理论中常用的麦克斯韦型无源条件 $\nabla\cdot\mathbf{B}_{\mathrm{xc}}=0$ 是否精确，并评估该条件在实践中的影响。

Method: 在实施无源条件的最优方式下构造修改的父代交换关联泛函，使Kohn-Sham过程产生无散、可表达为泛函导数、对均匀气体精确且能施加局域扭矩的 $\mathbf{B}_{\mathrm{xc}}$；以LSDA为父代在Mn₂上对比无源（SoF）与局域共线（LoC）两种构造。

Result: 无源构造与局域共线LSDA均给出过短的键长和过大的键能，但磁态判别不同：SoF得到实验观察的 $^1\Sigma^+_g$ 反铁磁态且耦合符号与大小正确，LoC则得到高自旋 $^{11}\Pi_u$ 铁磁态且符号错误。然而对称性破坏造成严重问题：刚体自旋旋转改变 $E_{\mathrm{xc}}$ 约1 eV，弱均匀场引发垂直于外场的磁化。

Conclusion: 尽管无源构造在Mn₂上看似成功预测了反铁磁态，该条件本质上是虚假的（spurious），其应用导致的非物理结果（对称性破坏与响应方向异常）表明 $\nabla\cdot\mathbf{B}_{\mathrm{xc}}=0$ 不应被视为非共线自旋密度泛函理论的精确约束条件。

Abstract: We show that the Maxwellian equation $\nabla\cdot\mathbf{B}_{\mathrm{xc}}=0$, where $\mathbf{B}_{\mathrm{xc}}$ is the exchange-correlation (xc) magnetic field, is not an exact condition of non-collinear spin density functional theory: it violates global spin-rotation symmetry, the operation which turns the electron spins. How much does this matter in practice? To find out we impose the condition anyway, in the best way we can: we modify any parent xc functional so that the Kohn-Sham procedure yields a divergence-free $\mathbf{B}_{\mathrm{xc}}$ that is a functional derivative, stays exact for the homogeneous gas, and exerts the local torques a locally collinear functional cannot. Any non-physical result from the calculation must be due to the spurious condition, not the implementation. On Mn$_2$ with LSDA as parent, the source-free (SoF) construction and locally collinear (LoC) LSDA both give too short a bond and too large a bond energy, but differ on the magnetic exchange coupling and on the state itself: SoF finds the experimentally observed $^1Σ^+_g$ antiferromagnet, with a coupling of the right sign and roughly the right size, while LoC finds a high-spin $^{11}Π_u$ ferromagnet and the wrong sign. Despite this seeming success, breaking the symmetry has serious consequences: a rigid spin rotation of the magnetization changes $E_{\mathrm{xc}}$ by about 1 eV (it should not change at all), and the magnetization induced by a weak uniform field points perpendicular to the field (it should be antiparallel to it).

</details>

### [230] [Efficiently classifying shocks in complex systems requires dormant reporters](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.00725)
*David A. Brewster,Philippe Cluzel*

Main category: q-bio.QM

TL;DR: 本文研究复杂动力系统中如何选择少量报告节点（reporter nodes）以区分外部冲击信号。研究发现传统的"最灵敏节点"排序策略在匹配理想初始条件存在误差时会严重失效，而最优面板需混搭两种类型的报告者：广泛响应型（promiscuous）和潜伏型（dormant），且仅需极少数节点即可准确识别冲击类型。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 许多自然与工程系统是大型复杂网络，外部扰动可驱动系统沿不同动力学路径演化。识别具体发生了哪种扰动对诊断、控制和预测至关重要，但实际中往往只能同时监测少数组件，因此需要确定应监测哪些组件以最大化冲击分类能力。

Method: 采用一个连接异质性（heterogeneous connectivity）的简单动力学模型，考虑重复试验无法精确复现理想初始条件的情况，系统比较了基于灵敏度（sensitivity）排序的报告者面板与基于混合策略的面板在冲击分类任务中的表现，并提出了高效的面板组装算法。

Result: 当初始条件复现不完美时，灵敏度排序型面板远非最优，且其劣势随噪声增大而扩大。最优面板需混搭两类报告者：广泛响应型响应大多数冲击但结果在试验间波动大，潜伏型仅强响应少数冲击但结果稳定。随噪声增大，两类报告者的代价趋于平衡。仅需极少量报告者即可准确识别冲击类型，且混合面板显著优于任一单一类型的面板。

Conclusion: 为大型复杂动力学系统的监测提供了低成本的实际设计原则：最优监测面板应混合不同类型属性的报告节点，而非仅依据个体灵敏度选择最灵敏位点；极少的报告者数量足以实现高精度冲击分类。

Abstract: Many natural and engineered systems are large complex networks of interacting components, and external perturbations drive them along different dynamical paths. Identifying which perturbation occurred matters for diagnosis, control, and prediction. Yet often times only a few components can be jointly monitored. Which components should be monitored? Experimental practice usually favors placing reporters at the most sensitive sites, where perturbations produce the largest effects. Using a simple dynamical model for complex systems with heterogeneous connectivity, we ask how sparse reporter panels should be chosen to classify shocks from partial trajectories when repeated trials only approximately reproduce an ideal initial condition. Once that reproduction is imperfect, sensitivity ranked panels fall far short of optimal, and the shortfall grows with the noise. We find that the best panels mix two kinds of reporters. A promiscuous reporter responds to most shocks, so it separates them mainly by degree, and degree fluctuates from trial to trial. A dormant reporter responds to only a few shocks but strongly, and its answers do not scatter as much between trials. As noise grows, the cost of losing a dormant reporter rises to meet the cost of losing a promiscuous one. Panels of either kind alone classify worse than the mixture, and no property of the members collected individually explains the ordering. Most of all, we find that only a minuscule number of reporters are needed on a panel to accurately identify which shock hit the system. We implement an efficient algorithm to assemble such a panel. Together these results provide a low cost practical design principle for monitoring large complex dynamical systems.

</details>


<div id='cond-mat.str-el'></div>

# cond-mat.str-el [[Back]](#toc)

### [231] [Green-function Zeros Encode Competing Mott and Charge-ordering Scales](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.00124)
*Peizhi Mai,Philip W. Phillips*

Main category: cond-mat.str-el

TL;DR: 本文通过解析与数值方法研究扩展Hubbard模型，确立了强关联物质中的一个新范式：格林函数零点（Green function zeros）的色散关系由微观自旋-自旋关联和缺陷运动学共同决定，且该色散在Mott绝缘体与棋盘电荷密度波相之间的转变中发生不连续变化，反映了强关联导致的有序物理。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 关联绝缘体（correlated insulators）虽然缺乏低能准粒子极点（quasiparticle poles），但其格林函数通过零点保持清晰的动量结构；然而零点的精确物理含义此前尚不明确，本文旨在阐明其深层意义。

Method: 作者同时采用解析方法与数值方法对扩展Hubbard模型（extended Hubbard model）进行研究，系统分析格林函数零点的色散行为及其与自旋关联、电荷缺陷运动学的关系，并考察了最近邻外跳迁项对该物理的影响。

Result: 研究发现格林函数零点的色散由微观自旋-自旋关联（spin-spin correlations）与缺陷运动学（defect kinematics）共同决定；该色散在Mott绝缘体相与棋盘电荷密度波（checkerboard charge-density wave）相之间的一级相变处发生不连续变化；上述物理对加入更远邻跳迁具有鲁棒性，后者仅对自旋关联或电荷缺陷运动学进行微调。

Conclusion: 格林函数零点的色散本身编码了由强关联所产生的有序物理（ordering），这为理解强关联物质提供了一种新范式，超越了传统聚焦于极点行为的分析框架。

Abstract: While correlated insulators are devoid of low-energy quasiparticle poles, their Green functions retain clean momentum structure through zeros. However, precisely what zeros imply is not clear. By studying the extended Hubbard model both analytically and numerically, we establish a new paradigm for strongly correlated matter: the dispersion of Green function zeros is determined by both microscopic spin-spin correlations and defect kinematics. In fact, we find that the dispersion changes discontinuously across the transition between the Mott and the checkerboard charge-density wave phases as is expected for a first-order transition. This physics is robust to the inclusion of further neighbor hopping which simply fine tunes the spin-correlation or charge-defect kinematics. We conclude that it is the {\it dispersion} of the Green-function zeros that encode the physics of ordering resultant from the strong correlations.

</details>


### [232] [Higher-Winding Fractionalization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.00139)
*Kishore Iyer,Christophe Mora,Daniele Guerci*

Main category: cond-mat.str-el

TL;DR: 本文研究了高绕数斯格明子（higher-winding skyrmion）纹理产生的具有多量子磁通量（multiple flux quanta per unit cell）的涌现磁场（emergent magnetic field），发现增加该磁场在晶格尺度上的不均匀性会驱动一个贝雷辛斯基-科斯特利茨-索利斯（BKT）转变，使系统在分数量子化液相（fractionalized liquid）与晶体绝缘相（crystalline dielectric state）之间转变，且该转变具有拓扑特征：许多体极化（many-body polarization）定义的量子化绕数（quantized winding number）仅在分数量子化相中非零。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 高绕数斯格明子纹理可以产生每个原胞含多个磁通量量子的涌现磁场，这为在微观原胞整数填充下实现分数量子反常霍尔态（fractionalized quantum anomalous Hall states）提供了可能路径，值得深入研究其中的相变行为及其拓扑性质。

Method: 通过调控涌现磁场的晶格尺度不均匀性（lattice-scale inhomogeneity），分析系统在分数量子化液相与晶体介电态之间的BKT相变，并借助通量插入（flux insertion）条件下多体极化（many-body polarization）所定义的量子化绕数提供拓扑诊断标志。

Result: 增大涌现磁场的晶格尺度不均匀性会驱动分数量子化液相与晶体介电态之间的BKT转变。该相变具有拓扑标记：通量插入下的多体极化绕数在分数量子化相中为量子化非零值，在介电晶体中则消失为零。

Conclusion: 研究表明，即便在微观原胞整数填充下，通过高绕数斯格明子产生的多磁通量量子涌现磁场也能实现分数量子化态，且该体系中的BKT相变具有明确的拓扑可观测诊断量（多体极化绕数），为识别和表征此类拓扑相变提供了有效途径。

Abstract: Higher-winding skyrmion textures can generate emergent magnetic fields with multiple flux quanta per unit cell. This opens an intriguing route toward fractionalization, allowing fractionalized quantum anomalous Hall states to arise even at integer filling of the microscopic unit cell. We show that, in this setting, increasing lattice-scale inhomogeneity of the emergent magnetic field drives a Berezinskii--Kosterlitz--Thouless (BKT) transition between a fractionalized liquid and a crystalline dielectric state. This transition carries a topological signature: under flux insertion, the many-body polarization defines a quantized winding number that is nonzero in the fractionalized phase and vanishes in the dielectric crystal.

</details>


### [233] [Engineering tunable $p$-wave magnetism in antiferromagnetic bilayers](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.00277)
*Yu-Han Lin,Jin-Wei Dong,Ziqiang Wang,Sen Zhou*

Main category: cond-mat.str-el

TL;DR: 本文提出一种基于对称性引导的方案，在由两个非平行共线磁矩构成的AB堆叠反铁磁方形晶格双层体系中，通过面内相对平移选择性地打破保护自旋简并的对称性，从而在无自旋轨道耦合（spin-orbit coupling）的完全补偿磁性态中产生可调的p波自旋劈裂（p-wave spin-splitting），并通过平均场方法进一步揭示该体系中自发涌现的p波磁性相图。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 二维体系中p波磁性（p-wave magnetism）的构建与调控此前缺乏最小且可行的平衡态平台。现有路线通常依赖自旋轨道耦合或复杂的磁序设计，而如何在无自旋轨道耦合的全补偿磁性状态中产生奇宇称自旋劈裂并实现连续调控仍是一个悬而未决的问题。

Method: 该方法基于对称性设计路线（symmetry-guided route）：构造由两个AB堆叠反铁磁方形晶格组成的双层系统，两层共线磁矩彼此不平行；通过施加面内相对平移来选择性地打破保护自旋简并的对称性，同时保留与时间反演相关的约束，从而生成奇宇称自旋劈裂。随后将双层体系映射为有效的键调制（bond-modulated）方形晶格，并在平均场（mean-field）框架内研究相应的Hubbard模型，计算相图。

Result: 研究结果表明：面内相对位移能够连续调控p波自旋劈裂的方向、幅度及其关联的自旋响应；平均场相图计算显示，体系中存在广泛的参数区域，其中p波磁性以共面（coplanar）或手征性（chiral）自旋纹理的形式自发涌现。

Conclusion: 该工作为在二维体系中实现和操控p波磁性建立了一个最小的平衡态平台，为无自旋轨道耦合条件下的磁性调控提供了潜在的实验操控手段。

Abstract: We propose a symmetry-guided route to engineer tunable $p$-wave magnetism in a bilayer system composed of two AB-stacked antiferromagnetic square lattices with their collinear moments nonparallel to each other. We show that an in-plane relative shift between the two layers selectively breaks the symmetries protecting spin degeneracy while preserving time-reversal-related constraints, thereby generating odd-parity spin splitting in a fully compensated magnetic state without spin-orbit coupling. The direction and the magnitude of the resulting $p$-wave spin-splitting, together with the associated spin responses, can be continuously tuned by the in-plane displacement, providing a potential knob for control. We further map the bilayer system onto an effective bond-modulated square lattice and investigate the corresponding Hubbard model within a mean-field framework. The calculated phase diagram reveals extended regions where $p$-wave magnetism with coplanar or chiral spin textures emerges spontaneously. Our results establish a minimal equilibrium platform for realizing and manipulating $p$-wave magnetism in two-dimensional systems.

</details>


### [234] [Bound states, resonances, and their thermodynamic properties in pseudospin-1 systems with short-range impurities](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.00433)
*E. V. Gorbar,Pavlo Sukhachov*

Main category: cond-mat.str-el

TL;DR: 本文研究了由圆形势阱模拟的短程杂质在带隙和无带隙赝自旋-1（pseudospin-1）系统中平坦带和色散带附近诱导的束缚态（bound states）与共振态（resonances）。研究发现，源自平坦带的束缚态呈现出与赝自旋-1/2费米子情形截然不同的特性：任意总角动量下均可出现束缚态，其能量随角动量增大而降低，且波函数定域在势阱边缘并随角动量增大而增强。基于Anderson模型进一步获得了热力学势、熵密度和热容。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 赝自旋-1系统中的平坦带（flat band）具有独特的电子性质，但短程杂质诱导的束缚态与共振态在这些系统中的行为尚未被充分理解。本研究旨在阐明平坦带与色散带附近的杂质态特征，并揭示其与赝自旋-1/2系统之间的本质差异。

Method: 通过将短程杂质建模为圆形势阱（circular potential wells），在带隙和无带隙的赝自旋-1系统中系统分析平坦带与色散带附近的束缚态和共振态；利用Anderson模型考察无序势中独立电子的热力学性质，计算热力学势、熵密度和热容。

Result: 源自平坦带的束缚态与赝自旋-1/2系统的束缚态存在显著差异：在带隙系统中，与较高色散带附近的束缚态不同，平坦带衍生的束缚态对任意总角动量均可出现；高角动量（j）束缚态的能量随|j|增加而降低，波函数定域于势阱边缘且定域程度随|j|增大而增强。在平坦带束缚态主导的区域内，熵密度随温度单调增加并趋于饱和，热容则呈现单一极大值。

Conclusion: 赝自旋-1系统中平坦带衍生的杂质束缚态具有多分量的独特物理起源，导致其存在条件、能量色散和空间分布均与赝自旋-1/2系统及色散带束缚态不同，并在局域态密度中留下可识别的特征；这些态在热力学性质上导致了熵密度饱和与热容单峰等可观测行为。

Abstract: Bound states and resonances induced by short-range impurities modeled by circular potential wells are analyzed in the vicinity of flat and dispersive bands in gapped and gapless pseudospin-1 systems. We find that the bound and resonant states derived from the flat band show unusual characteristics originating from the multicomponent structure of pseudospin-1 fermions, which are distinct from those for pseudospin-$\tfrac{1}{2}$ fermions. Contrary to gapped Dirac systems and unlike bound states in the vicinity of the upper dispersive band, the bound states derived from the flat band occur for any value of the total angular momentum. The energies of these bound states with higher angular momentum $j$ tend to decrease with $|j|$. In addition, it is found that their wave functions are localized at the potential well edge and the localization increases with $|j|$. The signatures of the impurity states in the local density of states are determined. Using the Anderson model for independent electrons in the disorder potential, the thermodynamic potential, entropy density, and heat capacity are obtained. In the regime dominated by bound states derived from the flat band, the entropy density monotonically increases with temperature and saturates, whereas the heat capacity exhibits a single maximum.

</details>


### [235] [Correlation-Driven Nonlinear Magnetoelectric Response in an Altermagnet: A Dynamical Mean-Field Study](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.00697)
*Robert Peters,Jun Ōiké*

Main category: cond-mat.str-el

TL;DR: 本文利用动力学平均场理论（dynamical mean-field theory）在强关联交变磁体（altermagnet）中研究了光学非线性磁电效应（NMEE），发现该效应在交变磁相中有限而在顺磁相中消失，其频率依赖可反映自旋分辨的谱结构与交变磁劈裂尺度，并可能通过调控至相互作用驱动的相边界附近来增强响应。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 与施加固定自旋劈裂的有效能带描述不同，需要一种自洽的方法来确定强关联体系中的交变磁有序及其光学非线性响应，从而探究光学NMEE能否作为关联交变磁有序的有效探针。

Method: 采用动力学平均场理论，自洽求解交变磁有序、电子谱与光学非线性响应，避免了人为预设自旋劈裂的有效能带近似；系统考察了不同相互作用强度与温度下的响应行为。

Result: NMEE在交变磁相中为有限值而在顺磁相中为零；其频率依赖反映了自旋分辨的谱结构并可估计特征交变磁自旋劈裂尺度。低温下减小相互作用强度（趋近磁相边界）可增强响应，而升高温度则抑制响应并在临界温度以上使其归零。

Conclusion: 光学NMEE可作为关联交变磁有序的探针；调控压力、应变或化学替代等参数使其逼近相互作用驱动的相边界，可能是最大化响应的有效途径。

Abstract: We investigate the optical nonlinear magnetoelectric effect (NMEE) in a strongly correlated altermagnet using dynamical mean-field theory. Unlike effective band descriptions with an imposed spin splitting, our approach determines the altermagnetic order, electronic spectrum, and optical nonlinear response self-consistently. We find that the NMEE is finite in the altermagnetic phase and vanishes in the paramagnetic phase. Its frequency dependence reflects the spin-resolved spectral structure and provides an estimate of the characteristic altermagnetic spin-splitting scale. Interaction and temperature tuning produce qualitatively different behavior: at low temperature, reducing the interaction strength toward the interaction-driven magnetic phase boundary enhances the response, whereas increasing the temperature suppresses it and drives it to zero above the critical temperature. These results establish the optical NMEE as a probe of correlated altermagnetic order and suggest that tuning parameters such as pressure, strain, or chemical substitution toward an interaction-driven phase boundary may provide a promising route to maximizing the response.

</details>


### [236] [Self-Healing Diffusion Monte Carlo applied to a simple fermionic model: A critical assessment of the method](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.01301)
*Michel Caffarel,Manon Pinar,Anthony Scemama*

Main category: cond-mat.str-el

TL;DR: 本文通过一个带周期边界条件的一维模型研究自修复扩散蒙特卡罗（Self-Healing Diffusion Monte Carlo, SHDMC）方法。模型引入反演对称性以模拟费米子波函数反对称性质，偶奇本征态分别对应玻色子和费米子扇区。研究发现SHDMC标准配方下节点演化呈动力学系统行为，存在吸引和排斥不动点，固定节点（fixed-node）能量随迭代增加且节点收敛到错误值，表明SHDMC并非总能收敛到正确解；修改节点更新准则以强调节点区域可部分缓解该问题，实现一般情形下的收敛很可能需要采用局域化基组。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 量子蒙特卡罗方法模拟费米子体系时面临符号问题（sign problem），SHDMC作为一种节点优化算法被提出以缓解这一问题。本文旨在考察SHDMC方法的可靠性和收敛行为，特别是在节点结构仅部分受对称性约束的非平凡情形下。

Method: 采用一维周期边界条件模型，引入反演对称性模拟费米子反对称性，将玻色子和费米子扇区分别建模为偶、奇本征态。将SHDMC迭代过程中的节点演化描述为动力学系统，分析其不动点的吸引与排斥特性；并尝试修改节点更新准则以赋予节点区域更大权重。

Result: 在标准SHDMC配方应用于该模型时，固定节点能量随迭代增加而上升，节点收敛到错误的数值，表明SHDMC在该情形下不能收敛到正确解。通过修改节点更新准则、增强节点区域的权重可部分修复该问题。一般情形下的收敛很可能需要使用局域化基组（localized basis set）。

Conclusion: SHDMC并非在所有情形下都能稳定收敛到正确固定节点解，标准配方可能存在系统性问题。节点更新准则的调整可部分改善收敛，而局域化基组的引入被视为实现通用情形下可靠收敛的关键前提。

Abstract: We investigate the Self-Healing Diffusion Monte Carlo (SHDMC) method using a one-dimensional model with periodic boundary conditions. An inversion symmetry is introduced to mimic the antisymmetry property of fermionic wavefunctions, with the bosonic and fermionic sectors being modeled by the even and odd eigenstates, respectively. As in realistic fermionic systems, the nodal structure is only partially constrained by symmetry, making this model a non-trivial testbed for nodal optimization algorithms such as SHDMC. We show that the nodal evolution under SHDMC iterations can be cast into a dynamical system exhibiting both attractive and repulsive fixed points. In the standard formulation of SHDMC applied to this model, the fixed-node energy is found to increase upon iteration, and the node converges to a wrong value, indicating that SHDMC does not always converge to the correct solution. We further show that this problem is partially cured by modifying the nodal update criterion to give more importance to the nodal region. Achieving convergence in the general case very likely requires the use of a localized basis set, as is the case for this model.

</details>

### [237] [Polarized quantum effects in countable signals from intense laser - electron beam interactions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.01494)
*Toseo Moritaka,Kensuke Homma,Kazunori Itakura*

Main category: physics.plasm-ph

TL;DR: 本文基于激光-电子束相互作用的精确计数实验，提出了一个使用光子包（photon packets）、多维相空间加权、层级网格累积分布函数和可变时间步长方法的精确蒙特卡洛（Monte-Carlo）模型，用以验证强场量子电动力学（quantum electrodynamics）效应。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 旨在验证强场量子电动力学效应，特别是光子发射的随机性、光子偏振和自旋不对称等量子效应，为未来高精度实验提供理论基础和预测工具。

Method: 开发了一个精确的蒙特卡洛模型，采用光子包配合多维相空间加权、层级网格累积分布函数以及可变时间步长方法，对激光-电子束相互作用中的辐射过程进行模拟。

Result: 该模型定量重现了先前SLAC E-144实验中光子能谱最高边缘和正电子产额；发现量子化辐射反作用（radiation back-reaction）决定高能边缘尾部及正电子产额，光子发射中的自旋不对称和自旋翻转决定高能尾部光子偏振，而电子对产生中的效应可通过大角度正电子散射观测。

Conclusion: 这些效应依赖于电子束能量与激光强度的组合，预测可在未来ELI-NP实验中通过亚千兆电子伏（sub-GeV）电子束和强度约10^22 W/cm^2的光学激光加以验证。

Abstract: We investigate the feasibility of precision counting experiments based on laser-electron beam interactions to verify strong-field quantum electrodynamic effects, with particular emphasis on the stochastic nature of photon emission, photon polarization, and spin asymmetry. A precise Monte-Carlo model is developed using photon packets with multi-dimensional phase-space weighting, hierarchical-mesh cumulative distribution functions, and a variable time-step method. This model quantitatively reproduces the highest edge of the photon energy spectrum and the positron yield in the previous SLAC E-144 experiment. The quantized radiation back-reaction determines the highest edge tail and thus the positron yield for higher laser intensities. The spin asymmetry in photon emission and spin flip determines photon polarization in the high-energy tail, while that in pair production can be observed through the large-angle positron scattering. Our simulations predict that these effects depend on the combination of the electron-beam energy and the laser intensity, and that they could be verified in future ELI-NP experiments using sub-GeV electron beams and optical lasers with intensities ~10^22W/cm2.

</details>
